Result: 合成样本对MDM方法的点估计有提升作用，但增益不大且存在较高不确定性，整体提升表现依赖于具体分类方法。对于其他分类算法，增益有限，部分场景效果微弱甚至无明显变化。

Conclusion: 合成的CVAE-EEG数据更适合作为具有类别结构和正确协方差特点的补充数据，但不足以完全替代真实EEG。增加合成数据对实际分类能力提升有限且需视具体分类器而定，相关增益较为保守。

Abstract: We investigate whether a generative model can supply useful synthetic motor-imagery (MI) electroencephalography (EEG) trials that improve the accuracy of independent downstream classifiers. We train a class-conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) with an integrated latent classifier on the Zhou motor-imagery dataset, using the learned per-class prior as a generator: sampling the prior for a given label and decoding it into a synthetic, label-consistent signal. A constraint on the covariance matrix of the generated data encourages preservation of covariance structure, and the model is trained with a schedule that alternates ordinary VAE training with a decoder-focused phase that sharpens the generative pathway used for augmentation. We measure the effect of adding synthetic trials to the training set under two evaluation protocols -- within-user (pooled 60/20/20 split across subjects) and cross-user (leave-one-subject-out, LOSO) -- across four representative EEG classification pipelines: Common Spatial Patterns with Linear Discriminant Analysis (CSP+LDA), tangent-space features with a Support Vector Machine (TGSP+SVM), Minimum Distance to Riemannian Mean (MDM), and a neural network based on EEGNetv4 (henceforth EEGNet). Results are aggregated across independent augmentation draws, random seeds (within-user), or leave-one-subject-out folds (cross-user), with uncertainty reported as 95\% confidence intervals (Student's $t$-distribution) computed over per-seed/per-fold averages. We find that synthetic EEG from the CVAE is most credible as a source of class-structured, covariance-like data rather than as a substitute for real raw EEG: it can raise the point estimate for MDM, but the broader augmentation claim remains conservative -- observed gains are small and classifier-dependent.

</details>


### [28] [Fast Fourier Convolutional GAN for 30 m Clear-Sky Land Surface Temperature Gap-Free Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22734)
*Marwa Alfouly,Smajil Halilovic,Nils Bochow,Thomas Hamacher,Niklas Boers,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于多模态快速傅里叶卷积生成对抗网络（GAN）的算法，用于修复Landsat高分辨率（30米）影像中由云层引起的数据缺失，生成无空缺的地表温度（LST）产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星获取的地表温度（LST）数据空间覆盖广，是地气相互作用研究的关键，但云层遮挡常导致大面积的数据缺口，尤其在高空间分辨率影像下，缺失重建极具挑战。开发一种高效、可扩展的无缺口LST数据生成流程，对于气候、生态等研究核心数据的持续获取至关重要。

Method: 作者提出了一种多模态快速傅里叶卷积生成对抗网络（GAN），利用快速傅里叶卷积获得全局感受野，结合多源数据（包括卫星观测和SAR数据）进行引导，实现30米分辨率的LST影像云污染像元修复。该方法可重建大面积缺失区域，并适用于全球接近全覆盖的辅助数据。

Result: 在全部LST分位数上，场景平均重建像元的均方根误差（RMSE）四分位距均保持在0.8 K至1.8 K之间。对于云层导致空缺超过70%的场景，该方法仍能够有效填补大范围缺失区域。

Conclusion: 本文方法可广泛生成无空缺LST产品，有效修复大面积因云层引起的高分辨率数据空缺，对LST相关研究和实际应用具有重要推动作用。

Abstract: Satellite-derived Land Surface Temperature (LST) provides spatially comprehensive data that ground stations cannot match. However, its utility is frequently limited by severe data gaps due to the presence of clouds. As LST is essential for understanding land-atmosphere interactions, numerous methods have been proposed to address this challenge. Yet, the development of a scalable and adaptable pipeline for generating gap-free LST datasets and reconstructing cloud-contaminated pixels remains challenging. Moreover, the reconstruction of extensive missing regions in fine-spatial-resolution observations is particularly difficult. To address this challenge, we propose a Multimodal Fast Fourier Convolutional GAN for reconstructing cloud-contaminated pixels in fine-resolution (30 m) Landsat imagery to generate gap-free clear-sky LST products. The method leverages Fast Fourier Convolution to enable a global receptive field across the image, and is guided by a stack of data consisting of satellite observations and Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) data. Across all LST quantiles, the interquartile range of scene-averaged RMSE (computed over reconstructed pixels) is consistently between 0.8 K and 1.8 K. The proposed approach enables the recovery of extensive missing regions, including scenes with more than 70% cloud-induced gaps, while relying on auxiliary data that are readily available at a near-global scale.

</details>


### [29] [Benchmarking the Domain Gap: Model Selection Instability Under Domain Shift in Video Capsule Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22736)
*Dan Hanson,Debesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了视频胶囊内镜（VCE）分类模型在不同数据集间的一致性，发现单一数据集的优异表现无法可靠预测跨数据集的性能。主张未来模型选择应关注跨目标的排名稳定性，而非单一数据集的峰值表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前VCE分类模型通常只在单一数据集内评估，但临床实际应用时需面对不同数据源、标注策略和患者群体。因此，研究模型跨数据集的泛化能力和稳定性迫切且重要。

Method: 作者选用Kvasir-Capsule、CV2024和Galar（标签共享部分）三大数据集，采用通用预训练骨干网络，在Kvasir-Capsule官方折上标准化微调，并将同一组模型在非源目标数据集（Galar和CV2024）上评测，分析了它们在两个非源目标上的排名一致性和表现分歧。

Result: 结果显示，在源域内排名靠前的模型在跨数据集时表现不稳定：Kvasir-Capsule上最优的模型在Galar上也优异，但在CV2024表现一般；Galar和CV2024之间相关性弱。换句话说，没有一个评测目标可以稳定预测其他目标的效果。该不稳定性在另一组以CV2024为源域训练的实验中被重复验证。

Conclusion: 胶囊内镜模型的选择与评价应以跨数据集的排名稳定性为核心指标，而不是仅关注在单一数据集上的最佳性能，这有助于提高模型实际临床部署的可行性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Video capsule endoscopy (VCE) classification is typically evaluated within a single dataset, yet clinical deployment demands robustness across acquisition sources, labeling policies, and patient populations. We examine this gap using Kvasir-Capsule, Capsule Vision 2024 (CV2024), and a shared-label subset of Galar. We fine-tune a suite of general-domain pretrained backbones on the official Kvasir-Capsule folds under a standardized protocol and evaluate the same checkpoints on two non-source targets within a documented shared-label decision space. We find that the predictive value of in-domain ranking is target-dependent: Kvasir-Capsule ranking aligns more closely with Galar than with CV2024, while the two non-source targets agree only weakly. Consequently, the strongest in-domain backbone leads on one target yet falls to mid-pack on the other, and no single evaluation target reliably predicts the others. A second CV2024-trained configuration set reproduces this target-dependent instability. We conclude that capsule endoscopy model selection should report cross-target ranking stability rather than peak single-dataset performance.

</details>


### [30] [Cortex: Compact Behavior Cloning for Quake with Frozen Visual Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22739)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了一个基于行为克隆（behavioral-cloning）的简易策略Cortex，在无需强化学习和显式记忆的条件下，在第一人称游戏Quake中的表现。结果显示，尽管Cortex无法通关，但在所有测试回合中均取得了初步进展，并且杀敌表现优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索在视觉丰富且复杂环境（如第一人称射击游戏）下，单纯利用行为克隆策略，在不依赖强化学习或显式记忆模块的情况下究竟能走多远。这有助于理解行为克隆策略的潜力与局限，也为简化决策策略提供依据。

Method: 提出Cortex模型，以冻结的DINOv3编码器获取视觉特征，搭建六层Transformer，参数~1100万，用于决策。数据来自Pixels2Play的Quake录像，离线学习键鼠操作。训练流程为四帧窗口，使用RTX 5080优化策略头，测试与基线对比及消融分析。

Result: Cortex策略未能完成一级关卡，但所有回合均能到达主要目标区域，大部分回合实现至少一次击杀。与其他已发布的行为克隆模型P2P-150M和NitroGen对比，Cortex在等时长测试下表现更佳。消融实验表明更密集的视觉token有助于提升战斗和生存能力。

Conclusion: Cortex在没有强化学习和显式记忆的情况下，在复杂视觉环境下表现出显著的学习能力，超越现有行为克隆基线，但仍遭遇协变量转移引发的失败现象，后续需针对性纠正数据以进一步提升性能。

Abstract: We study how far a deliberately simple behavioral-cloning policy can progress in a visually rich first-person game before adding reinforcement learning or explicit memory. Cortex is a compact Quake policy with 10.98 million trainable parameters in a six-layer transformer over a frozen DINOv3 encoder. It is trained on the Quake subset of the public Pixels2Play corpus: 6,849 recordings (about 474.7 hours), represented as 17.09 million cached decision frames with keyboard and mouse actions. One sampled training epoch uses 517,048 four-frame windows and takes 3.3 minutes of policy-head optimization on one RTX 5080, excluding one-time feature extraction. We evaluate two independent batches of 20 stochastic, 120-second episodes on Quake E1M1. Cortex does not complete the level, but every episode reaches the opening door, button room, and gate descent; 19 of 20 episodes in each batch record at least one kill. Under the same time-controlled harness, released P2P-150M and NitroGen checkpoints remain shallower in five matched-duration episodes each. These comparisons are limited by small reference samples and different native interfaces. Ablations show that denser visual tokens improve combat and survival, while longer optimization and naive action history improve offline metrics without consistently improving play. The remaining failures are consistent with covariate shift and motivate targeted corrective data. We release the policy implementation, checkpoint, and a representative rollout.

</details>


### [31] [A Diagnostic Gap Framework for Evaluating Reconstruction Fidelity in Weakly Supervised Mammography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22740)
*Vinceline Bertrand,Ionut Cardei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了diagnostic gap框架，可以衡量医学影像弱监督多阶段系统中，诊断和解释信息在重建等不同环节的保存情况。通过三种重建器和不同保真度横向对比，量化了诊断信号的损失情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标难以具体分析多阶段医学影像管线中每一环节（如重建、定位）对于临床相关诊断信息的保留或损失。需要有针对性的工具揭示任意环节对诊断性能的影响。

Method: 提出diagnostic gap评估框架，测量决策和解释信息在不同重建保真度下的保存情况。方法包括：1）采用三种条件重建模型（VQ-VAE-GAN、VAE-GAN、扩散模型SDEdit），2）选用感知距离分层（LPIPS指标）, 3）在特定病灶ROI区间，对AUC及归因相似度进行分析。

Result: 在高保真（自编码器级别）时，诊断效果和解释性几乎无损；在低保真（扩散模型）时两者均迅速崩溃，AUC显著降低，归因分数下降。诊断信息的丢失与重建保真度密切相关。

Conclusion: 诊断信息的损失并非重建本身的必然代价，而取决于重建质量。所提framework可架构无关地检测多阶段管线中诊断信号丢失的位置与程度。

Abstract: Weakly supervised pipelines for medical imaging have become increasingly popular over the years. These systems often include multiple stages and components, such as reconstruction, generation, and localization, yet standard evaluation metrics provide limited insight into whether clinically relevant information is preserved across each stage. We present the diagnostic gap framework, a practical evaluation tool that measures decision preservation and explanation preservation as a function of measured reconstruction fidelity. To isolate the effect of reconstruction from localization, we evaluate on curated lesion ROI crops using a fidelity ladder of three class-conditional reconstructors---VQ-VAE-GAN, VAE-GAN, and diffusion (SDEdit)---spanning a twenty-fold range in perceptual distance (LPIPS 0.029--0.584). At autoencoder fidelity, both decision and explanation are preserved: AUC changes remain within $\pm$0.005 and attribution similarity (HiResCAM, Grad-CAM++) stays high. At diffusion fidelity, both collapse: pooled AUC drops by 0.253 and mass-pathology AUC falls below chance. The diagnostic gap is thus a measurable function of reconstruction fidelity rather than an intrinsic cost of reconstruction, and the framework provides an architecture-agnostic instrument for identifying when and where multi-stage pipelines lose diagnostic signal.

</details>


### [32] [AI-generated Images Challenge Visual Trust in High-risk Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22745)
*Yi-Zhi Wang,Yichen Xiao,Linan Yue,Weibo Gao,Yichao Du,Pengfei Fang,Shimin Di,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个名为SafeIMG的安全导向型基准，用于测试在公共和个人安全相关场景中检测AI合成图像的能力，并指出现有检测模型在此类高风险场景下的效果远逊于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成技术的快速发展正在削弱视觉内容作为证据的价值，传统检测基准很少关注那些误导性视觉内容会造成重大公共或个人安全风险的场景。本研究希望解决当前合成图像检测在安全敏感场合下欠缺有效评估和高准确度的问题。

Method: 研究团队设计了SafeIMG基准，以GPT Image 2生成12种与安全相关的典型场景。SafeIMG不仅评估检测器是否能识别合成图，还引入了人工注释，包括定位可疑区域、解释局部伪影及常识或物理矛盾。基于该基准，对专业检测模型和视觉语言模型(VLMs)进行了系统评测。

Result: 最强的视觉语言模型仅能识别49.5%的合成图像，最好的专用检测器仅能识别33.1%，而人类评估者的准确率高达81.7%。在异常区域解释覆盖上，模型仅能覆盖29.8%的人工注释，大多局限于局部缺陷（文本、面部、手部）。对于常识冲突和物理矛盾的解释覆盖率仅15.0%和12.0%；图像质量下降后，检测表现进一步恶化。

Conclusion: 当前的合成图像检测器在公众和个人安全关键场景下，缺乏足够的准确性、解释对齐性和鲁棒性，难以对AI生成图像做出可靠评估。

Abstract: Rapid advances in image generation are eroding the evidentiary value of visual content in settings where authenticity can affect public safety and personal reputation. Yet existing detection benchmarks rarely examine synthetic images in public- and individual-safety contexts, where misleading visual content may carry substantial risks. Here we introduce SafeIMG, a safety-oriented benchmark spanning 12 public- and individual-safety scenarios generated using GPT Image 2. Unlike benchmarks centred on generic imagery and image-level labels, SafeIMG evaluates not only whether detectors recognise synthetic images, but also whether their decisions reflect human-identified anomalies. To this end, SafeIMG provides human annotations that localise suspicious regions and explain local artefacts and higher-level commonsense or physical inconsistencies. We evaluate specialized synthetic-image detectors and vision-language models (VLMs), and find that neither provides reliable detection. The strongest VLM identifies only 49.5% of generated images, whereas the best specialised detector identifies 33.1%, compared with 81.7% accuracy for human evaluators. Model explanations cover only 29.8\% of human-annotated anomalies and predominantly capture local defects in text, faces and hands. Their coverage falls to 15.0% for commonsense conflicts and 12.0% for physical inconsistencies, while detection performance deteriorates further after dissemination-induced image degradation. These findings show that current detectors lack the accuracy, explanatory alignment and robustness needed to evaluate AI-generated images reliably across public- and individual-safety settings.

</details>


### [33] [Advancing All-Weather Building Damage Mapping to the Instance Level: Outcomes and Insights from the 2026 Bright Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22746)
*Hongruixuan Chen,He Huang,Haifeng Wang,Jian Song,Junjue Wang,Weihao Xuan,Hamish Mitchell,Jiepan Li,Wei He,Liangpei Zhang,Zijie Wang,Chen Zhong,Jiazhen Zhao,Lei Hu,Ting Hu,Hongyan Zhang,Gregory Angelides,Miriam Cha,Clifford Broni-Bediako,Junshi Xia,Taylor Perron,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了Bright Challenge竞赛，其任务是利用一次灾前光学影像与一次灾后SAR影像，实现建筑物级别的损毁自动识别，在多类自然灾害场景下评测方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后快速响应需要及时的建筑损毁信息，而传统光学遥感影像常常受云层、烟雾、黑夜等影响，难以获取。如何利用SAR等全天候遥感手段提升灾后建筑损毁检测的可用性和精度变得亟需解决。

Method: 本次挑战基于submeter级分辨率遥感数据，要求选手基于一张灾前光学和一张灾后SAR影像对每座建筑进行检测、描绘，并赋予三类损毁标签。比赛采用经过精细标注的Bright数据集，跨越16个灾害事件，终测在“未见过”的2025年加州火灾和牙买加飓风上评测。优秀方案普遍采用光学/SAR分模态编码、分阶段或后期融合、建筑定位与损毁识别分离策略，冠军方案还采用了场景自适应阈值调整和伪标签自适应机制。

Result: 共有157人次参与，46支队伍进入决赛；最高得分方案mAP为0.182和0.181，是基线的8.7倍，但距离域内最佳持出分数0.513还有较大差距。跨事件泛化和稳定的损毁级别判别仍是主要难点。

Conclusion: 对比各队伍方案，发现多模态分离和后期融合更优，同时稳健的跨事件泛化和损伤判别仍待提升。所有数据、代码和方案已公开，为今后相关研究提供资源支持。

Abstract: Rapid post-disaster response requires timely, building-level information on whether structures remain intact, are damaged, or are destroyed. Post-event optical imagery, however, may be unavailable because of cloud, smoke, or darkness. The Bright Challenge evaluated all-weather building damage mapping from a submeter-resolution pre-event optical image and a post-event SAR image. Participants were required to detect and delineate each building and assign exactly one of three mutually exclusive damage labels. The challenge extended the globally distributed \textsc{Bright} dataset with instance-level annotations for about 291,000 buildings across 16 disaster events spanning seven disaster types. The final phase was evaluated exclusively on two 2025 events absent from training: a wildfire event in California and a hurricane in Jamaica. A total of 157 participants made 1,289 submissions, and 46 teams entered the final phase. The two winning solutions achieved test mAPs of 0.182 and 0.181, approximately 8.7 times the public baseline of 0.021, but remained far below the best in-domain holdout score of 0.513. Across teams ranked in both phases, performance declined sharply and the rank order changed substantially. The two leading solutions independently favored modality-specific encoding, staged or late optical--SAR fusion, and an optical-dominant separation of building localization from damage recognition. The winning method additionally used scene-aware threshold adjustment and pseudo-label adaptation. These results identify cross-event generalization and stable severity discrimination as the principal remaining challenges. All data, annotations, baseline code, and winning solutions are publicly available at https://github.com/ChenHongruixuan/BRIGHT.

</details>


### [34] [Post-Operative Glioma Segmentation via Loss Stabilization, Normalization and Subspace Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22749)
*Alexandru Crişan,Diana Borza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文旨在提升术后胶质瘤分割的泛化性能，对现有方法不足进行分析并提出一系列改进，最终显著提升了分割准确率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术后残留肿瘤追踪对于早期发现复发至关重要，而当前自动分割方法在不同临床协议间泛化不佳。尤其是目前流行的Generalized Dice Loss（GDL）在域迁移下性能急剧下降，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 作者在MU-GLIOMA-POST和UCSF-ALPTDG数据集上进行了消融实验，分析GDL损失在域迁移下的表现，并提出了两项重要方法改进：1）结合脑掩模分位点归一化与体素级对比学习以提升模型泛化性；2）提出了子空间感知类注意力（SACA）模块以重新校准瓶颈特征，提升增强肿瘤（ET）的敏感性。最后与nnU-Net做集成增强表现。

Result: 改进方案下，模型在外部UCSF测试集上的Whole Lesion (WL) Dice分数从0.73提升至0.94；SACA集成变体获得MU-GLIOMA-POST集上边界误差（HD95）2.92 mm的最佳表现，且内部验证集ET敏感性提升了8%。

Conclusion: 通过有效的损失函数改进、特征归一化与注意力机制，本文大幅提升了术后胶质瘤分割的泛化能力及准确性，显示新方法有潜力实际应用于临床术后肿瘤追踪。

Abstract: Tracking residual tumor after surgery is essential for catching recurrence early, but automating post-operative glioma segmentation remains a difficult task. Although transformer-based architectures, such as SwinUNETR, achieved impressive results, few studies test how well they generalize across clinical protocols. In this paper, we conduct an ablation study on the MU-GLIOMA-POST and UCSF-ALPTDG datasets and show that the standard Generalized Dice Loss (GDL) is unstable under domain shift: the Whole Lesion (WL) Dice drops from 0.88 on the internal validation set to 0.73 on the external UCSF test set. To address this, we pair brain-masked percentile normalization with voxel-level contrastive learning. We also propose a Subspace-Aware Class Attention (SACA) module that re-calibrates the bottleneck features and raises Enhancing Tumor (ET) sensitivity by 8% (9.1% relative improvement) on internal validation. Ensembling these refinements with nnU-Net brings every stable configuration to a WL Dice of 0.94, and the SACA variant ensemble achieves the best boundary error (HD95) of 2.92 mm on MU-GLIOMA-POST.

</details>


### [35] [Beyond Error-vs-Discard Characteristic: Toward Stable and Reliable Evaluation for Face Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22752)
*Bhavesh Wani,Žiga Babnik,Vitomir Štruc,Philipp Terhörst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现现有的人脸图像质量评估（FIQA）主流评估方法EDC存在重要局限性，并提出了改进的评估手段以提升评测的可靠性与可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前FIQA方法主要依赖EDC进行效果评估，但EDC在实际应用中存在测试集分歧和阈值漂移等问题，影响了方法的客观评估与横向对比。作者希望解决这一评估方法上的不足。

Method: 作者分析了EDC的局限性，提出了基于丢弃的EDC变体和一种基于排序的一致性评估指标RCE。RCE无需丢弃样本，且采用固定决策阈值。在5组数据集、4个人脸识别模型和15种FIQA方法上进行了实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，传统EDC评估存在实际局限性，而新提出的评估方法可以更可靠、更公平地评价和对比不同FIQA方法的性能。

Conclusion: EDC评估方法的限制不仅存在于人脸识别领域，其改进方法可广泛适用于其它生物特征质量评估的场景，提升对各种生物识别质量评估方案的可靠性。

Abstract: Face Image Quality Assessment (FIQA) aims to estimate the utility of facial images for reliable recognition. The evaluation of FIQA methods is predominantly based on the Error-versus-Discard Characteristic (EDC), which evaluates performance by progressively discarding low-quality samples and measuring recognition error on the retained subset. In this work, we demonstrate that the widely used EDC protocol has fundamental limitations: Test-Set Divergence and Threshold Drift, which together limit the reliability and comparability of FIQA methods. To address this, we propose discard-based EDC variants and a rank-based Rank Consistency Evaluation (RCE) metric that operates on the entire test set without discarding samples, using a fixed decision threshold. Extensive experiments on five datasets, four face recognition models, and 15 state-of-the-art FIQA methods demonstrate both the limitations of EDC and the effectiveness of the proposed approaches in enabling a more reliable and comparable evaluation. Despite evaluated on face images only, the limitations arise from the EDC protocol rather than the biometric modality, suggesting a broader applicability to biometric quality assessment in general.

</details>


### [36] [Real-time Reconstruction of Human Visual Perception from fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22753)
*Rishab S. Iyer,Jiaxin Cindy Tu,Cesar Kadir Torrico Villanueva,Anish Mahishi,Ross P. Kempner,Jacob S. Prince,Ernest W. Lo,Akash Bhowmick,Hritik Arasu,Amaar Chughtai,Elizabeth A. McDevitt,Paul S. Scotti,Kenneth A. Norman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种适用于实时fMRI神经反馈的高阶图像解码方法，实现了秒级重构被试感知的自然图像，并通过云计算平台顺利部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前实时fMRI研究的分析手段落后于最新fMRI解码技术，主要是因为先进的图像解码方法计算量大，难以满足实时反馈的要求，限制了其科研和临床应用潜力。

Method: 作者对功能强大的MindEye2图像重构解码流程进行了适配和优化，使其能够实时运行，并通过开放源代码的云平台（RT-Cloud）实现单试次、秒级的视觉感知重构，同时还通过模拟分析对影响从离线到实时的性能变化因素进行了探究。

Result: 优化后系统可以在刺激呈现后几秒内准确解码和重构单次视觉感知图像，证明即便有实时计算的限制依然能够获取可靠细粒度的结果，对比分析还揭示了从离线到实时机制下性能的变化来源。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了先进的fMRI图像解码流程可以高效用于实时分析，为科学研究和脑机接口相关的临床治疗提供了新的可能性和技术基础。

Abstract: Real-time closed-loop neurofeedback based on functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) has led to important scientific and clinical advances. However, the sophistication of the analysis methods used in real-time fMRI lags behind the state-of-the-art in fMRI decoding, largely due to computational factors: Most advanced decoding pipelines do not fit within the envelope of real-time processing, where the analysis needs to be conducted in a matter of seconds and without leveraging data acquired later in the session. Here, we present a real-time compatible adaptation of a computationally intensive state-of-the-art pipeline for reconstructing perceived natural images (MindEye2), and we demonstrate that reliable fine-grained decoding is still achievable in this setting. Using RT-Cloud, an open-source, scalable cloud-based platform, we performed a real-time scan where we decoded single-trial visual perception within seconds after an image was shown to the participant. Finally, we use simulated analyses to document the factors driving changes in performance from offline to real-time analysis. This work serves as a proof-of-concept that it is feasible to deploy these powerful fMRI decoding pipelines in real-time analysis, paving the way for their use in brain-computer interfaces for scientific discovery and clinical treatment.

</details>


### [37] [Cheap Probes Predict Expensive Training in 3D-CT Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22771)
*Renjie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效筛选3D CT视觉-语言模型（VLM）冻结图像编码器及其token压缩方案的方法。作者通过在编码器缓存的嵌入上施加“廉价”探针，快速评估不同编码器×压缩方案组合的效果，无需对每组组合都进行耗费巨大的大模型微调。结果表明，该方法对于候选方案的排序接近于传统微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在构建3D CT视觉-语言模型时，需要在多种冻结编码器与token压缩方案的组合中选择最佳方案。由于微调每一种组合成本极高，亟需一种高效筛选的替代方法。

Method: 作者搭建了一个图像探针基准测试平台，基于（编码器×压缩）组合，通过设计两种验证机制（尺度一致性校验scale-sanity、探针可分性probe-separability）保障评测的公平性和有效性。使用多种探针头（read-out heads）对参数分布排序，并与下游任务的实际表现进行初步比对。

Result: 实验表明，廉价探针方法评估的排序结果与昂贵的微调评估结果高度一致（相关系数r≈0.95），证明该方法在组合筛选上的有效性。作者指出这是顺序性（排序）预测，而非精确性能估计。

Conclusion: 如果这一方法在更多实验中成立，只需几分钟便可用冻结探针筛选出最优编码器和压缩方案，大幅节省计算与开发资源，将完整训练留给进入决赛的方案。

Abstract: Picking the frozen image encoder for a 3D~CT vision--language model (VLM), together with the token-compression scheme on top of it, is a search over many candidates. There are several encoders, several ways to compress their tokens, and several token budgets, and the combinations grow fast. Comparing them the usual way means fine-tuning a large language model (LLM) on each combination, and running the whole sweep this way needs far more compute than most groups can spend. We ask whether a cheap probe on the encoder's cached embeddings can stand in for that comparison. We build an image-grounded probing benchmark over (encoder $\times$ compression) cells, with clinical attribute families and two validation gates, scale-sanity and probe-separability, that keep each attribute well-scaled and decodable. These gates are the main methodological contribution. On this benchmark we compare a range of read-out heads, and in a preliminary study we pair each probe with its matched downstream task. The early signal is encouraging: the cheap probe orders the candidates in close agreement with expensive fine-tuning, at about $r\approx0.95$ on the cells measured so far. We read this as an ordinal claim, a ranking predictor rather than an exact estimate, and we are explicit about where it stays preliminary. If it holds up, encoder and compression choices can be screened in minutes with frozen-token probes, with full training spent only on the finalists.

</details>


### [38] [Hybrid Semantic and Spectral Ensemble for Robust Synthetic Image Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22808)
*Md. Ajwad Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合深度学习和数学取证特征的双分支归属框架，针对后处理退化场景下的文本生成图像归属问题，显著提升鲁棒性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对T2I合成图片大规模涌现，现有归属方法在面对实际应用中常见的压缩、模糊等未知后处理会出现分布偏移，导致模型性能大幅下降，亟需鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出双分支集成框架：语义分支采用EfficientNet-B0（结合EMA与标签平滑正则化）；取证分支从高通残差中提取126维数学特征（如SVD谱型、LBP），用SVD降维，用XGBoost分类，最终融合输出。

Result: 在含10种生成器、55%样本退化的大型测试集上，私有榜准确率达95.60%；无需GPU，整套流程在普通CPU下6.5小时内完成，体现高效可扩展性。

Conclusion: 融合深度语义和数学取证特征能显著提升合成图像归属的鲁棒性和实用性，为后处理常态下的大规模部署提供了可行、经济的解决思路。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of text-to-image (T2I) models has necessitated robust Synthetic Image Source Attribution (SIA) methodologies. A critical challenge in SIA is the distribution shift between pristine training images and real-world deployed images, which undergo unknown post-processing operations such as JPEG compression and blurring. In this work, proposed for the DLMMDD Challenge at ICANN 2026, we introduce a dual-branch ensemble framework fusing Semantic Deep Learning with Mathematical Forensic Feature Extraction. The semantic branch employs EfficientNet-B0 regularized with Exponential Moving Averaging (EMA) and Label Smoothing. The forensic branch extracts 126 mathematical features -- including SVD spectral profiles and Local Binary Patterns -- from high-pass noise residuals, compressed via Truncated SVD and classified with XGBoost. Evaluated on a dataset of 10 generators where 55% of the test set is degraded, our approach achieves a private leaderboard accuracy of 95.60%. Furthermore, the entire pipeline is highly computationally efficient, requiring no GPU acceleration and executing end-to-end on a standard CPU in under 6.5 hours, highlighting the practicality and scalability of mathematical forensics for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [39] [ID-V2V: Identity-Preserving Video Restylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22830)
*Yuancheng Xu,Mingming He,Pablo Salamanca,Li Ma,Yash Kant,Emmett Steven,Paul Debevec,Ning Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够在保证人物身份和表演特征（如表情、眼神、唇动）不变的前提下，实现视频风格重塑的新方法ID-V2V。该方法无需稀缺的配对训练数据，通过对人像与编辑驱动信号的解耦，实现了灵活、美观的人物视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频生成与编辑技术难以同时保证人物身份和表演细节的准确传递，尤其是在需要更改场景风格或光照的情况下。此外，缺乏真实的成对视频数据也极大限制了相关技术的发展。

Method: 该方法将身份保持建模为视频重光照（relighting）任务，而将风格迁移建模为由编辑过的关键帧引导的视频生成任务。ID-V2V框架融合了重光照后的人脸区域和法线图以约束面部特征，同时用编辑关键帧及深度序列实现灵活且时序一致的视频编辑。通过这种方式，无需成对数据，仅用一段视频即可构建训练数据。

Result: 实验结果显示，ID-V2V 在保持面部身份、细粒度表演和生成视频质量方面均超过现有方法，同时支持单人和多人的场景。

Conclusion: ID-V2V为面向现实内容制作的人物视频编辑提供了更人性化且实用的技术方案，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: In visual storytelling, human performances are central to creative intent and narrative meaning. However, preserving human identity and performance while enabling flexible visual edits remains challenging for generative video models. We formalize this challenge as identity-preserving video restylization, which propagates scene, lighting, and style changes specified by an edited keyframe across a source video, while preserving facial likeness and performance, including expressions, eye gaze, and lip synchronization. A key obstacle is the absence of paired training data, as identity-preserving restylized video pairs are rare in real-world settings. To address this, we propose a decoupling of source-grounded identity preservation and edit-driven video synthesis. Our key insight is that facial appearance and expression should remain invariant, with illumination being the primary permissible variation. We therefore cast identity preservation as a video relighting problem, while modeling visual edit propagation as controlled video synthesis guided by the edited keyframe. Building on this formulation, we introduce ID-V2V, a video-to-video generative framework integrating complementary control signals: relit facial regions and facial normal maps tightly constrain facial likeness and performance, while edited keyframes and depth sequences enable flexible and temporally coherent generation. This design enables constructing training pairs from a single video, eliminating the need for scarce paired data. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ID-V2V significantly outperforms existing methods in preserving facial likeness and fine-grained facial performance, supports both single- and multi-subject scenarios, and delivers high visual quality, highlighting its potential as a human-centric tool for real-world content production. The code is available at: https://github.com/Eyeline-Labs/ID-V2V.

</details>


### [40] [Gaze-Anchored Social Net: Decoding Implicit Relations via Joint Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22847)
*Yuqi Hou,Zhuo Chen,Han Hu,Je Woo Kim,Jianbo Jiao,Hyung Jin Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ANCHOR的模型，用于在人群图片中同时解读目光轨迹和背后的隐含社交意图，取得了领先的效果，并首次定量识别了从目光中蕴含的社交层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么把目光独立看待，要么在预测社交语义时忽略两者的内在联系，难以揭示目光背后的细腻社交动机，因此需要一种能联合建模目光和隐含社交关系的方法。

Method: ANCHOR模型以目标为中心，建模视觉注意力与隐含的社交关系的联合分布。其核心是关系注意力机制，结合特征调制技术，实现单一视觉主干网络对多人互动的高效解析。同时通过多任务优化协同训练，平衡空间目光精度与社交关系推理的冲突，提高泛化能力。

Result: 在包含密集多人注释和新型社交影响排序的扩展基准数据集上，ANCHOR取得了业界领先的性能，首次定量证明了可从静态目光模式中直接学习出隐含的社交等级结构。

Conclusion: ANCHOR不仅提升了多人目光与社交关系解读的效果，还展示了目光行为背后隐含社交等级的可识别性，对理解复杂社交场景具有启发意义。

Abstract: Human gaze does more than point to visual targets; it serves as a subtle indicator of social intent within static images, whereas standard models typically process individuals independently, treating gaze as an i.i.d. quantity or predicting social semantics in isolation. Recent multi-person methods attempt to address this but often treat social relations as rigid, post-hoc classifications decoupled from the gaze estimation process. This oversimplification fails to capture the nuanced nature of social intent, which acts as an underlying driver of gaze behavior rather than a secondary categorical output. We address these limitations by proposing ANCHOR, a target-centric paradigm designed to decode gaze-anchored social intent by modeling the joint distribution of visual attention and latent implicit relations. Our approach surfaces these dependencies as the latent structural scaffolding of gaze behavior. The architecture utilizes a relational attention mechanism to capture fine-grained interpersonal links, leveraging feature-wise modulation for efficient multi-person parsing from a single vision backbone. To stabilize the training of this coupled formulation, we implement an optimization synergy to resolve the inherent conflicts between spatial gaze accuracy and latent social reasoning. This approach ensures robust generalization by seeking stable, flat minima while simultaneously harmonizing competing task gradients. We validate our framework on an extended benchmark featuring dense multi-person annotations and novel social influence rankings. Our results demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and provide the first quantitative evidence that implicit social hierarchies can be robustly disentangled and learned directly from static gaze patterns.

</details>


### [41] [Robustifying pathology foundation models via fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22861)
*Alexandre Filiot,Oskar Thaeter,Benoit Schmauch,Lionel Guillou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的微调方法，有效提升病理学基础模型（FM）对扫描仪与染色差异的稳健性，并提高下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学基础模型对不同实验室的扫描仪和染色差异敏感，影响其在现实场景的泛化能力和可靠部署。为解决这一问题，需要提高其稳健性。

Method: 作者开发了一种全新的微调策略，应用于10种不同的病理学FM，对比实验展示该微调方法如何改善模型对获取因素的鲁棒性和下游性能。

Result: 所有模型使用新方法微调后稳健性均有提升。平均PathoROB鲁棒性指数从0.72提升到0.87，跨多基准（Patho-Bench，HEST，THUNDER）总性能提升43%，部分模型在鲁棒性和性能方面提升高达72%和76%。

Conclusion: 新微调方法能普适提升病理学FM的跨采集因子的稳健性和任务表现，无观察到负面权衡，并公开了部分微调后的模型供社区使用。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (FMs) produce powerful tile-level representations which remain sensitive to scanner and staining variability, undermining deployment across laboratories. We develop a novel fine-tuning recipe that improves the robustness of pathology FMs to acquisition factors. Applied to ten different FMs, our fine-tuning strategy consistently improves robustness for every model as well as downstream performance, with no observed trade-off. On average, it raises the PathoROB robustness index by 23% (from 0.72 to 0.87) and increases the overall cross-benchmark performance by 43% on Patho-Bench, HEST and THUNDER combined, with individual gains reaching up to 72% in robustness (Phikon-v2) and 76% in performance (Midnight-12k). We publicly release the fine-tuned versions of Phikon-v2 (Phaet) and Midnight-12k (Mascaret) at https://huggingface.co/wearewaiv/models.

</details>

*Patrick Rim,Tom Long,Ekta Prashnani,Ruth Rosenholtz,Ben Boudaoud,Peter Xenopoulos,Alex Wong,Joohwan Kim,Jae-Hyun Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了Spatial-IQ，一个用于诊断多模态大模型空间推理能力的分层框架，通过细分任务和多种输出格式评估模型，揭示其在空间认知中的不足，并用分层链式推理和强化学习提升模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型虽在视觉解释方面表现优异，但在空间推理任务上明显逊色于人类。现有的评测方法作为黑盒，难以定位模型失效的具体原因，因此需要一个细致区分感知与认知障碍的诊断工具。

Method: 作者提出Spatial-IQ，将三维物体计数任务拆解为9个对应人类空间认知发展阶段的子任务（感知相关与认知相关），并加入心理旋转检测，共计10个小任务。他们利用NVIDIA Isaac Sim自动生成约8万组3D结构及子任务对应的标注，设计了自由文本、多选图片和图片编辑三种输出格式，并建立了人类成绩基线。通过逐层评估模型在这些子任务的表现，分析其空间推理能力。

Result: 实验表明，领先的多模态模型常常在计数主任务上取得好成绩，但在底层支持该任务的子任务表现却较差，且不同模型在子任务链上的鲁棒性有很大差异，揭示了模型“投机取巧”的现象。进一步，采用分层链式思考和带可验证奖励的强化学习训练，能显著提升空间相关子任务表现和总体准确率。

Conclusion: Spatial-IQ不仅作为空间推理的诊断工具，能揭示模型的推理短板；其链式推理和分层奖励也能作为有效训练信号显著提升模型空间推理能力，对未来多模态模型研究与开发具有重要意义。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at visual interpretation but fail on spatial reasoning tasks that humans solve reliably. Existing benchmarks evaluate these models as black boxes, limiting their ability to identify the underlying causes of lower performance: when a model fails a spatial reasoning task, it remains difficult to ascertain whether the hurdle is perceptual, such as recognizing object boundaries, or cognitive, such as reasoning about occlusion to infer hidden geometry. We introduce Spatial-IQ, a hierarchical diagnostic framework that decomposes object counting in stacked 3D structures into 9 perceptual and cognitive sub-tasks organized by the developmental stages of human spatial cognition, with mental rotation as an additional target probe. Using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, we procedurally generated a diverse dataset of roughly 80,000 stacked 3D structures with per-task ground truth. We evaluate models across three output formats (free-response text, multiple-choice images, and image editing) alongside a human baseline. The Spatial-IQ framework shows that top-performing models often succeed at the target task (object counting) without succeeding on the lower-level sub-tasks intended to support it, and that models differ in how much of these hierarchical chains they preserve, often revealing shortcut behavior that raw target-task accuracy alone would obscure. Finally, we demonstrate that training models with chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision over our hierarchical sub-tasks, combined with reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, significantly improves both spatial consistency across sub-tasks and target-task accuracy, supporting the value of the proposed decomposition as both a diagnostic tool and a training signal.

</details>


### [43] [AdaKAN: A dual-branch adaptive Kolmogorov-Arnold network for medical image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22891)
*Dalia Alzu'bi,Deep Bhattacharyya,Ali Ayub,A. Ben Hamza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为AdaKAN的新型医学图像分割模型，通过整合卷积操作和高效KAN（EffiKAN）模块，实现了细粒度和全局依赖的有效建模，并在多类医学影像数据集上取得了最优分割效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割任务由于医学结构的复杂性和成像模态多样性一直具有挑战性，现有的卷积和Transformer方法在建模依赖与捕捉细节方面各有局限，因此需要新的方法综合其优点。

Method: 提出AdaKAN，包括高效注意力机制和自适应KAN模块（AdaptKAN）；AdaptKAN模块有双分支设计，一条使用Bernstein多项式激活的KAN层实现平滑稳定的全局建模，另一条通过投影和自适应缩放进行通道细化。整体采用U型结构，并辅以跳跃连接确保空间信息的保留和解码准确性。

Result: 在多个医学图像数据集上进行广泛实验，证明AdaKAN在分割精度上达到了最新的最优效果。

Conclusion: AdaKAN通过结合卷积和KAN及其创新模块，能更好地同时捕获长程依赖和局部特征，优于传统方法，是医学图像分割领域有前景的新方法。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is a fundamental task in computer-aided diagnosis, yet it remains challenging due to the complexity of anatomical structures and the variability across imaging modalities. In this paper, we propose AdaKAN, an Adaptive Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) that synergistically integrates convolutional operations with a novel efficient KAN (EffiKAN) block, comprised of an efficient attention mechanism and an adaptive KAN (AdaptKAN) module. This module features a dual-branch design: one branch employs a KAN layer with Bernstein polynomial activations for globally smooth and stable function approximation, while the other branch performs channel-wise refinement through projection operations and adaptive scaling. AdaKAN adopts a U-shaped architecture that effectively captures both long-range dependencies and fine-grained local features, overcoming the limitations of conventional convolutional and Transformer-based segmentation models. Skip connections are employed to preserve spatial details during encoding and facilitate accurate reconstruction during decoding. Extensive experiments conducted on diverse medical imaging datasets demonstrate that AdaKAN achieves state-of-the-art performance in segmentation accuracy.

</details>


### [44] [Small-Pollinator Detection in Cluttered Field Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22913)
*Onur Onal,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了在单GPU下小型传粉者的检测方法，比较了YOLO与RF-DETR等模型，发现在高分辨率下RF-DETR效果最佳，模型与分辨率的选择对提升检测效果更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 田野视频中检测传粉者极具挑战，原因在于目标体积小、相似度高、环境复杂并且受遮挡与模糊影响。因此，作者希望在有限计算资源下，系统分析现有检测方法效果及其影响因素。

Method: 基于BuzzSpot数据集，实验比较了YOLO与RF-DETR模型在不同输入分辨率下的表现，评估了切片推理、类别门控融合、基于大小的集成、推理后时序处理等方法，并进行了误差分析。

Result: RF-DETR Large模型在1344分辨率下达到0.405 mAP50:95，优于低分辨率及YOLO基线模型。提升分辨率对小目标和稀有种类更有效，复杂的后处理和推理集成方法未带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 模型选择与高分辨率输入对小型传粉者检测提升最大，现有后处理方法帮助有限。未来应关注特征级的时序信息聚合，以解决物种区分难题。

Abstract: Detecting pollinators in field video is challenging: targets are small, visually similar, and observed against cluttered vegetation under blur and occlusion. We present a systematic empirical study of small-pollinator detection under a practical single-GPU compute budget. Using the BuzzSpot challenge dataset, we compare YOLO and RF-DETR models across input resolutions and evaluate sliced inference, class-gated fusion, size-routed ensembling, and post-hoc temporal processing. RF-DETR Large at 1344-pixel resolution achieved our best hidden-test result, reaching 0.405 mAP50:95 and outperforming the 1120-pixel model (0.379) and the best single-model YOLO26m baseline (0.366). The strongest gains came from adopting RF-DETR and increasing its input resolution, indicating that detector choice and input resolution were more effective levers than added inference-time complexity; the resolution gain was strongest for small objects and the rarer bumblebee and moth classes. Sliced-inference fusion, size-routed ensembling, and warm-started 1536-pixel continuation did not surpass this result, while post-hoc temporal processing did not improve the leaked diagnostic evaluation. Error analysis identified bee-hoverfly discrimination as the clearest remaining bottleneck: neighboring frames rarely supplied correctly classified hoverfly evidence for post-hoc correction. These findings motivate learned feature-level temporal aggregation before the final classification decision.

</details>


### [45] [Controlling Embedding Spaces with Text-Conditioned Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22919)
*Joseph Fioresi,Fabian Caba Heilbron,Pankaj Nathani,Mubarak Shah,Kushal Kafle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文本条件控制的视觉嵌入空间变换方法，使得像CLIP这类多模态模型的高维向量能够显式访问多种属性（如颜色、风格等），并在属性检索和多属性组织任务上取得了新SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CLIP等多模态嵌入空间虽然能够实现跨模态语义检索等强大功能，但其高维表达主要体现主语义（如主体物体），难以显式表达和操作诸如颜色、角度或艺术风格等其他重要属性，功能表达不够丰富且缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出一种网络，在给定属性类别的自然语言描述（如“颜色”或“艺术风格”）条件下，生成对应的视觉嵌入仿射变换，从而显式强化指定属性。该网络利用文本条件实现多属性并行学习且可控性强，训练时将变换后的嵌入向量对齐到冻结的原有大规模模型的潜空间，实现无需重编码即可与原始嵌入兼容检索。该机制可扩展到属性解耦（multi-clustering）等下游任务。

Result: 实验证明，该方法可以高效地实现在属性检索和多属性组织（multi-attribute organization）任务中对嵌入空间的可控化。其总体推断时延近乎为零，在上述任务中达到了当前最佳的效果。

Conclusion: 方法统一高效，赋予了多模态嵌入空间更强的可解释性和可控性，提高检索精度和属性解耦表现，并且几乎不增加推理开销，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Multimodal embedding spaces in models like CLIP enable powerful capabilities such as semantic similarity retrieval and cross-modal zero-shot classification. These embeddings compress high-level semantics into a single vector, which comes at the cost of primarily expressing a dominant semantics like main object while suppressing other important attributes such as camera angle or color tone. We propose a text-conditioned transformation of visual embeddings that makes such attributes explicitly accessible. Given a natural language description of an attribute category (e.g., "color" or "art style"), a network generates an affine transformation that emphasizes the specified attribute. Conditioning on text enables it to learn many attributes simultaneously, accessing them at inference time through an intuitive interface. The network is trained to align transformed embeddings with the frozen latent space, enabling retrieval using existing large-scale embeddings without any re-encoding. When applied to a full set, the same mechanism transforms the latent space for attribute disentanglement tasks such as multi-clustering. By operating directly in latent space, our method provides a unified and efficient framework for controlling embedding spaces, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance across both attribute-based retrieval and multi-attribute organization tasks with near-zero inference cost. Project page: https://joefioresi718.github.io/ControlEmbed_webpage/

</details>


### [46] [RRTrack: Robust and Recoverable Object 6D Pose Tracking for Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23669)
*Junyue Li,Ye Zheng,Yifan Chen,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了RRTrack，一种高效、可恢复的6D姿态跟踪方法，能应对快速运动与目标消失再现的情况，实现了更强鲁棒性和实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6D姿态逐帧估计虽然准确但代价高昂，主流跟踪器则容易在快速运动或完全遮挡下丢失追踪，因为它们依赖目标的持续可见性。亟需一种应对动态、遮挡环境下的高效、鲁棒的6D对象跟踪方案。

Method: RRTrack采用2D-6D闭环跟踪：2D分支利用基于记忆的视频目标分割（VOS）定位目标，6D分支检验几何一致性后再更新记忆。同时，引入基于DINOv2的双模板匹配模块，结合离线合成模板和在线观测锚点，在保持实时性的同时恢复丢失目标。此外，作者还构建了含有快速运动与全遮挡的合成RGB-D基准数据集。

Result: 在新基准数据集上，RRTrack相较于FoundationPose，equal-subset mean ADD-S AR和ADD-S AUC分别提升66.3%和65.7%，跟踪速度达55.2 FPS。真实环境测试显示该方法在有噪声感知条件下也具有较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RRTrack成功实现了高效且鲁棒的6D姿态跟踪，无论面对动态场景、遮挡、流失再出现或噪声感知都表现优异，推动了机器人动态场景自主感知的发展。

Abstract: Robust object 6D pose tracking is critical for robotic systems operating in dynamic and occluded scenes. Per-frame estimators are accurate but computationally expensive, while current trackers struggle with fast motion and complete occlusion due to their reliance on continuous visibility. To address these challenges, we present RRTrack, an efficient, recoverable object 6D pose tracker that enables robust tracking through fast motion and target disappearance--reappearance. RRTrack introduces a 2D--6D closed-loop tracking strategy that integrates memory-based video object segmentation (VOS) with 6D pose refinement. The 2D branch maintains target localization, and the 6D branch verifies geometric consistency before memory updates. In addition, a DINOv2-based dual-bank template matching module is developed to recover lost targets by jointly exploiting offline synthetic templates and online observation anchors while maintaining real-time efficiency. We also introduce a synthetic RGB-D benchmark comprising three robotic scenarios with fast motion and full occlusion. Experimental results on the synthetic benchmark demonstrate that RRTrack improves equal-subset mean ADD-S AR by 66.3\% and ADD-S AUC by 65.7\% over FoundationPose while achieving 55.2 FPS. Real-world experiments further validate the robustness of RRTrack under noisy sensing conditions. Project page: https://github.com/7kevin24/RRTrack

</details>


### [47] [Layering Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22924)
*Chun Feng,Bowei Chen,Mengyi Shan,Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多层服饰虚拟试穿方法（LVTON），突破了现有只能单层替换的VTON限制，实现了连续叠加和移除服饰层。该方法在新提出的数据集和基准上达到SOTA表现，并在传统VTON任务上展现优异泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的穿衣搭配注重服饰的层叠（如夹克叠衬衫），而现有虚拟试穿方法只能替换单一衣物层，无法实现真实的服饰层叠体验。为解决此脱节问题，论文提出对VTON进行创新以适应层叠试穿需求。

Method: 作者创造性地将LVTON问题分解为两大能力：(1) 通用VTON能力（如服饰变形、身份保持），通过自动数据生成管线从时尚视频中合成训练数据获得；(2) 特定的层叠知识（如穿衣顺序、遮挡推理），通过少量特定LVTON数据集微调模型学习。

Result: 新方法在自建LVTON基准上取得了最优结果，同时在传统VTON基准上经过微调后刷新了SOTA，未见样本也表现出零样本能力，展现良好泛化性。

Conclusion: LVTON方法不仅解决了现实层叠服饰虚拟试穿的实际需求，还推动了虚拟试穿技术向更接近真实应用的方向发展，且具有优异的通用性与扩展潜力。

Abstract: In the real world, fashion is about layering: adding a jacket over a shirt, or a sequence of adding and removing layers, rather than just a single-layer swap. This fundamental real-world task remains a challenge in existing Virtual Try-On (VTON) methods, which excel at single-layer replacement but are not designed to layer or de-layer an existing outfit. This paper proposes Layering Virtual Try-On (LVTON), a layering benchmark and method that preserves an existing outfit while enabling sequential layering. We find that current VTON paradigms are fundamentally ill-equipped for LVTON, as their reliance on cloth-agnostic representations and single-item datasets discards essential layering context. Our key insight is that the LVTON challenge must be disentangled into two distinct competencies: (1) General VTON Priors (e.g., deformation, identity preservation) and (2) Specific Layering Knowledge (e.g., layering order and occlusion reasoning). First, our model obtains general VTON priors by being trained on data produced by an automatic data generation pipeline that synthesizes samples from fashion videos via segmentation and inpainting. Second, the model is fine-tuned on a small, dedicated LVTON dataset to learn the layering logic. Our method achieves state-of-the-art results on our LVTON benchmark and demonstrates superior generalizability on traditional VTON benchmarks, setting new state-of-the-art results when fine-tuned and exhibiting zero-shot capabilities.

</details>


### [48] [SimBEV2X: A Large-Scale Dataset and Data Generation Tool for Multi-Task Vehicle-to-Everything Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23910)
*Goodarz Mehr,Sepideh Gohari,Montasir Abbas,Azim Eskandarian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SimBEV2X工具及其生成的最大规模合成V2X感知数据集，并基于该数据集验证了先进的多智能体协同感知方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前单车感知受限于视野和传感器物理约束，V2X协同感知成为突破瓶颈的方法，但高质量大规模V2X数据集严重短缺，数据采集和标注成本极高，制约了算法发展。

Method: 作者开发了基于CARLA的SimBEV2X工具，实现自动生成多场景、多模态、多任务的合成数据，涵盖3D框、高清地图、BEV分割、体素网格等多类型标注，支持车-路协同。此外，提出基线方法CoopDet3D，并创新性地融合轴向注意力模块（FAX）提出CoBEVFusion架构，用于多智能体特征聚合。

Result: 构建了目前最大的V2X感知数据集（SimBEV2X），包含258个场景，8辆车、4个路侧单元，十万帧级别规模和上千万个标注，远超现有数据集。基于该数据集，CoBEVFusion模型在性能上优于已有方法。

Conclusion: SimBEV2X工具、数据集及新提出的CoBEVFusion方法为V2X协同感知研究提供了坚实基础，将强力推动该领域数据和算法的发展。

Abstract: Cooperative perception through vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication can overcome the inherent physical limitations of individual autonomous vehicles, such as occlusions and limited sensor range. However, the development of robust V2X algorithms, particularly those relying on unified spatial representations like bird's-eye view (BEV) representation, is hampered by the lack of large-scale, multi-modal, multi-task datasets. Moreover, collecting and annotating a large set of synchronized, real-world multi-agent data is prohibitively expensive. This has resulted in a landscape where existing V2X datasets are notably limited in both size and scope. To overcome this, we introduce SimBEV2X, an advanced synthetic data generation tool built on the CARLA simulator. SimBEV2X automatically creates randomized driving scenarios to collect multi-modal sensor data alongside various types of ground truth including 3D bounding boxes with unique track IDs, HD map information, BEV segmentation maps, and semantic occupancy voxel grids from both vehicles and RSUs. We also present the SimBEV2X dataset, the largest V2X perception dataset to date. The dataset comprises 258 scenes, each involving up to 8 connected vehicles and up to 4 RSUs across a variety of road networks. The SimBEV2X dataset is an order of magnitude larger than existing V2X datasets and contains 102,200 frames, 588,520 lidar point clouds, more than 3 million images, over 27 million bounding boxes, and a comprehensive set of other annotations. Finally, we establish a strong baseline on the SimBEV2X dataset using CoopDet3D and propose CoBEVFusion, a novel architecture that combines CoopDet3D with fused axial attention (FAX) for context-aware multi-agent feature aggregation, resulting in superior performance. SimBEV2X, the SimBEV2X dataset, and CoBEVFusion are available at https://simbev2x.org and https://github.com/GoodarzMehr/SimBEV2X.

</details>


### [49] [HALLELUAI: A Hallucination-Aware AI System for Ultra-Realistic Image-to-Video Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22959)
*Aniket Sakpal,Yang Jiang,Rouzbeh Davoudi,Shayan Hassantabar,Mani Najmabadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HALLELUAI系统实现AI图像转视频的高精度、自动化质控与再生成，大幅提升大规模生产时的视频质量与真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI生成的视频广泛应用于营销和创意领域，但自动、高精度的质量控制仍是规模化生产的主要瓶颈。现有方法难以保证产出视频的美学一致性、动态真实感及与原图的高度一致。

Method: 提出HALLELUAI系统，包含视频审核模块（检测美学质量、动态一致性、细粒度失真/幻觉风险）与自适应再生成模块（通过调优提示词、相机控制、模型/图像切换和重试等方式修正缺陷）；审核逻辑结合领域创意标准，反馈可直接驱动再生成流程。

Result: 在人类创意专家参与的评测中，HALLELUAI展示出与专家标准高度一致，能稳定产出超真实、可用于生产级的视频，满足大规模产品和营销需求。

Conclusion: 该框架促进了可信赖AI视频内容生产，实现高水平的视觉真实感、品牌安全和输入图像一致性，并支持规模化图像转视频应用。

Abstract: AI-generated video is increasingly used across marketing, product storytelling, and creative workflows, yet automated; high-precision quality control remains a major constraint to scaling production. We present HALLELUAI, an end-to-end system that moderates and regenerates image-to-video outputs to meet expert-level creative standards and deliver ultra-realistic videos with consistent end-user quality of experience (QoE) at scale. The system integrates a video moderation module that evaluates frame-level aesthetics, temporal motion fidelity, and fine-grained hallucination risks relative to the source image, with an agentic regeneration module that iteratively fixes failures through prompt refinement, controlled camera adjustments, targeted model or image switching, and structured retry strategies. The moderation logic is aligned with domain-specific creative guidelines and produces granular, machine-actionable feedback that directly drives regeneration. In human-in-the-loop evaluations with creative experts, HALLELUAI shows strong alignment and reliably outputs ultra-realistic, production-grade videos suitable for product and marketing placements at scale. This framework advances trustworthy AI generated video content by enforcing visual realism, brand safety, and strict input-image fidelity while enabling image-to-video generation at scale.

</details>


### [50] [SILICA: Repurposing Diffusion Priors for Joint Glass Segmentation and Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24249)
*Tarun R,Anuj Verma,Laksh Nanwani,Sourav Garg,K. Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合文本-图像扩散模型先验的统一管道SILICA，实现对透明表面的可迁移感知，无需真实配对标签即可分割及估算玻璃深度，并在多个环境下显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度传感器在遇到透明表面时失效，导致三维地图误差和导航风险。虽然有专用硬件可感知玻璃，但其扩展性和通用性差，且硬件依赖性强。基于学习的单目深度估计虽然有潜力，但缺乏玻璃场景的标注，泛化性差。为此，作者探索了利用扩散模型丰富视觉先验改善玻璃面深度感知的可行性。

Method: 提出SILICA管道，结合文本-图像扩散模型的广泛先验，联合预测玻璃分割与面向玻璃的深度，实现二者特征的信息互补，完全无需真实配对玻璃深度标签。基于预测的分割掩码，过滤传统深度传感器在玻璃点上的错误深度，并恢复准确的玻璃深度，以服务下游三维建图与避障。研究提出了新的Mirage 18k数据集用于实验。

Result: SILICA在多个未知环境下的零样本迁移测试中表现优越，超越先进方法近20%，并在透明表面感知上建立新基准。

Conclusion: SILICA无需依赖真实配对标注，利用扩散模型先验实现了可靠的透明表面深度感知，大幅提升了3D建图和自动导航的可靠性，开辟了更具通用性与适应性的研究方向。

Abstract: Standard depth sensors systematically fail on transparent surfaces, creating corrupted 3D maps and severe navigation hazards. While specialized hardware sensors can detect glass, they lack modularity and have extensive hardware dependencies. Consequently, learning-based monocular depth estimation has emerged as a compelling alternative. However, domain-specific glass-aware monocular depth estimators struggle with unfamiliar indoor layouts; restricted by the severe scarcity of real-world glass depth annotations, they fail to generalize zero-shot to new settings. This motivates us to explore whether the extensive priors of text-to-image diffusion models can enable generalizable perception of transparent surfaces. We introduce SILICA, a unified pipeline leveraging these priors to jointly predict glass segmentation and glass-aware depth. This mutual information exchange establishes a robust spatial hierarchy, entirely eliminating the need for paired real-world glass depth annotations. Subsequently, we use the predicted segmentation mask to explicitly filter incorrect glass depth points from standard sensors, recovering accurate metric glass depth for downstream 3D mapping and autonomous collision avoidance. Supported by our novel Mirage 18k dataset, extensive experiments demonstrate that SILICA achieves remarkable zero-shot transfer across diverse, unseen environments, outperforming state-of-the-art models by almost 20% and setting a new benchmark for transparent surface perception.

</details>


### [51] [mmSimPrior: Learning Simulation Priors for Data-Efficient Real-World Generalizable Radar-Based Human Motion Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22973)
*Cheng Guo,Qiming Cao,Shengkai Xu,Haoyu Xie,Kaixiang Su,Pu Wang,Hongfei Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种mmWave雷达人体动作重建通用模型预训练方法mmSimPrior，通过引入多模态编码器与联合时序tokenizer，提升模拟数据到真实场景的迁移能力，并获得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mmWave雷达可隐私、抗光地感知人体动作，但收集大量现实场景下配对的雷达-动作数据代价高昂。尽管可借助模拟数据扩大规模，但模拟信号与真实分布存在较大域差，导致模型迁移能力弱。该工作旨在通过创新预训练框架，提升模拟训练在实际部署中的应用效果。

Method: 提出mmSimPrior框架，将可迁移知识分解为信号、动作与映射三个先验。利用物理启发的领域随机化课程，预训练多模态信号编码器，以模拟传播和采集层面的变化。联合时序tokenizer学习人体动作的离散先验。共享映射先用于零样本分类重建和有限数据回归。构建包含420万帧数据集，设置严格测试分集场景。

Result: 在mmSimPrior-Real和RT-Pose数据集上，mmSimPrior在仅有24条真实配对序列下，回归模块实现MPJPE降低24.7%-39.0%，零样本分类实现MPJPE降低8.5%，均优于现有最强基线。

Conclusion: mmSimPrior有效提升mmWave雷达动作重建模型的跨域泛化能力，为低成本、高隐私的人体运动感知提供了新思路。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers privacy-preserving and lighting-robust sensing for human motion reconstruction, but learning models that generalize across real deployments require diverse paired radar-motion data that are costly to collect. Simulation provides scalable supervision, yet models trained on clean synthetic signals transfer poorly because of multipath, clutter, response statistics, and resolution degradation. We present mmSimPrior, a simulation-pretrained framework that factorizes transferable knowledge into signal, motion, and radar-to-motion mapping priors. A multi-modal signal encoder is pretrained with a physics-informed domain-randomization curriculum that emulates propagation- and acquisition-level variations, while a joint-temporal tokenizer learns a discrete prior over plausible human motion. A shared mapping prior supports classification over a learned motion codebook for constrained zero-shot reconstruction and continuous regression for flexible limited-data adaptation. We further construct a 4.2M-frame, 31K-sequence dataset suite and introduce a No-Overlap Setting that excludes repeated complete subject-environment-location-motion configurations across adaptation and test. Experiments on mmSimPrior-Real and RT-Pose demonstrate consistent gains: with only 24 paired real sequences, mmSimPrior-Reg reduces MPJPE by 24.7% to 39.0% over the strongest baseline across the three environments, while mmSimPrior-Cls reduces zero-shot MPJPE by 8.5% without finetuning.

</details>


### [52] [Accuracy potential of visual localization exploiting high-end street-level imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24409)
*Jonas Meyer,Stephan Nebiker,Pascal Theiler,Norbert Haala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套可扩展的视觉定位系统，并公布了一个具有极高精度的户外定位数据集，实验显示视觉定位可实现厘米级甚至亚厘米级空间定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动导航、测绘、机器人、增强与混合现实等应用场景对高精度姿态信息的需求不断增长。传统GNSS定位受环境与精度的限制，视觉定位被视为一种重要补充。然而，针对测绘级高精度的视觉定位研究有限，主要受制于缺乏大规模高精度的公开数据集。

Method: 本文提出了一套基于高分辨率精确地理参考街景图像的视觉定位流程。方法包括基于先验的参考候选选择、即时本地SFM建模和基于PnP的姿态估算。同时，作者发布了FHNW Muttenz数据集，涵盖10公里街道网络，提供亚厘米级6-DoF真值姿态。

Result: 在公布的新数据集上，系统实现了平移误差中位数1-5厘米，旋转误差0.05-0.1度，在理想条件下可达1厘米和平移0.03度的旋转精度。

Conclusion: 实验表明，视觉定位技术有能力补充达到测绘级精度的GNSS定位，为利用消费级设备进行高精度3D地理信息采集与自动化地理参考提供了新途径。

Abstract: Accurate and reliable pose information with respect to a reference frame is increasingly demanded across applications such as autonomous navigation, surveying, robotics, and augmented and mixed reality. Visual localization can serve as a complementary positioning modality to GNSS, whose applicability and accuracy are often limited. Yet, the accuracy potential of visual localization has not been systematically investigated against survey-grade demands. This is mainly due to the lack of publicly available, large-scale outdoor datasets with ground-truth poses in the sub-centimeter range. In this work, we address both gaps. We introduce a scalable visual localization pipeline that employs precisely georeferenced, high-resolution street-level imagery directly as the scene representation. It combines prior-guided reference candidate selection with on-the-fly local Structure-from-Motion reconstruction and PnP-based pose estimation. We further present the FHNW Muttenz dataset, a real-world dataset covering a contiguous 10 km street network mapped in two mobile mapping campaigns approximately 1.5 years apart. It consists of high-resolution reference imagery and query sequences acquired by four different cameras across five representative scenes. All images are precisely co-registered, yielding 6-DoF ground-truth poses in the sub-centimeter range. Using this dataset, we evaluate the accuracy potential of visual localization. Our experiments demonstrate median pose accuracies in the range of 1-5 cm for translation and 0.05-0.1° for rotation, reaching as low as 1 cm and 0.03° under favorable conditions. These results show that visual localization can complement survey-grade GNSS positioning, paving the way for 3D geospatial data acquisition using consumer devices and fully automated georeferencing approaches. The dataset is publicly available at: https://fhnw-muttenz-vl-dataset.github.io/.

</details>


### [53] [Breaking the Synthetic-Real Domain Shortcut for Training-Free Generative Replay-based Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22994)
*Tao Zhang,Qixuan Fan,Yiyuan Liang,Yanjie Wang,Song Yan,Tian Tian,Jiahuan Zhou,Luxin Yan,Sheng Zhong,Xu Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种新的类增量学习方法DREAM，通过消除合成与真实样本间的“域捷径”现象，实现无样本CIL的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式回放方法虽然不需存储旧类样本，保护隐私，但混合生成数据与真实数据会导致模型依赖领域差异而非语义特征，造成性能下降。

Method: 提出DREAM框架，利用冻结的文本到图像生成模型无训练地产生旧类别样本，通过子空间整流和正交投影消除域捷径，并用基于真实样本的类别原型正则促进语义对齐。

Result: 在4个公开数据集上的实验表明，DREAM显著优于现有的无样本类增量学习方法，并达到最新水平。

Conclusion: DREAM有效解决了生成数据与真实数据混合带来的域偏差问题，在不存储旧样本的前提下大幅提升了CIL的稳定性和准确性。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning (CIL) requires models to continuously acquire new knowledge while avoiding catastrophic forgetting. While exemplar replay is effective, it raises concerns regarding privacy and storage. Thus, generative replay has emerged as a viable alternative, synthesizing old data using frozen pretrained text-to-image (T2I) models without any extra training. However, we observe that directly mixing synthetic old-class data with real new-class data during incremental training leads to significant performance degradation. This issue stems from a "domain shortcut", where models rely on domain-discriminative features instead of semantic class cues. To address this, we propose DREAM ($\underline{\mathbf{D}}$omain-$\underline{\mathbf{R}}$egularized $\underline{\mathbf{E}}$xemplar-free $\underline{\mathbf{A}}$lignment $\underline{\mathbf{M}}$odel), which uses a training-free generator to synthesize old-class data and eliminates domain shortcut via subspace rectification and orthogonal projection, while reinforcing semantic alignment through real-anchored prototype regularization. Extensive experiments on 4 datasets demonstrate that DREAM outperforms existing exemplar-free CIL methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our source code is available at https://github.com/Light-ZhangTao/DREAM.

</details>


### [54] [OmniMate: Open-Ended Real-Time Streaming Audio-Visual Generation for Interactive Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23023)
*Quanyue Song,Yishan He,Yanbo Ding,Zhixiang He,Yongxiang Li,Caigui Jiang,Zhi Zhi Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniMate，一个用于实时交互式音视频虚拟人生成的统一框架，能够高质量、低延迟地合成视频、语音和音效，并保持多轮对话中的跨模态身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于扩散模型的生成方法在音视频合成领域取得了突破，但当前模型在实时互动流场景下难以兼顾生成时长的不确定性和长时间跨模态身份一致性，限制了沉浸式虚拟人应用落地。

Method: 提出OmniMate框架，核心创新包括：（1）Generation Progress Controller（GPC），自适应地调控流式生成过程、实现自然衔接；（2）Multi-Reference Conditioning Module（MRCM），通过多图像和语音参考，持续提供视觉和说话人身份信息。框架支持实时、多轮、音视频联动的互动体验。

Result: 在VerseBench互动扩展版上进行大量实验，OmniMate表现出高质量、低延迟的生成效果，并在长时间流式对话中，身份一致性显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: OmniMate支持高真实感、连贯且具备响应能力的多轮虚拟人互动，推进了音视频流式生成模型在实时交互系统中的应用前景。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion-based generative models have enabled real-time audio-driven avatar generation and unified audio-visual synthesis, providing a promising foundation for interactive avatar systems. However, extending these models to real-time interactive streaming remains challenging, as the generation horizon is unknown in advance and cross-modal identity consistency gradually degrades during long-term generation. To address these challenges, we propose OmniMate, a unified framework for open-ended real-time interactive audio-visual avatar generation. OmniMate jointly synthesizes visual content, speech, and audio effects in real time, enabling natural and immersive multi-turn interactions. To achieve adaptive response progression, we introduce a Generation Progress Controller (GPC) that explicitly models the generation progress of each streaming chunk, allowing the model to complete responses according to the desired progress and achieve seamless transitions between execution and listening states. To preserve long-term cross-modal identity consistency, we propose a Multi-Reference Conditioning Module (MRCM), which leverages multiple reference images and a reference speech segment to provide persistent visual and speaker identity cues throughout long-duration streaming interactions. Extensive experiments on an interaction-oriented adaptation of VerseBench demonstrate that OmniMate achieves high-quality, low-latency streaming generation while maintaining strong long-term audio-visual consistency. The results further show that OmniMate supports realistic, coherent, and responsive interactive avatar experiences over extended multi-turn conversations.

</details>


### [55] [When Less Is More: A Controlled Benchmark of Lightweight CNNs for Satellite Land-Cover Segmentation on DeepGlobe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23024)
*Atiq Ur Rehman,Joseph Michael Donovan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了五种经典卷积神经网络架构在高分辨率遥感影像地物分类任务中的表现，发现轻量化且迁移学习优化的模型在保证效率的同时可达到和更深模型相当甚至更优的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率遥感影像对于地表覆盖分类非常关键，但目前深度学习架构在效率与精度之间的权衡尚未在严格可复现实验下被充分量化。本研究旨在系统评估不同经典卷积神经网络在遥感语义分割任务中的实际效能。

Method: 在DeepGlobe地物分类数据集上，选取VGG16、MobileNetV2、InceptionV3、AlexNet和自定义CNN，共进行三轮逐步优化（正则化、迁移学习与结构深度调整），全部实验统一预处理、超参数和训练流程，未使用数据增强或类别不平衡校正。

Result: MobileNetV2_v1（模型体积仅24.98MB）获得了最高总体精度（0.7906）与平均交并比（0.4625），超过了更深的InceptionV3_v2 (125.17MB, 精度0.7610) 和VGG16_v2 (71.13MB, 精度0.7653)。在城市、农业和水体类别表现突出，但对于光谱上类似的少数类（如牧场和荒地）区分仍有局限。模型在留出测试集上的空间泛化能力和边界分割表现突出。

Conclusion: 轻量化、迁移学习优化的神经网络可在资源受限的遥感环境中达到或超越深层模型的表现，有望支持大规模、高效的地物覆盖制图。

Abstract: High-resolution satellite imagery is the backbone of good land-cover classification, and without that, environmental monitoring, urban planning, and sustainable resource management all fall short. Deep learning architectures perform well in semantic segmentation, but the efficiency-accuracy trade-off across classical convolutional encoders is not well quantified under controlled, reproducible conditions. This study compares five architectures VGG16, MobileNetV2, InceptionV3, AlexNet, and CNN on the DeepGlobe Land Cover Classification dataset using three progressively optimized iterations to isolate regularisation, transfer learning, and architectural depth. To ensure performance differentials reflect architectural properties, all experiments used identical preprocessing, hyperparameter, and training protocols without data augmentation or class-imbalance correction. At 24.98 MB, MobileNetV2_v1 had the highest overall accuracy (0.7906) and mean Intersection over Union (0.4625), outperforming deeper alternatives like InceptionV3_v2 (125.17 MB, accuracy 0.7610) and VGG16_v2 (71.13 MB, accuracy 0.7653). Class-wise analysis showed strength in urban, agricultural, and water categories, but rangeland-barren confusion showed that architectural optimization alone cannot optimize spectrally similar minority classes. Strong spatial generalization and crisp boundary delineation were confirmed on held-out test imagery, validating operational applicability. These results show that lightweight, transfer-learned models can match or outperform deeper models in resource-constrained remote-sensing environments, enabling scalable land-cover mapping.

</details>


### [56] [Structured Redundancy Modeling for Efficient Visual Token Pruning in High-Resolution MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23046)
*Jouwon Song,Woohyeong Kim,Kyeongbo Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的视觉token剪枝方法SFPruner，显著加速多模态大模型（MLLMs）的推理过程，减少系统延迟，并保持较强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大模型规模增大，视觉token数量暴涨，影响推理速度。现有token剪枝方法往往需要迭代优化，难以把FLOPs的减少转化为实际推理加速，存在显著瓶颈。

Method: SFPruner通过结构性改造，将冗余控制直接融入评分空间，实现了一次前向传播完成冗余感知的重要性选择。方法包括：1）语义引导的ridge leverage机制整合指令相关性和视觉显著性，抑制主协方差方向，减少表示偏置；2）基于排序的方向掩码，通过并行张量操作让高分token抑制冗余低分token。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL等模型上，SFPruner将token选择阶段的延迟从112.4ms降至2.5ms（处理512个token时），性能与SOTA方法相当，在高压缩率下仍能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SFPruner有效将理论上的token压缩转化为实际加速，提升了多模态大模型的推理效率，为后续模型优化和实际部署提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Recent high-resolution Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) generate thousands of visual tokens per input, leading to a visual token explosion that introduces severe latency bottlenecks. While token pruning mitigates this issue, state-of-the-art subset-optimization methods typically rely on iterative subset construction to jointly capture visual diversity and instruction relevance. As visual token counts scale, this sequential dependency introduces significant selection overhead, severely limiting the translation of theoretical FLOPs reductions into actual wall-clock speedups. To address this limitation, we propose Single-Forward Pruner (SFPruner), a structural reformulation of visual token pruning that embeds redundancy control directly into the scoring space, bypassing the need for iterative combinatorial optimization. Our non-iterative framework achieves redundancy-aware importance selection in a single forward pass through two complementary mechanisms. First, to attenuate redundancy at the covariance level, we introduce a semantics-guided ridge leverage scheme. By integrating instruction relevance and visual saliency, this mechanism suppresses dominant covariance directions and mitigates representation bias. Second, ranking-based directional masking resolves residual overlap through asymmetric similarity competition, where higher-scoring tokens explicitly suppress redundant lower-scoring alternatives via parallel tensor operations. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our approach maintains stable selection costs, reducing the token selection process by up to 110 ms, from 112.4 ms to just 2.5 ms at 512 tokens in Qwen2.5-VL. This structural efficiency successfully translates theoretical token reductions into tangible inference speedups while preserving highly competitive performance against state-of-the-art techniques under aggressive compression.

</details>


### [57] [Similarity Is Not Logic: Factored Inference for Dual-Encoder Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23052)
*Sultan Alshehri,Zhantao Yang,Han Zhang,Marios Savvides*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了双编码器视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在组合性约束下的表现，指出其检索接口无法精确处理逻辑组合查询如“有雨伞且无人”，提出了新的外部逻辑约束编辑（LCSE）方法显著提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLMs虽能进行零样本检索，但无法正确理解和执行如“X且非Y”这样的逻辑组合式检索，根本原因在于其相似度接口的Bag-of-Concepts效应，无法有效区分和执行复合语义约束，造成误检。

Method: 提出了‘factored inference’思想，将证据提取与约束执行分离，进而提出一种无需再训练的新方法LCSE（Logic-Constrained Score Editing），在冻结原模型的情况下，用外部方法对各概念分数执行逻辑约束。还构建了FACTOR-Bench数据集验证方法性能。

Result: 在FACTOR-Bench上，LCSE准确率达到85.5%，相比最佳精调基线的73.2%有大幅提升。应用于SigLIP 2模型准确率提升到90.7%。NegBench COCO MCQ任务准确率由27.2%提升到65.2%，同时保持检索性能。

Conclusion: VLMs的核心检索瓶颈在于证据聚合方式而非编码表达。所提LCSE方法无需再训练且大幅提升组合性查询准确率，是面向实际检索需求的有效改进。

Abstract: Dual-encoder vision-language models (VLMs) expose a similarity interface that enables zero-shot retrieval but fails compositional constraints: queries like "umbrella and no person" retrieve images containing both, even when concept detection is reliable. We trace this to an interface-level Bag-of-Concepts effect, where similarity scores approximate mean pooling of concept evidence regardless of operators. Although operator-dependent signals exist in text embeddings, they are too weak or misaligned to affect rankings. Fine-tuning does not reliably resolve this failure because the dominant bottleneck is how similarity aggregates evidence rather than what encoders represent. We propose factored inference, which separates evidence extraction from constraint execution, and introduce LCSE (Logic-Constrained Score Editing), a training-free method that executes constraints externally using concept scores from frozen encoders. We also introduce FACTOR-Bench, where LCSE achieves 85.5% accuracy versus 73.2% for the best fine-tuned baseline, 90.7% when applied to SigLIP 2, and improves NegBench COCO MCQ accuracy from 27.2% to 65.2% while preserving retrieval performance.

</details>


### [58] [DishSeg24k: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Food Segmentation with Stochastic Expert Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23070)
*Yilin Wang,Haochen Shi,Guanyu Chen,Weiqing Min,Jinkai Zheng,Chenggang Yan,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了用于餐饮场景下食物分割的大规模基准数据集DishSeg24k，并设计了新模型FEAST来提升分割性能，取得了显著领先的实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食物分割数据集和方法难以应对真实餐饮环境中菜品高度重叠、类别细粒度相似以及类别分布极端长尾等挑战，因此需要更复杂的数据集和更先进的方法。

Method: 1）构建了DishSeg24k数据集，包含24,096张图片、112,281个实例、278个细粒度类别。2）提出FEAST分割框架，将基于查询的解码过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），每一层为一步决策，沿菜品边界探索不确定性；3）解码器中引入强化学习引导的混合专家（MoE）模块，并采用双评论家分离优化策略，将任务导向查询精细化与结构感知专家路由分开优化，提高了专家特化，避免长尾分布下专家崩溃。

Result: 在DishSeg24k数据集上，FEAST取得了比现有方法高3.21%的mIoU、3.68%的mDice和4.00%的mAcc。并在FoodSeg103数据集上进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: DishSeg24k数据集和FEAST模型能更好地应对真实餐饮场景下的复杂分割挑战，为后续食物分割研究提供了更高标准和有效工具。数据及代码将公开释放，推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Food segmentation is essential for applications such as intelligent catering, dietary assessment, and recommendation. However, existing benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of real-world dining scenes. The challenges of dense inter-dish overlap, fine-grained class similarity, and extreme long-tail class distributions exceed the fidelity of current datasets. To fill this gap, we introduce \textbf{DishSeg24k}, a large-scale dish-level segmentation benchmark with 24,096 images, 112,281 instances, and 278 fine-grained categories in real-world dining environments. Based on DishSeg24k, we further propose \textbf{Food Expert-Adaptive Segmentation Transformers (FEAST)} to address these challenges. FEAST models query-based decoding as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), where each decoder layer update is treated as a sequential decision step that explores uncertainty along dish boundaries. We further redesign the decoder with a reinforcement learning (RL)-guided Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) module, in which a dual-critic decoupled optimization scheme separates task-oriented query refinement from structure-aware expert routing. This design promotes expert specialization and prevents expert collapse under long-tail category distributions. Finally, extensive experiments on DishSeg24k demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of FEAST, which outperforms previous methods by {+3.21\%} mIoU, {+3.68\%} mDice, and {+4.00\%} mAcc, respectively. We further validate the effectiveness of FEAST on FoodSeg103. The dataset and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [59] [Inverse Bayesian Inference for Extracting Lesion Dynamics from Longitudinal Spectral CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23078)
*Lukas Förner,Melina Wördehoff,Julian Steffens,Maximilian Schmutz,Rainer Claus,Josua Decker,Thomas Kröncke,Kartikay Tehlan,Thomas Wendler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种逆贝叶斯框架，从纵向光谱CT影像中推断病灶动力学参数，有效分解并解释病灶与周围环境的交互动态，并在NSCLC转移灶数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向医学影像能够揭示病灶随时间演变的信息，但如何从中定量推断驱动病灶演化的动力学参数具有挑战性。作者希望开发一个数学与推断框架，揭示病灶本身和其周围病变的作用机制，以获得更具生物解释性的参数。

Method: 提出逆贝叶斯推断框架，将病灶光谱特征的演化分解为三部分：自发动力学(A)、局部肿瘤负担(B)和卫星灶状态变化(C)，分别建模单个病灶本身变化、肿瘤负担的耦合与环境影响。方法在NSCLC转移灶摄影数据上实现，并进行合成数据验证与交叉耦合分析。

Result: 在真实的NSCLC病灶CT数据中，肺部病灶显示显著的竞争性卫星耦合（B=-0.34，p<0.05），而肝脏病灶表现为协同性卫星耦合（C≈+1.0，p<0.05）。合成实验表明方法能准确恢复参数，并可检测到实际存在的耦合行为。

Conclusion: 首次系统性提出逆动力学推断方法，使从纵向影像中提取可解释动力学参数成为可能，为深入理解病灶行为机制提供新工具，推动从静态影像特征走向机制性动力学特征的研究。

Abstract: Longitudinal medical imaging captures temporal evolution of lesions, yet extracting the underlying dynamical parameters governing this evolution remains challenging. We propose an inverse Bayesian framework for inferring lesion dynamics from longitudinal spectral CT. We decompose spectral feature ($x$) evolution into three components: \begin{equation*}
  \frac{dx_i}{dt} = A_i x_i + B \cdot n + C \cdot Δx_{\text{sat}} \end{equation*} where $A_i$ captures intrinsic dynamics (lesion-autonomous evolution), $B$ captures local environment tumour burden (organ tumour burden through satellite count coupling), and $C$ captures environment/satellite state change (i.e., whether surrounding lesions move similarly or not).
  We demonstrate the framework on photon-counting NSCLC CT data from metastases, recovering distinct dynamical regimes: lung lesions exhibit significant satellite count coupling ($B=-0.34$, $p<0.05$) suggesting competitive dynamics, while liver lesions show synergistic satellite behaviour coupling ($C\approx+1.0$, $p<0.05$). Synthetic validation confirms parameter recovery, and cross-coupling analysis validates that our method detects non-zero coupling when present.
  This work establishes inverse dynamical inference as a principled methodology for extracting interpretable parameters from longitudinal imaging, moving beyond static feature extraction toward mechanistic characterisation of lesion behaviour. The code and data are available at: https://github.com/lukasf98/inverse-bayesian-inference

</details>


### [60] [SHReg: Strictly Rotation-Equivariant Point Cloud Registration via Spherical Harmonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23096)
*Chongjian Wang,Junjie Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严格旋转等变的点云配准方法SHReg，通过SO(3)表示理论实现，无需依赖局部参考系，实现在任意旋转下的精确等变与显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云配准需本地特征兼具区分性与对任意3D旋转的鲁棒性。现有方法多靠脆弱的局部参考系或数据增强近似旋转不变，效果有限且易受未知旋转影响。

Method: 提出SHReg框架，通过将局部几何特征表示为SO(3)的不可约表示，实现任意旋转下的严格等变。采用基于球谐的等变网络骨干，联合学习旋转不变描述符（便于匹配），及旋转等变特征（保留姿态信息），支持每对匹配直接推断变换，减少RANSAC假设采样需求。

Result: 在3DMatch、3DLoMatch及KITTI数据集上的大量实验结果表明，SHReg在配准精度上均优于现有最新方法，尤其在存在大旋转扰动时表现突出。

Conclusion: SHReg通过SO(3)等变特性，结合新颖特征学习方式，实现了显著提升的点云配准鲁棒性与精度，对高旋转场景有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Point cloud registration critically depends on local features that are both distinctive and robust to arbitrary 3D rotations. Existing learning-based methods typically approximate rotation invariance via fragile local reference frames or extensive data augmentation, providing only empirical invariance and often degrading under unseen rotational transformations. In this paper, we propose SHReg, a strictly rotation-equivariant point cloud registration framework grounded in the representation theory of $SO(3)$. By representing local geometric features as irreducible representations of $SO(3)$, SHReg guarantees exact equivariance under arbitrary rotations without relying on local reference frames. Built upon a spherical-harmonics-based equivariant backbone, SHReg jointly learns rotation-invariant descriptors for robust correspondence matching and rotation-equivariant features that preserve fine-grained orientation information. The preserved equivariant structure enables each correspondence to directly hypothesize a rigid transformation, reducing reliance on large-scale hypothesis sampling in conventional RANSAC-based pipelines and leading to improved robustness under challenging rotational variations. Extensive experiments on 3DMatch, 3DLoMatch, and KITTI demonstrate that SHReg consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in registration accuracy, particularly under large rotational perturbations.

</details>


### [61] [DispatchRAG: Grounding Emergency Dispatch Decisions in Real-World Protocols from Traffic Accident Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23132)
*Muhammad Sulthan Adhipradhana,Ehsan Javanmardi,Naren Bao,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DispatchRAG框架，将视觉-语言模型（VLM）与日本交通事故响应协议结合，提升了事故情境下的救援服务调度决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型虽能在交通事故推理上取得进展，但未结合实际交通事故应急响应协议，导致无法直接用于事故严重性评估，影响急救服务的准确调度，尤其在涉及行人事故时可能造成严重后果。

Method: 作者提出DispatchRAG框架，结合RAG（检索-生成）机制，先检索与交通事故最相关的协议，再利用大语言模型（LLM）进行推理，提出合适的应急响应措施。并构建了基于日本应急协议的Accident Dispatch Dataset作为评估基准。

Result: 在Accident Dispatch Dataset上，DispatchRAG在多种事故情景下相较于基础VLM表现更优，能够更准确地进行事故严重性判断与响应决策。

Conclusion: DispatcherRAG框架有效提升了VLM在真实交通事故场景中的应急响应能力，为未来自动驾驶车辆实现自动事故上报和应急调度提供了依据和基础。

Abstract: Assessing the severity of a traffic accident scenario is important to decide which emergency service to dispatch. Missing an ambulance dispatch on a pedestrian accident is a fatal issue that can lead to death. Recently, Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been a promising tool for accident reasoning, yet many VLMs are not grounded in real-life accident response protocols, making them not usable in accident severity assessment off-the-shelf. We introduced DispatchRAG, an accident assessor and dispatcher framework grounded in real-life Japanese traffic-accident response protocols, designed to enhance VLMs to generate an appropriate emergency response during an emergency scenario. Utilizing a RAG-based retrieval mechanism to retrieve the most relevant accident protocol and an LLM-powered reasoner to suggest the most proper response. To support evaluation, we introduce Accident Dispatch Dataset, a comprehensive dataset of accident assessment and emergency response according to Japanese accident response protocols adapted from the MM-AU dataset. We validate our framework on the Accident Dispatch Dataset, showing strong performance across various accident scenarios compared to the baseline VLM, pointing toward integration in autonomous vehicles that can automatically report both their own and nearby accidents.

</details>
