### [62] [Towards Dual-Brain Minimal Sufficient Representation for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23181)
*Yihao Wu,Chenyi Xu,Liqi Yan,Chenhuan Cai,Geyong Min,Bin Lin,Fangli Guan,Jianhui Zhang,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BrainNav的视觉-语言导航(VLN)新框架，致力于通过信息精简提升智能体泛化与效率，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言导航任务中，常用的大模型和世界模型方法保留了过多与任务无关的信息，不利于模型泛化且提高了计算负担，因此需要一种能够根据指令筛选、精简关键信息的机制。

Method: BrainNav框架包括三大模块：1）逻辑锚点模型，结合导航指令实现有选择的感知，抑制环境噪音；2）极简约束对齐模块，将离散的语言意图与连续的潜在空间动态高效同步，过滤冗余信息；3）压缩世界模型，在压缩的低秩潜在空间内预测动作相关的状态。整体设计旨在对齐语义意图与空间感知。

Result: 实验证明，BrainNav在R2R-CE和RxR-CE的val-unseen集上，SR（成功率）/SPL（路径效率）指标分别提升2.0%/1.0和0.94%/0.78，优于先前SOTA。

Conclusion: 极简且足够的世界表示能有效提升VLN任务的鲁棒性与效率，BrainNav为该领域提供了有力新基础。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to ground language in egocentric observations and plan in unseen scenes. Although recent multimodal large models and world-model-based methods have improved navigation, they often preserve excessive task-irrelevant detail, weakening generalization and increasing computational burden. We propose BrainNav, a navigation framework grounded in the Principle of Minimal Sufficiency. BrainNav consists of three components: a Logical Anchor Model that implements instruction-aware selective perception to suppress environmental noise, a Minimalist Constraint Alignment module that serves as a compact cross-modal bottleneck, efficiently synchronizing discrete linguistic intent with continuous latent dynamics while filtering out redundant information, and a Compression World Model that predicts action-conditioned states within a condensed, low-rank latent space. These modules align semantic intent with spatial perception, enhancing the agent's robustness and efficiency in complex tasks. Experiments show that BrainNav improves over prior SOTA by 2.0 % / 1.0 in SR/SPL on R2R-CE val-unseen and 0.94 % / 0.78 on RxR-CE val-unseen. These results indicate that minimally sufficient world representations provide an effective foundation for robust VLN.

</details>


### [63] [Fashion-3DLR: A Controllable 3D Garment Generation Using Pairwise Fashion Elements for Intelligent Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23189)
*Shenghao Yang,Hongtao Zhang,Yuhan Yi,Zhihao Tang,Zihao Cui,Lian Wen,Han Yan,Yuan Gao,Mingbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Fashion-3DLR框架，能高质量生成多样化的3D服装，支持3D物理仿真和虚拟试穿，效果优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC技术在2D服装生成方面已成熟，但3D服装生成尚处于初级阶段，尤其是多样化3D服装中的不同设计元素在三维空间中的复杂语义耦合成为主要难点。

Method: 提出了Fashion-3DLR框架，通过一个Garment Feature Fusion Diffusion Transformer (GFF-DiT)模块在潜空间融合2D服装设计元素（如草图、纹理），再用rectified flow transformer生成用于3D服装重建的几何特征，最后解码为高质量的3D高斯表示和三维网格。该方法支持后续3D物理仿真和虚拟试穿。

Result: 实验表明，Fashion-3DLR在3D服装物理仿真和虚拟试穿等任务上优于当前主流方法，生成的3D服装结构良好、不封闭，可直接用于下游任务。

Conclusion: Fashion-3DLR是一个多功能、表现优异的3D服装设计生成工具，有望推动数字时尚产业3D内容创作的发展。

Abstract: AI-generated content (AIGC) has made significant progress, with 2D generative models becoming ready-to-use tools for the digital fashion industry. However, 3D garment generation remains in its nascent stage, where in the realm of fashion, the semantic information of diverse design elements exhibits intricate coupling relationships in 3D representations, posing substantial challenges for generating diverse 3D garments. In this work, to handle the above problem, We introduce Fashion-3DLR, a novel 3D garment generation framework that utilizes diverse design elements to create high-quality, versatile 3D garment assets. Specifically, to bridge the semantic gaps between different fashion elements, we propose a Garment Feature Fusion Diffusion Transformer (GFF-DiT) module to integrate 2D fashion design elements, e.g., sketch and texture, into latent space. Within the latent space, we then employ a rectified flow transformer to generate geometry latents, which can be decoded into various 3D garment representations, including 3D Gaussians and meshes. Furthermore, we integrate Fashion-3DLR into downstream tasks, achieving the 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-driven cloth physical simulation and mesh-based virtual try-on. Experimental results indicate that Fashion-3DLR surpass the previous state-of-the-art methods, which verify that the proposed work can generate well-structured, non-watertight garments capable of physical simulation and virtual try-on, underscoring its potential as a versatile 3D garment design tool.

</details>


### [64] [OmniScope: Modality-Decoupled Token Compression for Omnimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23193)
*Jinsen Su,Yongdong Luo,Yuexiao Ma,Yibo Hu,Meiguang Jin,Xiaowu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有大模型音视频压缩常用单一模态指导另一模态，但这种做法面临跨模态信息不匹配的问题。OmniScope方法在无需训练的前提下，分别估算音频和视频的重要性，大幅提升压缩条件下的准确性和资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往方法通过一种模态（如视频）决定另一模态（如音频）的保留信息，但实验发现两种模态的信息高峰时间点常常不同，导致重要信息在压缩中丢失。

Method: OmniScope以查询（query）作为共享语义锚点，但分别为音频和视频分配token，采用视觉锚点-变化值策略剪枝视频token，音频则在每秒内合并token，实现冗余减少及时间连续性。无需训练即可应用。

Result: 在四个音视频基准和两种Qwen2.5-Omni模型规模下，OmniScope所有压缩比例下准确率均最佳。在仅保留25% token情况下，推理预填加速3.53倍，GPU内存降低超15%，准确率下降仅0.35。

Conclusion: OmniScope实现了高效且几乎无损的跨模态token压缩，表明大模型推理中应共享查询但分别计算重要性。这为多模态大模型压缩提供了新的简单有效原则。

Abstract: Existing token compression methods for omnimodal large language models typically rely on one modality to determine what to retain in the other. We show that this assumption often breaks down: for the same query, audio and video relevance often peaks at different moments. This cross-modal salience mismatch makes unidirectional guidance prone to discarding answer-critical cues under aggressive compression. We propose OmniScope, a training-free token compression framework that uses the query as a shared semantic anchor while estimating relevance separately for audio and video. OmniScope allocates modality-specific token budgets, prunes visual tokens with an anchor-delta strategy that preserves both global context and temporal changes, and merges audio tokens within each second to reduce redundancy while maintaining temporal continuity. Across four audio-video benchmarks and two Qwen2.5-Omni model scales, OmniScope achieves the best average accuracy across all compression settings. At 25% overall token retention, it delivers up to 3.53x prefill speedup and more than 15% GPU memory reduction, with only a 0.35-point drop in average accuracy. These results suggest a simple design principle for OmniLLM inference: share the query across modalities, but not the salience estimates. The code is available at https://github.com/MAC-AutoML/OmniScope.

</details>


### [65] [Out-of-Length Scene Text Recognition: A Two-Axis Diagnosis and a Training-Free Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23194)
*Zobeir Raisi,John Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析并解决了场景文本识别模型在处理超长文本时的失效问题，通过将长文本图片切片并独立解码，再通过几何对齐拼接，只需解码端的推理修正即可大幅提升长文本识别能力，无需更改模型结构或额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景文本识别（STR）模型主要在短文本上训练，无法处理实际应用中常见的超长文本（如标识牌、产品标签等），文中旨在揭示其失效机制并寻找无需大幅改动或重新训练的新解法。

Method: 作者系统地分析了失效原因，发现主要障碍在于编码器宽度限制。经过一系列尝试（特征重缩放及加权微调）效果有限。核心贡献是在推理阶段将长图像切分为与模型训练宽度一致的重叠小块，分别推理后通过几何对齐和编辑距离拼接为完整识别结果，无需修改模型或额外训练。

Result: 该方法在Long Text Benchmark（LTB）上取得了42.79-43.05%的平均单词准确率，对比现有SOTA（41.57%）提升明显，在最困难分组提升11-12个百分点。直接应用于公开网络（PARSeq checkpoint）可达47.11%。该方案简单高效，推理速度不逊于原模型解码。

Conclusion: 推理阶段的切块加拼接方法可在不改动网络结构和训练策略的前提下，显著提升STR模型对长文本的泛化性能，全面超越部分定制架构或训练方法。相关代码及评测工具已开源。

Abstract: Scene Text Recognition (STR) models are trained almost exclusively on word crops of at most 25 characters, yet real deployments (signage, product labels, dense captions) require reading much longer text. This paper diagnoses that failure and then closes it. The diagnosis separates out-of-length failure into two simultaneously extrapolating axes (the encoder's width axis and the decoder's time axis) and shows that encoder width, not decoder length, is the dominant failure mode. Representation-side fixes bring only partial relief: training-free rotary rescalings recover at most 2-4 points of character error rate (CER), and a weighted fine-tuning recipe recovers 6-8 points while improving standard-benchmark accuracy, yet word accuracy on the Long Text Benchmark (LTB) stays near zero, because the residual gap lies in the decoding mechanism rather than the representation. We then close that gap at inference time, on an unmodified word-level checkpoint: the long image is sliced into overlapping crops at the model's training width, each decoded independently and in-distribution, and the reads stitched by geometry-anchored edit-distance alignment. This procedure reaches 42.79-43.05% bucket-average word accuracy on LTB across two base checkpoints, matching the published state of the art (41.57%) and beating it by 11-12 points on the hardest bucket, at wall-clock parity with plain decoding; applied unchanged to the public PARSeq checkpoint it reaches 47.11%. Once chunking is applied fine-tuning no longer helps: the decoding-side fix alone matches purpose-built architectures. We release the diagnosis harness and implementation.

</details>


### [66] [Counterfactual Motion Reliability Learning for Robust UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23209)
*Yuehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 红外无人机跟踪因目标小、对比度低且易被干扰，追踪难度大。本文提出CMRTrack，通过反事实运动可靠性学习，有效分辨真实目标运动与背景干扰，提升了跟踪性能，在多个数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对红外无人机跟踪领域目标易与背景混淆且运动线索不可靠的问题，现有Transformer方法在目标外观弱时表现受限，迫切需要能分辨目标一致性运动的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出CMRTrack，设计运动证据编码器用于提取相邻区域的时序运动信息，并加入反事实目标擦除分支，在训练中生成困难的运动参考，驱动编码器区分真实目标运动与伪运动。再通过运动引导的token调制和基于可靠性的分数融合，实现感知运动的特征增强。

Result: 在Anti-UAV410数据集上，CMRTrack方法比主流跟踪器表现更优，并在OSTrack基线基础上有显著提升。消融实验和定性分析进一步证实了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CMRTrack通过反事实运动可靠性学习，有效利用运动线索，提升了红外无人机目标的跟踪鲁棒性，为复杂场景下视觉跟踪提供了新思路。

Abstract: Infrared unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) tracking is challenging because the target is often small, low-contrast, and easily confused with thermal distractors or cluttered backgrounds. Recent Transformer-based trackers have achieved promising performance by learning strong appearance representations, but their responses can still be dominated by background structures when the target appearance is weak or ambiguous. A natural solution is to introduce temporal motion cues. However, in infrared UAV tracking, motion cues are not always reliable: camera jitter, dynamic backgrounds, sensor noise, and target disappearance may produce temporal variations that are stronger than the true target motion. Therefore, the key challenge is not simply how to use motion, but how to distinguish target-consistent motion from background-induced pseudo motion. To this end, we propose CMRTrack, a counterfactual motion reliability learning framework for robust infrared UAV tracking. CMRTrack first extracts temporal evidence from adjacent search regions using a lightweight motion evidence encoder. During training, a counterfactual target-erased history branch is introduced to construct hard motion references, encouraging the motion encoder to learn reliable target-consistent motion rather than arbitrary temporal changes. The learned motion evidence is then incorporated into a one-stream tracking framework through motion-guided token modulation and reliability-aware score fusion, enabling adaptive feature enhancement and response refinement. Extensive experiments on Anti-UAV410 demonstrate that CMRTrack consistently outperforms representative state-of-the-art trackers and significantly improves the OSTrack baseline, with ablation studies and qualitative analysis verifying the effectiveness of the proposed counterfactual motion reliability learning.

</details>


### [67] [BoneAgeTW2: Automated Skeletal Maturation Assessment via the Tanner-Whitehouse 2 Method, Deep Learning, and Clinical Report Generation with Distribution Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23224)
*Juan Manuel Castillo Pinto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了BoneAgeTW2，这是第一个完全开源、能够端到端自动化实现Tanner-Whitehouse 2（TW2）骨龄评估临床流程的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨龄评估在儿科生长发育中非常重要，但传统方法耗时且主观，亟需高效、自动化、标准化的开源工具来替代人工操作，提升效率和一致性。

Method: 系统采用YOLOv8模型精确定位X光手部20块骨骼，并基于EfficientNet-B3主干网络，设置20个独立的分类头同时判定每一骨骼的成熟分期（A-I）。使用公开RSNA数据集，通过伪标签法，将整体骨龄标签细化为单骨阶段标签，进行训练。同时系统自动生成带有交互式分布曲线的临床PDF报告。

Result: 模型在12,611张手部X光片的大规模公开数据集上训练，实现了对20块手部骨骼成熟分期的自动识别和评估，结果以临床可用的标准报告形式自动输出。源代码已完全开源。

Conclusion: BoneAgeTW2可实现骨龄自动化标准评估，极大提高效率和一致性，便于临床应用与科学研究，为骨龄评估提供了高效可靠且完全开源的解决方案。

Abstract: We present BoneAgeTW2, the first fully open-source system to automate the complete Tanner-Whitehouse 2 (TW2) clinical protocol for skeletal maturity assessment end-to-end. The system employs YOLOv8 for precise detection and localization of the 20 TW2 hand bones from radiographic images, and an EfficientNet-B3 backbone with 20 independent classification heads to assign maturation stages (A-I) to each bone simultaneously. From these predictions, the system automatically generates clinical PDF reports including interactive Gaussian distribution curves for all 20 bones, enabling direct comparison with population norms. The model is trained on the public RSNA Pediatric Bone Age Challenge dataset (12,611 hand radiographs) using a pseudo-labeling strategy to derive per-bone stage labels from global bone age annotations. The full codebase is publicly available at https://github.com/jmmana/BoneAgeTW2.

</details>


### [68] [A Reconstruction-Based Framework for Caption Evaluation Beyond Reference Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23235)
*Zhijiang Tang,Jiaxin Qi,Kaihua Tang,Yuhua Zheng,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考答案的图像描述自动评估方法，实现通过重建原始图像并利用下游任务判定语义一致性，从而衡量描述质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像描述评价方法主要依赖人工参考描述，存在主观性强、难以全面衡量描述与原图语义一致性等问题，因此需要开发不依赖参考描述的自动化评价方案。

Method: 作者提出以“图像重建”为核心原则：优质的描述应能支持对原始图像的有效重建，并提出修正原则，即只需重建出与原图语义等价的内容。具体实现中，利用下游视觉-语言任务对重建与原图的语义一致性进行测试，给出一种任务相关且无须参考答案的评价得分。同时，作者还提出了CTTD数据集以降低实验成本。

Result: 验证了该方法在语义评估上的有效性，并分析了方法在不同组成部分下的局限性，展示了CTTD数据集作为低成本替代的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究为图像描述的自动化、无参考答案评估开辟了新思路，强调对语义传达的关注，为后续视觉-语言研究和相关实际应用提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Image captioning is a primary task in vision--language research, yet assessing how faithfully a caption preserves image semantics without relying on reference captions remains unsettled. Prevailing evaluations rely on human-annotated references, whose content reflects annotator intent and captioning proficiency. In this paper, we study a reconstruction-based principle for caption evaluation: a caption is as good as its capacity to enable reconstruction of the original image. However, because captioning inherently compresses visual information, it is impossible to recover all details, and pixel-wise comparison between reconstructed and source images is neither feasible nor meaningful. Through our in-depth analysis of the nature of captions, whose fundamental purpose is to transmit the semantic content of an image, we propose a revised principle: a caption is as good as its capacity to enable a reconstruction that is semantically equivalent to the original. To assess semantic equivalence, we test whether the reconstruction matches the original image across a suite of downstream vision--language tasks, yielding a reference-free, task-conditioned caption score. We characterize component-dependent limitations and introduce the lower-cost Captioning Turing Test Dataset (CTTD) surrogate.

</details>


### [69] [SARATR-X-v2: Scale-Aware Structural Pre-Training for SAR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23238)
*Weijie Li,Yafei Song,Yongxiang Liu,Bowen Peng,Jie Zhou,Jingyuan Xia,Wei Yang,Tianpeng Liu,Zhen Liu,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的合成孔径雷达（SAR）自监督预训练目标SARATR-X-v2，通过多尺度固定结构提取器与可学习融合权重，兼顾物理稳定性与语义尺度兼容性，实现了在12个SAR任务上的最先进迁移表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR预训练中的重构目标尚未建立合理设计原则，主要难点在于需要同时满足物理上的Speckle稳定性和语义上的多尺度兼容性。传统方法很难同时兼顾这二者，因此需要一种新的重构目标设计框架。

Method: 提出SARATR-X-v2，利用涵盖六个感受野的固定结构提取器（如局部聚合和方向对比），并通过可学习的加权方式将其融合成统一的重构监督信号，用于掩码重建式的自监督训练。

Result: 在12个SAR下游任务（分类、检测、分割）上实现了领先的迁移性能。在合成Speckle干扰下，该方法比像素空间重建显著降低了表征扰动漂移（近2个数量级）。

Conclusion: 物理稳定性和语义尺度兼容性可作为SAR自监督预训练目标设计的原则。论文表明，有效的SAR预训练关键在于重建恰当的结构目标而非更多信号。

Abstract: Masked image modeling has become a dominant paradigm for SAR pre-training, yet the design of the reconstruction target remains fundamentally unsettled. This article argues that a SAR pre-training target should satisfy two conditions to produce transferable representations: (i) physics-grounded stability, i.e., approximate invariance of the target operator to multiplicative speckle inherent in coherent imaging; and (ii) semantic scale compatibility, i.e., coverage of the heterogeneous spatial scales that downstream tasks demand. These two conditions are individually achievable but jointly difficult: physics-grounded stability favors fixed operators, while semantic scale compatibility favors data-driven composition. To this end, SARATR-X-v2 reconciles both within a single design. The target is constructed through fixed structural extractors spanning six receptive fields, from blind-spot local aggregation to directional log-ratio region contrast, and fused via learnable weights into one unified supervision signal for masked reconstruction. On twelve SAR benchmarks across classification, detection, and segmentation, SARATR-X-v2 achieves state-of-the-art transfer performance. Under synthetic speckle variation, the proposed target reduces perturbation drift in the learned representation by nearly two orders of magnitude relative to pixel-space supervision. Taken together, these results establish physics-grounded stability and semantic scale compatibility as a principled framework for pre-training target design under coherent imaging, and suggest that effective SAR pre-training is not about reconstructing more signal, but about reconstructing the right structural target.

</details>


### [70] [WaveZip: Wavelet-Driven Space-Time Decoupling for Video Token Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23265)
*Yuhui Zeng,Wang Chen,Jinfa Huang,Tianyu Xie,Yongdong Luo,Jiayi Ji,Xiawu Zheng,jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WaveZip方法，通过在时域和空域同时利用离散小波变换，实现高效且高保真的大模型视频理解，在10倍压缩下几乎无性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在长视频理解时计算开销高，主要因为视觉token数量多。已有压缩方法要么硬剪切，要么等距合并，效果一般且损失结构和语义信息。

Method: 作者提出WaveZip，在时域用1D离散小波变换（DWT）提取帧内冗余，按高频信号和帧间差异动态分配token预算；在空域用2D DWT，分离低频（结构信息）和高频（细节），对高频部分进行query相关调制重构。无需专门训练，可插入现有LVLM提升推理效率。

Result: 在多个长视频理解基准测试中，WaveZip实现了10倍token压缩下保留99.6%原模型性能，且始终优于当前最优方法。

Conclusion: WaveZip在大幅压缩token数量同时可极小损失性能，高效提升LVLM视频长序列推理能力，兼容主流现有模型，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Existing Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) struggle with long-form video understanding due to the quadratic computational cost of visual tokens. While recent efficient methods attempt to compress tokens via hard pruning or uniform merging, they operate strictly in the spatial feature domain, where robust structural context and discriminative semantic details are inherently entangled. In this work, we propose WaveZip, a joint signal-frequency-domain framework for efficient video inference. Driven by the insight that temporal redundancy resides in low-pass approximation scales while spatial saliency strongly correlates with high-frequency components, WaveZip leverages Discrete Wavelet Transforms (DWT) to disentangle these signals. Temporally, it employs 1D DWT to analyze query-frame relevance, and the resulting high-frequency coefficients are further gated by inter-frame differences, with both signals jointly driving the dynamic allocation of a precise frame-level token budget. Spatially, a 2D DWT decomposes features into low-frequency approximations and high-frequency detail components, where the high-frequency coefficients are modulated within query-salient regions to regulate spatial reconstruction. Importantly, WaveZip requires no task-specific training and can be seamlessly integrated into off-the-shelf LVLMs to boost inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on long video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that WaveZip retains 99.6% of the full performance under an extreme 10x compression ratio, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [71] [What CLIP Knows but Cannot Say: Recovering Negation from Frozen Intermediate Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23271)
*Chen-Yi Lu,Yueh-Shao Chen,Somali Chaterji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PeakPatch方法解决CLIP等视觉-语言模型对否定语义不敏感的问题，并在多个数据集上大幅提升了否定理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对比视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）经常难以区分带否定和原始短语（如“a dog”和“not a dog”），主要原因是最终输出层为追求与视觉对齐导致了否定等成分信息丢失（表征坍塌）。解决否定理解对于提升多模态智能系统的语义推理能力至关重要。

Method: 作者发现CLIP文本编码器中间层具有较强的组合语法能力，但最终层为与视觉特征对齐失去了这种能力。作者据此提出PeakPatch方法，在语法能力达到峰值的中间层拦截表征，并通过嵌入修正网络（ECN）捕捉否定信号，再预测一个偏移向量将丢失的语法信息重新注入最终输出空间。同时提出分数修正网络（SCN），对判别任务调整分数偏移。两模块联合训练，仅新增很少参数且不需调整主模型权重。

Result: 在NegBench基准上，PeakPatch在COCO MCQ任务上达到74.3%（比CLIP提升35.1个百分点），在VOC MCQ达65.5%，并在完全分布外的否定检索任务中超越所有微调方式，同时修正版嵌入也能迁移到文本到图像生成任务和不同主干网络。

Conclusion: PeakPatch无需改变原有视觉-语言模型参数，就能显著增强其否定语义理解与泛化能力，并且开创新思路：通过中间层语法信号拦截与修正，轻量提升已有大型多模态模型的表达能力。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models such as CLIP map semantically opposite phrases (e.g., "a dog" vs. "not a dog") to nearly identical embeddings, rendering them insensitive to negation. We attribute this failure to a phenomenon we call Representational Collapse: by tracking compositional divergence and visual alignment across the CLIP text encoder, we show that middle layers build compositional syntax, but the final layers collapse this structure as visual alignment rises, producing a syntax-blind final representation. To recover the lost negation signal without altering pretrained weights, we propose PeakPatch, a lightweight post-hoc correction system that intercepts the encoder at its compositional peak. An Embedding Correction Network (ECN) uses cross-attention to extract a negation-specific signal from the peak layer, anchored to a stable baseline, and predicts a deviation vector that re-injects the lost syntax into the final-layer embedding space. A complementary Score Correction Network (SCN) predicts bounded scalar score offsets for discriminative tasks. Both modules are trained jointly end-to-end while all CLIP parameters remain frozen, adding only 5.2M parameters (3.5% of the backbone) and preserving the standard cosine similarity interface. On NegBench, PeakPatch achieves 74.3% on COCO MCQ (+35.1 over CLIP, +17.8 over the best encoder fine-tuning method) and 65.5% on VOC MCQ, while outperforming all fine-tuning baselines on fully out-of-distribution negation retrieval despite training only 3.5% of the parameters. The corrected embeddings also transfer to text-to-image generation (+18.4 negation score) and generalize across ViT-B/32, ViT-L/14, and SigLIP backbones. Project URL: https://stevencylu.github.io/PeakPatch/.

</details>


### [72] [The Gate Always Closes: On Injecting Auxiliary Signals into Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23335)
*Moshiur Farazi,Sameera Ramasinghe,Bekir Sait Ciftler,Mahbub Ahmed Turza,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨辅助信号通路在视觉语言模型（VLMs）中的门控机制，发现优化器总是选择关闭辅助通路，通过正则化和几何损失在训练时提升模型表现，并提出用固定、不可选注入取代可学习门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在VLM中常用可学习门控来控制辅助信号通路，但实际应用时这些门控几乎总是关闭，这影响了辅助信号的利用和模型潜力的发挥，本研究旨在分析原因并提出改进方法。

Method: 作者测试了五种注入设计、分析门控关闭现象的两种机制（dead-gradient和negative-utility），实验中用几何辅助损失（如超曲面关系图中的IoA逻辑锥和角度排斥）对LoRA微调正则化。这些损失仅用于训练的前向传播，推理时省略。通过GQA和VSR任务以及不同配置下的消融实验评估效果。

Result: 所有门控辅助通路在推理时实际被关闭；训练时用几何损失正则化，且推理时保留几何通路，可在不损失属性问答精度的前提下，保持或提升关系推理能力。去除几何损失会导致VSR任务性能下降。二次发现：embedding norm对安全生成很重要，建议用固定注入取代可学习门控。

Conclusion: VLM中辅助通路的门控易被压制，通过结合几何正则化并训练、推理期间保留损失信号，可实现更优的关系推理，并建议采用固定注入代替可学习门控以提升稳定性和安全性。

Abstract: Auxiliary signal pathways in VLMs are routinely fitted with learnable gates so the optimiser can decide how much of the signal to admit. We find that the optimiser almost always decides on zero: across five injection designs, every gated pathway becomes behaviourally closed, with accuracy invariant to ablating the pathway at inference even when the gate parameter would nominally pass 30-45% of the signal. We attribute this suppression phenomenon to two regimes, a dead-gradient regime formalised through the caption-invariance of image-derived signals, and a negative-utility regime in which the auxiliary signal actively hurts the loss. Rather than fight suppression, we exploit it: we regularise LoRA fine-tuning with geometric auxiliary losses from hyperbolic visual relational graphs (IoA-driven entailment cones and angular repulsion on the Lorentz manifold), coupled only through the forward pass at training time and dropped at inference. Disaggregating GQA by question type exposes a clean dissociation. Three configurations without geometric losses at inference lose 2.85-3.39pp on relational questions while gaining ~1pp on attribute questions; a fourth that trains with the losses but infers through a soft prompt loses 5.14pp on rel for only +0.23pp on attr, so training-time regularisation alone does not protect relational accuracy without a geometric inference pathway. Configurations that keep the geometric pathway at inference preserve vanilla-level relational accuracy and match the attribute gain. Out of distribution on VSR, the RMS-prefix recipe preserves the spatial signal; stripping the geometric losses (G2) collapses VSR by 4.6pp, isolating them as the OOD source. A secondary result: embedding-norm alignment is necessary for generation-safe prefix injection, and learnable gates should be replaced with fixed, non-optional injection at matched scales.

</details>


### [73] [Explaining BiomedCLIP with Weighted Banzhaf Interactions Supported by Tree-Gram Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23368)
*Jakub Rymarski,Adam Rempała,Bartłomiej Sobieski,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ParseFIxLIP方法，通过基于依存句法树的分组，提升了视觉语言模型在医学领域可解释性的跨模态归因能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在医学任务表现突出，但其解释能力受限于分词方法导致的医学概念碎片化，从而影响模型解释的可信度和临床应用。

Method: 作者提出ParseFIxLIP，将Tree-Gram Parsing嵌入FIxLIP的Banzhaf交互博弈，通过依存句法树将相关token合并成有语义连贯性的组来解释模型决策，利用spaCy工具按依存树深度分组。

Result: ParseFIxLIP显著提升了模型解释跨模态互动的语义一致性和可解释性。在长文本场景下，依然保持了统计稳健性及语义精炼度，优于原有方法。定量和定性分析均证实了其有效性。

Conclusion: ParseFIxLIP为医学视觉语言模型提供了更直观、符合临床需求的解释方式，有助于模型在医学领域的可靠应用。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are demonstrating significant capabilities in medical tasks like radiology analysis, yet providing faithful and interpretable explanations remains a key consideration for their responsible deployment in clinical settings. However, existing explanation methods, such as the widely used FIxLIP framework, often struggle with the fine-grained nature of modern tokenizers. The tokenization problem fragments clinical concepts---splitting terms like "saddle embolus" into scattered, meaningless subwords---which leads to noisy, semantically incoherent cross-modal attributions. Such fragmentation also results in a combinatorial explosion of interaction possibilities, obscuring the model's true reasoning. To address this, we introduce ParseFIxLIP, an extension that incorporates the Tree-Gram Parsing into the Banzhaf interaction game used by FIxLIP. This semantically informed strategy utilizes dependency parsing trees to define explanation players by grouping related text tokens into semantically coherent units. Our smart_depth grouping strategy, merging tokens according to spaCy token dependency tree, successfully mitigates concept fragmentation, yielding substantially more interpretable cross-modal interactions by unifying complex medical concepts. Quantitatively, while baselines struggled with the high dimensionality of long captions, our parsing approach maintained statistical robustness and semantic parsimony. Qualitative analysis on BiomedCLIP, validated on medical imagery (ROCOv2) and general examples, confirms that the approach accurately captures the synergistic influence of grouped words on model predictions. In conclusion, our work offers intuitive and clinically relevant insights into VLM decision-making, fulfilling the critical need for coherent explanations in the medical domain.

</details>


### [74] [UltraViT: Latency-Optimized On-device Vision Encoder for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23373)
*Ioannis Maniadis Metaxas,Adrian Bulat,Alberto Baldrati,Anestis Zaganidis,Yassine Ouali,Hyeonuk Kim,Georgios Tzimiropoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UltraViT，一种专为端侧高效视觉-语言模型（LVLM）设计的视觉编码器，在设备上速度比同类方案快1.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LVLMs因视觉编码器计算消耗大，不适合边缘设备部署。以往模型压缩集中在视觉token减少或语言模型缩小，忽略了视觉编码器自身的优化，且没有针对设备延迟优化的编码器设计。

Method: 提出UltraViT视觉编码器，采用以端侧真实延迟为导向的金字塔结构，在宏块级集成多种空间混合器；并设计两阶段生成预训练方法，先通过稠密蒸馏学习空间特征，再从容量混合冻LLM直接生成监督，实现更优的语义对齐。

Result: 实验结果显示，UltraViT结合设备延迟信息和定制预训练策略，在效率上大幅优于已有视觉编码器，在设备端推理速度提升至1.7倍，同时在生成式多模态任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: UltraViT为需要端侧部署的LVLM提供了高效低延迟的视觉编码解决方案，突破了现有视觉编码器的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) remain bottlenecked by massive computational footprints, precluding their deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. While efforts to compress LVLMs focus heavily on vision token reduction or smaller language models, the vision encoder is largely overlooked, typically deployed as a monolithic, computationally heavy feature extractor. Moreover, there is no previous effort that designs a vision encoder for LVLMs directly optimized for on-device latency. In this paper, we present UltraViT, a vision encoder for LVLMs, explicitly designed and optimized for on-device performance. Specifically, by taking into account real on-device latencies, we systematically design a pyramidal architecture that strategically integrates and adapts heterogeneous spatial mixers at the macro-block level. Furthermore, to pre-train UltraViT, we propose a novel two-stage generative pre-training strategy: cultivating rich spatial features via dense distillation, followed by direct generative supervision from a capacity-mixed frozen LLM. Compared to standard contrastive and SSL, we show that our pre-training is much more effective for achieving high-level semantic grounding for UltraViT needed for the subsequent generative multimodal alignment of LVLM training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our on-device latency-informed design combined with our tailored training strategy establishes a new state-of-the-art for efficient LVLM encoding, significantly outperforming existing encoder-centric baselines while operating on-device at nearly 1.7xthe speed.

</details>


### [75] [Omni-Prune: Query-Aware Unified Token Pruning for Efficient Omnimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23445)
*Yiming Zhong,Chang Nie,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Omni-Prune，一种针对OmniLLMs（全模态大型语言模型）的、无需训练的、具查询感知能力的音视频Token剪枝方法，在保持模型性能的同时大幅提升推理速度并降低显存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OmniLLMs扩展了多模态推理能力，但音视频token序列过长，导致推理延迟和显存占用过高，现有剪枝方法无法有效适应音视频及用户查询的需求。

Method: 1. 将token序列按音频突显峰值自适应划分时间窗口；2. 结合编码器注意力和文本查询相关性对音频和视频token打分，并配对相关token以保证保留关键跨模态信息；3. 在每个窗口内采用K-medoids选取少数代表性token，以增加信息多样性。该流程完全训练无关。

Result: Omni-Prune在综合实验中优于现有基线方法，实现最高3.25倍推理预填加速和1.3倍显存节约，同时保持超过99%的原始模型性能。

Conclusion: Omni-Prune显著提高了OmniLLMs音视频推理的效率与可用性，有效解决了多模态输入在实际应用中的延迟与算力瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Omnimodal large language models (OmniLLMs) are rapidly extending multimodal reasoning to cover synchronized audio and video. However, the resulting audio-video token sequences are long, leading to high prefill latency and GPU memory usage at inference time. Existing token pruning methods, designed mainly for vision-only inputs, miss both the cross-modal links between audio and video and the user query that decides which content matters. To bridge this gap, we present Omni-Prune, a training-free, query-aware audio-visual token pruning framework that jointly removes redundancy from both modalities while keeping task-relevant cross-modal evidence. Specifically, Omni-Prune first splits the token sequence into adaptive time windows placed at audio saliency peaks, then scores audio and video tokens on a single scale that combines encoder attention with text-query relevance, and pairs related audio-video tokens so that they are kept together. Within each window, a final K-medoids step then selects a few representative tokens, adding diverse cues that score-based selection alone would miss. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Omni-Prune outperforms established baseline methods, delivering up to 3.25x prefill speedup and 1.3x memory reduction while retaining over 99% of full-model performance.

</details>


### [76] [Multi-Modal Object Re-Identification with Prompt-S6 and Semantic-Aware Knowledge Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23451)
*Weixiang Zhou,Jiabei Zuo,Yuhao Wang,Cong Wang,Huchuan Lu,Zhixun Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRISM，一个新颖的多模态对象重识别(ReID)框架，解决了现有方法在背景干扰抑制和三模态特征对齐方面的不足，并显著提高了效率和识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ReID方法主要采用两两特征融合，不能有效抑制背景干扰，也难以实现三模态（如RGB、红外、深度）之间的全面对齐。此外，当前的特征聚合算法大多计算复杂度高，难以在实际场景中广泛应用。因此该文旨在提出一种同时兼顾效率和效果的新ReID框架。

Method: 本文提出的PRISM框架结合了基于Prompt的S6（Prompt-S6, PS6）结构与语义感知知识引导。具体包括两个核心模块：1）语义驱动的Token剪枝（SDTP），通过解析分割基础模型的语义先验，动态地剪枝与背景相关的Token，有效抑制噪音并提升特征表征能力；2）渐进式融合网络（PFN），逐步聚合多模态特征，实现三模态高效对齐与信息互补。整个模型保持线性计算复杂度，实现高效的跨模态交互。

Result: 在四个多模态对象ReID基准数据集上进行广泛实验，PRISM在准确率和效率上都优于现有主流方法。该方法能在各种复杂场景下生成更加鲁棒的多模态表征。

Conclusion: PRISM框架通过语义驱动的噪音抑制和高效的三模态逐步融合，显著提升了多模态对象重识别的性能和效率，为相关任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal object Re-Identification (ReID) aims to retrieve specific objects by integrating complementary information from multiple modalities. However, existing multi-modal ReID methods do not effectively address background interference suppression or achieve tri-modal alignment, instead focusing on pairwise feature fusion. Moreover, many current aggregation approaches suffer from high computational complexity. To address these limitations, we propose PRISM, a novel multi-modal ReID framework built upon Prompt-S6 (PS6) and semantic-aware knowledge guidance. PS6 maintains the linear complexity and strong sequence modeling capability of Mamba while enabling efficient cross-modal interaction. Leveraging these advantages, we design two key components: Semantic-Driven Token Pruning (SDTP) and Progressive Fusion Network (PFN). Parsing semantic priors from the segmentation foundation models, the SDTP then leverages these priors and applies dynamic token pruning to suppress background noise and refine feature representations. The PFN progressively aggregates multi-modal features to achieve tri-modal alignment and fully exploit modality complementarity. With the proposed modules, PRISM generates more robust multi-modal representations under complex scenarios. Extensive experiments on four multi-modal object ReID benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our approach. The source code is available at https://github.com/zw-absin/PRISM.

</details>


### [77] [Robust 6-DoF Object Pose Tracking with Built-In Recovery under Occlusions and Rapid Object Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23468)
*Balázs Opra,Léo Ghafari,Thomas Stewart,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合高效的基于学习的关键点匹配、优化对齐与新颖故障检测和恢复模块的6-DoF物体位姿实时追踪方法，尤其针对遮挡与快速运动场景下的鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的6-DoF物体位姿跟踪方法在遇到临时完全遮挡和物体快速运动时易丢失跟踪，且难以自动检测和恢复，对机器人应用造成限制。

Method: 方法结合了基于学习的关键点匹配与优化对齐算法，并设计了故障检测和恢复模块：持续监控位姿可靠性，能检测跟踪发散或遮挡，自动进行全局重检测与位姿估计，验证恢复候选后重启跟踪。

Result: 在标准跟踪基准和新集合的遮挡及快速运动场景数据集上，方法在易于跟踪场景中达到SOTA精度，在57.6帧/秒速度下，在挑战性条件下展现出最佳鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了遮挡和快速运动下的6-DoF物体追踪鲁棒性，是RGB-D数据下物体追踪的重要进步。

Abstract: Real-time 6-DoF object pose tracking is essential for many robotics applications, and several approaches exist. Yet even today's approaches remain unreliable under temporary full occlusions and rapid object motions. Once tracking is lost, most methods struggle to detect the failure and recover automatically, often requiring manual re-initialization. In this paper, we address the problem of robust model-based 6-DoF tracking of unseen objects from RGB-D data, especially in scenarios with occlusion and fast motion. We propose a novel method that combines efficient learning-based keypoint matching with optimization-based alignment and introduces a novel failure detection and recovery module. Our system monitors pose reliability, detects tracking divergence or occlusions, and performs a global re-detection and pose estimation step that robustly verifies recovery candidates before resuming tracking. Our evaluation on standard tracking benchmarks and on a new dataset of occluded and fast-moving scenes shows that our method matches state-of-the-art accuracy on easy tracking sequences, maintains high tracking speed at 57.6 frames per second, and provides the most robust tracking performance under challenging conditions. Thus, we believe that our approach is a relevant step forward in robust 6-DoF object tracking from RGB-D data.

</details>


### [78] [VIPER: Visual In-Context Physics Reasoning for Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23472)
*Tianxiao Chen,Hanmo Chen,Huajin Chen,Bo Li,Qi Ye,Peng-Tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIPER是一种通过参考视频引导图像生成视频的视觉物理推理框架，能提升物理行为迁移和生成视频的控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成模型虽在视觉连贯性上表现良好，但对物理行为的控制有限，主要因为物理细节难以用文本/图片完全描述。许多连续且复杂的物理线索（如材料响应、运动轨迹等）更适合用视频范例展示。研究旨在解决物理行为控制的瓶颈。

Method: 提出VIPER框架，输入目标图片、简要文本描述和参考视频，将参考视频作为期望物理过程的密集视觉演示。利用多模态大语言模型(MLLM)提取物理线索，并结合分层训练策略，驱动预训练图像到视频生成器，实现物理行为迁移。还构建了含有物理标注和精选参考-目标对的数据集VIPER-19K。

Result: 在未见过的验证集上，VIPER生成的视频在物理行为一致性和用户偏好上优于主流视频生成及基于视频提示的方法，同时保持一般视频画面质量的竞争力。

Conclusion: VIPER能有效迁移参考视频中的物理行为到新场景，无需复杂提示词，显著提升视频生成过程中的物理控制与参考一致性。

Abstract: Modern video generation models can synthesize visually compelling and temporally coherent clips, yet controlling their physical behavior remains difficult with standard text and image conditions. The core challenge is a conditioning bottleneck: material response, contact interaction, deformation, and motion trajectory are continuous and relational physical cues that are hard to specify exhaustively in language but can be demonstrated naturally by video. We propose VIPER, a Visual In-Context Physics Reasoning framework for reference-guided image-to-video generation. Given a target image, a brief target prompt, and a reference video, VIPER treats the reference as a dense visual demonstration of the desired physical process rather than an appearance template. It uses a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) to extract reference-derived physical cues and guide a pretrained image-to-video generator through a hierarchical training strategy, enabling physical behavior transfer while preserving the visual prior of the base generator. To support this setting, we construct VIPER-19K, a curated dataset with material, trajectory, and physical-impact annotations, together with filtered reference-target pairs. Experiments on an unseen validation set show that VIPER achieves stronger reference-video physical similarity and higher human preference than representative video generation and video-as-prompt baselines, while maintaining competitive general video quality. Qualitative results further demonstrate that VIPER can transfer reference-derived physical behavior to new target scenes without requiring carefully engineered prompts.

</details>


### [79] [PlanCraft: Sketch, Refine, and Furnish for Architect-Inspired Progressive 3D Residential Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23491)
*Pengyu Zeng,Yuqin Dai,Jun Yin,Ziyang Han,Ng Cheuk Hei,Jing Zhong,Chaoyang Shi,ZhanXiang Jin,Maowei Jiang,Yuxing Han,Shuai Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了PlanCraft，一个分阶段生成住宅平面图并自动进行室内布局的系统，有效解决了现有方法忽视的关键结构问题，显著提升了布局的合理性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化住宅平面图生成方法与实际建筑设计流程存在脱节：设计应是逐步细化的过程，而非一次性完整输入；并且2D平面图是设计过程中不可替代的空间契约，直接操作3D布局或语言大模型会导致结构不合理、空间重叠等问题。

Method: PlanCraft系统包括三部分：SketchPlan通过重演建筑师绘图过程，生成不同完成度的草图；PlanCraft-Diff采用粗到细的方式，将不完整草图逐步细化为精确的2D平面图；最后PlanCraft-Agent在固定房间边界内自动摆放家具。训练过程中用8万份真实平面图增强。

Result: PlanCraft在评测中FID比现有最佳2D方法降低了61.1%，专家评分的空间合理性比现有最佳3D系统高15分。仅用完成度25%的草图，PlanCraft已超越所有全指定输入的基线方法。

Conclusion: 分阶段、空间契约驱动的自动住宅平面图生成方法有效提升了布局合理性和生成质量，对实际建筑设计自动化具有重要意义。

Abstract: Two structural insights have been overlooked in automated residential floor plan generation. First, design is inherently progressive. Architects begin with rough strokes and refine them over time, whereas existing methods typically require their conditioning representation to be fully specified before generation, a fundamental mismatch with how design actually works. Second, the 2D floor plan is not an optional intermediate but an irreplaceable spatial contract. Once room boundaries, doors, and windows are fixed, furnishing reduces from open-ended spatial reasoning to bounded constraint satisfaction. Bypassing this contract, as existing 3D systems do by delegating layout to language models, yields overlapping rooms and implausible proportions; directly calling general-purpose language models likewise produces geometrically invalid layouts. Guided by these insights, we present PlanCraft. SketchPlan supplies the missing training signal by replaying the architect's drawing process on 80K real floor plans, producing partial sketches at every completeness level. PlanCraft-Diff progressively sharpens an incomplete sketch into a geometrically precise, vectorizable floor plan through a coarse-to-fine strategy. With the spatial contract established, PlanCraft-Agent then furnishes the scene within well-defined room boundaries. Experiments show that PlanCraft achieves a 61.1\% lower FID than the best existing 2D method and surpasses existing 3D systems by 15 points in expert-rated spatial rationality, with a sketch at only 25\% completion already outperforming all fully specified baselines.

</details>


### [80] [To Erase, or Not to Erase: Robust Training-Free Concept Erasure with Preservation aware Adaptive Ranked Subspace Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23492)
*Shaswati Saha,Rajasekhar Anguluri,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的概念抹除技术PARSE，用于在不牺牲原有模型功能的前提下，更加稳健地移除扩散模型中的不良或不需要的目标概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念抹除方法在移除不良或敏感内容（如NSFW或版权风格）与保持模型对正常内容的能力之间存在权衡问题。强力抹除通常导致模型在无害内容上性能损失，主要原因是抹除与保留的概念定义不精确，难以区分边界。

Method: 提出Preservation-aware Adaptive Ranked Subspace Expansion（PARSE）框架：不需额外训练，仅通过模型自带的引导机制，动态挖掘需抹除和需保留的概念方向，并在cross-attention空间中进行有保留意识的投影，将需抹除方向剔除，而最大限度保留需保留方向。同时，针对超出静态词汇表的新诱发触发词，通过“文本反演+子空间自适应扩展”迭代扩容，仅当新抹除方向不与需要保留的语义冲突时才扩展子空间。此外，还引入了衡量抹除鲁棒性与保留效用的新指标BEUS。

Result: 在NSFW、艺术风格及物体抹除等任务和现有多种CET基线对比下，大规模实验表明PARSE能更稳健地移除多种目标概念，同时模型对正常内容的效用显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PARSE有效打破了现有方法在抹除鲁棒性与模型功能之间的权衡，能够兼顾多目标稳健抹除与模型实用性，对文本到图像扩散模型的安全和可用性具有重要促进作用。

Abstract: Concept erasure techniques (CETs) edit text-to-image diffusion models to erase undesired targets such as NSFW content or copyrighted styles, while preserving model utility on benign concepts. Current CETs face a trade-off between erasure robustness and utility: stronger edits erase the target more reliably but degrade utility on non-target concepts, and vice versa. This stems from how existing methods define what to erase and what to preserve. Many CETs rely on static concept banks specified manually, generated by LLMs, or selected by CLIP image-text similarity. Such banks do not model how prompts steer the model during denoising, leaving it vulnerable to triggers that reintroduce the target while suppressing nearby benign concepts. We present Preservation-aware Adaptive Ranked Subspace Expansion (PARSE), a training-free framework for robust concept erasure in latent diffusion models. Given a target, PARSE queries the diffusion model with classifier-free guidance to dynamically discover target-inducing erase concepts and nearby retain concepts in the model vocabulary. It then edits the cross-attention value space with a preservation-aware projection that removes target directions while leaving retain directions intact. For triggers beyond this vocabulary-indexed space, PARSE iteratively searches for re-emergence triggers by textual inversion and adaptively expands the erased subspace only when a new trigger direction does not conflict with retain semantics. We also introduce the Balanced Erasure Utility Score (BEUS), which combines robustness (ASR under multiple attacks) and utility preservation (FID) via bounded monotone transforms and harmonic mean aggregation. Experiments on NSFW, artistic style, and object erasure, with a large-scale robustness-utility analysis over many CET baselines, show that PARSE erases multiple concepts robustly without sacrificing post-edit utility.

</details>


### [81] [Token-Region Guided Cross-Attention Fusion for Multimodal Affect Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23493)
*Musa Tur Farazi,Nufayer Jahan Reza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态交叉注意力融合框架，用于检测孟加拉语网络迷因中的政治意图，并在相关数据集上取得了当前最优的识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络迷因作为网络舆情和信息传播的重要内容，其自动化分析对理解公众情绪至关重要。然而，迷因中视觉与文本（特别是非主流语言、结构复杂的文本）交互错综复杂，目前相关识别方法存在准确率低、数据资源稀缺等挑战。特别是在孟加拉语等低资源语言下，现有方法难以高效处理。

Method: 1）首先使用视觉-语言模型从噪声较大的迷因图片中提取高质量的OCR文本；2）对视觉和文本特征分别编码；3）通过多头交叉注意力机制融合两模态信息，实现视觉区域与语义token的对齐；4）进一步引入政领域专用词汇知识库，提升模型感知。

Result: 在PoliMemeDecode1数据集上实验，作者提出的注意力融合方法在宏平均F1值上达到约0.94，显著优于单一模态或简单拼接的方法。

Conclusion: 研究证明交叉注意力融合能有效对齐文本语义与视觉证据，且知识先验能提升识别表现，为多模态、低资源语言环境下的互联网迷因内容分析提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Automated analysis of multimodal content on social networks has become a critical task for understanding public sentiment and information diffusion in the digital age. However, classifying internet memes remains computationally challenging due to the intricate interplay between visual cues and embedded, often stylized, text, particularly in low-resource languages like Bengali Language. This paper addresses the detection of political intent in Bengali memes by introducing Multimodal Cross-Attention Fusion framework. We first leverage a Vision-Language Model to extract high-fidelity OCR text from noisy meme images. Subsequently, we encode visual and textual features and synthesize them through a cross-modal multi-head attention mechanism that aligns semantic tokens with visual regions. We also investigate the integration of a domain-specific political lexicon as a knowledge prior. Experimental evaluation on the PoliMemeDecode1 dataset shows that our attention-based fusion significantly outperforms unimodal baselines and standard concatenation methods, achieving a state-of-the-art Macro-F1 of approximately 0.94. Interpretability analyzes further confirm that the model effectively learns to ground textual semantics in visual evidence.

</details>


### [82] [MemVLN: Episodic and Procedural Memory for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23504)
*Yuqi Liu,Shengju Qian,Tianyuan Qu,Mingxian Lin,Zixuan Wang,Xin Wang,Bei Yu,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MemVLN框架，通过创新的记忆管理机制和精简的动作词汇，实现了视觉-语言导航中的高精度和实时性，在基准测试上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航（VLN）方法难以兼顾长时序历史保持与低延迟决策，特别是在连续环境下，既要保证路径一致性，又要实现高效推理。

Method: MemVLN采用视觉编码器处理连续视觉输入，使用大型语言模型理解指令和生成动作。核心创新在于采用金字塔分辨率的情景记忆机制，对当前观测集中的信息进行高效处理，同时压缩保存长期历史。其次，提出了过程记忆，用精简的中层动作词汇代替自回归动作解码，从而加速推理速度。

Result: 在VLN-CE任务上，MemVLN-4B在R2R和RxR测试集上分别比Qwen3-VL-4B提升了5.8%和9.7%的成功率（SR），推理速度提升了7倍。

Conclusion: MemVLN框架能够有效平衡导航精度与推理实时性，显著超过主流基线，推动了视觉-语言导航系统的实用化进展。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to maintain long-horizon visual history for trajectory consistency while executing actions with low latency. Existing video-based VLN approaches typically struggle to satisfy both demands simultaneously. To address these challenges, we propose MemVLN, a novel VLN framework that achieves state-of-the-art performance with real-time inference efficiency (14 FPS). MemVLN utilizes a visual encoder to process continuous observations and a Large Language Model (LLM) to interpret instructions and generate actions. Central to our approach is an Episodic Memory management that applies pyramidal resolutions. This mechanism concentrates computation on immediate percepts while retaining compressed long-term history. Complementing to this design, we introduce Procedural Memory for fast action with a compact vocabulary of atomic mid-level actions to bypass auto-regressive decoding latency. Experiments on VLN-CE show that MemVLN-4B surpasses the baseline Qwen3-VL-4B architecture by 5.8\% SR in R2R and 9.7\% SR in RxR, while achieving a 7$\times$ speedup in inference latency.

</details>


### [83] [MOJITO: Modal Joint Learning for Unified End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23511)
*Zhijing Cheng,Xuancheng Zhang,Donglin Di,Lei Fan,Baorui Ma,Hao Li,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MOJITO的端到端自动驾驶框架，通过模态联合注意力机制直接连接传感器与动作，提升了系统对多模态信息的利用，取得了新的数据集最好成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统多采用“感知-规划”两阶段级联结构，将多模态感知输入压缩成精简表示后再进行路径规划，丢失了细粒度信息，阻碍了高级视觉模型的能力利用。此论文旨在解决这一结构性瓶颈。

Method: 提出MOJITO框架，摒弃传统分阶段接口，采用块式模态联合注意力（Modal Joint Attention），同步更新动作、图像和LiDAR特征，使动作预测可以直接访问多模态信息。

Result: MOJITO在NAVSIM v1数据集取得88.9 PDMS，在更具挑战的NAVSIM v2数据集上取得88.4 EPDMS，均刷新了最新最优成绩。实验还显示其可扩展性强、能遵循指令和生成多样化轨迹。

Conclusion: MOJITO化解了传统自动驾驶系统感知与规划间的信息瓶颈，能更有效地利用多模态信息，提升了自动驾驶的性能和灵活性，为日后发展提供了新范式。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems commonly follow a cascaded two-stage pipeline where a perception stage compresses multi-modal sensor inputs into a compact context and a downstream planner predicts trajectories conditioned on this context. We argue that this one-way perception-to-planning interface forces sensor inputs into a compact representation, losing the fine-grained details critical for planning. Moreover, by constraining the planner to this compressed context, it is difficult to leverage the rich representations offered by modern vision foundation models. To address these issues, we propose MOJITO, a unified sensor-to-action framework for end-to-end autonomous driving built on modal joint learning. MOJITO removes the cascaded interface and instead performs block-wise Modal Joint Attention that simultaneously updates action, image, and LiDAR features, allowing the planner to directly access multi-modal features during action generation. MOJITO achieves 88.9 PDMS on the NAVSIM v1 dataset and 88.4 EPDMS on the more challenging NAVSIM v2 dataset, setting a new state-of-the-art. Extensive experiments further demonstrate strong scalability, instruction following, and diverse trajectory generation. Code and models are available at https://github.com/mumucc01/MOJITO.

</details>


### [84] [Real-Time Human-Centric World Modeling for Upper-Body Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23517)
*Chaonan Ji,Jinwei Qi,Peng Zhang,Bang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以人为中心的、可实时交互的上半身世界建模方法，实现了人物本体与物体协调互动的高质量动态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的动作生成和交互建模方法难以兼顾精细的人体（包括手和脸）、与物体的可控制互动以及实时性，限制了在虚拟现实、机器人等领域的应用。因此，亟需结合连续的人体动态和离散的人物体交互状态，提升拟人性、交互性和生成效率。

Method: 创新性地提出了连续-离散联合控制方案：1）针对人体复杂动态，基于多尺度运动编码将上半身、手和脸的运动隐变量融合于统一隐空间，实现细粒度、无需显式retargeting的直接控制；2）针对人-物交互状态，以文本编码为核心，将有限的离散互动状态（如“无接触”“抓握”）作为明确动作指令，同时构建了专门的数据渲染与监督流程。两者结合既精确建模人体动作，也能灵活控制交互局部场景；最后对模型蒸馏加速，实时25FPS。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在精细动作还原、手部-物体协调、实时交互等方面优于现有方案，显著提升了人本交互生成的真实感与控制性。

Conclusion: 通过连续人体状态与离散人-物交互状态的联合建模，本文实现了高效、真实和可控的以人为中心的实时交互世界建模，为虚拟环境、交互动画等应用迈出关键一步。

Abstract: We present a real-time human-centric world model for upper-body interactive generation, aiming to synthesize coherent local world dynamics centered on a person, where coordinated body, hand, and facial motions evolve jointly with controllable human-object discrete interaction. To this end, we adopt a continuous-discrete joint control scheme with two complementary components: a continuous human state and a discrete interaction state. For continuous human-state control, we introduce a unified implicit representation based on multi-scale motion encoding, in which motion latents from the upper body, hands, and face are fused into a shared latent space. This multi-scale design improves expressiveness across different spatial scales, captures fine-grained human dynamics more effectively, and enables direct control without explicit retargeting. For discrete object interaction-state control, we represent object contact using a small set of language-encoded discrete interaction states, where text serves as an explicit interaction-state command, such as \emph{no contact} or \emph{grasp}, rather than an open-ended generation prompt, and we further construct a dedicated rendering pipeline for human-object interaction data to supervise such discrete interaction states. By combining continuous implicit human-state control with discrete interaction-state control, our model enables precise modeling of how a person moves and interacts with the local environment, including controllable changes to nearby scene states. Finally, we distill the model for efficient streaming real-time inference, achieving 25 FPS on two H100 GPUs. Experiments demonstrate improved fine-grained motion fidelity, more realistic hand-object coordination, and effective real-time interaction, establishing a practical step beyond motion reproduction toward real-time human-centric world modeling.

</details>


### [85] [ATCNet-CIAM for Multi-Session Motor Imagery EEG Signal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23522)
*Le Huu Son Hai,Nguyen Chi Hai,Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Phuc Nguyen,Nguyen Thai Anh,Ngo Hoang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种增强型注意力时序卷积网络ATCNet-CIAM，并在多个脑机接口数据集上表现优越，通过有效利用信道空间特征提升运动想象任务的解码准确率与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动想象相关的脑电（EEG）信号在非侵入式脑机接口中有广泛应用，但受限于被试间差异和跨时段不稳定性，解码效果难以保证。因此，提升模型对特征的提取能力及其鲁棒性成为亟需解决的问题。

Method: 作者将轻量级信道整合注意力模块（CIAM）嵌入到基础ATCNet结构中，强化信道空间特征学习。模型在三大公开数据集（BCI Competition IV-2a、IV-2b和WBCIC-MI）上，采用标准、同场景和跨场景协议进行评估，并通过消融实验验证模块有效性。

Result: ATCNet-CIAM在BCI IV-2a和IV-2b数据集标准协议下分别达到了86.32%与87.96%的准确率，在WBCIC-MI跨天数据集同场景协议下分别取得了89.46%（2类别）和83.64%（3类别）的准确率。模型在多种实验条件下稳定提升解码性能，并提升了跨场景的稳定性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的ATCNet-CIAM结构通过集成信道注意力，有效提升了运动想象解码的识别准确率和可迁移性，为鲁棒脑机接口系统的设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Motor imagery (MI)-based electroencephalography is widely used in non-invasive brain--computer interfaces (BCIs), but robust decoding remains challenging due to inter-subject variability and cross-session non-stationarity. This work proposes ATCNet-CIAM, an enhanced attention temporal convolutional network that integrates a lightweight channel-integrated attention module (CIAM) into the ATCNet framework to improve channel-spatial feature representation for MI decoding. The proposed model is evaluated on BCI Competition IV-2a, BCI Competition IV-2b, and the multi-day WBCIC-MI dataset under standard, within-session, and cross-session protocols. Experimental results show that ATCNet-CIAM achieves 86.32% accuracy on BCI IV-2a and 87.96% on BCI IV-2b under the standard protocol, while reaching 89.46% and 83.64% in the within-session WBCIC-MI on 2C and 3C, respectively. The proposed framework consistently improves classification stability and robustness under session-varying conditions, and ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of the proposed architectural components.

</details>


### [86] [Geometry Meets Semantics: Fractional Gradient Stabilization for Semantic-Driven Bounding Box Optimization in Visual Detection Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23530)
*Qi Ming,Haitian Yang,Xudong Zhao,Mingjing Zhao,Liuqian Wang,Nanqing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FrSIoU损失函数，以解决现有IoU损失在方向框检测中的语义缺失与梯度不稳定问题，有效提升目标检测任务的定位精度和收敛稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测任务的边界框回归普遍依赖IoU损失，但IoU损失(1)只利用几何属性，忽视语义信息；(2)在方向框任务中梯度不稳定，致使方向收敛不良。为此，作者希望提出方法同时优化语义与几何，并提升训练稳定性。

Method: 作者首先提出语义相似度度量来引导IoU优化，结合自适应梯度门控机制，构建SIoU损失。随后针对方向框的梯度不稳定问题，将SIoU损失泛化为分数阶形式（FrSIoU loss），通过累计历史IoU状态对异常梯度进行正则化。

Result: 在不同边界框形式和多种视觉检测任务中，FrSIoU损失均带来稳定和显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: FrSIoU损失有效融合语义与几何特征，提升目标检测中的定位精度与训练收敛稳定性，为后续检测任务提供更鲁棒的损失优化方案。

Abstract: Bounding boxes are fundamental for object localization in visual detection tasks. Among them, oriented bounding boxes are widely used in visual detection tasks, which provide a more precise directional representation. Generally, IoU-based losses are widely adopted to optimize box regression. However, we observed that IoU-driven box optimization suffers from two key issues: (1) it relies solely on geometric properties while ignoring semantic cues; (2) orientation optimization suffers from unstable gradients, causing oscillations in orientation convergence. In this paper, we propose a Fractional Semantic IoU loss to achieve unified semantic-geometric learning with gradient stabilization. First, we design a semantic similarity metric to guide IoU optimization, building a Semantic IoU loss (SIoU loss) with an adaptive gradient gating mechanism. Then, we revisit the gradient instability issue in oriented box optimization and extend the SIoU loss to a fractional-order formulation to build the \textbf{Fr}actional \textbf{S}emantic \textbf{IoU} \textbf{loss} (FrSIoU loss). The FrSIoU loss accumulates historical IoU states to regularize abnormal gradients during bounding box optimization process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves stable performance gains across different bounding box formulations and diverse visual detection tasks. The code will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [87] [GaitFace: A Multimodal Dataset for Long-Range Person Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23542)
*Alain Komaty,Luis S. Luevano,Vidit Vidit,Anjith George,Zeina Al Amine,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了GaitFace数据集，聚焦于远距离边境控制下的人脸与步态识别难题，并发现现有模型在低分辨率和高视角情况下表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球边境管控效率成为挑战，等待时间长，需要更好、自动化的身份识别方案。生物识别技术虽有用，但在远距离复杂环境中受限。缺乏公开、高质量的多模态远距离数据集制约了研究进展。

Method: 作者采集了GaitFace数据集，包含真实边境场景下的远距离人脸与步态数据，包括预注册与多角度野外采集。并用最新（SOTA）人脸和步态模型在该数据集上做基准测试，评价其识别能力。

Result: 实验显示，尽管现有模型在有光学辅助时有效，但在低分辨率和高视角条件下大幅失效，表现远低于理想水平。

Conclusion: GaitFace揭示了远距离生物识别技术当前关键不足，为今后更鲁棒和适应性强的模型研究提供了公开数据和基准。

Abstract: Efficient border control is becoming a significant global challenge, mainly due to severe congestion and extended passenger waiting times. To mitigate these bottlenecks and facilitate passenger flow, biometric technologies are increasingly deployed to streamline identity verification and enhance crossing efficiency. Technical limitations frequently impede biometric identification, particularly in long-range surveillance, where systems must deal with adverse atmospheric conditions and degraded image quality. While high-quality frameworks like BRIAR exist, they are frequently restricted to specific government agencies. This paper introduces GaitFace, a new public dataset that contains face and gait data captured at long distances. To ensure that the research reflects authentic border scenarios, we use Pre-Enrollment data, where a traveler registers via a mobile device, and "In-the-Wild" captures, which records individuals at a distance across multiple viewing angles and different cameras. Benchmarking SOTA face and gait models reveals that current architectures fail under low-resolution and elevated viewpoints despite success with optical assistance. GaitFace exposes these critical vulnerabilities, providing a rigorous public benchmark to drive more robust, unconstrained biometric research.

</details>


### [88] [D3O: Dynamic Distribution Distillation for Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23575)
*Chunlai Dong,Yaojun Hu,Yuyang Xu,Haochao Ying,Jian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的动态分布蒸馏（D3O）框架，针对主观离散标签噪声问题，通过自蒸馏和标签增强，提升有序回归任务的鲁棒性和表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有序回归方法通常依赖静态监督目标，但实际标签常常因人为主观判断，导致类别界限模糊且有噪声；这些方法容易强化偏见和鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出D3O框架，核心包括两个模块：一是对比式有序标签增强模块，通过视觉-语言对齐细化标签分布，捕捉模糊性与实例不确定性；二是基于累积分布函数（CDF）的跨层次蒸馏机制，保证网络中间表征保持一致的有序结构。

Result: 在四个通用有序回归任务上，D3O都优于当前主流方法，尤其在类别分布极度不均和监督噪声严重时，表现更加突出。

Conclusion: 动态分布监督能有效提升有序回归模型的鲁棒性和表征能力，优于传统静态目标设计。

Abstract: Ordinal regression is widely used in scenarios where labels are discrete yet inherently ordered. In practice, however, ordinal labels are often obtained by discretizing underlying continuous semantics through subjective human judgment, resulting in ambiguous boundaries and annotation noise. Such uncertainty challenges existing methods that rely on fixed supervision targets, which may reinforce biased ordering under subjective annotations. To address this limitation, we propose D3O, a dynamic distribution distillation framework that replaces static supervision with training-driven evolution of ordinal label distributions via self-distillation. Specifically, we introduce a contrastive ordinal-aware label enhancement module that leverages vision-language alignment to recover refined label distributions capturing both inter-class ambiguity and instance-level uncertainty. Furthermore, we design a CDF-based cross-layer interaction distillation mechanism to propagate cumulative ordinal structure across network hierarchy, ensuring consistent ordinal geometry in intermediate representations. Extensive experiments on four general ordinal regression tasks demonstrate that our proposed D3O consistently outperforms existing approaches, particularly under severe class imbalance and noisy supervision. These results highlight the effectiveness of dynamic supervision in learning robust ordinal representations beyond fixed targets. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [89] [Neuromorphic Object Detection: An In-Depth Study and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23576)
*Jianing Li,Dianze Li,Arren Glover,Xiaopeng Fan,Guoqi Li,Chiara Bartolozzi,Ryad B. Benosman,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文综述并评测了神经形态相机在目标检测领域的最新算法，并探讨未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧式相机在高速运动模糊或弱光条件下难以检测目标，神经形态相机则以高时空分辨率和宽动态范围带来新的可能，因而有必要对该领域算法进行系统性调研与评测。

Method: 作者全面梳理神经形态目标检测问题，回顾数据集与评测指标，并从事件表示、时序建模、多模态融合、异步与低延时处理、能效计算等视角系统总结现有方法，最后对代表性模型进行定量评测和细致对比分析。

Result: 论文汇总了现有数据集、常用评测标准，梳理各类方法，量化比较了多种主流神经形态目标检测模型的性能，揭示其优缺点。

Conclusion: 神经形态目标检测虽取得进展，仍面临诸如标准化评测、关键问题解决等挑战，需在算法创新、评价体系与应用拓展上持续努力，本文的综述和基准有助于推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Conventional frame-based cameras face significant challenges in detecting objects under high-speed motion blur or in low-light environments. Neuromorphic cameras provide asynchronous visual streams with high temporal resolution and a wide dynamic range, offering a promising solution for object detection under challenging conditions. Despite the development of numerous models and the emergence of various applications in neuromorphic object detection, there is still a lack of deep understanding and standardized benchmarks to assess progress and address key challenges. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive survey and benchmark of existing neuromorphic object detection algorithms. Specifically, we first present a problem description, review the available datasets, and revisit the evaluation metrics. We then explore existing neuromorphic object detection approaches from various perspectives, including event representation, temporal modeling, multimodal fusion, asynchronous processing, low-latency processing, and energy-efficient computing. Furthermore, we evaluate a wide range of representative neuromorphic object detection models and offer detailed analyses of the comparative results. Finally, we discuss unresolved issues in neuromorphic object detection and propose potential future research directions. We hope this survey and benchmark will be a valuable resource for researchers and provide guidance for future advancements in neuromorphic object detection.

</details>


### [90] [SketchMamba: A Lightweight State-Space Model for Joint Progressive Sketch Classification and Stroke Auto-Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23580)
*Kavish Jhaveri,Arya Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了SketchMamba模型，实现了矢量草图的流式理解和生成，并在Quick, Draw!数据集上取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的矢量草图模型将识别和生成任务分开处理，但实际流式操作中需要模型能一边理解用户当前画的内容，一边预测后续内容，弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出单一因果序列模型SketchMamba，在每一步采用稠密分类监督，由选择性状态空间主干网络实现，同时支持草图的实时分类与后续生成。

Result: SketchMamba在Quick,Draw! 58类数据集上最终分类准确率达94.93%，AUC为0.706，且在画出70%笔画时即可达到最终准确率的90%以上，优于递归和卷积基线，与因果Transformer持平，验证了稠密监督对早期预测能力的关键贡献。

Conclusion: 单一因果隐藏状态模型可统一逐步识别和自回归生成，简化架构，无需额外编码器或任务分支，适合流式矢量草图应用。

Abstract: Existing vector-sketch models treat recognition and generation as separate tasks, leaving a gap for streaming interfaces that must understand a drawing as it is being made. We present SketchMamba, a single causal sequence model that continuously classifies a sketch from any partial prefix while simultaneously generating its continuation. We achieve this by applying a dense per-step classification loss to a selective state-space backbone. Evaluated on a 58-class subset of the Quick, Draw! dataset, SketchMamba yields 94.93% final-step accuracy and a progressive-accuracy Area Under the Curve (AUC) of 0.706, crossing 90% of its final accuracy by the time 70% of the strokes are drawn. In a matched-budget comparison, the 1.55 million-parameter backbone ties a causal Transformer while outperforming recurrent and convolutional baselines. Ablations confirm that the dense supervision regime, rather than the architecture alone, drives the early-prediction capability. The results demonstrate that a single causal hidden state can unify progressive recognition and autoregressive generation without auxiliary encoders or task-specific branching.

</details>


### [91] [Do Current Retrievers Cover All the Evidence? A Controlled Study of Conjunctive Cross-Page Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24165)
*Sungguk Cha,DongWook Kim,Mintae Kim,Youngsub Han,Byoung-Ki Jeon,Sangyeob Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在需要满足多个条件的复合文档检索任务中，找到包含所有证据的文档比仅找到相关文档更具挑战性。提出n-Clue基准，以量化并分析现有方法的能力和局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文档检索系统常常只关注相对相关性，但对于多条件查询，往往难以保证每个请求的证据在同一篇文档中均被满足，因此需要专门分析这一差距。

Method: 设计了n-Clue基准，包含1,000个多条件查询，对2,021篇文档进行严格配对。分析了多种检索方法，例如条件分解、词向量与视觉信息融合、密集召回扩容、混合检索等对满足所有条件的影响，并引入了多项评价指标（如Gold-NDCG、complete-first等）。

Result: 条件分解可以提升现有密集召回模型6.8-7.3分，多模态融合提升8.7分。调优和扩容未能显著提升全部条件被满足的比例；最好的系统能在81.1%的查询中找到黄金文档，但真正“先找到所有条件”仅为35.8%，视觉系统能面向每个条件明确给出证据仅5%左右。瓶颈在于全面覆盖而不是仅找到相关文档。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的复合检索系统仍难以确保在同一文档中满足复杂多条件查询，‘条件覆盖’是系统性能的主要瓶颈，而非简单的文档发现。该方向需要更多关注和技术提升。

Abstract: Finding a long document relevant to a multi-part request is not the same as establishing that it contains every requested piece of evidence. We study this gap for conjunctive document retrieval, where two or three explicit conditions must be supported on different pages of one document. We use n-Clue as a controlled measurement instrument: 1{,}000 queries over 2{,}021 documents pair all-condition golds with naturally occurring documents that satisfy only a subset, and a complete-first success requires a top-10 gold to precede every released subset qrel. Across 70 configurations, condition-wise decomposition improves two dense backbones by 6.8--7.3 points and lexical--visual fusion adds 8.7, while four generic rerankers all reduce Gold-NDCG; these directions replicate on a four-source stress set. Scaling one dense family from 0.6B to 8B changes complete-first success by 0.0 points. The strongest displayed hybrid illustrates the resulting gap: it finds a gold for 81.1\% of queries but succeeds complete-first on only 35.8\%, and the gap persists across condition count, target length, candidate density, query rendering, and the four-source stress set. Finally, page-aware visual systems surface stored support for every condition on only 5.1--5.3\% of queries. These results identify condition coverage, rather than gold discovery alone, as the central bottleneck.

</details>


### [92] [JarvisHub: An Open Harness for Canvas-Native Multimodal Creative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23588)
*Yunlong Lin,Zixu Lin,Zhaohu Xing,Biqiang Li,Chenxin Li,Haonan Wang,Haitao Wu,Hengyu Liu,Jianghai Chen,Kaituo Feng,Kaixin Li,Shawn Chen,Shijue Huang,Sixiang Chen,Tsung-Yi Ho,Wenxuan Huang,Xiangyan Liu,Xiaomeng Hu,Xuanhua He,Yan Sun,Yunqing Zhao,Zhiqin Yang,Zehan Wang,Zhengyang Tang,Tianyu Pang,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了JarvisHub，一个支持长时程多模态创作的画布原生创意智能体平台，通过可编辑画布实现对复杂创作过程的持续管理与协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI创意系统往往只支持孤立的、单步的生成，缺乏对复杂创意项目（如反复编辑、失败尝试、版本管理等）状态的持续支持，难以满足真实世界的创作需求。

Method: JarvisHub采用以画布为核心的设计，将用户工作区、智能体外部记忆、动作空间及项目状态集成于可视的画布节点与链路，实现对多模态资产、依赖、版本和反馈的结构化管理。其架构分为画布状态、协议桥接和智能体运行三层。

Result: JarvisHub实现了创意智能体在可检查、可编辑的创作状态下持续行动，能够进行规划、生成、修订和组织多模态项目，支持用户全过程的检查和干预。

Conclusion: JarvisHub推动了创意AI从单步工具向持续化、可人类引导的自动创作转变，提升了智能体与用户的协作深度和创作项目的管理能力。

Abstract: Creative AI is moving from single-step asset generation toward long-horizon multimodal production. Although recent generative models can synthesize high-quality images, videos, audio clips, UI elements, storyboards, slides, and other creative assets, real-world creative work requires more than isolated prompt-output interactions. It involves references, drafts, alternatives, edits, failed attempts, version relations, tool actions, evaluation signals, and human feedback, which together form an evolving project state. Existing prompt-based, chat-based, and node-based generation systems only partially support this state, as they often discard intermediate context, rely on linear conversations, or require manually specified workflows. Recent commercial systems indicate a shift toward agent-assisted creative production, but their closed architectures make it difficult to study how agents represent context, choose tools, revise artifacts, recover from failures, and maintain consistency over time. To address this gap, we introduce JarvisHub, a canvas-native creative agent harness for long-horizon multimodal creation. JarvisHub treats an editable canvas as the user workspace, the agent's external memory, action space, and shared project state, representing multimodal artifacts, dependencies, versions, and feedback as typed canvas nodes and links. Through a three-layer architecture of canvas state, protocol bridge, and agent runtime, JarvisHub enables agents to act within an inspectable and editable creative state. This design moves creative agents beyond isolated tool use toward sustained, human-steerable creative automation, where agents can progressively plan, generate, revise, and organize multimodal projects while users remain able to inspect, guide, and intervene throughout the process.

</details>


### [93] [Bigger or Cheaper? Scale and Quantization Effects on Uncertainty Signals in Vision-Language Models Under Image Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24440)
*M M Asif Ferdous*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文评估了在固定内存预算下，视觉-语言模型（VLMs）规模与4-bit量化对模型置信度信号的影响，并为实际部署提出了推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，VLMs需要在内存有限的硬件下部署，并且需要置信度信号来决定是否给出应答。用户往往面临选择小型全精度模型、同小型量化模型或大型量化模型的抉择，但这三种配置对置信度信号有不同影响，有必要系统比较。

Method: 文章以Qwen2-VL模型家族为例，比较不同模型规模和4-bit量化情况下，模型用自然语言表达的置信度与其内部均值token概率这两个置信度信号。在六种真实图像退化和三种严重程度下，共5700个预测样本，系统地对这些信号进行了评估（包含AUROC等指标）。

Result: 实验表明，模型规模提升显著提升了内部不确定性信号（AUROC从0.80增至0.98），但模型用语言表达的置信度始终较弱（平均0.61-0.69）。4-bit量化对准确率几乎无影响（-1.6分），但显著削弱了置信度信号（内部AUROC从0.95降至0.80，语言表达置信度可解析比例从99%骤降至64%）。

Conclusion: 在固定内存预算下，应优先选择大规模量化模型（如7B-4bit），因其在准确率和不确定性信号上均优于小型全精度/量化模型。建议用错误检测AUROC作为主要置信度评估指标，论文结果直接可指导实际部署选择。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) deployed on consumer hardware must decide when to answer and when to defer, and that decision depends on having a confidence signal that tracks correctness. A practitioner with a fixed memory budget faces a choice between a small model at full precision, the same small model quantized, and a larger model quantized into the same footprint -- three configurations that push the confidence signal in opposing directions. We measure, on identical inputs, how model scale and 4-bit quantization affect two confidence signals in the Qwen2-VL family: the confidence a model states in natural language, and its own mean token probability over the answer it generates. Across 5,700 predictions spanning six realistic photographic degradations at three severities, we find that scale sharply improves the model's internal uncertainty signal (mean error-detection AUROC 0.80 to 0.98 from 2B to 7B) while its verbalized confidence stays weak and often at chance (mean 0.61 to 0.69): the gap between what the model knows and what it says widens rather than closes with size. We find that 4-bit quantization is nearly free for accuracy (-1.6 points) but expensive for the confidence signal (internal AUROC 0.95 to 0.80, and the verbalized-confidence parse rate collapses from 99% to 64%). For a fixed memory budget the recommendation is therefore to prefer a larger quantized model over a smaller full-precision one: 7B-4bit gives both the best accuracy and the best uncertainty signal (internal AUROC 0.98) of the three configurations that fit. We frame the results as selective-prediction operating points so they translate directly into a deployment recommendation, and we argue that error-detection AUROC, not calibration error, is the metric that exposes the difference between the two signals.

</details>


### [94] [Weakly Supervised Instance-Level Gleason Pattern Estimation Using Primary and Secondary Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23594)
*Nao Sugeta,Kaito Shiku,Shinnosuke Matsuo,Ryoma Bise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多实例学习（MIL）框架，在只有全片级Gleason评分标签而缺乏实例级标注的情况下，可以通过聚合实例预测，推断出实例级的Gleason分型，并在SICAP-MIL数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床常规中仅获取到全片级（slide-level）的Gleason评分标签，缺乏实例（patch）级的Gleason分型标注，这使得针对实例级的学习变得困难，因此需要新的方法来充分利用临床真实可用的弱标签。

Method: 采用多实例学习（MIL）框架，将Gleason评分的临床定义转化为对实例级预测结果的聚合，通过建模primary和secondary pattern及其主导关系，从slide-level标签中推断实例级分型。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法能够实现有效的实例级学习，并且在SICAP-MIL数据集上的性能优于当前主流的多实例学习方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在缺乏实例级标注情况下，实现更细粒度的分型预测，为临床病理图像分析任务提供了更有效的解决思路。

Abstract: In prostate cancer histopathology, the Gleason Score is determined by the most frequent (Primary) and second most frequent (Secondary) Gleason patterns within a whole-slide image. Although these slide-level labels are routinely available in clinical practice, instance-level Gleason annotations are rarely provided, making patch-level learning challenging. We propose a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) framework that estimates instance-level Gleason patterns from slide-level Primary and Secondary labels. The proposed method formulates instance-level learning according to the clinical definition of the Gleason Score by aggregating instance predictions into class counts and explicitly modeling the Primary pattern, Secondary pattern, and their dominance. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed formulation enables effective instance-level learning and outperforms existing MIL approaches on the SICAP-MIL dataset.

</details>


### [95] [Evidence Attribution in Visual Document Understanding without Coordinates or Region Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24651)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出，通过用文本引用和多模态检索的方式替代传统坐标标注接口，大幅提升视觉文档理解任务中的证据区域回溯能力，并显著降低归因幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉文档理解模型常需输出证据区域的坐标以证明答案来源，但该方式易导致归因幻觉，即模型即使答对也无法准确定位支持答案的区域。坐标接口的表达能力可能限制了模型的真实归因能力。

Method: 在经过验证的双语CiteVQA子集上，比较了坐标输出接口、纯文本（引用证据原文）接口，以及基于多模态检索的接口（由版面分析器返回文本所在页面区域），并在六个开源视觉语言模型上重复实验。进一步提出将quote-and-retrieve流程作为训练支架，采用新的GRPO奖励策略，无需区域标签即可提升证据引用表现。

Result: 与坐标输出接口相比，证据召回率从最高8分提升至26-47分，幻觉率下降约一半，答案准确性基本不变。8B模型背骨的严格归因准确率从22.4%提升至33.8%。

Conclusion: 用文本引用与检索机制替代坐标接口，并结合无需区域标签的训练策略，可大幅提升视觉文档归因效果，为更实用、更易获得标注的数据驱动的文档理解提供了新路线。

Abstract: Reliable visual document understanding requires a model to attribute each answer to the evidence regions that support it. Recent benchmarks and systems express this step through a coordinate interface: the model outputs the coordinates of bounding boxes that mark the evidence regions in the document. Under this interface, vision-language models often fail to identify the right regions even when the answer is correct, a failure known as Attribution Hallucination. We present a study that investigates whether this failure is partially limited by what the model can express through coordinates. On a verified bilingual CiteVQA subset, we compare the coordinate interface with a language interface in which the model outputs only text, quoting its evidence verbatim, and a multimodal retriever returns the location of each quote as a page region proposed by a layout parser (tables and figures are quoted through their captions or notes); the comparison is repeated over six open vision-language models. Compared with the coordinate interface, evidence recall rises from at most 8 points to between 26 and 47 and the hallucination rate roughly halves, with little change in answer quality. Building on this comparison, we use the same quote-and-retrieve pipeline as a training scaffold: because region-level evidence labels are expensive to collect for long documents, we introduce a GRPO recipe whose reward is a judge's reading of the gold answer and crops of the retrieved regions, training the model to quote better evidence without any region labels and raising an 8B backbone's strict attributed accuracy from 22.4 to 33.8. These findings indicate a practical path to improve attribution"without a coordinate interface and without costly region-level supervision.

</details>


### [96] [ConFusion: Continuous Fusion Space Learning for Fine-Grained Controllable Infrared and Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23600)
*Guo Yurong,He Yufei,Li Yonghao,Chang Dongliang,Zhang Ke,Ma Zhanyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的可控红外-可见光图像融合框架ConFusion，通过高斯条件的空间感知调控，实现实例级细粒度可控融合，并大幅提升融合图像质量和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前红外-可见光图像融合方法通常基于预设离散控制条件，导致调控空间稀疏，无法满足用户对细粒度可控性的需求。因此，亟需一个能实现连续、细致图像融合调控的方法。

Method: ConFusion框架采用双分支结构，分离融合图像中的模态无关和模态特定特征，并利用高斯条件的实例级调制变量和Grounded SAM实例掩码来引导融合。文本驱动模块确保语义一致性。推理时通过多模态大模型将用户意图解析为调制变量，实现灵活融合。

Result: 大量实验表明，ConFusion在多个融合质量指标和下游任务上均取得了最优性能，同时支持细粒度可控的图像融合。

Conclusion: ConFusion拓展了红外-可见光图像融合的可控性边界，为实际应用中实现灵活、个性化的信息集成提供了有效工具，在多个任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Controllable infrared-visible image fusion aims to integrate complementary thermal and structural information with flexible region-aware modulation, producing fused images that adapt to diverse user requirements and downstream tasks. However, existing methods typically rely on predefined discrete control conditions, leading to a sparse space that fails to support fine-grained modulation demands. To address this, we propose ConFusion, a novel framework that learns the continuous fusion space via Gaussian-conditioned spatial-aware modulation, enabling instance-level fine-grained controllable infrared and visible image fusion. ConFusion employs a dual-branch architecture to disentangle modality-invariant and modality-specific representations under joint reconstruction and text-guided semantic alignment. Gaussian-conditioned instance modulation variables coupled with Grounded SAM-based instance masks guide instance-level fine-grained modulation through the Mask-Guided Specific Feature Modulator, while the Text-Driven Invariant Feature Enhancer improves semantic consistency and enhances fusion. During inference, the multimodal large language model parses user intents into instance-level modulation variables to guide image fusion. Extensive experiments show that ConFusion achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics in both fusion quality and downstream tasks, while supporting fine-grained controllable image fusion. Our code is available at https://github.com/HeyufeiAnto/Confusion

</details>
