### [166] [MSVS-VAE: Multi-Scale Anchored VecSet for High-Fidelity 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24436)
*Dehao Hao,Kaiyi Zhang,Tanghui Jia,Xiangjun Gao,Dongyu Yan,Weikai Chen,Zeyu Hu,Lingting Zhu,Yingda Yin,Runze Zhang,Li Yuan,Xin Wang,Long Quan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次化Set-based变分自编码器（MSVS-VAE），结合紧凑性和高保真，显著提升了3D生成建模的重建质量和解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前高质量3D生成大都依赖VAE结合扩散模型，但3D VAE的重建能力成为主瓶颈。稀疏体素表示虽重建效果优异，但资源消耗大；Set-based表示虽紧凑但细节不足，尚存保真差距。作者希望打破紧凑性与高质量之间的权衡。

Method: 提出MSVS-VAE：通过层次化的点集上采样（point-shuffle upsampling），逐步提高VecSet潜变量密度以增强细节容量。为高效解析密集层次，提出局部几何感知聚合算子（AVS-Conv），替代全局注意力，仅在局部邻域集聚合特征。引入多尺度查询解码，融合粗-细潜在特征，有效结合全局与局部信息，减少因局部接受域过小带来的伪影。

Result: 在Objaverse、ABO及野外数据集上，MSVS-VAE在重建质量上优于现有Set-based与voxel-based VAE方法，解码速度比Set-based方法快约10倍，紧凑性比voxel基线高约10倍。

Conclusion: 该方法成功克服Set-based与Voxel-based代表性架构的各自缺陷，实现了3D VAE架构的高效、高保真重建和紧凑编码，为3D生成建模带来新的性能上限。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D generative modeling increasingly relies on the latent diffusion paradigm, where the reconstruction quality of the underlying 3D VAE becomes a primary bottleneck. Existing approaches largely follow two paradigms: sparse voxel-based representations achieve strong reconstruction quality but incur significant memory and computational overhead, while set-based representations are compact and continuous yet typically lag in fidelity due to latent sparsity and excessive global smoothness. We propose MSVS-VAE, a hierarchical set-based VAE that closes this fidelity gap without sacrificing compactness. Our key idea is to progressively densify anchored VecSet latents via hierarchical point-shuffle upsampling, increasing spatial capacity for fine-grained geometry modeling. To efficiently decode from the densified hierarchy, we replace global cross-attention with AVS-Conv, a geometry-aware local aggregation operator operating within local neighborhoods rather than the exhaustive latent set. We further introduce multi-scale query decoding to fuse coarse-to-fine latent features, where coarse scales provide stable global context, and fine scales refine localized geometry, reducing artifacts from overly local receptive fields. Extensive experiments on Objaverse, ABO, and in-the-wild benchmarks demonstrate that MSVS-VAE consistently outperforms prior set-based and voxel-based VAEs, delivering approximately 10x faster decoding than prior set-based methods and approximately 10x higher compactness than voxel-based baselines.

</details>


### [167] [RP-OPSD: Resolution-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24447)
*Qihui Zhu,Yuchen Wang,Zijian Wen,Tao Zhang,Mengjie Zhang,Yang Liu,Shuangwu Chen,Siying Wu,Jian Yang,Xiaofeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分辨率差异的特权自蒸馏方法（RP-OPSD），用于提升多模态大模型在有限分辨率下的性能，并加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有On-Policy Self-Distillation操作依赖于人工解答、外部模型生成的解释或人工标注，这些操作难以大规模推广到多模态大语言模型，因为人工和外部资源可能受限。需要一种更简单、自动化且可扩展的特权信息来源来提升多模态模型训练效率和效果。

Method: RP-OPSD利用同一张图片的高低分辨率差异构建特权信息。训练时，学生模型基于1/4分辨率图片生成轨迹，教师模型则用原始高分辨率图片对学生的决策过程进行密集监督，通过最小化两者输出分布的差异，达到知识迁移的目的。与原有方法不同，RP-OPSD不依赖人工标注或外部辅助模型，只需图片—问题对即可完成自蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B模型上，RP-OPSD在原始分辨率下性能相对提升5.45%，训练速度比传统OPSD快1.78倍。

Conclusion: 简单的分辨率差异即可作为多模态大模型自蒸馏的特权信息，无需额外标注或外部资源，既有效又高效，具备推广应用价值。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) uses privileged information available only to the teacher to provide dense token-level supervision on trajectories generated by the student. However, existing methods often rely on verified solution traces, explanations generated by external models, or manually localized visual evidence, which limits their scalable application to multimodal large language models. To address this issue, we exploit the information gap between high- and low-resolution views of the same image and propose RP-OPSD (Resolution-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multimodal Large Language Models). During training, the student policy generates on-policy trajectories from images at one-quarter of the original resolution, while the teacher policy provides supervision using the original-resolution images. By minimizing the divergence between their output distributions along the student trajectories, the student learns the predictive behavior of the teacher under high-resolution inputs, thereby strengthening its low-resolution capability and transferring the learned improvement to original-resolution inference. RP-OPSD requires neither additional human annotations nor external models to generate solution traces but only image--question pairs. Experiments on Qwen3.5-9B show that RP-OPSD achieves a 5.45\% relative improvement in average performance at the original resolution and a $1.78\times$ training speedup over OPSD. These results demonstrate that resolution differences can serve as a simple and scalable source of privileged information, providing an effective and efficient approach to on-policy self-distillation for multimodal large language models.

</details>


### [168] [ESRVS: Extreme Semi-Supervised Retinal Vessel Segmentation with a Single Annotated Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24453)
*Mingzhi Xu,Yizhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法(ESRVS)，通过极少量（仅一个标注样本）监督，实现高效视网膜血管分割，并在八个公开数据集上取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分析中专家密集标注成本高昂，极小监督学习成为提升效率的重要方向，尤其是在视网膜血管分割等关键任务中。

Method: 方法包括：（1）自动选择最具代表性的参考图像进行人工标注；（2）利用DINOv3模型适配特征，将血管信息迁移到未标注图像；（3）构建多粒度的血管原型，将原型相似度图和物理先验结合生成初始伪标签；（4）通过加权伪标签训练和对抗性优化进一步精炼分割结果。

Result: 在八个公开数据集上，ESRVS在六个数据集上获得了Dice和clDice的最优结果，在所有数据集上获得HD95最优结果，且仅需1个标注样本，超越了使用10-20%标注数据的其他半监督方法。与全监督方法相比，ESRVS的分割Dice均值可达93.7%，clDice达95.1%。

Conclusion: ESRVS方法极大提高了视网膜血管分割对标注的利用效率，验证了基础模型标签迁移在小样本场景下的潜力，为降低医学图像分割的人工成本提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Learning from minimal human supervision is a long-standing goal in medical image analysis, where dense expert annotations are costly. We study retinal vessel segmentation in an extreme semi-supervised setting with one annotated image and a pool of unlabeled images. We propose ESRVS, which selects a representative reference image for manual annotation and transfers vessel cues using target-domain-adapted DINOv3 features. ESRVS constructs a multi granular vessel prototype, combines prototype-similarity maps with a physics-inspired prior to generate initial pseudo-labels, and refines the transferred supervision through weighted pseudo-label training and adversarial refinement. Across eight public datasets, ESRVS achieves the best Dice and clDice on six datasets, and the best HD95 on all eight datasets among the compared semi-supervised methods, although those methods use 10 to 20% labeled data. With Mask2Former, ESRVS retains on average 93.7% of fully supervised Dice and 95.1% of fully supervised clDice. These results demonstrate the potential of foundation-model label propagation for highly label-efficient retinal vessel segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/IAANNH/ESRVS.

</details>


### [169] [Rethinking Expert Training for Model Merging with Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24465)
*Christos Georgakilas,Aniello Panariello,Samir El Karrat Moreno,Simone Calderara,Dimosthenis Karatzas,Joost van de Weijer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文关注多任务模型合并，提出了一种通过双阶段训练专家（Dual-Tuned Experts, DTEs）策略，提升了不同专家合并时的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法在专家训练环节普遍采用全参数微调，容易引入参数干扰，同时忽视了改进专家训练策略对合并效果的影响。本文试图在专家训练端进行改进，提升模型合并兼容性。

Method: 首先，作者发现仅基于提示（prompt）的适配，能在固定主干网络的前提下充分利用各任务提示，避免参数合并导致的干扰。基于此，提出了DTEs双阶段训练：先学习各自任务的提示（prompt），再在此基础上微调视觉编码器，从而有效降低任务特定参数更新量。

Result: 在多种CLIP架构中，通过全参数微调和LoRA专家实验，结果显示DTEs方案可持续提升常规合并方法下的性能，即便异质专家组合时仍然有效。

Conclusion: 通过优化专家训练流程，特别是采用DTEs双阶段策略，可以显著增强不同任务专家模型的合并兼容性与性能。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple domain-specialized experts trained from a shared foundation model into a single multi-task model. Existing approaches largely focus on improving the merging procedure itself and typically assume experts obtained through full-parameter fine-tuning. In this work, we revisit expert training for model merging. We first show that prompt-based adaptation provides a strong baseline: independently learned prompts can be exploited across tasks while keeping the backbone fixed, avoiding the interference introduced by weight merging. Building on this observation, we introduce Dual-Tuned Experts (DTEs), a two-stage training strategy that first learns prompts and then fine-tunes the vision encoder. This reduces the magnitude of task-specific parameter updates and produces experts with higher merge compatibility. Experiments across multiple CLIP architectures, full fine-tuning, and LoRA experts show that DTEs consistently improve merged performance of standard merging approaches and remain effective even when combining heterogeneous sets of experts.

</details>


### [170] [NSL-SLAM: High-Fidelity Neural Structured-Light Depth for Practical SLAM and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24495)
*Jiaheng Li,Binsheng Zhang,Xinhai Chang,Wenzheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合神经结构光及深度先验的新型结构光SLAM系统NSL-SLAM，实现了更准确和鲁棒的三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构光摄像头广泛用于深度感知，但传统方法深度精度有限。近期神经结构光方法虽有提升，但尚有进一步优化空间。更高质量的深度感知对SLAM跟踪与重建具有重要意义，因此需开发能高效利用高保真深度的SLAM系统。

Method: 作者提出在结构光深度解码中引入强单目深度先验，减少解码误差，并以高精度深度作为SLAM主跟踪信号，仅在必要时辅以稀疏视觉配准和轻量级束调整，实现端到端深度中心的SLAM管线。

Result: 在Replica-SL合成数据集上，NSL-SLAM深度RMSE相比NSL降低35%，跟踪精度最优，重建F-score提升1.6分。在8个真实场景基准测试中，NSL-SLAM是唯一在所有序列中无重大失败的方法，轨迹偏差比基线低43.3%。系统能实现20.9FPS实时运行。

Conclusion: 强结构光深度与深度中心化SLAM设计的结合，显著提升了SLAM系统的精度和鲁棒性，实现了高效、实用的在线三维重建。

Abstract: Structured-light (SL) cameras power depth sensing in millions of devices, and recent neural SL decoding methods have substantially improved their depth quality. SLAM systems can benefit greatly from such strong depth sensing, where reliable geometry enables stable tracking and faithful reconstruction. In this work, we present NSL-SLAM, a practical SLAM system tailored for high-fidelity structured-light depth. We first strengthen SL depth sensing: inspired by the neural structured-light (NSL) method, we further incorporate strong monocular depth priors into the SL stereo decoding, reducing depth RMSE by 35% on Replica-SL compared to NSL. We then build a depth-centric SLAM pipeline with this stronger depth: because structured-light geometry is dense and metrically accurate, we keep it as the primary tracking signal, and add only sparse visual correspondences for geometrically degenerate cases and lightweight bundle adjustment for long-range drift. Our depth estimator and SLAM design reinforce each other: stronger depth makes a simple SLAM pipeline effective, and the depth-centric pipeline ensures this advantage transfers to downstream reconstruction. Experimentally, on the synthetic Replica-SL benchmark, NSL-SLAM achieves the best tracking accuracy and improves reconstruction F-score by 1.6 points over the SOTA baseline under a shared-depth protocol. On a real benchmark of 8 challenging scenes, it is the only method that avoids catastrophic failure on all sequences while achieving 43.3% lower trajectory deviation than selected baselines. The SLAM system runs online at 20.9 FPS, demonstrating that stronger structured-light depth and depth-centric system design together enable practical, robust SLAM.

</details>


### [171] [DecoupleMix: Decoupled Ratio Search and Convex Allocation for Scalable VLM Data Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24516)
*Jiahao Xie,Zhongbin Guo,Qianle Wang,Ruiqi Lu,Dongling Xiao,Wanxuan Sun,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的数据混合优化方法（DecoupleMix）用于视觉语言模型（VLM）的预训练数据集构建，并验证了其优于传统启发式做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型的数据集构建主要依赖直觉和启发式方法，缺乏可重复和有据可依的准则，也没法明确指导新增数据的选择。这限制了数据构建的科学性和工程重复性。

Method: 将数据混合分解为两类独立的子问题：跨类别能力的分配（inter-class）和同类别内数据的选择（intra-class）。前者采用单变量迭代搜索，后者基于数据集的质量和难度评分，进一步通过约束凸优化和多样性目标进行选取。整体提出了DecoupleMix框架实现数据集构建系统化。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多个任务上一致优于传统启发式构建方式。此外，小规模proxy实验上找到的最优比例，可以直接迁移到大规模数据集，无需重新调参。最终，以80B multimodal tokens进一步预训练的VLM在性能上可与更大规模数据预算的开源强模型竞争。

Conclusion: DecoupleMix使得VLM预训练数据构建变成可控、可归因的工程问题，给出了明确、可重复的指导方法，并显著提升了模型训练效果。

Abstract: While data curation for Vision Language Models (VLMs) is increasingly active, public practice for constructing pretraining mixtures remains largely heuristic: practitioners stack datasets that pass quality filters, set cross-domain ratios by intuition, and lack a principled, attributable criterion for admitting new data, while frontier recipes remain undisclosed. We formulate data construction as a systematic mixture-optimization problem and turn it into a reproducible engineering discipline by decoupling the mixture into two orthogonal sub-problems: inter-class ratios across capabilities and intra-class ratios within a category. For inter-class allocation, we use a single-variable iterative search; for intra-class composition, we apply a multidimensional, dataset-level assessment scoring Quality and Difficulty, and formulate selection as a constrained convex optimization with a diversity objective. The DecoupleMix framework delivers two critical capabilities: guiding what data to collect next and rendering dataset validation a controlled, attributable experiment. Experiments show our approach consistently surpasses heuristic baselines. Moreover, optimal ratios discovered on small-scale proxies transfer seamlessly to larger scales without retuning. Using 80B additional multimodal continue-pretraining tokens, our VLM is competitive with strong open-source models trained with substantially larger multimodal budgets.

</details>


### [172] [EgoPlay: Event-Triggered Video Editing for Egocentric Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24560)
*Jinjie Mai,Gordon Guocheng Qian,Willi Menapace,Arpit Sahni,Chaoyang Wang,Ashkan Mirzaei,Runjia Li,Sergey Tulyakov,Bernard Ghanem,Peter Wonka,Rameen Abdal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoPlay，一种面向第一视角流的事件驱动视频编辑器，通过细化预训练V2V扩散变换器在事件条件数据上的表现，实现复杂事件驱动视频自动编辑，并在Ego4D基准上大幅优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的第一视角（egocentric）视频编辑多依赖于分离的事件检测和编辑器流程，效率低且难以实现高度集成的事件驱动编辑。因此，作者希望开发一种端到端、能根据事件触发自动执行编辑操作且性能优异的新方法。

Method: 作者收集了一个包含106K事件触发视频片段及提示的大规模数据集，覆盖正触发、负触发和多事件提示。使用该数据，通过事件触发监督训练了一个双向视频扩散编辑器，并派生出适配流式推理的因果变体。EgoPlay集成了事件识别、时间约束及像素级编辑于单一端到端模型中。

Result: EgoPlay在Ego4D基准上，相比EgoEdit和VLM引导的检测-编辑基线，在编辑质量、视觉质量和背景一致性等指标分别有17.7%、16.9%、16.4%和15.7%、14.5%、13.5%的提升，并消耗更少的GPU显存。

Conclusion: EgoPlay能够以更高效与准确度实现事件驱动的第一视角视频编辑，显著优于当前SOTA方法，并为相关应用和未来研究提供了有效工具和评测方案。

Abstract: We introduce EgoPlay, an event-triggered video-to-video editor for egocentric streams, obtained by fine-tuning a pretrained V2V diffusion transformer on event-conditioned data built primarily from Ego4D. Given a monocular video and an event-triggered prompt of the form "when X happens, do Y," EgoPlay infers whether and when event X occurs, preserves pre-event frames, and applies edit Y only to the post-event continuation. Rather than cascading a separate event detector with an editor, EgoPlay learns event recognition, temporal restraint, and pixel-level editing jointly in a single end-to-end model, while also handling negative and multi-event prompts. To support this, we construct a large-scale dataset of 106K event-triggered clip-prompt pairs spanning positive triggers, fabricated-trigger negatives, and multi-event prompts. We then train a bidirectional video diffusion editor with event-triggered supervision and derive a causal variant for chunk-by-chunk streamable inference. We further introduce an event-aware evaluation protocol that separately measures post-trigger editing quality, pre-trigger preservation, and false-trigger robustness. On the Ego4D benchmark, EgoPlay substantially outperforms EgoEdit, the state-of-the-art instruction-based egocentric video editing baseline, with relative gains of 17.7%, 16.9%, and 16.4% in editing quality, visual quality, and background consistency. It also surpasses a VLM-guided detector-editor baseline by 15.7%, 14.5%, and 13.5% on the same metrics, while using less than half the GPU memory.

</details>


### [173] [The Visual Bottleneck: Sparse-Frame Adaptation of MLLMs for Joint Spatial-Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24570)
*Jiameng Zhang,Srikanth Madikeri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对大规模视频平台在低帧采样（稀疏帧）情况下做违规内容定位的难题，系统分析了合适的训练策略，发现通过调优视觉特征提取层并采用边界感知采样方法可显著提升模型表现，优于简单扩大模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统因为效率考量，每个视频只能提取8到16帧用于分析，但主流多模态大模型常在远多于此的帧上进行预训练，两者的采样密度不匹配导致部署时模型性能大幅下降。

Method: 开展系统性实证研究，逐步调整模型组件（主要是ViT视觉特征提取器的后几层）并探索新的帧采样方式（如Hybrid16），对比在不同采样密度和模型规模下的定位性能。

Result: 只需微调ViT最后三层（占总参数4%），就能大幅提升稀疏帧输入下的时序定位mIoU（68.8%），超过基于密集帧的8B大模型12.8个百分点。边界感知采样法进一步比均匀采样多提升26个点。语言模型层微调收效甚微。

Conclusion: 在稀疏帧场景下，合适的训练策略（例如视觉层针对性调优、边界感知采样）比单纯加大模型体量更关键，小参数量精调模型甚至优于大参数零样本推理，给大规模视频审核系统选型、部署以重要指导。

Abstract: Large-scale video platforms process millions of uploads hourly, requiring moderation systems that can localize when and where policy violations occur within each video. Processing every frame is infeasible at scale, so systems are constrained to sparse inputs of 8 to 16 frames per video. Yet state-of-the-art multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are pretrained on dense sequences of hundreds of frames, creating a fundamental mismatch between training and deployment conditions. This mismatch causes severe performance collapse: the Qwen3-VL 8B model drops from 56.0% to 22.3% temporal mIoU when frames are reduced to 16, a 60.2% relative degradation.
  We present a systematic empirical study of training strategies to close this gap for spatial-temporal video grounding. Our results suggest that visual feature extraction is the dominant bottleneck under sparse-frame inputs. Adapting only the final three ViT layers, 4% of total parameters, achieves 68.8% temporal mIoU and surpasses a zero-shot 8B model using dense inputs by 12.8 points. Language model fine-tuning, by contrast, offers negligible or negative returns. A boundary-aware sampling strategy, Hybrid16, further improves temporal mIoU by 26 points over uniform sampling when temporal boundaries are available. We conclude that for sparse-frame video grounding, training strategy dominates model scale: a fine-tuned 2B model consistently outperforms a zero-shot 8B model, with or without dense frame access.

</details>


### [174] [CADER: Confidence-Aware Dynamic Evidence Reasoning for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24582)
*Jinlong Yang,Wenhao Zhang,Kuanwei Lin,Sijie Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对长视频理解的新框架CADER，通过动态判断样本难易度，自适应地选择是否调用工具辅助推理，提高推理效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频推理系统对所有问题一视同仁，即使简单问题也调用计算量大的工具辅助处理，既浪费资源，又缺乏对复杂问题的细致控制。为此，需要一种能够根据具体问题难度动态调整推理流程的方法。

Method: CADER框架分两阶段：首先对整体视频帧全局推理并估计答案置信度，对高置信度样本直接输出。对不确定样本，则启动第二阶段——通过工具辅助的裁剪、语义验证和基于相关性重采样等方法，逐步定位与问题相关的证据。这种方式将是否使用工具的决策下放到样本级别。

Result: 在多个视频问答基准测试中，CADER能有效提升推理表现，对高置信度样本可跳过复杂工具流程（节约资源），而不影响推理准确率。在只用无工具链式思维训练的模型基础上，CADER推理时也能达到与专门工具增强框架相当的性能。

Conclusion: CADER提供了一种实用、灵活的长视频推理路径，做到高效利用工具，仅在有必要时动态调用，为长视频文本理解场景下的推理效率与鲁棒性提供新思路。

Abstract: Long-video understanding increasingly relies on large vision-language models and tool-augmented reasoning, but most systems apply the same inference procedure to every example regardless of difficulty. This uniform strategy invokes unnecessary tool-assisted processing for easy questions and provides limited control when difficult questions require fine-grained temporal evidence. We propose CADER (Confidence-Aware Dynamic Evidence Reasoning), a training-free framework for adaptive and reliable long-video reasoning. CADER first performs global reasoning over uniformly sampled frames and estimates answer confidence with a logit-margin signal, allowing high-confidence examples to exit early. For uncertain examples, CADER activates a second-stage tool-augmented loop that combines temporal cropping, lightweight semantic verification, and Relevance-Guided Resampling to progressively localize question-relevant evidence. This design treats tool use as a sample-level decision: a single global pass handles easy cases, while additional reasoning is reserved for examples where uncertainty suggests that more evidence is needed. Experiments on multiple VideoQA benchmarks show that CADER improves long-video reasoning while bypassing Stage~2 for high-confidence samples. Moreover, when applied to a backbone trained only with tool-free chain-of-thought supervision, CADER achieves competitive performance against specialized tool-augmented frameworks, suggesting a practical inference-time route for adaptive long-video reasoning.

</details>


### [175] [CameraAnything: Refilming Videos with Arbitrary Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24591)
*Yixuan Li,Yanhong Zeng,Ka Leong Cheng,Jiayi Zhu,Hanlin Wang,Wen Wang,Yihao Meng,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Yue Yu,ZiDong Wang,Yiyuan Zhang,Yujun Shen,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出CameraAnything，首个支持内/外参联合控制的摄像机视频编辑框架，无需昂贵3D重建，内外参解耦难题通过新型编码方案解决，具备灵活视频重拍和多机位切换能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摄像机视频编辑方法要么依赖计算成本高的3D重建以获得完整相机控制，要么仅能修改外参，且内外参耦合给模型带来极大挑战，缺乏有效、通用的编辑方案。

Method: 方法核心在于：1）采用逐像素Plücker光线注入与分辨率感知3D RoPE（旋转位置编码）结合，将相机位姿、焦距等编码进目标潜表示上，实现相机内外参联合控制及分辨率编辑，无需裁剪扩图；2）针对缺乏配对训练数据，设计大规模合成管线，用多相机构建结构化动态场景，生成不同相机配置下的同步视频；3）专门的正交训练策略以提升模型泛化能力。

Result: CameraAnything实现了任意视角重拍、焦距调整、分辨率自适应和多镜头切换等复杂编辑，只需一次生成流程即可完成多个编辑需求。相比现有方法，框架具备更强的实用性和适应性。

Conclusion: CameraAnything首次实现了无需3D重建的高效摄像机全参数视频编辑，解决了内外参耦合难题，能广泛应用于影视后期、多平台内容适配等场景，具有重大实际价值。

Abstract: We introduce CameraAnything, the first unified framework for camera controlled video editing that enables joint control of both intrinsic and extrinsic camera parameters. Existing approaches either rely on expensive 3D reconstruction to achieve full camera functionality or restrict editing to extrinsic parameter manipulation. Moreover, the coupled influence of intrinsic and extrinsic parameters on video appearance makes disentangled modeling particularly challenging. To address this, we adopt per-pixel Plücker ray injection alongside resolution-aware 3D RoPE in self-attention, building both camera conditioning and spatial positional encoding on the target latent to jointly control camera position, focal length, and native resolution editing without cropping or outpainting. To overcome the scarcity of paired training data, we further develop a scalable synthetic pipeline that constructs diverse dynamic scenes through structured multi-camera recording and generates synchronized videos with varied camera configurations. With a tailored orthogonal training strategy, CameraAnything enables expressive video reshooting with arbitrary viewpoint control, focal length adjustment, resolution adaptation, and multi-shot transitions within a single generation process, offering strong practical value for cinematic video editing and cross-platform content adaptation in video production.

</details>


### [176] [QueenVIS: Rethinking Image-Only Training for Video Instance Segmentation via Query Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24598)
*Arian Kheirandish,Fardin Ayar,Ehsan Javanmardi,Manabu Tsukada,Mahdi Javanmardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QueenVIS提出了一种仅用图像训练却能有效提升视频实例分割性能的新框架，通过加强Mask2Former的query设计显著优于前代方法，在无需视频级标注的情况下取得了与视频监督方法相竞争的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VIS方法大多依赖于耗时且昂贵的视频级身份一致性标注，部分最新方法尝试仅用图像训练，但存在时序一致性差、实例关联不稳等问题。本文旨在进一步探索如何利用单帧图像训练提升VIS性能，降低成本并简化训练流程。

Method: QueenVIS在单帧训练中为Mask2Former的object queries引入两个辅助头：特征预测损失（引导query贴合实例特征）、中心预测损失（注入空间结构信息）。推理时这两个辅助头被弃用；时序一致性通过训练外的query-propagation与memory bank机制保证，无需特殊视频训练或增加模型参数。

Result: 在YouTube-VIS和OVIS数据集上，QueenVIS在ResNet-50下对比MinVIS分别提升了最高6.7 AP、4.8 AP，在长序列上提升10.3 AP，结果达到50.9 AP，与最新的视频监督方法表现接近甚至超越。

Conclusion: 仅用图像训练但通过提升query判别力和时序稳定性，QueenVIS在VIS任务中实现了新突破，表明object query设计仍有广阔可挖掘空间，这为降低VIS训练成本和简化流程提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Video instance segmentation (VIS) requires models to detect, segment, and track object identities across frames, and most methods enforce temporal consistency through video-level supervision. Image-only training approaches, with MinVIS as one prominent example, have challenged this assumption, reaching competitive VIS without video training by treating frames as independent images and associating instances only at inference. The field has nonetheless moved toward ever more elaborate video-trained trackers, which depend on costly identity-consistent annotations, leaving the image-only direction under-explored. A diagnostic analysis identifies object query quality as the bottleneck: queries trained only to localize objects within a frame drift apart across frames and destabilize association. QueenVIS introduces a query-centric framework for strengthening image-trained VIS. During single-frame training, we enrich Mask2Former queries with two auxiliary heads: a feature-prediction loss that aligns each query with the pooled backbone descriptor of its instance, and a center-prediction loss that injects spatial structure. Both heads are discarded at inference, adding zero parameters, and temporal identity is maintained by a training-free query-propagation and memory-bank scheme. On YouTube-VIS and OVIS with a ResNet-50 backbone, QueenVIS improves over MinVIS, up to +6.7 AP on YouTube-VIS, +4.8 AP on OVIS, and +10.3 AP on the long-sequence YouTube-VIS split. QueenVIS achieves 50.9 AP on YouTube-VIS and remains competitive with recent video-supervised state-of-the-art, without processing a single video clip during training. Our findings suggest that strengthening the discriminative power and temporal stability of object queries is an important, underexplored axis for VIS. Code and models: https://github.com/ArianKheir/QueenVIS

</details>


### [177] [Test-Time Adaptation via Dual Distillation for Videos Under Severe Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24611)
*André Sacilotti,Samuel Felipe dos Santos,Jurandy Almeida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TADD（Test-time Adaptation via Dual Distillation）的在线自适应框架，以应对视频领域测试时适应问题，在多个基准上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在实际场景由于分布转移表现大幅下降。测试时自适应(TTA)虽能缓解，然而对于连续、时序相关的视频以及剧烈领域转移的目标域，TTA仍具挑战性。现有文献对此类极端条件下的视频TTA研究甚少，有效方案匮乏。

Method: 作者提出TADD框架，利用轻量级投影适配器作为域间桥梁。该模块源域预训练，目标域通过提出的互补损失进行自适应：（1）零样本蒸馏，对齐预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）中的领域无关特征；（2）目标蒸馏，保留适配器中的源域判别知识。核心骨干为冻结的CLIP，仅适配器模块在推理阶段可更新。

Result: 在UCF-HMDB、Daily-DA、Sports-DA三大视频动作识别基准上，TADD方法在闭集场景始终优于现有TTA基线。具体提升最高分别为+3.81%、+2.63%、+3.03%。

Conclusion: TADD通过双蒸馏实现高效测试时自适应，有效提升了极端域转移下的视频动作识别性能，展现了其广泛的实用性和先进性。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved state-of-the-art performance in several computer vision tasks. However, they experience severe performance degradation when applied to real-world scenarios due to unanticipated distribution shifts. Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) attempts to solve this problem by using unlabeled data from the target domain to dynamically adapt to the test distribution at inference time, without access to the source data. However, TTA remains a challenging problem when adapting to continuous, temporally correlated data, such as videos, and in scenarios where the target domain contains severe domain shifts. For this reason, few works in the literature explore TTA for videos under such extreme conditions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Test-time Adaptation via Dual Distillation (TADD), an online adaptation framework that relies on a lightweight projection adapter to bridge the domain gap. The adapter module is pre-trained on the source domain and then adapted to the target using our proposed complementary losses: (i) zero-shot distillation, which encourages alignment with the domain-agnostic features from a pre-trained vision-language model (VLM); and (ii) target distillation, which retains the source domain discriminative knowledge encoded in the pre-trained adapter. Built upon a frozen CLIP backbone, our method introduces this lightweight projection adapter as the sole updatable component during inference. We conducted extensive evaluations on three well-known video action recognition benchmarks: UCF-HMDB, Daily-DA, and Sports-DA. Our experiments in the closed-set scenario demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art TTA baselines. Notably, our TTA approach improves upon previous methods by up to +3.81% on UCF-HMDB, +2.63% on Daily-DA, and +3.03% on Sports-DA.

</details>


### [178] [MMOE: Modernizing Diffusion Transformers with Efficient Expert Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24665)
*Yanhao Jia,Jiepeng Wang,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Erik Cambria,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种现代化混合专家网络（MMOE）架构，将高效LLM的稀疏专家机制系统地引入AIGC基础模型（AFMs）的扩散-变换器骨干中，实现了更好的收敛速度和生成质量与成本的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AIGC基础模型在采用稀疏专家时，只关注参数量和稀疏性提高，缺乏类似大语言模型高效扩展的机制，导致生成质量与成本平衡不好。作者提出应借鉴LLM的高效扩展机制以提升AFMs效率。

Method: 作者提出MMOE，将路由专家、共享与轻量专家、门控-残差路由、注意力残差信息复用等高效稀疏专家设计引入扩散-变换器模型，对多种稀疏专家组件进行组合，并使用单节点八卡H100、256批量、400k步的训练预算进行所有实证实验。

Result: 在同等训练和采样协议及预算下，MMOE在所有检查点上都实现了比致密和中间稀疏基线更快的收敛速度（更低FID），且在稀疏模型中实现了最佳质量-成本平衡。路由分析显示专家分工稳定，轻量路由被大量利用，去噪过程中路由变化适中。

Conclusion: 证明了AFMs可以通过引入LLMs已经验证的高效设计，走向高效扩展与质量-成本平衡的道路，而不必仅仅增加参数量或稀疏比例。

Abstract: Modern large language models scale successfully by pairing capacity growth with efficiency, keeping per-token and deployment costs under control as capacity grows. AIGC Foundation Models (AFMs), especially diffusion-transformer backbones, have begun to adopt sparse experts, but recent efforts mostly enlarge total parameter counts and sparsity ratios without importing the efficiency mechanisms that made LLM scaling practical, so generation quality is seldom balanced against training and deployment cost. This raises a natural question: can the architectural principles behind efficient LLM scaling be adapted to AFMs in a more balanced way? We introduce ModernMOE (MMOE), a modernization of SiT-style diffusion transformers that systematically adapts routed experts, shared and lightweight experts, gate-residual routing, and attention-residual information reuse to AIGC generation. Rather than treating MoE as a single plug-in replacement, MMOE studies how different modern expert components affect convergence, efficiency, and generation quality when composed inside a diffusion transformer. Every experiment in this paper is trained on a single eight-GPU H100 node with batch size 256 for 400k steps, an accessible single-machine budget. Under matched training and sampling protocols and at this budget, MMOE reaches lower FID at every recorded checkpoint, that is, it converges faster per training step, than dense and intermediate sparse-expert baselines, and among the sparse variants it attains the best quality-cost balance. Routing analysis further shows stable expert specialization across depth, substantial use of lightweight routes, and modest step-to-step routing changes during denoising. These results suggest that AFMs can follow the balanced scaling path of LLMs by importing proven efficiency designs, rather than by simply increasing total parameters and sparsity ratios.

</details>


### [179] [Co-Learning for Missing Arbitrary Modalities in Multi-modal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24683)
*Francisco Mena,Dino Ienco,Roberto Interdonato,Cassio F. Dantas,Simon Besnard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两种面向任意模态缺失场景的多模态协同学习方法，并在两个基准数据集上验证了其在不同缺失条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多模态分类中，训练与推理阶段可能由于设备故障或隐私等原因导致模态不一致，现有工作多局限于双模态且侧重鲁棒融合，难以普适应对各类模态任意缺失情形。

Method: 提出多模态协同学习框架，优先考虑模态间协作而非简单融合。针对任意模态缺失情形，分别在特征层和决策层设计了两种协作方式。其中，一种在单一模态缺失时表现更优，另一种在仅剩一个模态时效果更佳。

Result: 在两个多模态分类基准数据集上进行实验，结果表明两种方法在不同缺失模态条件下均明显提升了模型鲁棒性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作打破了传统多模态融合的局限，证明了协同学习框架下的信息互补及特征/决策层协作设计能极大提升多模态分类系统对任意模态缺失的适应能力，对实际应用具有很大意义和推广价值。

Abstract: Multi-modal classification leverages complementary information across diverse data sources to enhance predictive performance. However, real-world scenarios subject to operational constraints, such as sensor failures or privacy restrictions, lead to inconsistent modality availability between training and inference times. To handle missing modalities, prior studies have mainly covered bimodal data setups and focused on designing robust fusion processes. Instead, we adopt a multi-modal co-learning framework that prioritizes inter-modal collaboration rather than multi-modal fusion. Specifically, we consider that any subset of modalities may be absent, without assuming predefined missing-modality patterns, an inference scenario we refer to as missing arbitrary modalities. To address this challenge, we introduce two alternative approaches that leverage information at both feature- and decision-level. Experiments on two multi-modal classification benchmarks demonstrate significant robustness gains in various missing modality conditions. The first method shows more robust behavior under minimal missing conditions, where a single modality is absent, whereas the second performs better under extreme missing conditions, where all-but-one modalities are missing. Our code is available at https://github.com/fmenat/Co4Miss.

</details>


### [180] [Spatio-Temporal Conditional Denoising Transformer for Modality-Missing RGBT Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24701)
*Andong Lu,Ziyi Zha,Jiandong Jin,Shihao Li,Chenglong Li,Jin Tang,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的时空条件去噪Transformer（SCDT），可在可见光-红外（RGBT）目标跟踪中，有效处理模态缺失问题，实现更鲁棒的多模态特征重建和增强，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RGBT目标跟踪任务中，部分模态缺失或弱化会导致多模态特征不完整、表现不稳定，严重影响跟踪精度。目前方法常试图从现有模态恢复缺失信息，但在复杂场景下生成的数据质量难以保证，并且对完整和缺失数据的处理灵活性不足。

Method: 作者提出SCDT模型，创新性地结合空间信息与时间上下文，实现对缺失模态的重建和弱模态的特征增强。方法利用短期与长期时间线索，捕捉细粒度时间相关性和模态演化的全局背景，结合去噪机制与噪声调节适配，根据模态可用情况动态调整特征学习方式。该方法在同一架构下统一处理缺失和完整模态，无需结构或参数修改。

Result: 实验证明，SCDT在三个公开基准数据集上取得了显著优于现有先进方法的效果，展现出更强的鲁棒性和信息重建能力。

Conclusion: SCDT为模态缺失情况下的RGBT目标跟踪提供了一种有效、统一且灵活的建模框架，实现了更鲁棒的多模态特征学习，拓展了多模态视觉任务的研究与应用边界。

Abstract: Missing modalities in RGBT tracking often lead to incomplete and unstable multimodal feature representations that greatly degrade the performance. Existing methods typically attempt to recover missing modalities from available ones, but the quality of data generated in challenging scenarios might be unsatisfactory. In addition, current approaches exhibit limited flexibility in processing both missing and complete data. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Spatio-temporal Conditional Denoising Transformer (SCDT), which integrates the spatial cues and the temporal context to adaptively perform information reconstruction of missing modalities and feature enhancement of weak modalities in a unified framework, for robust modality-missing RGBT tracking. In particular, SCDT leverages the short-term temporal cues from recent historical frames to capture the fine-grained temporal correlations and the long-term temporal cues encoding modality evolution to capture the global context. By jointly exploiting long short-term temporal contexts as the conditions, SCDT progressively guides noisy features of available modalities to learn reliable and temporally consistent multimodal representations. Furthermore, SCDT introduces a noisemodulated adaptation mechanism that dynamically adjusts its behavior according to the modal availability, enabling a single framework to unify feature learning under both modality-missing and complete scenarios without changing the architecture or parameters. Extensive experiments on three public benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is available here.

</details>


### [181] [Panda: Unsupervised Pelvic Anomaly Detection for Real-Time MR Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24703)
*Anika Knupfer,Maximilian Lindholz,Johanna Paula Müller,Jordina Aviles Verdera,Smiti Tripathy,Susanne Schulz-Heise,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对盆腔MRI的无监督异常检测框架，实现了高效、免标注的实时异常定位，并在实际数据集上达到了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性盆腔疾病研究不足，诊断常延迟。现有监督方法需要大量标注数据，且不适用于稀有、多样的异常事件，亟需无需标注且能适应实际场景的异常检测方法。

Method: 提出基于Dinomaly的无监督异常检测框架，利用冻结的DINOv3 Vision Transformer编码器，结合噪声MLP瓶颈和线性注意力解码器，防止身份映射，增强效率。通过编码器与解码器的每个token余弦距离进行异常定位，生成空间异常图，实现实时反馈。

Result: 在Uterine Myoma Dataset的精选子集上，像素级AUROC达88.06%，帧级特异性高达95.45%，速度为40.5切片/秒，满足临床实时应用需求。

Conclusion: 该方法无需标注即可实现对盆腔MRI异常的实时、高效、精细定位，为放射科医生提供即时决策支持，有望提升复杂妇科疾病的诊断效率与精准度。

Abstract: Female pelvic diseases remain an under researched area characterized by often delayed diagnosis. While pelvic MRI offers superior soft-tissue contrast for diagnosis and image-guided procedures, real-time anomaly detection remains challenging due to physiological motion, tissue deformation, and instrument artifacts. Existing supervised approaches are impractical, as adverse events are rare, heterogeneous, and difficult to annotate. We present a Dinomaly-based unsupervised anomaly detection framework adapted for pelvic MRI that learns normative representations from healthy cases and flags deviations without requiring labels. Our approach leverages a frozen DINOv3 Vision Transformer encoder combined with a noisy MLP bottleneck and Linear Attention decoder to prevent identity mapping while maintaining computational efficiency. Anomalies are localized via per-token cosine distance between encoder and decoder representations, yielding spatial anomaly maps that provide immediate feedback at the scanner to support radiologist decision-making and adaptive protocol adjustment. Evaluated on a curated subset of the Uterine Myoma Dataset, the framework achieves a pixel-level AUROC of 88.06% and high specificity (95.45%) at frame level at 40.5 slices/s, meeting real-time clinical deployment requirements. The spatial anomaly maps and frame-level scores provide immediate, localized feedback at the scanner to support radiologist decision-making and adaptive protocol adjustment during active procedures.

</details>


### [182] [SADe: Sparse-Atom Support Decontamination for Few-Shot Segmentation with Weak Support Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24706)
*Hang Xing,Guangjun Liu,Yan Xia,Xueming Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的支持去伪装层SADe，用于提升弱标注Few-shot分割的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Few-shot segmentation方法通常依赖于像素级的干净支持掩膜，但实际应用中常用框、涂鸦、粗掩膜等弱标注，这些容易引入与目标纹理相似但无关的干扰和背景，造成支持信息污染，进而影响分割性能。

Method: 作者提出SADe模块，在无需查询信息的条件下，通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取支持掩膜内外的激活差异性作为可靠性线索。一个轻量路由器结合原子证据、密集相似性和统计特征，预测掩膜中不同patch的可靠性，从而生成更干净的支持掩膜。该路由器在合成弱支持集上预训练后冻结，可对接多种现有Few-shot分割模型。

Result: 在六种独立prompt-shot实验中，SADe在九类组合中的六类上获得了最高的query mIoU得分。在使用ProMi查询头的实验里，SADe在紧盒掩膜下与SAM3接近，在box-r2和box-r4弱标注下分别有11.17和19.49的显著提升。作为插件，SADe在四个下游模型的绝大多数box-family实验中均优于原始支持标注。

Conclusion: SADe无需更改分割模型结构即可作为普适插件，有效去除弱标注支持的混杂信息，全面提升了Few-shot分割在多种弱标注下的鲁棒性和泛用性。此外，其可靠性来源于新引入的原子证据，优于仅用密集相似性的传统方法。

Abstract: Few-shot segmentation (FSS) commonly assumes clean pixel-level support masks, yet practical support supervision often uses boxes, scribbles, coarse masks, or pseudo-masks. These weak annotations may include texture-similar distractors and background context alongside the target, contaminating class prototypes or visual prompts before query prediction. We introduce SADe, a predictor-agnostic support decontamination layer that estimates the reliability of selected support patches without query information. Central to SADe is sparse autoencoder (SAE) atom evidence: dense similarity may respond to both target and texture-similar context, whereas contrasting atom activations inside and outside the weak-support region provides factor-level reliability cues. A lightweight router combines atom evidence with dense similarity and episode statistics to predict patch reliability and generate a cleaned support mask. Trained once on synthetic weak-support episodes from FSS-1000, the router is frozen for all target evaluations. The resulting mask supports standalone prediction or can be supplied to heterogeneous FSS models through native support interfaces without altering query-side inference. Under a matched weak-support protocol, SADe achieves the highest query mIoU in six of nine standalone prompt-shot combinations. With the same ProMi query head, it is within 0.03 mIoU of SAM3-derived masks under tight boxes and surpasses them by 11.17 and 19.49 points under box-r2 and box-r4, respectively. As a plug-in, SADe improves over raw support in 70 of 72 matched box-family comparisons across four frozen downstream models and two datasets. On point and scribble prompts, its average performance remains close to the corresponding raw-support baseline. Ablations and atom-removal controls show that atom evidence contributes reliability information beyond dense similarity.

</details>


### [183] [DreamStyle3D: Efficient 3D Stylized Asset Generation via Dual-Attention Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24721)
*Kai Wang,Ziheng Ouyang,Xuying Zhang,Ming-Ming Cheng,Qibin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DreamStyle3D框架，能够高效生成具备高度风格保真度和几何一致性的3D资产。其创新机制和高质量数据集显著提升了3D内容的风格化生成速度和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D风格化方法多依赖于2D到3D的间接流程，难以兼顾风格保真、几何一致与生成效率。因此，亟需一种本地3D的高效风格化方法，显式解耦风格与几何特征。

Method: 提出基于Decoupled Dual Cross-Attention机制的DreamStyle3D框架，在几何与风格特征之间进行显式分离，实现高效风格注入，并采用轻量训练策略提升风格一致性与泛化能力。同时，创建了包含约1.5万组样本的自动化内容-风格-风格化三元组数据集。

Result: 大量实验显示，DreamStyle3D可在10秒内生成高保真、几何一致的风格化3D资产；无论在效率还是风格质量上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DreamStyle3D为3D内容风格化生成提出了新思路，大幅提升效率与质量，推动3D游戏、动画、虚拟现实等领域的发展。

Abstract: With the growth of gaming, animation, and virtual reality industries, the demand for efficient generation of stylized 3D assets is rapidly increasing. However, existing approaches still struggle to jointly preserve style fidelity, geometric consistency, and generation efficiency, as most of them still rely on indirect 2D-to-3D stylization pipelines. This motivates a native 3D stylization framework that can explicitly disentangle style from geometry while remaining efficient. To this end, we propose DreamStyle3D, an efficient framework for stylized 3D asset generation built on a Decoupled Dual Cross-Attention mechanism. Our method explicitly separates geometric and stylistic features to enable efficient style injection while preserving structural consistency, and further adopts a lightweight training strategy to enhance style consistency and model generalization. In addition, we build an automated data pipeline and construct a dataset of about 15K content-style-stylized triplets for training and evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our DreamStyle3D can generate high-fidelity, geometrically consistent stylized 3D assets within 10 seconds, substantially improving efficiency while maintaining superior style quality and offering a new solution for 3D content creation. The code and data are available at https://github.com/HVision-NKU/DreamStyle3D.

</details>


### [184] [Infrared Imaging Empowered by Artificial Intelligence for Pediatric Skeletal Triage: A Narrative Review and Future Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24727)
*Sajad Amiri,Pardis Afshar,Elham Anjomshoa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出通过结合多光谱红外成像与深度学习，实现无需辐射的儿童骨骼损伤影像学诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童肌骨损伤诊疗高度依赖放射性X光，但低剂量辐射累计提高了患白血病和脑部恶性肿瘤的风险，因此亟需无辐射替代方案。

Method: 系统梳理了5个红外光谱窗口(650nm-1mm，包括NIR-I, NIR-II, SWIR, MIR/LWIR和THz)，利用双几何（透射/反射）采集方式提升组织穿透深度与生化灵敏度。采用多种图像到图像转换网络（如Pix2Pix、CycleGAN、Swin-Unet）及特征匹配算法将红外数据对齐融合，合成与X光类似的重建影像。

Result: 儿童具有解剖优势（骨薄、层浅），利于红外光穿透，适合开发便携、无辐射的分诊设备。已有fNIRS和脑光调控实践证明红外能穿透皮肤、骨等，满足信号需求。然而，实际应用还需解决IR/X-ray配对数据集构建、AI医疗器械合规性、种族差异泛化及采集协议标准化等难点。

Conclusion: 多谱段红外与AI结合的影像重建是现有X光的有前景的无辐射补充，未来需多中心数据、多场景验证及红外安全性规范认证。

Abstract: Background. Pediatric musculoskeletal trauma represents up to 18% of pediatric ED visits, yet diagnosis still depends on ionizing radiography. Cumulative low-dose radiation in early life raises lifetime leukemia and brain malignancy risk, motivating radiation-free triage alternatives. Objective. To synthesize evidence for a hybrid framework coupling broad-spectrum infrared (IR) imaging with deep-learning cross-modal translation to generate clinically interpretable synthetic-radiograph reconstructions from non-ionizing data.
  Approach. We review five IR spectral windows spanning 650 nm to 1 mm - NIR-I, NIR-II, SWIR, MIR/LWIR, and THz - and how dual-geometry (transmission/reflection) acquisition exploits wavelength-specific tissue depth and biochemical sensitivity. We summarize image-to-image translation networks (Pix2Pix, CycleGAN, Swin-Unet) and feature-matching algorithms (SuperPoint, SuperGlue, ALIKED, LightGlue) used to align and fuse IR data into radiograph-equivalent reconstructions.
  Implications. Pediatric anatomy - smaller cross-sections, thinner cortical bone - favors IR penetration, enabling compact, portable, non-ionizing triage hardware. Feasibility is grounded in fNIRS and transcranial photobiomodulation evidence: near-infrared light passes through skin, skull, and cortex with sufficient signal for hemodynamic monitoring - a longer, more attenuating path than through a pediatric forearm or distal leg. Key barriers: paired IR/X-ray dataset construction, AI-as-medical-device regulatory pathways, generalization across body habitus and skin pigmentation, and acquisition-protocol standardization.
  Conclusions. Integrated multi-spectral IR+AI imaging is a promising radiation-free complement to pediatric skeletal radiography. Progress requires multi-center paired datasets, externally validated models, and IR source safety qualification under IEC 60825-1.

</details>


### [185] [MicroZoom: Structure-Preserving Detail Synthesis at Extreme Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24729)
*Huy Huynh,Jingwei Ma,Brian Curless,Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman,Steven M. Seitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MicroZoom框架，可利用普通照片和有限的显微镜局部图像，生成微观尺度的超大分辨率（千兆像素）图像，实现对对象微观纹理的无缝可视化。该方法注重合成的合理性而非精准还原，采用两阶段级联结构保证图像的整体结构和局部细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术难以在缺乏高精度全景输入的情况下，还原大尺度物体的微观纹理分布，尤其在材料边界和大范围花纹重复上存在挑战。本研究希望利用少量低成本显微镜图像打破这一限制。

Method: 方法包括两个阶段：第一阶段注重恢复图像的全局图案一致性，第二阶段细化局部微观纹理，并引入分割掩码以引导边界的合理合成，提升细节处理能力。

Result: 在自采集的日常物品数据集上实验，MicroZoom能够合成在材质上真实、全局上连贯的千兆像素微观图像，远超输入的质量和细节表现。

Conclusion: MicroZoom为微观场景下极限超分辨率生成提供了新路径，实现了低成本下的高分辨率材质合成和全范围可视化，对材料科学、文物保护等领域具重要意义。

Abstract: We introduce MicroZoom, a generative framework for gigapixel image synthesis at the microscopic scale. Given a standard photograph and a sparse set of consumer-grade microscope close-ups, MicroZoom synthesizes a seamless, gigapixel-resolution image grounded in the material character of the real references, enabling exploratory visualization of microscopic texture across the full spatial extent of an object. Our goal is plausible synthesis, not exact reconstruction. We focus on full-image, reference-based, extreme-scale super-resolution at magnification levels of up to 350x, a setting that introduces two major challenges: (1) recovering texture-specific detail from highly lossy inputs near ambiguous material boundaries, and (2) preserving correct large-scale pattern structure, such as the repeating geometry of a fabric weave, across millions of local predictions. We address these with a two-stage cascaded design, where the first stage recovers global pattern coherence and the second refines local texture detail, supplemented by a segmentation mask to guide synthesis at ambiguous boundaries. We verify our approach on a collection of self-captured everyday objects and demonstrate globally coherent, materially grounded gigapixel imagery.

</details>


### [186] [KANEx: Translating Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks' Interpretability to Medical Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24730)
*Krithi Shailya,Ananya Lakshmi Ravi,Venkatanathan K. V.,Sowmya S. Sundaram,Gokul S. Krishnan,Aditi Anand,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合可解释神经网络(KAN)和视觉-语言模型(VLM)的新框架KANEx，用于提升医学X光影像诊断中解释的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然计算机视觉模型在医学领域取得了显著进展，但模型的黑箱特性限制了临床医生的信任。目前主流通过视觉-语言模型生成自然语言解释，但并未根本解决视觉模型本身的不可解释性，因此需要在模型结构层面提升透明度与解释性。

Method: 作者提出利用具备内在可解释性（样条基函数）的Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN)，并结合传统视觉骨干网络（如ResNet/ViT），设计KANEx框架。该方式允许模型结构本身直接支持解释。同时，提出从KAN直接生成可解释热力图（KAN-Map），结合生成的上下文喂入下游VLM以增强解释能力。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR公开数据库上，KAN与ResNet/ViT联合的模型相较传统基线在语义相似度、显著性图的忠实度上表现更优。KAN有助于提升可视化定位及下游推理质量约10%。

Conclusion: 将可解释的数学单元(KAN)引入视觉-语言解释系统，使医学AI的语言解释和视觉归因更可靠，是实现可信医学AI的重要一步。

Abstract: Computer vision models have become highly effective for medical applications, yet their black-box nature continues to undermine clinician trust. In clinical workflows, chest X-ray classifiers are increasingly paired with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate natural-language explanations. However, these systems add linguistic fluency without addressing the underlying opacity of the visual model. With the emergence of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), whose spline-based components provide inherently interpretable functional units, we investigate whether this architectural transparency can be leveraged to produce more trustworthy textual explanations. We introduce KANEx, the first ever framework that leverages the symbolic transparency of KANs to ground VLM reasoning. This interpretability also made it possible to design KAN-Map, a novel heatmap generation method derived directly from KAN models rather than gradient approximations. We feed these grounded contexts into downstream VLMs for enhanced explainability. Benchmarked on the MIMIC-CXR dataset, we demonstrate that KAN-based architectures with ResNet/ViT baselines demonstrate improved semantic similarity while producing significantly more faithful saliency maps. KAN architectures improve visual localization and downstream reasoning quality by 10%. Our findings suggest that grounding linguistic explanations and visual attributions in mathematically interpretable units is a necessary step toward trustworthy medical AI.

</details>


### [187] [Rethinking Classifier-Free Guidance in On-Policy Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24731)
*Bingnan Li,Haozhe Wang,Haozhong Xiong,Fangtai Wu,Jinpeng Yu,Yang Shi,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于策略蒸馏（OPD）在现代扩散模型的无分类器引导（CFG）下的行为，并发现了现有方法可能出现的负分支不对称性（NBA）问题，提出了分支感知的PDM方法以更有效地解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代扩散模型常用无分类器引导（CFG）提升生成结果，而基于策略蒸馏（OPD）被用来高效适配或微调这些模型。但CFG引入了教师模型（Teacher）和学生模型（Student）之间的复杂相互作用，原有的蒸馏目标如何适应CFG机制并未明确。本文旨在理清该机制下OPD的局限与潜在失败模式，并探索更合适的解决方案。

Method: 作者首先系统分析了现有OPD在CFG下的分支误差补偿与不对称现象。通过对比两种条件下的分支误差动态，揭示了负分支携带教师模型的特权信息时易出现负分支不对称（NBA）问题。为此，提出了正向匹配（PDM）这一新的OPD目标，使正分支预测和CFG引导方向分别受限，避免双方误差的对抗性变化。并在稠密到稀疏视频控制场景进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，在传统OPD方案下，CFG分支指导条件变化时模型表现波动剧烈，负分支失衡；而采用PDM后，指导尺度变化下模型性能更加稳定，知识迁移更加高效和健壮。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了OPD在CFG机制下的负分支不对称失败模式，并提出了有效的分支感知蒸馏目标，为扩散模型的鲁棒知识迁移提供了新方案，有助于推动实际应用中更稳健的模型适配和微调。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) adapts diffusion models by querying a teacher along trajectories generated by the current student, but how it should behave under classifier-free guidance (CFG), a default component of modern diffusion systems, remains poorly understood. Existing OPD methods naturally extend velocity matching to the CFG-composed prediction, directly matching teacher and student guided velocities. We show that this objective is under-identified at the branch level: positive- and negative-branch errors can compensate in the guided prediction. Through two contrasting cases, we find that naive matching remains effective under shared negative conditioning, where both branch errors decrease jointly. When the model's native CFG schema retains privileged information in the teacher's negative branch that is unavailable to the student, however, this joint reduction breaks down and the composed objective induces antagonistic branch-error dynamics, reducing the positive-branch error while increasing the negative-branch error. We term this failure mode Negative Branch Asymmetry (NBA). To address NBA, we introduce Positive--Direction Matching (PDM), a branch-aware OPD objective that separately constrains the positive prediction and the CFG conditional direction. We apply PDM to dense-to-sparse video control, where naive guided matching is highly sensitive to inference guidance scales, while branch-aware supervision enables more robust and effective knowledge transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Explaining GAND: A Resource on Gender-Ambiguous Natural Data & Contrastive Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22546)
*Janiça Hackenbuchner,Jasper Degraeuwe,Arda Tezcan,Joke Daems*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布了GAND数据集，用于评估机器翻译系统在性别暧昧情境下的性别翻译表现，并通过解释性实验分析了上下文对性别决策的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机器翻译系统在性别翻译上存在偏见，会基于默认行为和刻板印象产生错误翻译，对用户造成伤害。现有的评估资源缺乏自然且具性别歧义的场景，难以分析系统如何在性别信息模糊时做出决策。

Method: 作者构建了GAND数据集，包含了一组英语语句，专门设计以包含性别歧义。利用该数据集，将一部分句子翻译为语法有性别的两种语言，并添加了对比性的人工作品。随后，作者通过特征归因分析，识别出源句上下文中影响目标语言性别决策的关键信息。

Result: 通过GAND数据集和分析，确认了上下文中的某些词汇对机器翻译性别决策有显著作用。提供的数据和工具有助于揭示并缓解现有MT系统的性别偏见。

Conclusion: GAND为分析和解释MT中的性别歧义问题提供了新的基准资源和分析方法，有助于开发更公平、更敏感于性别表达的翻译系统。

Abstract: Machine translation (MT) systems continue to produce gender-biased translations. In a time where self-expression is paramount, mistranslations based on default behaviour and stereotyping can lead to harm for users of these systems. To better understand how these systems translate gender in the absence of clear gender cues, we need benchmarking resources that reflect gender-ambiguous scenarios in a natural way. To this end, we present GAND, a gender-ambiguous natural data benchmarking resource for MT consisting of English source sentences, specifically designed to analyse the influence of contextual cues on gender in translation. We leverage GAND to conduct an interpretability analysis: we translate a subset of GAND into two grammatical gender languages and extend these with manually crafted contrastive translations. A following feature attribution analysis reveals source words in context that inform the gender translation of an ambiguous referent entity in the target translation.

</details>


### [189] [MioFFAn: an Annotation Software for Formula Formalization with LLM Automation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22552)
*Nicolas Sibuet,Horacio Saggion,Riccardo Rossi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MioFFAn，一个开源且以文档为中心、可定制的框架，用于科学文献中数学公式向可执行符号代码自动化转化（公式形式化）的快速标注。它兼容不同科学领域，并融合LLM进行部分自动化，通过标准化流程提升效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前科学文献中数学表达式到可执行符号代码的自动转化面临高质量、针对技术科学领域的标注数据集极其稀缺的问题，极大限制了相关自动化和人工智能方法的发展。

Method: 在MioGatto架构基础上，MioFFAn扩展了若干功能，支持选中感兴趣公式和辅助生成符号代码，支持用户自定义符号属性和运算符，适配不同科研领域。同时引入LLM辅助自动化，设计了可迭代、模块化的自动子任务及严格输出格式，便于自动化策略的评估和优化。

Result: 实现了MioFFAn系统，并用现有自动化流程进行了初步实证评估，显示人机协作（human-in-the-loop）模式在本任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: MioFFAn提升了面向科学领域的公式形式化标准化、自动化能力，为相关研究者提供了强大定制和扩展工具，有助于推动数学表达自动化理解与应用。

Abstract: The automatic translation of mathematical expressions in scientific literature into executable symbolic code (a process we refer to as Formula Formalization) is hindered by a severe scarcity of high-quality, ground-truth datasets specialized for technical scientific domains. In this paper, we present MioFFAn, an open-source, document-centric, and customizable framework designed to facilitate rapid annotation for this task. Building upon the MioGatto architecture, we extend existing features to overcome structural limitations and pivot its scope by introducing specific functionalities for Formula Formalization, such as selection of equations of interest and aided symbolic code specification. By allowing users to configure custom taxonomies and properties for identified symbols, and compatible symbolic operators, we ensure the framework is adaptable to diverse specialized scientific fields. Furthermore, MioFFAn is designed to incorporate partial automation via Large Language Models. By defining a modular set of automated sub-tasks with strict output formats, we enable researchers to iteratively refine automation capabilities and evaluate competing strategies using standard NLP metrics. We specify the current automation methodology and perform a preliminary evaluation that demonstrates to efficacy of this human-in-the-loop approach.

</details>


### [190] [Evaluating the Impact of Reviewer Guideline Design on LLM-Based Automated Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22553)
*Haowen Li,Yoichi Ishibashi,Masafumi Oyamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文对自动化同行评审中不同类型评审标准的影响进行了分析，发现官方会议标准效果最佳，而过于严格的评分准则可能适得其反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科学研究中同行评审任务量的增加，自动化评审变得越来越有必要。本研究关注如何通过不同类型的评审标准提升自动化评审的表现。

Method: 比较了官方会议评审标准和使用LLM从高质量人类评审中提取出的模仿型评审准则在自动化同行评审中的效果，并考察了强制性细则化评分和主观整体评分的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，官方会议评审标准产生的自动评审结果与人类判断最一致，模仿型标准效果较差；强制严谨的细则化评分会持续降低自动评审的表现。

Conclusion: 官方会议实践中完善的评审标准对自动化评审极具指导意义，自动评审系统应避免过度细化评分，保留一定主观性和整体性。

Abstract: Peer review is an essential process in scientific research, yet the growing workload has made its automation increasingly necessary. In this study, we analyze how different types of reviewer guidelines, such as official conference guidelines and reviewer-imitating ones generated from high-quality human reviews using LLMs, affect automated peer review. Our experiments show that official conference guidelines produce review results most consistent with human judgments, suggesting that evaluation criteria refined through conference practice serve as effective guidance for automated reviewing as well. In contrast, reviewer-imitating guidelines were generally less effective than official conference guidelines. Furthermore, enforcing strict rubric-style scoring consistently degraded performance, highlighting the importance of allowing subjective and holistic scoring.

</details>


### [191] [Learning When to Reason for Text-to-SQL via SFT and DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22622)
*Soohyuk Jang,Jiheum Yeom,Nohil Park,Sang Hun Kim,Yoonyoung Choi,Kiwook Bae,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoThinkSQL框架，实现根据查询复杂度动态选择是否进行推理，在提升Text-to-SQL性能的同时降低推理时间与生成长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL方法多采用Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理机制，虽提高了处理复杂查询的性能，但推理耗时高，而现实场景中大量查询相对简单，强制多步推理造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出AutoThinkSQL，将自动思考机制结合到有监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）中，模型自动判别查询复杂度，简单查询无需推理，复杂查询才调用CoT推理。

Result: 在Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B模型及Spider、BIRD数据集上，AutoThinkSQL相比最佳基线方法获得一致性能提升，平均生成token数减少24.6%和18.3%，推理延迟分别降低17.1%和11.5%。

Conclusion: AutoThinkSQL能有效根据难度动态选择推理方式，既保证准确率，又提高效率，适合实际使用场景。

Abstract: Recent Text-to-SQL methods rely heavily on reasoning-centric paradigms such as Chain-of-Thought (CoT), achieving substantial gains on complex benchmarks at the cost of high inference-time overhead. However, a large fraction of real-world queries are simple lookups or aggregations that can be resolved without multi-step deduction, making forced reasoning wasteful. Thus, we propose AutoThinkSQL, a framework that integrates an auto-thinking mechanism into both Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) on Text-to-SQL. Our approach enables the model to dynamically bypass reasoning for simple queries while invoking deep CoT for complex queries. On Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B, our method achieves consistent gains compared to the best counterpart baseline on both Spider and BIRD benchmarks while simultaneously reducing average output tokens by 24.6% and 18.3%, and average latency by 17.1% and 11.5% compared to CoT-only generation. Further analysis indicates that the model learns to align its reasoning decisions with query difficulty.

</details>


### [192] [Between Suppression and Collapse: Evaluating Narrative Unlearning with LENS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22657)
*Viktoriia Makovska,George Fletcher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的评估协议LENS，用于测试大语言模型在去除特定叙事时的效果，并引入了新的评估指标SCE，在多种主流模型上验证了去除叙事的有效性和副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型可能无意中再现带有虚假信息色彩的叙事框架，因此亟需评估和提升机器去除（unlearning）虚假叙事的能力。

Method: 提出LENS评估协议，从不同的抵抗级别（直接、归因、对比、抽象）测试目标叙事的再现，同时引入SCE分数综合评价抑制成效与模型输出质量，在四个12B级多语言模型上进行实验。

Result: 经过筛选的模型检查点能有效降低对目标叙事的再现，并且抑制能力可转移到更广泛的提示；但也观察到‘实体恢复’副作用，即抽象提示下模型仍可恢复涉事真实角色。

Conclusion: LENS作为诊断协议能够系统反映和引导关于大语言模型去除特定叙事结构的研究，其评估思路和标准为后续深化叙事unlearning相关工作提供了依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can reproduce disinformation-aligned narrative frames as plausible explanations, raising the question of whether existing machine-unlearning algorithms can suppress this behavior. We introduce Level-based Evaluation of Narrative Suppression (LENS), a contextualization based evaluation protocol for testing target narrative reproduction across direct, attributed, contrastive, and abstract resistance levels. We evaluate two source-grounded narratives: one framing Russia's war against Ukraine as forced by NATO expansion, and one framing the United States as exploiting or abandoning Taiwan. The experiments cover four near-12B multilingual instruction models: Lapa LLM, Gemma-12B, Qwen-14B, and TAIDE-Gemma.
  We introduce the Suppression-Collapse Efficiency (SCE) score as a checkpoint selection summary that rewards target-narrative suppression while penalizing degraded outputs. Our results shows that selected checkpoints can reduce narrative reproduction and suppression may transfer beyond direct forget prompts. We also report entity recovery as a separate side effect: abstract A/B/C prompts can cause models to recover the real-world actors associated with the target frame after unlearning. These findings demonstrate that LENS is a successful diagnostic protocol for both reporting and guiding the further study of the deeper structure of narrative unlearning.

</details>


### [193] [PatiGonit22K: A Comprehensive Dataset for Solving Complex Bengali MWPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22859)
*Swastika Kundu,Azizul Hakim Fayaz,Tashreef Muhammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发布了22,441道孟加拉语数学文本题新数据集PatiGonit22K，大幅提升了该语言领域的题目数量和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管主流语言在数学文本题理解方面取得进展，低资源语言如孟加拉语因为缺乏充足的带注释的大规模数据集而研究受限。

Method: 作者扩展了原有PatiGonit数据集，新增大量复杂的数学题目，覆盖简单和多步运算题目。所有题目经过翻译、注释、本地化和校验，确保语言和数学的准确性。

Result: 数据集包含超过2万道题，内容难度跨度大，更能全面衡量模型在孟加拉语下的数学推理能力。

Conclusion: PatiGonit22K为低资源语言的数学推理和教育NLP研究提供了规模更大、题型更全的新基准，有助于推动相关领域进步。

Abstract: Mathematical Word Problems (MWPs) are an important benchmark for evaluating natural language understanding and quantitative reasoning. Despite recent progress in high resource languages, Bengali remains underexplored due to the limited availability of large scale annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce PatiGonit22K, an expanded Bengali MWP dataset containing 22,441 problems, developed by extending the original PatiGonit dataset with a substantially larger collection of complex mathematical problems. The dataset includes both simple and multi operation equations, providing a balanced benchmark for evaluating mathematical reasoning across different difficulty levels. Each problem is carefully translated, annotated, culturally adapted, and verified to ensure linguistic consistency and mathematical correctness. By increasing both the scale and complexity of Bengali MWPs, PatiGonit22K provides a more comprehensive resource for future research on mathematical reasoning and educational NLP applications in low resource languages.

</details>


### [194] [CHiPS: Character Histograms and Positional Signals for Lightweight Authorship Attribution in Romanian Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22884)
*Sanda-Maria Avram,George C. Ţurcaş*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHiPS，一种面向罗马尼亚文本的轻量级字符级作者归属方法，通过简单但有效的字符分布与频谱特征，实现无需大模型或复杂预处理的高精度作者识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前复杂的作者归属方法（如需要分词、大模型预训练或使用高阶n-gram等特征）计算/实现成本较高，缺乏对简单可解释字符层证据能力边界的系统了解，因此作者希望探索在严格信息泄漏控制下，仅靠简单透明的字符层特征，能实现多高的归属准确率。

Method: CHiPS包含两个互补指纹：一是CH-SVM，依赖单字符分布的直方图分类器；二是FFT12-LR，针对代表性字符和标点构造二值脉冲序列并提取傅里叶/韦尔奇频谱作为特征。两者预测结果用CHiPS-F安全融合，并可选用只在fold外数据训练的top-5重排序器。方法无需分词、句法分析或预训练语言模型，也避免直接用n≥2的n-gram特征。所有评测均为作者在候选集内的封闭集合任务。

Result: 在ROST语料分组，10位作者任务上（400文件），CHiPS-F准确率0.9310，宏F1为0.9341。在更大、作者数更多的ROSTories-cleaned数据上（19位作者，1248文件），准确率为0.8919，宏F1为0.8708。对照组用2-5元TF-IDF SVM取得满分（1.0），说明方法并非当前准确率最高。

Conclusion: CHiPS展示了结构极为简单、透明、低资源要求的字符级证据，在严格控制下可以取得相当高的作者归属准确率，为理解简单特征边界和实际部署轻量模型提供了启发，但准确率不及最先进的复杂模型。

Abstract: We propose CHiPS, a lightweight character-level authorship attribution method for Romanian texts. All reported experiments are closed-set: the true author is one of the candidate authors in the training data. CHiPS studies two complementary fingerprints of writing style: CH-SVM, a character-histogram classifier based on one-character marginal distributions, and FFT12-LR, a positional-signal classifier that represents selected characters and punctuation classes as impulse trains (binary indicator sequences over character positions) and extracts Fourier/Welch spectral descriptors. We also report CHiPS-F, a leakage-safe decision-level fusion variant, and an optional top-5 listwise reranker trained only on out-of-fold predictions. The method requires no tokenization, syntactic analysis, pretrained language model, or transformer fine-tuning, and it avoids character $n$-gram features with $n \geq 2$ in the histogram component. On a locked grouped ROST split comprising 400 files from 392 source-text groups, written by 10 authors, with source-text-level evaluation and grouped five-fold model selection, CHiPS-F reaches 0.9310 accuracy and 0.9341 macro-F1. A matched but unrestricted character 2--5-gram TF--IDF SVM comparator reaches 1.0000 accuracy and macro-F1 on the same held-out groups, so the contribution is not a claim of best possible classification accuracy. Instead, the experiments ask how far restricted, transparent character evidence can go under strict leakage control. On ROSTories-cleaned, a secondary ROST-overlapping corpus comprising 1,248 files from 1,240 source-text groups, written by 19 authors, the same protocol gives 0.8919 accuracy and 0.8708 macro-F1 for CHiPS-R.

</details>


### [195] [Simple Language Normalization Wins: Cross-Lingual Speaker Verification for the TidyVoice 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22923)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文面对跨语言语音验证任务，探讨了简单的语言归一化方法在嵌入空间中的有效性，提出通过Nuisance Attribute Projection（NAP）显著降低了错误率，表现接近更复杂方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言不匹配导致说话人验证性能下降。当前复杂系统多，但低复杂度、易实现的语言归一化方法是否有效，尚需探究。

Method: 以SimAM-ResNet34为基线，在VoxBlink2和VoxCeleb2上预训练，并在TidyVoice训练集进行微调。提出通过同一说话人的跨语言差异，估算语言子空间，运用NAP将嵌入投影到该子空间的正交补。最后用AS-Norm余弦评分。

Result: 开发集EER由基线2.97%/2.70%降低到2.18%（分别为普通余弦与AS-Norm），Codabench评测评分8.40，表现优于基线。

Conclusion: 简单的嵌入空间语言归一化（NAP）即能有效提升系统跨语言鲁棒性，可与复杂系统媲美，是值得关注的实用方法。

Abstract: Cross-lingual mismatch remains a key source of overall degradation in modern speaker verification. The TidyVoice2026 Challenge targets this setting with text-independent verification, comprising 3,666 training and 808 development speakers in 40 languages and 2,200 evaluation speakers in 38 unseen languages, without language labels at test time. Starting from the official SimAM-ResNet34 baseline pretrained on VoxBlink2 and VoxCeleb2 and fine-tuned on TidyVoice, we revisit Nuisance Attribute Projection (NAP) as a simple language-normalization step in the embedding space. We estimate a compact language subspace from cross-language same-speaker differences and project embeddings onto its orthogonal complement before cosine scoring with Adaptive Symmetric score normalization. This reduces development EER from 2.97\% with cosine and 2.70\% with AS-Norm to 2.18\% and yields a Codabench evaluation score of 8.40, showing that simple back-end language normalization can rival more complex systems.

</details>


### [196] [Not All LLM Reasoning is Visible in the Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22925)
*Vatsal Baherwani,Tom Goldstein,Ashwinee Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现大语言模型可能通过无关填充符号实现不可见的推理，使得部分推理过程不体现在输出内容中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全领域关心大语言模型是否会把全部推理过程都显性地表达在输出中。如果模型有隐藏推理路径，则监控与安全评估会受限，存在风险。

Method: 作者设计了三类合成推理任务，在13个前沿大模型上测试是否通过插入语义无关的填充符号（filler tokens）提升模型表现，并对比不同模型和token选择效果。

Result: 多个大模型在填充符号参与下任务准确率提升最高达13个百分点。这种效应取决于具体token及模型。比如Claude Opus 4.5可在不影响主要任务表现下满足隐藏的模运算约束，显示隐藏推理目标的存在。Qwen3-235B在强化学习后对填充符号内容表现出强烈偏好，但强化学习与监督微调并未让填充符号益处持续到测试阶段。

Conclusion: 最新的大语言模型已经能进行无输出可解释踪迹的重要计算，表明推理过程有可能不可被直接输出监控检测，给AI安全性带来新的挑战。

Abstract: A key question for AI safety is whether a language model expresses all of its reasoning in its output tokens. We demonstrate a concrete failure mode where frontier models exhibit invisible reasoning by leveraging semantically irrelevant filler tokens to improve performance on synthetic reasoning tasks. We evaluate 13 frontier language models across three tasks and find that many models benefit significantly from filler tokens, with accuracy improvements of up to 13 percentage points. The benefit depends on which tokens are used and differs across models. We further show that filler tokens enable Claude Opus 4.5 to satisfy a hidden modular arithmetic constraint without sacrificing accuracy on its primary task, demonstrating that invisible reasoning can serve objectives entirely invisible to CoT monitoring. Reinforcement learning gives Qwen3-235B strong preferences over filler token content, but neither RL nor supervised fine-tuning produces a filler token benefit that persists at test time. Our results indicate that frontier models already perform consequential computation with no interpretable trace in their output tokens.

</details>


### [197] [Toward Automated Detection of Documentation Inconsistencies in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22954)
*Jian Lu,Panyu Chen,Miriam Treggiari,Robert Blessing,Danyang Zhuo,Chunhua Weng,William W. Stead,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出并评估了基于大语言模型的出院小结内部文档一致性检测方法，发现模型能够揭示多类不一致，但在时序推理与深入医学知识上存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中的出院小结存在大量内部信息不一致，影响临床推理与患者安全。随着大语言模型的兴起，评估其用于自动检测这些不一致的有效性具有重要实际意义。

Method: 作者设计了两阶段LLM管线，先由Gemini 2.5 Pro自由识别候选不一致，再通过Gemini 2.5 Flash结合上下文验证，应用于MIMIC-IV-Note的3000篇出院小结，部分结果由临床专家人工审查。

Result: 管线发现3460个候选不一致，涉及69.7%的入院。类型涵盖人口统计、过敏、手术、诊断、实验室、用药和护理计划等，与临床推理或患者安全直接相关。专家审查进一步揭示模型在时序推理、动态诊断及门诊处方知识上的不足。

Conclusion: 研究初步建立了规模化EHR一致性分析的方法与概念框架，并提出了按矛盾强度分级的本体与标注体系，为后续大规模验证性研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Objective: To characterize the kinds of internal documentation inconsistencies a general-domain large language model (LLM) can surface from real-world discharge summaries, and to identify recurring failure modes that limit reliability at scale.
  Materials and Methods: We applied a two-stage LLM pipeline---open-ended candidate identification (Gemini 2.5 Pro) followed by context-grounded verification (Gemini 2.5 Flash)---to 3,000 randomly sampled MIMIC-IV-Note discharge summaries. A subset of the pipeline output was then reviewed manually by clinical experts.
  Results: Our pipeline surfaced 3,460 candidate inconsistencies, affecting 69.7% of admissions. Representative examples spanned demographics, allergies, procedures, diagnoses, laboratory, medications, and care-planning domains, with direct implications for clinical reasoning or patient safety. Expert review also revealed recurring failure modes that arise when verification requires temporal reasoning, evolving-diagnosis context, or knowledge of outpatient-prescribing conventions the model does not natively possess.
  Discussion: Detection is highly context-dependent: many flagged pairs require anchoring each statement to its source section and clinical domain, then assessing whether the conflict reflects a true contradiction or missing context. We propose a graded ontology spanning strict contradiction and ambiguity, with a schema characterizing each flagged case by category, section, domain, and inconsistency axis.
  Conclusion: This formative study establishes a methodological foundation and conceptual framework to guide subsequent validated, large-scale EHR-inconsistency analysis.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Direct Answering: Aligning Educational LLMs as Socratic Guides via Heuristic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22996)
*Xiaokun Wang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Xiaodong Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HeuristicEdu的两阶段训练管道，将大语言模型Qwen2.5-7B校正为符合苏格拉底式教学（强调引导与启发）的辅导对话系统。通过从真实平台重构的多轮中文对话数据，结合多维启发式奖励与修正，显著提升模型的引导教学效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在教育场景中过于直接地给出答案，未能实现苏格拉底式循循善诱的教学方式。作者旨在让模型能更好地引导学生自发探索与思考，而不仅仅是提供结论。

Method: 首先，作者提出HeuristicEdu管道，分为监督微调（warm-up）和基于Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)的强化学习。训练用797例多轮儿童科学对话，设置基于认知深度、好奇心调动、直接性等多元奖励，并通过K_query矫正学生引入的术语，对模型进行行为对齐。评估指数包括Scaffolding Effectiveness（支架引导有效性）和Conversation Depth（对话深度）。

Result: 在30个未见过的问题测试集上，最佳GRPO策略将SE由30.0%提升到63.3%，泄露关键词概率由30.0%降至13.3%。而一个未对齐的更大基线模型Qwen-72B，SE仅0%，泄露高达96.7%，表明仅靠模型规模无法获得苏格拉底式效果。

Conclusion: HeuristicEdu能够有效提升大语言模型引导式教学能力，减少给出答案的直接性。模型规模并不能取代针对性行为对齐训练；同时，过于明确的反泄露惩罚机制可能反而影响行为调整的效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed in educational settings often behave as direct answerers: they disclose target concepts in the opening turn instead of guiding students through progressive inquiry, as Socratic pedagogy prescribes. We present HeuristicEdu, a two-phase pipeline that aligns Qwen2.5-7B toward Socratic tutoring via supervised warm-up and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Training uses SocraticEdu, 797 multi-turn Chinese children's science dialogues reconstructed from a live platform, with a heuristic reward over cognitive depth (R_cog), curiosity engagement (R_eng), and directness (R_dir), together with a K_query correction for student-introduced terms. We introduce Scaffolding Effectiveness (SE) and Conversation Depth (CD) to evaluate outcomes beyond surface fluency. On 30 held-out questions, the best GRPO variant improves SE from 30.0% to 63.3% and lowers keyword leakage from 30.0% to 13.3%. Notably, this best variant omits the directness penalty during optimization, suggesting that explicit anti-leakage terms can conflict with gradient-based behavioral alignment. An unaligned Qwen-72B baseline reaches 0% SE and 96.7% leakage, showing that scale alone does not induce Socratic behavior.

</details>


### [199] [Speech Signals Complement LLMs for Predicting Interpersonal Attraction in Speed Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23037)
*Yuriko Kikuchi,Takato Hayashi,Ryusei Kimura,Naoya Inoue,Ryo Ishii,Shogo Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了大语言模型（LLM）仅用对话文本与结合语音特征（speech predictor）预测两人吸引力（attraction）的效果。结果发现语音特征能补充LLM的预测，但补充性并非普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM能基于对话文本预测人际吸引力，但尚不清楚加入语音特征能否、以及在何种情景下产生额外预测价值。

Method: 利用日本相亲对话数据，将仅基于转录文本的LLM预测与监督训练的语音预测器结合，评估它们对参与者吸引力打分的预测能力。分析两种融合方式下对吸引力排序和相关性指标的变化。

Result: 结合语音特征和LLM预测在所有评估条件下提升了配对排序的准确率。但在每位参与者的Pearson相关分析上，提升效果不稳定，经过多重校正后无统计显著提升。这些提升主要出现在语音预测本身更准确的参与者中。

Conclusion: 语音特征在部分情况下能够增加只用文本LLM的预测力，但其作用具有条件性而非普适性。未来研究应关注语音特征补充作用出现的具体场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can predict interpersonal attraction from conversation transcripts, but it remains unclear what a speech predictor can add beyond transcript-only LLM prediction. Using Japanese speed-dating conversations, we combine predictions from a transcript-only LLM and a supervised speech predictor to estimate participants' reported liking of their partners. We show that speech can complement transcript-only LLM prediction, but that this complementarity is conditional rather than universal. Combining the two predictions significantly improves pairwise ranking accuracy over the transcript-only LLM alone in all evaluated conditions. By contrast, gains in per-participant Pearson $r$ vary across conversation rounds and rating directions, with none significant after correction. Retrospectively, these $r$ gains are concentrated among participants for whom the speech predictor is more accurate. Speech can therefore retain predictive value even when an LLM predicts attraction from transcripts. The relevant question is not simply whether speech helps, but where its complementarity emerges.

</details>


### [200] [ADAGE: A Language-Agnostic Pipeline for Analogical Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23058)
*Ahmed Haj Ahmed,Alvin Grissom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADAGE，一种不依赖英译、面向多语言类比推理的基准构建流程，并在阿拉伯语、阿姆哈拉语、日语三种语言上验证，发现主流大模型在这些语言的推理表现远逊于英语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语种推理评测方法主要依赖英文基准的翻译，这容易引入语言偏差且无法真实反映跨文化推理能力。为解决这一问题，需要一种能原生构建多语种推理任务的新方法。

Method: 设计并实现了ADAGE，一个结合母语者参与和大模型辅助生成的新流程，用于直接构造无需翻译的类比推理基准题。流程首先由母语者设定题干，随后借助大模型生成难题，并进行人工筛选和难度调整。

Result: 在阿拉伯语、阿姆哈拉语和日语的真实场景下，ADAGE构建了各自的推理基准，并对14个开源大模型进行了评测。结果显示：即便是英文推理表现优秀的大模型，在这些原生多语言推理任务上准确率显著下降（相较英语下降12-52个百分点）。

Conclusion: 现有大模型在多语言文化类比推理方面存在显著差距，仅依赖翻译无法真实反映其推理能力。ADAGE为未来多语种推理评测提供了更可靠的工具和数据集。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning evaluation overwhelmingly relies on translating English benchmarks, a practice that introduces linguistic artifacts and fails to test culturally-grounded reasoning. We introduce ADAGE (Analogical Difficulty-by-design Assessment for Grounded Evaluation), a language-agnostic pipeline that combines native-speaker curation with LLM-assisted generation to construct challenging, translation-free benchmarks for abstract analogical reasoning. We validate ADAGE by constructing benchmarks for Arabic, Amharic, and Japanese. Evaluating 14 open-weight models, we find a consistent cultural reasoning gap: models that perform well on English proverb reasoning struggle substantially on all three native benchmarks, with accuracy dropping by 12--52 percentage points relative to English. We release the pipeline, all three benchmarks, and the full evaluation suite.

</details>


### [201] [Attention-Guided Layer Selection for Contrastive Decoding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23067)
*Yusuke Sakai,Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn,Kiyoaki Shirai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用自注意力结构信息指导层选择的新对比解码方法，该方法在TruthfulQA任务上提升了大模型事实性表现，优于原有DoLa方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对比解码方法如DoLa，动态层选择只依赖输出词汇分布的差异，未充分利用模型内部结构信息。作者认为内部自注意力机制包含更丰富的结构信号，有可能更有效辨别事实性。

Method: 提出三种基于自注意力机制的信息来进行动态层选择的策略：Attention-JSD、Attention-Entropy-Max和Attention-Entropy-Min。这些策略通过对层级内自注意力分布的度量进行评分，来替代原来仅对输出词汇概率分布的对比方式。

Result: 在TruthfulQA数据集上，基于注意力信息的方法显著提高了模型的多答案准确率，特别是Attention-JSD和Attention-Entropy-Min策略，在MC2和MC3指标上表现突出，超过原始DoLa。

Conclusion: 利用自注意力结构分布作为层选择信号比传统基于输出分布的方法更敏感、更有效，有助于提升大语言模型在事实性问题上的表现。

Abstract: Contrastive decoding methods such as DoLa improve the factuality of Large Language Models (LLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of mature and premature layers. However, DoLa's dynamic layer selection relies solely on divergences in output vocabulary distributions. In this work, we propose three attention-guided strategies: Attention-JSD, Attention-Entropy-Max, and Attention-Entropy-Min, which leverage structural information carried by internal self-attention mechanisms as a signal for layer selection. Experimental results on TruthfulQA demonstrate that our strategies, particularly Attention-JSD and Attention-Entropy-Min, consistently outperform the original DoLa. We observe significant gains on multi-answer metrics (MC2 and MC3), suggesting that attention distributions can provide a more sensitive signal for resolving factual knowledge than output vocabulary distributions.

</details>


### [202] [LoRA for Gender-Inclusive Rewriting and Activation Steering for Counter-Narrative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23083)
*Akhil Rajeev P,Manoj Balaji J*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IHLC系统，用于生成性别包容性语言和反叙事文本，并在LT-EDI 2026竞赛中获得了较高分数。核心创新在于不用修改模型权重，仅通过推理时的表示工程来实现行为引导，有效提升了生成结果的包容性和得体性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型在生成文本时常带有性别偏见，无法满足社会对包容性语言的需求。现有方法多依赖模型权重的重新训练或微调，计算消耗大，且灵活性差。作者旨在开发一种高效、无需大规模参数更新的包容性文本生成方法。

Method: （1）性别包容性重写任务采用低秩适应（LoRA）微调方法，提升生成包容性文本的能力；（2）反叙事任务创新性地提出推理时激活引导：通过主成分分析（PCA）从隐藏状态的对比激活中提取主要方向，将其注入Gemma-3-4B-it模型推理过程，实现无需权重更新的包容性引导，并结合受限提示提升生成质量。
