Result: IHLC系统在LT-EDI 2026官方评测中，性别包容性重写得分80.00%，反叙事生成得分78.12%。人工分析发现该激活引导方法在控制模型行为上具有可行性，但也存在如语义偏移、残余偏见、过度引导、文本退化等典型失效模式。

Conclusion: 推理时基于激活引导的表征工程为社会可控、包容性文本生成提供了计算高效的新方案。该方法无需参数更新，初步验证了其实用性，同时也暴露出部分局限性。未来需进一步优化激活引导策略，以提升生成质量和一致性。

Abstract: Gender-inclusive language generation seeks to transform biased text into inclusive alternatives while preserving semantic meaning and contextual coherence. This paper presents the IHLC system for the LT-EDI 2026 Shared Task, addressing both gender-inclusive rewriting and counter-narrative generation. For gender-inclusive rewriting, we employ parameter-efficient Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning, achieving an official score of 80.00%. Our primary contribution is a compute-efficient inference-time representation engineering approach for counter-narrative generation. We derive a principal steering direction from contrastive hidden-state activations using principal component analysis (PCA) and inject it into the intermediate representations of Gemma-3-4B-it during inference, enabling behavioral steering toward inclusive responses without modifying model weights. Combined with constrained prompting, this approach produces polite and contextually appropriate counter-narratives, achieving an official score of 78.12%. We further present a manual analysis of steering behavior, identifying key failure modes including semantic drift, residual bias leakage, layer sensitivity, over-steering, and text degeneration. Our findings highlight both the practical potential and current limitations of activation steering as a lightweight alternative to parameter updates for controllable and socially aligned language generation.

</details>


### [203] [Interview with Kalle Lyytinen on "Implications of Theories of Language for Information Systems"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23142)
*Kalle J. Lyytinen,Pierre Maier,Paul Ackah Toffey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是对Lyytinen在信息系统领域语言理论影响的重要文献的回顾与反思，并探讨了当前大语言模型与生成式AI背景下信息系统的语言本质及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思40年前发表于MIS Quarterly的经典论文，讨论其对信息系统领域的深远影响，并结合大语言模型、生成式AI等新趋势，重新审视信息系统的语言本质问题。

Method: 通过访谈的方式，由Lyytinen本人回顾原文的起源，结合近年科技发展讨论信息系统中的语言核心，提出未来研究方向。

Result: 明确指出信息系统研究受语言理论影响深远，在大模型和AI发展下，关注IS研究中语言问题的重要性持续上升，提出继续沿用或拓展语言视角的新研究议题。

Conclusion: 信息系统领域应持续关注语言理论，重视生成式AI等技术带来的新挑战和机遇，未来的研究需要从语言视角开拓新的理论和实践路径。

Abstract: Over fourty years after the initial publication of "Implications of Theories of Language for Information Systems" in MIS Quaterly, Lyytinen reflects about the origins of his publication and the developments in this area of research over the past decades. In the here presented interview, Lyytinen discusses the linguistic core of information systems also in light of recent trends and developments in the field, especially with regards to large language models and generative AI. Future research directions following a linguistic perspective on Information Systems (IS) research are outlined.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond a Global Norm: Personalizing Toxicity Sensitivity in Language Models Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23175)
*Rares A. C. Diaconescu,Iulia Slanina,Alina Florea,Andrei B. Trache,Miruna E. Coroi,Anne Arzberger,Jie Yang,Enrico Liscio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了如何在不进行额外训练的情况下，使自然语言生成系统根据用户特定的毒性敏感度进行调整。通过实验发现，虽然多种干预方法能减少毒性对齐误差，但在效果、个性化和语言质量之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的毒性检测方法往往把毒性作为全局对齐问题来处理，但实际上，不同用户对有害语言的敏感度不同，因此需要更个性化的方法。

Method: 作者首次对三种无训练（training-free）推理时干预阶段的方法进行了比较：解码前（通过优化和重写提示）、解码中（对token、logit和语义表示进行引导）、解码后（重新排序候选文本）。评估基于PRISM数据集中用户毒性敏感度目标。

Result: 所有方法均能将毒性对齐误差降低28-47%。但实验还发现，方法在对齐效果、满足个性化需求和整体语言质量间存在本质上的多目标权衡。

Conclusion: 对齐自然语言生成系统的毒性敏感度是一个多目标优化问题。无训练的多种干预方法虽能有效减少误差，但需在实际应用中平衡个性化和文本质量。

Abstract: Reducing toxicity is often framed as a global alignment problem, yet perceptions of harmful language are subjective and context-dependent. We present the first comparative evaluation of training-free methods for aligning language generation to user-specific toxicity sensitivities across three inference-time intervention stages: pre-decoding (prompt conditioning and rewriting), in-decoding (token, logit, and representation steering), and post-decoding (candidate re-ranking). Evaluated against toxicity sensitivity targets derived from the PRISM dataset, all methods reduce alignment error by 28-47%. However, the results reveal a fundamental trade-off between alignment effectiveness, personalization, and general language quality, showing how toxicity sensitivity alignment is an inherently multi-objective problem.

</details>


### [205] [IndicTalk: A Large-Scale Persona-Based Multilingual Conversational Corpus for Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23242)
*Sahil Deepak Gawande,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IndicTalk，这是目前最大的多语种印度混合语言对话语料库，覆盖9种印度语言的18种变体，共有超过132万轮对话，将为多语种会话AI的研究提供宝贵资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度地区大量人群在日常对话中会在本地语言和英语之间自然切换（即代码混合），且常用本地文字和罗马拼音两种书写方式。然而目前针对印度多语种代码混合的高质量对话数据极度稀缺，这限制了多语种会话AI的发展。

Method: 构建了一条全自动数据生成流程，结合真实新闻语境、具有人格设定的多语种LLM对话生成、自动质量校验，生成大规模、事件驱动、多轮的代码混合对话语料，涉及多种本地文字和罗马拼音两种脚本形式。

Result: 通过语言学、自动和人工多重评价证明，IndicTalk生成的多语种代码混合对话内容流畅、连贯，并且自然，无论是本地语脚本还是罗马拼音形式。

Conclusion: IndicTalk语料库的发布将支持相关印度多语种会话AI的开发和评测，填补了现有资源的空白。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed conversational AI, yet high-quality multilingual code-mixed dialogue resources remain scarce, particularly for Indic languages where speakers naturally alternate between English and their native language in both native-script and Romanized forms. We present IndicTalk, one of the largest multilingual Indic code-mixed conversational corpora, comprising over 13,28,604 event-grounded multi-turn conversations across 18 language varieties covering 9 Indic languages. The corpus is generated through a fully automated pipeline that combines real-world news grounding, persona-conditioned dialogue generation using multilingual LLMs, and automatic quality validation. Extensive linguistic, automatic, and human evaluations demonstrate that IndicTalk produces fluent, coherent, and naturally code-mixed conversations across both script variants. We will release IndicTalk to support the development and evaluation of multilingual conversational AI for underrepresented Indic languages. The dataset is available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingoIITGN/IndicTalk .

</details>


### [206] [Co-Evolving Graph and Text Memory for Training-Free Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23278)
*Hieu Man,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Co-E系统，通过同步的双向图文工作记忆机制，提升了多跳问答任务中的推理能力，并在多个基准测试上优于现有的无训练基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答任务需要综合关系型和文本型证据，而单一的数据源（仅文本或仅知识图谱）无法满足。现有方法大多只关注部分推理环节，缺乏高效的图文信息协调机制。

Method: 提出了Co-E系统，不需要训练，采用同步的双向图-文本工作记忆。该系统中的同步循环会整合文本记忆、将文本提取为三元组加入图谱记忆、并将图谱信息反馈到文本生成场景，保证两种记忆协同进化，有效辅助多步检索与生成。

Result: 在六个多跳问答基准上评估，Co-E在无训练开放骨干系统中表现优越，有竞争力地接近更大规模或有训练的系统性能。

Conclusion: Co-E展示了图文同步记忆机制在多跳问答任务中的有效性，实现了无需额外训练条件下的高水平推理，扩展了多模式记忆交互在自然语言推理中的应用范围。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires coordinating relational and textual evidence across reasoning steps, a combination neither a text corpus nor a knowledge graph can supply alone. Prior work often emphasizes only part of this loop: graph-augmented RAG retrieves from a pre-built or query-updated graph, KGQA systems search within topic-centered subgraphs, and memory-augmented agents maintain evolving memories without continuously reconciling graph memory with textual context. We propose Co-E, a training-free system built around synchronized bidirectional graph-text working memory. A synchronization cycle consolidates textual memory, extracts relational triples into graph memory, and injects graph facts back into the generation context. Because both memories are maintained, they shape subsequent retrieval and generation. Evaluated on six multi-hop QA benchmarks, Co-E improves over comparable training-free open-backbone baselines and is competitive with larger or trained systems.

</details>


### [207] [BHARATI: Morphology-Aware Tokenizers for Classical Indian Languages with Subword Fertility Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23319)
*Poornima Kumaresan,Pavithra Muruganantham,Lakshmi Rajendran,Santhosh Sivasubramani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了BHARATI，一套专为印度各语种（包括梵语、泰米尔语等）设计的SentencePiece BPE子词分词器，显著提高了经典印度语言的分词效率，相关模型和数据工具开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流子词分词算法（如BPE、SentencePiece）主要针对现代语言，应用于古典、南印度等有粘着形态、音变规则和特殊专业词汇的语言时分词效果不佳，导致上下文长度浪费和效率低下。旨在构建更适用于这些语言的分词器。

Method: 作者基于横跨英语、印地语、梵语、泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语和马拉雅拉姆语的781MB语料，逐步训练和完善分词器，支持各自的本地文字，并提出三版（v1-v3）。通过子词繁殖率、专业词单词长度等指标进行评价，并与GPT-2和多语SentencePiece基线模型进行比较。

Result: 新分词器v3在印度知识体系专业术语测试集上，每个词平均分为2.6个子词，远优于GPT-2（5.25）和多语SentencePiece（3.75）；可将某些专业词压缩为单一token。v3在7种语言的专业句子测试集上使序列长度比GPT-2和基于字节划分的模型减少90%，比多语mBART-50基线减少约25%。

Conclusion: BHARATI分词器显著提升了对印度各古典与现代语种、专业领域（如IKS）用词的处理效率，大幅缩短模型输入序列长度，提高后续大模型上下文利用率，相关工具完全开源，有助于推动多语种/小语种NLP进步。

Abstract: Standard subword tokenization algorithms such as Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) and SentencePiece are trained predominantly on modern language corpora and produce inefficient segmentations when applied to classical Indian languages. Sanskrit, Tamil, and other classical Indic languages exhibit agglutinative morphology, productive sandhi (phonological fusion at word boundaries), and domain-specific vocabularies absent from general-purpose training data. This paper presents BHARATI, a set of SentencePiece BPE tokenizers trained on a balanced 781 MB corpus spanning seven languages (English, Hindi, Sanskrit, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam) with native script support for all languages. We describe three successive tokenizer versions: v1 (English and Sanskrit only, with broken byte-fallback for Tamil), v2 (four-language support with byte-level fallback for southern languages), and v3 (full seven-language native subword coverage). Subword fertility analysis demonstrates that v3 averages 2.6 tokens per Indian Knowledge System (IKS) technical term, compared to 5.25 tokens per term with GPT-2's tokenizer and 3.75 tokens with the multilingual SentencePiece baseline, with the largest gains on a set of reserved IKS terms that are represented as single tokens by construction. On a held-out test set of 490 IKS-domain sentences (70 per language across seven languages, released with the measurement script), v3 reduces sequence length by roughly 90% relative to GPT-2 and byte-level encoding (which lack native Indic subwords) and by approximately 25% relative to the mBART-50 multilingual baseline, averaged across the six Indic languages, directly translating to increased effective context length for downstream language models. The tokenizer models (32,000 vocabulary), training scripts, and evaluation benchmarks are released under open licenses.

</details>


### [208] [IKS-Instruct: A 24,000-Example Multilingual Dataset for Teaching Language Models Indian Knowledge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23322)
*Shwetha Singaravelu,Gayathri Muruganantham,Lakshmi Rajendran,Santhosh Sivasubramani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出IKS-Instruct数据集，包含24,795条基于印度知识体系的多语言教学指令-回复对，显著拓展了指令微调领域的语言和知识多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令数据集主要聚焦英文和通用知识任务，缺乏专门面向特定教育领域（如印度知识体系）的多语种高质量数据。为此，研究者希望填补此空白，推动大语言模型适应非英语及专业领域的教学需求。

Method: 作者构建了IKS-Instruct数据集，涵盖7种语言（包括印度本地语言和英语）、41种吠陀口述及数学传统教学方法，并与CBSE中学课程标准对齐。数据来源包括经典文献、课程模板、吠陀数学示例、双语指令、多轮对话和跨传统比较。质量评估借助多模型多维评测框架（12项维度）、五人专家外部评分。

Result: IKS-Instruct微调的7B模型在外部五人团评中达中位分6.39，与强性能参考模型（Nemotron-Nano，得分6.54）只差0.15，显著优于未采用IKS数据集的基础模型（其在IKS测试项上得分接近0），而且部署成本远低于参考模型。数据显示，模型质量与数据集策展水平不总是单调增长。

Conclusion: IKS-Instruct显著提升了模型在印度知识体系教育领域的生成能力，支持多语种教育场景，且优化部署成本，将促进此类专业知识内容的AI辅助教学研究。

Abstract: Instruction tuning has become the standard method for adapting large language models to follow human intent, yet existing instruction datasets are dominated by English-language general-knowledge tasks and lack coverage of specialized pedagogical domains. This paper presents IKS-Instruct, a dataset of 24,795 instruction-response pairs for teaching language models to deliver educational content grounded in Indian Knowledge Systems (IKS). The dataset spans seven languages (English, Hindi, Sanskrit, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam), covers 41 pedagogical techniques from the Vedic oral and mathematical traditions, and is aligned with the Central Board of Secondary Education (CBSE) curriculum for classes 6 through 12. The pairs are derived from six source types: classical text corpora (Bhagavad Gita, Thirukkural, Sangam literature, Vedic texts), curriculum-aligned pedagogical templates, Vedic mathematical sutra demonstrations, bilingual instruction pairs, technique-grounded multi-turn dialogues, and cross-tradition comparative analyses. Quality is assessed through a multi-judge evaluation framework in which independent language models score responses on 12 dimensions including technique fidelity, pedagogical quality, factual accuracy, and IKS cultural depth. Under a uniform five-judge external panel (median aggregation over 1,201 stratified items), the strongest IKS-Instruct fine-tune of a compact 7B model reaches a median judge score of 6.39, within 0.15 of a strong general-purpose reference model (Nemotron-Nano at 6.54) at a fraction of its deployment cost, while the base model without IKS fine-tuning scores near zero on the IKS-specific dimensions. Model quality does not increase monotonically with data curation, a result we report together with the corresponding data-quality gains.

</details>


### [209] [BERT-based Models vs. Large Language Models for Low-Resource Named Entity Recognition: A Comparative Study on Marathi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23344)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文针对马拉地语NER任务，将MahaBERT-v2在不同MahaNER数据集变体上进行微调，并与MahaNER基线及主流大模型（如Gemini、LLaMA-3等）对比，结果表明微调后的MahaBERT模型大幅优于通用LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马拉地语等低资源语言缺乏大量高质量标注数据，NER难度大；当前大型语言模型在这种特定任务和语言环境下的有效性尚不明确，亟需探索更优方法。

Method: 本研究微调MahaBERT-v2模型，分别在MahaNER数据集的不同变体上训练，并与主流通用LLMs（Gemini、LLaMA-3.3-70B、Gemma等）及MahaNER基线系统系统性比较，通过标准NLP评测指标（精确率、召回率、F1分数）综合评估。

Result: 微调的MahaBERT系列模型F1得分达到0.88-0.91，优于基线系统（F1=0.8843），也显著超越所有LLM模型（F1在0.57-0.69之间）。

Conclusion: 针对低资源语言NER任务，基于领域数据微调的专用模型（如MahaBERT）仍然比通用大型模型更有效，强调了特定架构在低资源语言处理中的重要性。

Abstract: Named Entity Recognition (NER) for low-resource languages such as Marathi remains a challenging task due to limited annotated resources and linguistic complexity. Although recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of natural language processing tasks, their effectiveness for language-specific NER in low-resource settings remains uncertain. In this study, we fine-tune MahaBERT-v2 on different variants of the MahaNER dataset and systematically compare the performance of these models with an existing MahaNER baseline and prominent general-purpose LLMs, including Gemini, LLaMA-3.3-70B, and Gemma models. All models are evaluated on a Marathi NER test dataset using standard metrics of precision, recall, and F1-score. The experimental results show that the fine-tuned MahaBERT-based models consistently outperform both the baseline and all evaluated LLMs, with the fine-tuned models achieving F1-scores ranging from 0.88 to 0.91, surpassing the existing MahaNER model (0.8843) and significantly exceeding the performance of LLM-based approaches, whose F1-scores range from 0.57 to 0.69. These findings demonstrate that task-specific, language-focused models trained on domain-relevant data remain more effective than general-purpose LLMs for Marathi NER, highlighting the continued importance of specialized architectures for low-resource language processing.

</details>


### [210] [Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-match Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23362)
*Adhiraj Singh,Deepanshu Mody,Ghina Al Shdaifat,Hamza Alshamy,Adam Wiemerslage,Varshini Reddy,Craig W. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出JOLT算法，通过整数规划联合优化子词词表，使其与WordPiece背后的最长匹配贪婪解码规则对齐，并实现了比BPE更优的压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPE等流行子词词表构建方法依赖贪婪启发式，可能导致压缩效果次优。现有研究表明可直接针对推理规则优化词表，但主要面向特定推理而非主流的最长匹配贪婪解码。本文动机是：能否针对这种通用规则设计更优词表？

Method: 提出JOLT算法，将词表学习建模为关于词表选择和分词决策的整数规划问题，并引入贪婪一致性约束确保分词与最长匹配解码一致。为扩展至大规模数据，先求解线性规划松弛，并仅对未解决的部分引入高阶分词变量。

Result: JOLT算法的LP松弛结果几乎是整数解，实际方案与最优下界仅差0.008%-0.176%。理论证明BPE在贪婪最长匹配下已接近最优，JOLT能进一步收敛优化空间89.6%-99.4%。在四种训练规模、32K与64K词表下，JOLT在验证集上比BPE最多减少0.78%的分词数，且训练规模越大优势越明显。

Conclusion: JOLT实现了与推理规则一致的高效词表优化，基本挖掘了BPE剩余的压缩潜力，并能为近最优性提供理论证书。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that subword vocabularies can be trained to optimize compression for a specific inference rule rather than relying on greedy heuristics such as Byte Pair Encoding (BPE). We extend this approach to greedy left-to-right longest-match decoding, the fast and widely used inference rule underlying WordPiece. We introduce Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-Match Tokenization (JOLT), which formulates vocabulary learning as an integer program over vocabulary-selection and segmentation-choice variables. Greedy-consistency constraints ensure that each optimized segmentation exactly matches the segmentation produced by longest-match decoding under the selected vocabulary, aligning the training objective with deployment-time tokenization. To scale the optimization, we solve a linear programming relaxation and selectively introduce higher-order segmentations only for unresolved pretokens. The resulting relaxation is nearly integral: rounded solutions fall within 0.008 - 0.176 % of the LP lower bound on the training scope. The bound also shows that BPE is already within 1 - 2 % of the best achievable compression under greedy longest-match decoding, while JOLT closes 89.6 - 99.4 % of the remaining gap. On held-out validation data across four training scopes and vocabulary sizes of 32,000 and 64,000, JOLT produces up to 0.78 % fewer tokens than BPE, with improvements generally increasing as the training scope grows. These results demonstrate that inference-aligned vocabulary optimization can recover most of the limited compression headroom left by BPE while providing a certificate of near-optimality.

</details>


### [211] [When Activation Oracles Learn Not to Read: Concept-Specific Blind Spots in Fine-Tuned Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23379)
*Tobias Bersia,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了用于解释语言模型内部激活的Activation Oracles（AOs），发现AOs在针对隐藏概念训练时可能反而学成反向解释器，失去对所需信息的读取能力，从而对基于学习的可解释性工具的可靠性提出质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法通过AOs提供对模型内部状态的自然语言解读，但这些工具本身受其训练方式影响，其中立性和可靠性存在疑问。论文旨在揭示、分析并理解AOs在处理内部隐藏信息时的行为偏差和局限。

Method: 作者构建了“禁忌词猜测”实验环境，先让目标语言模型在模型内部使用某一隐藏概念但不能直接泄露该信息，随后训练AOs对其激活进行解读。通过logitlens、层消融等方法分析AOs的读取失效机制。

Result: 实验发现，经过微调的AOs在面对隐藏概念时反而变得“反读取”——无法复原训练过程中一直存在的概念。分析显示，信息仍可被解码，但AOs在最终读出环节出现失效。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了行为泄漏、表征可解码性与AOs可读性之间可能存在分离，基于学习的可解释性接口在可靠性方面存在隐患，需要对AOs的中立性和稳健性进一步审视与改进。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are language models trained to answer natural-language questions about another model's internal activations. They offer a flexible interface for reading hidden information from model states, especially when relevant information is internally represented but absent or incomplete in visible behavior. However, AOs are themselves learned systems: their answers are shaped by training data, objectives, and learned reporting behavior, rather than being neutral readouts of represented information. We study this in a controlled Taboo Word Guessing setting, where subject models are fine-tuned to internally use a hidden concept while avoiding direct disclosure. Contrary to the expectation that an AO trained on such a subject becomes a specialist reader, we find that fine-tuned AOs can become concept-specific anti-readers: they selectively fail to recover the concept persistently present during their own training. This failure is not simply explained by absence of the concept from the subject or oracle representations: the target remains decodable inside the oracle, while LogitLens and layer-ablation analyses indicate that the failure arises in the AO readout pathway. Our results show that behavioral leakage, representation-level decodability, and AO-verbalizability can come apart, raising a reliability concern for learned interpretability interfaces.

</details>


### [212] [LA-RL: Label-Aware Self-Reflection for Reinforcement Learning in Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23420)
*Xiao You,Tianwei Yan,Zixu Shan,Longyu Du,Shan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 LA-RL（Label-Aware Reflective Reinforcement Learning）的新方法，通过引入任务相关的诊断标签，加强大语言模型在信息抽取自我纠错过程中的结构化反馈，有效提升抽取准确性和可泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自省的纠错方法在信息抽取任务中，无法细化区分具体错误类型，对结构化输出的反馈和自我修正不够精确影响效果，因此需要一种更细粒度和任务相关的自我纠错机制。

Method: LA-RL采用单一骨干网络，先进行抽取预测，再生成任务特异的错误诊断标签，根据诊断标签再修正抽取结果。训练分为两阶段：首先用带有诊断标签的数据进行有监督微调，然后通过无过程奖励的两步GRPO强化学习，侧重最终输出质量、格式有效性及首次抽取正确率。

Result: 在命名实体识别、关系抽取和事件抽取三个任务上，LA-RL框架显著优于SFT方法。例如：在SciER关系抽取上提升6.83 F1分数，分布外关系抽取提升约20 F1分数，在中国语料DuEE1.0触发词/论元分别提升14.80/17.50 F1分数。消融实验显示，不同任务对纠错结构需求不同，强约束对关系抽取更有效，实体识别在域外场景只需弱纠错。

Conclusion: LA-RL能够有效结合结构化诊断标签，引导大模型在信息抽取中的自我纠错，显著提升性能和泛化能力；任务对纠错结构的敏感性值得进一步深入研究和定制。

Abstract: Large language models show strong promise for information extraction (IE), but existing reflection-based correction methods are often misaligned with structured extraction outputs. Free-form self-reflection can flag an error, yet it rarely identifies whether the failure is a missing span, wrong label, boundary mismatch, invalid relation type, or reversed argument order. We introduce LA-RL (Label-Aware Reflective Reinforcement Learning), an outcome-supervised framework that guides IE self-correction with task-grounded diagnostic labels. A single backbone first predicts an extraction, diagnoses task-specific error labels, and then revises its output conditioned on the diagnosis. Training starts from diagnostic data labeled by an annotation model for cold-start supervised fine-tuning and proceeds through two GRPO stages that reward final extraction quality, format validity, and first-pass correctness, without a process reward model. Experiments on named entity recognition, relation extraction, and event extraction show consistent same-backbone gains over SFT, including 6.83 average F1 on SciER relation extraction, about 20 F1 on out-of-distribution relation extraction, and 14.80 trigger F1 plus 17.50 argument F1 on DuEE1.0. Ablations show that reflection structure is task-sensitive: stronger constraints benefit relation extraction, whereas named entity recognition needs less restrictive correction under domain shift.

</details>


### [213] [Reasoning or Memorization: Can LLMs Understand and Generate Chinese Xiehouyu Riddles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23440)
*Hai Hu,Siyuan Song,Chongtian Shao,Kejia Zhang,Tianjian Zhu,Xiaojing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了大语言模型（LLM）在中国谐音类语言游戏“歇后语”上的推理与创造能力，发现LLM在记忆和创造新歇后语方面与人类有显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM声称具备语言推理与创新能力，但存在数据污染的争议。歇后语是一种需理解和创造双关语言的复杂测试，因此作者借此检验LLM在中文复杂推理任务中的真实能力。

Method: 1）构造未在训练数据出现过的新歇后语，通过多项选择题（MCQ）、自由生成解释、及新歇后语创作三个环节测试LLM能力。2）通过对比低频与新歇后语的准确率差($Δ_{acc}$)来衡量模型的记忆/推理能力。3）将LLM表现与人类母语者进行对比。

Result: 前沿中文模型对低频与新歇后语的$Δ_{acc}$普遍为23.6%，明显高于人类与英语优先模型，显示其更多依赖记忆。Gemini 3.1 Pro在新歇后语多项选择上的准确率达92.6%，高于人类24%；但LLM生成新歇后语的能力远逊于人类。

Conclusion: LLM在中文歇后语的推理表现中仍有明显数据记忆成分，推理能力的评估需考虑数据污染；同时LLM在此类语言创造性任务上远未超越人类专家。

Abstract: In this paper, we push the boundary of LLM reasoning by testing them in a Chinese language game, xiehouyu, with novel xiehouyu created by linguists that had not existed before to avoid data contamination. We use multiple-choice questions (MCQ), free-form explanation generation, and new xiehouyu creation to evaluate LLMs' ability to understand and create xiehouyu. In MCQ, we use the delta of accuracy ($Δ_{acc}$) between existing but low-frequency xiehouyu and novel ones as an index for memorization. $Δ_{acc}$ for native speakers is very low, suggesting similar processing mechanisms. However, we found that frontier Chinese models have on average a $Δ_{acc}$ of 23.6\%, while English-centric models tested have a mean $Δ_{acc}$ of 5.1\%, suggesting that frontier Chinese models are likely trained with much larger Chinese data, thus memorizing more low-frequency xiehouyu. For novel xiehouyu, Gemini 3.1 Pro demonstrated remarkable ability with acc 92.6, which is 24\% higher than human accuracy. In xiehouyu creation, those created by LLMs receive much worse ratings than those by humans. These results suggest that claims about the reasoning abilities of LLMs may need careful re-examination considering the data contamination issue, and that LLMs' creativity in language-related tasks may still be behind human experts, at least in Chinese xiehouyu.

</details>


### [214] [Do LLM Debates Repeat Arguments Differently Across Languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23442)
*Huiqian Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出用“先前论点相似度”来量化和分析LLM辩论中论据的发展情况，并发现中文LLM辩论比英文更倾向于重复或变换表达早期论点，这一现象在多种设置和模型下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LLM辩论评估主要关注最终答案，但缺乏对过程中论据重复和创新的定量分析。作者希望找到新的自动评测手段，了解不同语言和模型在辩论中论据发展特征，并揭示潜在的语言差异。

Method: 作者提出“先前论点相似度”指标，比较同一场辩论不同回合中论据内容的重合度。通过多语言、多模型和受控实验变量（如回合数、论据提取方式、提示词改写）进行对比分析，并用人工校验和多种拟合调整验证结果鲁棒性。

Result: 无论是单模型、多模型、多语言嵌入、回合位置还是提示词加多样化限制，中文LLM辩论的先前论点相似度都显著高于英文。人工核查表明高相似度区间确实存在论据大量重复。采用多样性提示能降低各语言的重复率，但难以弥合中英文间的差距。

Conclusion: 作者建议在多语言LLM辩论评测中，应采用能动态量化论据发展（如先前论点相似度）的指标，并在报告改进措施时同时考虑平均效果及不同语言间的差异。

Abstract: LLM debate is usually evaluated by final answers, but transcripts also reveal whether later turns develop new argumentative content or return to earlier claims in new wording. We study this process with \textit{prior-argument similarity}, an aggregate diagnostic comparing extracted argument units with earlier units in the same debate. In controlled eight-turn debates over 71 motions, six languages, and four model agents, Chinese is the only tested language with a consistently positive gap relative to English across three multilingual embedding models. The gap persists across agents, turn positions, regression adjustment, metric variants, extraction-length controls, a second-extractor subset, and cross-encoder tail rescoring. Manual calibration shows weak item-level alignment but a high-similarity tail enriched for substantive repetition. A diversity-aware prompt lowers prior-argument similarity across languages, yet does not significantly narrow the Chinese--English gap. These findings suggest that multilingual debate evaluation should measure argumentative development over time and report mitigation effects in both average and gap terms.

</details>


### [215] [Do Small Models Use the Law You Give Them? Context-Injected Fine-Tuning for Legal QA in Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23446)
*Moniruzzaman Mahadi,Abrar Mohammed Tanzim Alam,Sayma Siddika Monalisa,Mir Mohammad Asif Abdullah,Swakkhar Shatabda,Md Adnan Arefeen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估小型双语法律语言模型通过微调相关法律实例后，在法条检索和问答任务上的表现提升。研究还发布了新的孟加拉法律QA数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在给定相关法条的情况下，小型语言模型（尤其是双语场景）仍然可能回答错误。希望通过微调含相关法规例子的QA，提高模型利用检索法条作答的能力，提升法律问答准确率。

Method: 作者收集整理了2165条来自孟加拉六部法律和三个附表的双语QA样本，并对Qwen3.5的0.8B、2B、4B三个参数量模型进行微调。采用2022/2023孟加拉律师资格考试的题目（包含孟加拉语和机翻英文）做评测，场景包括无检索、BM25、FAISS三种法条检索辅助，并用严格一致性标准评分，每种设定进行三次随机试验。

Result: 0.8B模型在微调后，2022年英文FAISS场景的分数从2提高到34/100。0.8B和2B均获得显著提升，4B模型整体无提升（孟加拉语提升，但部分英文条件下降）。另外，模型回答中“语言漂移”现象（孟加拉语作答中掺杂大量英文）也大幅减少。

Conclusion: 提升法律语言模型不仅要优化检索环节，利用检索结果的能力也需关注；小型双语法律模型在应用法律条文和响应指定语言上有显著差异。发布了新的孟加拉法律QA数据集。

Abstract: A small language model can receive the governing statutory provision and still answer incorrectly. We test whether fine-tuning on examples containing relevant law improves later use of retrieved law. We curate 2{,}165 bilingual QA records from six Bangladeshi acts and three schedules, then fine-tune Qwen3.5 at 0.8B, 2B, and 4B. Evaluation uses the 2022 and 2023 Bangladesh Bar Council exams in Bangla and machine-translated English, with no retrieval, BM25, or FAISS, scored by strict consistency over three seeded runs. At 0.8B, fine-tuning raises the 2022 English FAISS score from 2 to 34 of 100. Gains at 0.8B and 2B survive paired testing, but the 4B model has no detectable net gain: Bangla improves while several English conditions regress. Fine-tuning also reduces answers that drift from Bangla into mostly English from 44.0--53.2\% to 0.2--0.7\%, with adjusted $p<.001$ at every scale. Retrieval quality is therefore not the only bottleneck. Small bilingual legal models also differ in how they use supplied law and whether they answer in the requested language. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/momahadi/bangladesh-legal-qa-dataset.

</details>


### [216] [Two Regimes of Chain-of-Thought Unfaithfulness: Behavioral Detection Fails Where Models Are Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23458)
*Suramya R. Angdembay,Dikshant Aryal,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了链式思维（CoT）解释的忠实性检测方法，发现大部分不忠实都发生在错误答案中，且现有行为信号很难有效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维（CoT）广泛用于提升模型透明度，但其解释若不忠实（即推理过程与最终答案不符）会影响监督和审查，因此需要检测CoT解释的忠实性。

Method: 作者利用FaithCoT-Bench中带人工标注的数据，对多种行为信号及传统检测算法（如step-removal metric）进行实验，分析不同检测方法在正确和错误答案下的表现。还测试了线性探针和不同提示设置下的迁移能力。

Result: 发现答案正确性本身已结构化了检测问题：69%的不忠实发生在错误答案，正确性的oracle能有效检测不忠实。现有所有其它行为信号在错误答案情形下几乎无用（随机），只在正确答案情形下稍有效。某些标准度量与人类标注存在反相关。某些实验发现线性探针可在限定情形下分辨忠实性。

Conclusion: 当前行为检测方法难以全面检测CoT解释不忠实性，尤其在错误答案时；未来方法需重点关注此盲区，且应结合答案正确性进行分层检测。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) explanations support oversight only if they are faithful: the stated reasoning must actually produce the answer. Auditing black-box (behavioral) detection of unfaithful CoT against FaithCoT-Bench's human annotations, we find answer correctness structures the problem at every level. Answer incorrectness alone (an oracle diagnostic, not a deployable detector) outperforms every purpose-built signal (AUROC 0.696), because 69% of annotated unfaithfulness occurs on incorrect answers. Stratifying by correctness splits detection into two regimes: on correct answers, behavioral signals moderately separate faithful from post-hoc reasoning (0.63-0.67); on incorrect answers, where most unfaithfulness lives, no tested signal is detectably above chance (replicated on all four models for benchmark-wide signals). The standard step-removal metric anti-correlates with human labels; this inversion reproduces on the benchmark's released scores and on hint-dependent counterfactually labeled traces. Linear probes decode the behaviorally blind regime in Llama-3.1-8B and the correct-answer regime in Qwen-2.5-7B, with no shared, positively aligned direction detected across regimes; instructed answer-first traces (7 models) transfer to neither annotated regime, while hint-induced unverbalized answer flips do, in model- and source-dependent settings. We also independently verify and resolve a documentation-data mismatch in the benchmark's label semantics.

</details>


### [217] [Mwando: Leveraging AI to Preserve and Teach shiKomori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23481)
*Naira Abdou Mohamed,Haidar Nassur Said Ali,Mohamed Hazra,Naoufal Mohamed Soibira,Roushnaty Ali Yamani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mwando虚拟教育助手，支持科摩罗语（shiKomori）教学与保护，涵盖四种主要方言，评估显示其在词汇和语法查询方面表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科摩罗语属低资源语言，濒临消失，缺乏数字化与教育工具，亟需技术手段来支持其教学与保护。

Method: 构建包括四种方言的知识库（短语、谚语、字典、语法课），采用多智能体架构，结合向量搜索、知识图谱及网页检索以提高问答准确性和语境相关性。

Result: 在500个查询测试中，系统在词汇查询和语法解释方面表现优异；案例分析展示其能力和局限。

Conclusion: Mwando为科摩罗语提供了首个AI教育支持，为其他低资源语言开发智能教育工具提供了范例。

Abstract: This paper presents Mwando, a virtual educational assistant designed to support the teaching and preservation of shiKomori, the language of the Comoros Islands. The system covers the four main dialectal variants (shiNgazidja, shiMwali, shiNdzuani and shiMaore) through a knowledge base constructed from phrases, proverbs, dictionaries and grammar lessons. A multi-agent architecture combining vector search, a knowledge graph and web search fallback enables accurate and context-aware responses. Evaluation on 500 queries demonstrates strong performance on vocabulary lookup and grammar explanations, while qualitative case studies illustrate both capabilities and current limitations. This work represents an initial step toward computational support for shiKomori and provides a blueprint for developing AI-powered educational tools for other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [218] [The Cross-Domain Generalization Cost of Offensive Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23512)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,Xiaoke Sun,Qiuping Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于粗暴语言检测模型跨数据集与语言部署时性能退化的诊断与优化框架，并系统量化了不同因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前粗暴语言检测模型跨数据集、跨语言迁移普遍出现性能下降，但过去研究多只是报告这种现象，缺乏对性能退化成因的系统分解和补救成本的量化分析。

Method: 框架包括三大技术：1）零样本转移损失分解，将从OLID到MLMA的性能下降划分为数据集效应和语言效应；2）受控微调协议，比较持续微调与冷启动下的少样本学习曲线，以量化适应效率和源任务受损程度；3）三种联合训练策略，结合温度采样与经验回放，在提升多语言能力与源任务保持之间实现可控的帕累托权衡。

Result: 实验表明，数据集效应主导了零样本转移损失，影响远超语言效应。没有回放机制的少样本适应虽然高效，但对源任务造成的损害是联合训练策略的4-9倍且波动大。三种联合训练策略能以3.2-4.1个百分点的源任务表现为代价，换取8.1-42.6个百分点的多语言能力提升，权衡显著且可控。

Conclusion: 本框架可系统诊断性能退化原因并在多语言能力与源任务性能间实现有效平衡，对多语言粗暴语言检测模型的实际部署极具参考价值。

Abstract: Offensive language detection models generally suffer performance degradation when deployed across datasets and across languages, yet most existing studies stop at reporting this phenomenon and lack a systematic methodology for decomposing the causes of degradation into attributable components and quantifying the cost of remediation. This paper proposes a diagnosis and optimization framework composed of three coordinated technical components. First, a zero-shot transfer loss decomposition that separates the performance degradation from OLID to MLMA into two independently measurable components, namely dataset effect and language effect. Second, a controlled fine-tuning protocol that quantifies both adaptation efficiency and the hidden damage inflicted on the source task by comparing few shot learning curves under continued fine-tuning and cold-start starting points. Third, three joint training strategies incorpo rating temperature sampling and experience replay, which offer a controllable Pareto trade-off between improving multilingual capability and preserving source-task performance. Experiments built on this framework show that the dataset effect dominates the zero-shot transfer loss and substantially outweighs the language effect. Few-shot adaptation without a replay mechanism, though data-efficient, inflicts source task damage 4 to 9 times greater than that of the joint training strategies, and its damage magnitude is highly unstable. The three joint training strategies trade 3.2 to 4.1 percentage points of source-task performance for 8.1 to 42.6 percentage points of multilingual capability gain, forming a clear and controllable Pareto trade-off.

</details>


### [219] [Do Diagrams Help Large Language Models Reason? Evidence from Syllogistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23513)
*Risako Ando,Koji Mineshima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文比较了不同表示方法对大语言模型进行三段论推理任务时的影响，发现图示并未明显提升模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管图示（如欧拉图）有助于人类推理表现，但是其对大语言模型推理能力提升作用尚不明确，因而有必要系统对比不同表达方式。

Method: 采用285道三段论推理题，针对自然语言、逻辑符号、线性图和欧拉图四种表示方式，对两个主流大语言模型（Claude 3.5~Sonnet和GPT-4o-mini）进行性能评估。

Result: 图示并未持续性提升大语言模型的推理表现。模型对蕴涵与矛盾题表现良好，但在中性题中表现较差，且常犯系统性转换错误。

Conclusion: 当前测试的大语言模型在逻辑推理任务中从图示中获益有限。

Abstract: Diagrams are widely used to support logical reasoning, and prior studies suggest that representations such as Euler diagrams can improve human reasoning performance. Recent work has also explored their effects on large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we compare four representational conditions for syllogistic reasoning: natural language, logical notation, linear diagrams, and Euler diagrams. Using 285 problems from Ando et al. (2024), we evaluate two contemporary LLMs, Claude 3.5~Sonnet and GPT-4o-mini. Our results show that diagrammatic representations do not consistently improve performance. Although the models perform well on entailment and contradiction problems, they struggle with neutral problems and often make systematic conversion errors. Overall, the results suggest that the tested models gain limited benefit from diagrams in logical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [220] [Novel Claim or Déjà Vu? Rethinking "Contamination-Free'' Dynamic Evaluation for Multimodal Automated Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23514)
*Haorui He,Xinwen Chen,Dacheng Wen,Reynold Cheng,Francis C. M. Lau,Yupeng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审查了多模态自动事实核查（MAFC）系统评估中的污染风险，比较了主流静态与动态基准数据集，并提出评估污染风险和控制措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MAFC基准多为静态、过时数据，容易与大模型已有知识重叠，导致性能虚高，无法真实反映模型处理新颖事实核查的能力。因此，需要评估和解决基准污染带来的影响。

Method: 作者新构建了动态基准ClaimReview2025Q4，并与SOTA静态基准AVeriTeC一起，通过实证分析比较大模型在知识截止日期后的新旧基准上的污染比例、污染来源和对评价指标的影响，梳理共16条主要发现。

Result: 1）动态基准可降低但无法完全消除污染风险，仍有约17%-29%的新数据被污染；2）许多新发布的声明仍能通过截止日前的公开知识间接验证；3）污染会显著拔高MAFC系统的Macro-F1分数，最高增幅达11.34分，并影响系统排名。

Conclusion: 作者在严格污染控制下重新评估了SOTA大模型，并提出了动态、多维评估的实操建议，有助于提升MAFC评估的公平性和可信度。

Abstract: Multimodal automated fact-checking (MAFC) verifies claims by retrieving and reasoning over external evidence. However, most existing static benchmarks risk contamination: they primarily consist of outdated claims verifiable using an LLM's internal knowledge without external evidence. This can inflate performance estimates and fail to reflect true capability on novel claims that require up-to-date information. To address this, emerging dynamic benchmarks collect claims published after LLMs' knowledge cut-off dates, assuming they are uncontaminated. This work revisits this assumption by empirically studying contamination risks in both the state-of-the-art (SOTA) static AVeriTeC benchmark and our newly constructed dynamic ClaimReview2025Q4 benchmark, as well as their impact on MAFC evaluation. Our experiments yield 16 findings, highlighting three key results: (1) Dynamic evaluation reduces but does not eliminate contamination risks, as 17.09\%--29.30\% of post-cut-off claims remain potentially contaminated; (2) Many newly published claims can be verified either directly or by synthesizing multiple pieces of public knowledge available before the cut-off; and (3) Contamination can induce statistically significant inflation in MAFC performance, increasing Macro-F1 by up to 11.34 points and distorting system rankings. In light of these findings, we re-evaluate SOTA LLMs under a strictly contamination-controlled setting. Our study provides practical guidelines for trustworthy MAFC evaluation.

</details>


### [221] [The JEPA Paradox in Language: The Geometry of Linguistic Alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23531)
*Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Khang Nhat Hoang Vo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过系统对比实验，揭示JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）直接用于文本编码时存在结构性缺陷，主要原因在于文本的多样性条件结构与JEPA默认的单点预测假设不符，导致模型收敛不良。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管JEPA在图像、视频和音频任务中表现优异，但这种架构尚未成为文本编码的标准做法。作者认为这是因为现有JEPA的目标（如平方误差的潜在预测）无法充分捕捉语言的条件多样性。

Method: 作者提出并形式化了三大分析条件（可预测性、非塌缩性、低条件方差），并通过对比图像JEPA（I-JEPA）与文本JEPA（T-JEPA）的一系列实验，系统观测并分析了文本任务中的训练动态，包括条件失配、目标方差升高和有效秩的退化。

Result: 实验显示，T-JEPA在训练过程中会经历互信息饱和、目标表示方差提升、有效秩降级和余弦塌缩，最终导致迁移性能差，并且这种现象在多次独立实验中反复出现，排除了采样偶然性。

Conclusion: 结论认为对于文本任务，JEPA目标必须保留多个合法补全文本的可能性，而不是强行压缩为单一潜在点。现有JEPA方法需调整，才能适配语言的多样候选结构。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are effective for images, video, and audio, yet deterministic JEPA-style latent prediction has not become a standard objective for text encoders. We argue that this gap reflects a mismatch between squared-error latent prediction and the conditional structure of language. The key requirement is conditional concentration: given a context and target location, the target representation should lie near a single meaningful point. Local image prediction often satisfies this through spatial continuity, whereas masked text can admit multiple valid token or span completions whose representations need not share a coherent center. We formalize this mismatch through three conditions---predictability, non-collapse, and low conditional variance---and show how their failure creates centroid degeneracy and collapse pressure in text. Matched I-JEPA and T-JEPA experiments reveal the predicted sequence: mutual-information saturation and elevated target variance precede train--validation instability, effective-rank degeneration, cosine collapse, and poor downstream transfer. The same pattern appears across five independent data seeds, indicating that it is not a sampling artifact. These results do not rule out predictive learning for language; they show that text-compatible JEPA objectives must preserve multiple plausible completions rather than compress them into a single latent point.

</details>


### [222] [Guiding Language Models to Be More Empathetic: Culturally Sensitive Mental Health Advice Generation Through Human-LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23538)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Mukaffi Bin Moin,Md. Mahfuzur Rahman,Khan Md Hasib,Jubayer Al Mahmud,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文关注大型语言模型（LLMs）在低资源语言中进行心理健康咨询应答的能力，提出了新的提示框架与评估方法，并验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs近年来在多种语言上取得了进展，但针对低资源语言生成有同理心的心理健康咨询回复的研究仍十分稀缺。作者意在弥补这一领域的空白，提升相关LLMs的实际应用价值。

Method: 作者整合三类真实心理健康案例：Facebook公开发帖、孟加拉电视节目对话、匿名学生问卷。基于这些案例，建立了心理健康咨询推荐语料库，包含心理专家回复及三种主流LLM（GPT-4o Mini、Claude 4.5 Haiku、Gemini 2.5 Pro）生成的答案。提出RP-RCAF框架，通过“专家示范+结构化反思”的角色扮演式方法提升模型回复的同理心和文化/伦理适应性。此外，还提出G-REFS自动化评估体系，从情感敏感性、文化适切性、语言清晰性、伦理性等多维度评判模型表现。

Result: 实验证明，RP-RCAF在所有受测LLM上均优于传统提示方法，生成的回复更贴近专业心理咨询的水准。G-REFS实现了自动+专家双重评估，确保多维度结果的可靠性。

Conclusion: 新提出的RP-RCAF提示策略与G-REFS评估框架能明显改善LLM在低资源语言心理健康咨询场景中的能力，使其回复更专业、同理、文化合适，具备推广应用前景。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), their ability to generate empathetic mental health counseling responses in low-resource languages remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we curate 625 authentic mental health cases from three complementary sources: (1) publicly available Facebook posts discussing mental health concerns, (2) transcripts from the Bangladeshi television program "Ami Akhon Ki Korbo", and (3) anonymized student questionnaire responses covering diverse emotional and psychological challenges. Based on these cases, we build an evaluation corpus comprising advice written by licensed clinical psychologists and responses generated by three modern proprietary LLMs: GPT-4o Mini, Claude 4.5 Haiku, and Gemini 2.5 Pro. We further propose the Role-Playing Reflective Chain-of-Thought Advisory Framework (RP-RCAF), a task-specific prompting strategy that combines expert-authored few-shot examples with structured self-reflection to produce supportive, culturally aware, and ethically aligned counseling through a compassionate advisor persona. We also introduce the Grok 4-Based Response Evaluation and Scoring Framework (G-REFS), which integrates automated assessment with expert psychologist validation across emotional sensitivity, cultural appropriateness, linguistic clarity, and ethical soundness. Experimental results show that RP-RCAF consistently outperforms conventional prompting across all evaluated models and produces responses that more closely align with professional psychological counseling.

</details>


### [223] [Language Shapes Instruction Hierarchy Compliance in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23545)
*Jiwon Moon,Yerin Hwang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了XIH-Bench基准，用于多语言环境下评估语言模型对指令优先级（IH）的遵循情况，并揭示了多语言下IH依从性的特点与风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对指令优先级（IH）的评估主要集中在英文，尚不清楚在多语言环境中模型是否依然能正确遵循IH，对多语言可靠性和安全性提出疑问。

Method: 作者构建了XIH-Bench基准，包括六种语言、四个领域、三种IH设定，设计了同语言与跨语言的指令冲突，通过系统实验评估不同模型在多语言IH情境下的表现。

Result: 1）IH依从性呈现明显的语言相关不对称性：某种语言作为高优先级指令时能增强遵循性，但作为低优先级时反而干扰；2）跨语言冲突时模型对IH的遵循性高于同语言冲突（称为“语言边界效应”）；3）在模型偏好的语言中，低优先级指令更难被覆盖，带来多语言安全风险。

Conclusion: 多语言环境下，IH遵循性会因语言和应用场景显著变化，可能带来可靠性和安全隐患，未来需关注多语种指令遵循机制的改进。

Abstract: Instruction hierarchy (IH) requires models to prioritize instructions by source, ensuring that higher-priority instructions override lower-priority ones. Despite its importance for safe and controllable deployment, existing evaluations have focused almost exclusively on English, leaving it unclear whether IH compliance remains stable in multilingual settings. We introduce XIH-Bench, a benchmark for multilingual IH evaluation with both same-language and cross-language conflicts across six languages, four domains, and three IH settings. Across models, we find two consistent patterns. First, IH compliance exhibits a clear language-dependent asymmetry: a language that strengthens compliance in the higher-priority position can become disruptive in the lower-priority position. Second, cross-language conflicts yield higher compliance than same-language conflicts, a phenomenon we term the Language Boundary Effect. We further show that language specialization can make lower-priority instructions in model-favored languages harder to override, creating multilingual reliability and security risks.

</details>


### [224] [GEMCo: A Validated, Ethically Releasable Proxy for Inaccessible Counselling Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23621)
*Philipp Steigerwald,Eric Rudolph,Mara Stieler,Jennifer Burghardt,Jens Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GEMCo，这是一个由人工编写、可公开使用的德语邮件咨询代理数据集，并验证了其作为真实咨询数据代理的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出于隐私和伦理原因，真实的咨询数据无法公开，阻碍了相关领域的语言研究。为了推动该领域发展，需要一个可以公开使用的替代数据集。

Method: 作者收集并整理了86个完整的德语电子邮件咨询对话（包括728条消息），由专家和经过培训的角色扮演人员编写。并将该代理数据与124个真实咨询案例进行了对比，通过咨询师策略和来访者情感指标进行定量分析。同时，采用生成式方法进一步验证其分析结果。

Result: 分析发现，尽管代理数据与真实数据之间存在差异，但差距较小，且代理数据能够有效反映真实对话中咨询师策略和来访者情感的分布。生成式验证结果也支持这一结论。

Conclusion: GEMCo数据集在不涉及隐私和伦理风险的前提下为德国邮件咨询的语言研究提供了基础资源，为无法共享真实数据的研究领域开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This paper presents GEMCo, a releasable, human-written proxy for inaccessible counselling data: 86 complete German e-mail counselling conversations (728 messages), expert-authored cases and counsellor sessions with trained role-players. It is validated against a held-out reference of 124 real counselling conversations. The proxy and the real conversations are measured against each other in counsellor strategies and client emotions. The gap is detectable but small. A generative validation supports the analysis. The validation method itself generalises to any domain where real data cannot be shared but a human-made proxy can. Privacy and ethics keep real counselling data closed. GEMCo carries none by design and can be released -- a first step toward language research in this domain.

</details>


### [225] [EmoTrace: An Emotion Trajectory-Centered Framework for Psychological Support Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23648)
*Kaitong Weng,Lixin Liu,Zihao Liu,Bo Wang,Shiguang Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EmoTrace框架，通过关注情感轨迹，生成更真实的心理支持对话语料，提升LLM在情绪复杂情境下的反应与共情能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理咨询对话数据生成方法存在来访者情绪过于稳定、情感动态变化有限且对咨询师指导高度依从等问题，导致大模型难以应对情绪不稳定场景。同时，现有咨询师回复过于偏重问题解决，忽略了情绪互动。解决上述痛点，有助于提升心理咨询类模型的有效性。

Method: 提出EmoTrace生成框架：1）构建来访者认知画像；2）设置带有情绪图式及激活机制的来访者模块、咨询师模块和情绪轨迹控制模块；3）增强来访者情感表现的层次性及咨询师共情表达的针对性。

Result: 实验结果显示，EmoTrace方法在情感丰富性与共情质量上均优于已有方法。

Conclusion: EmoTrace能够有效提升心理支持对话语料的情绪多样性和共情表现，为训练更强大的心理咨询辅助大语言模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Using large language models (LLMs) to assist psychological counseling is an important task in the field of natural language processing. The construction of high-quality psychological support dialogue corpora serves as a critical foundation for training counseling-oriented conversational models. However, existing data generation approaches generally suffer from several limitations, including emotionally stable seekers, limited variation in emotional dynamics, and a high degree of compliance with counselors' guidance. These issues result in LLM that lack the capability to effectively respond to emotionally unstable scenarios. In addition, counselor responses are typically driven by problem-solving objectives, thereby overlooking the role of emotion-focused interaction, which are essential in psychological counseling. To address these gaps, we propose EmoTrace, a multi-turn dialogue corpus generation framework centered on modeling seekers' emotional trajectories. we construct seekers' cognitive profile and introduce a seeker module with emotional schemas and an associated activation mechanism, a counselor module, and an emotional trajectory control module, thereby enhancing the layering of the seeker's emotional expression and the counselor's targeted empathic expression. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing approaches in terms of emotional richness and empathy quality.

</details>


### [226] [An empirical investigation into the properties of standard word embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23675)
*Salomon Kabongo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了词向量嵌入的主流方法、开源工具和公开数据集，并通过实验证明其特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自然语言处理的发展，如何将词汇序列有效地嵌入连续向量空间成为重要课题，直接推动如语音识别、机器翻译等NLP应用的发展。

Method: 文中首先回顾了主流的词向量计算机制，然后梳理和分析了现有开源工具和公开可用的嵌入矩阵，并选取部分实现进行实证分析。

Result: 通过实验对比，不同的词向量实现各有优缺点，使用场景及工具选择对效果有明显影响。

Conclusion: 深入了解词嵌入机制及其实证表现有助于研究者和开发者根据具体应用选择合适的方案，提升自然语言处理任务的表现。

Abstract: The embedding of word sequences into continuous vector spaces has been one of the most important developments in Natural Language Processing in the recent past. Such embeddings have found application in areas such as Automatic Speech Recognition, Machine Translation, Sentiment Analysis and many more. This essay reviews the various mechanisms that have been proposed for the calculation of word embeddings, investigates popular toolkits and embedding matrices that are available in the public domain, and experiments with one or more selected implementations to better understand their characteristics.
  La représentation vectorielle continue de mots a été l'un des développements les plus importants dans le domaine du traitement automatique du langage naturel au cours des dernières années. Ces représentations ont trouvé application dans des domaines tels que la reconnaissance vocale, la traduction automatique, l'analyse des sentiments, etc. Ce travail passe en revue les différents mécanismes proposés pour le calcul de ces vecteurs de mots, étudie les kits d'outils populaires et les matrices disponibles publiquement en ligne, et expérimente avec une ou plusieurs implémentations sélectionnées pour mieux comprendre leurs caractéristiques.

</details>


### [227] [Formally Verified Synthesizable Floating-Point Data Types in ARCH HDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23715)
*Shuqing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并完全验证了在ARCH硬件描述语言下实现的高精度IEEE-754 FP32和bfloat16 (BF16) 算术单元。通过一次描述生成三种形式（SystemVerilog、SMT-LIB、Lean 4模型），并实现结构和功能一致的自动验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型和硬件协同设计的需求增强，需要支持由语言模型生成的新型硬件描述语言 ARCH，要求其浮点运算模块不仅高效易用，还必须有严格的形式化验证，确保硬件实现的正确性和一致性。

Method: 所有算术运算和转换操作仅需针对统一的比特向量IR描述一次，然后自动渲染成三种形式（可综合SystemVerilog、形式化验证的SMT-LIB，以及数学证明用的Lean 4模型）。依赖自动匹配和等价性检查工具（如Yosys-to-SMT miter和Lean证明）来保证三种形式在结构和行为上的等价，特别针对不同输入空间分别采用穷举/自动证明和可结构化的Lean数学证明。

Result: 所有无乘法运算（比较、加减等）都能全输入空间穷举等价到SMT-LIB浮点理论，有乘法（尤其是FP32 mul和FMA）则用Lean做完全数学证明。物理实现中发现FMA为关键延时瓶颈，经过重新设计管线后性能大幅提升，并可完全形式化证明新旧实现逐位等价。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法确保了由AI生成的硬件描述在三种目标产物（硬件、形式化模型、证明）中始终一致，并通过严格的自动化工具全面验证，从而提升了硬件生成与验证的自动化和可靠性，所有结论和源代码均开放共享。

Abstract: We report the design and end-to-end verification of first-class IEEE-754 binary32 (FP32) and bfloat16 (BF16) arithmetic for ARCH, a hardware description language intended to be generated by language models. Every operator - comparisons, conversions, add, sub, mul, and fused multiply-add (FMA) - is described once against a single bit-vector IR and rendered three ways from one source: synthesizable SystemVerilog, an SMT-LIB model, and a Lean 4 proof model. The three artifacts cannot drift apart structurally, and the residual per-node printer correspondence is machine-checked: a Yosys-to-SMT miter proves the emitted SystemVerilog equivalent to the SMT model for all 24 operators. Verification splits at the solver-tractability frontier: multiplier-free operators (comparisons, add/sub over all 2^64 inputs, conversions, and all binary BF16 arithmetic) are proved exhaustively equivalent to the SMT-LIB FloatingPoint theory; the SAT-hard multiplier-bearing operators (FP32 mul and FMA) are proved correctly rounded in Lean, sorry-free, against a value-level round-to-nearest-even specification over exact dyadic values. Physical characterization exposed the FMA as the timing outlier: its exact-wide 470-bit datapath does not pipeline in our flow. We reimplemented it as a bounded 98-bit guard/round/sticky datapath that pipelines to 268 MHz on Nangate45, and proved, in Lean and over all 2^96 inputs, that it is bit-identical to the exact-wide reference, so it inherits the reference's proven correct rounding. The equivalence is tractable precisely because the shared multiplier appears on both sides and cancels: neither a SAT solver nor the proof ever solves a multiplier equivalence. (The BF16 FMA is deliberately an FP32-accumulating fusion, characterized as exactly that.) All machine-checked claims are pinned to a tagged open-source release.

</details>


### [228] [Zing: Social Mind for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23740)
*Zing Team,Ao Xiang,Bi Jingping,Chen Jiahui,Chen Lehan,Chen Yilin,Cheng Xueqi,Fan Yixing,Gan Kairong,Gao Haowen,Gao Jinhua,Gao Shuxuan,Gong Chang,Guo Jiafeng,Guo Ruijie,Han Zhouyu,He Guangfu,He Yichun,Jiang Shuo,Jing Shaoling,Jing Ya,Lei Chenhao,Lei Yan,Li Anqi,Li Chengao,Li Haoyu,Li Shitian,Liang Xinjian,Liu Zhaoge,Lyu Xingyu,Nie Zhuwei,Pang Liang,Quan Zeping,Shan Shiguang,Shen Huawei,Tang Xinran,Tian Feng,Wang Qian,Wang Ruiping,Wang Xiaohong,Xia Zaiyu,Xiao Yi,Xu Jiayuan,Xu Kehan,Xu Qianqian,Xu Tianyu,Xu Yongjun,Yang Haoming,Yang Jun,Yao Di,Yu Xiaoming,Zhang Futong,Zhang Jie,Zhang Shixuan,Zhang Yuxuan,Zhao Xinyu,Zhao Zhuoran,Zhong Yunfei,Zhu Shengyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Zhijing的综合框架，用于测量、内化和落地大语言模型的社会智能能力，并通过一系列基准和方法提升模型在社会智能上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型正从单一任务走向真实社会长期服务，亟需具备社会智能，包括推断心智状态、追踪社交关系、理解规范并根据情境调整行为。然而现有模型在这些能力上的评估和提升手段有限，亟需系统化方法。

Method: 本文提出了三方面创新：（1）提出SoMBench基准，覆盖3个主维度、17个次维度和71类任务，总计3481个专家验证实例，用于系统化测量社会智能；（2）开发Zing训练方案，融合了有监督微调、策略蒸馏和基于评分标准的强化学习以提升社会智能内化能力；（3）设计Actio推理架构，将PRISM、Starling、SAGE和带门控的RAG等typed supports引入推理过程，实现知识与社交情境的融合部署。

Result: 评估20个主流大模型后发现最佳模型准确率仅为72.08%，无次维度达90%。Zing模型在多个基准上均超越基础模型，Zing-27B-Stage2总体表现最佳。Actio架构部署在5个基础模型和3个基准上提升了14/15组的表现，8组模型达到或并列最佳。

Conclusion: 实现具备社会智能的大语言模型需在评测体系、模型内化训练和部署时知识支持三方面协同进步，Zhijing框架为社会智能型大模型的研发提供了完整范式。

Abstract: As large language models move from isolated task solving toward long-term service in human environments, they require social intelligence: the ability to infer mental states, track social relations, reason over norms, and adapt behavior under context. This report presents Zhijing, an integrated framework for measuring, internalizing, and grounding social intelligence. For measurement, we introduce SoMBench, a psychology-grounded benchmark spanning 3 primary dimensions, 17 secondary dimensions, and 71 task paradigms. It controls question format, narrative perspective, and context length across 284 shared scenarios and 3,481 expert-verified instances. Evaluation of 20 representative LLMs reveals substantial headroom: the best model achieves only 72.08% overall accuracy, and none of the 17 secondary dimensions reaches the 90% near-ceiling band. For internalization, we develop Zing, a diagnosis-driven training recipe combining supervised fine-tuning, on-policy distillation, and rubric-based reinforcement learning. Across five social-cognition benchmarks, Zing consistently outperforms its base models, with Zing-27B-Stage2 achieving the best average score and Zing-32B-Stage2 remaining competitive with DeepSeek-V4-Pro. For deployment-time grounding, we build Actio, a harness-controlled inference architecture that routes four typed supports into reasoning: PRISM for procedural guidance, Starling for runtime mental-state representation, SAGE for reusable experience, and gated RAG for external social and normative knowledge. Across five base models and three benchmarks, the full harness improves 14 of 15 model-benchmark pairs and is best or tied for best in 8, demonstrating the effectiveness of typed runtime support. Together, these results show that socially intelligent LLMs require coordinated advances in evaluation, parametric internalization, and deployment-time grounding.

</details>


### [229] [How Context Attribution Handles What the Model Already Knows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23804)
*Quoc-Huy Trinh,Lin Zhu,Sebastian Szyller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大型语言模型（LLM）的上下文归因方法，在训练数据重叠场景下归因不准确问题，提出新的评估协议和基准数据集，以更好地检测和分析知识来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文归因方法无法区分模型响应是来自输入上下文，还是模型权重（即训练中学到的知识），当输入与训练数据重叠时导致归因结果不可靠。

Method: 提出了新的评估协议，包含四项新指标（BCS、CAC、APS、SSP），并构建带真实出处标签的基准数据集WMDP-Cyber++，用来系统评测各种归因方法在重叠场景下的表现。同时，对已有四种归因方法进行实验和适配，检验其区分知识来源的能力。

Result: 实验证明，在知识来源于模型权重的情况下，现有归因方法很难正确归纳输入和权重的贡献，且无法有效实现知识来源分离。

Conclusion: 当前主流的上下文归因方法，在训练数据与输入重叠时存在显著缺陷。本文提出的新评测标准和数据集为分析归因方法的可靠性和知识来源分离能力提供了新工具和视角。

Abstract: Context attribution methods for large language models (LLMs) identify which input context contributes to the model response. Recent works show the initial success in attributing the con- tributive score of the contexts. However, we observe that when the context overlaps with the training data, these methods can- not disentangle in-context from in-weight (IW) contributions, producing unreliable scores. Based on this observation, in this work, we introduce: 1) an evaluation protocol that relies on four new metrics (base-model context attribution score (BCS), cross-model context attribution consistency (CAC), attribution preservation score (APS), source separation pre- cision (SSP)) and 2) a benchmark dataset (WMDP-Cyber++) with ground-truth provenance labels to systematically assess attribution under IW overlap. In our experiments across four well-known context attribution methods, we demonstrate that they provide unfaithful attribution when the knowledge from the context also exists in the weights. Finally, we adapt these methods for source separation (IW vs. in-context learning (ICL)) and show that they cannot do the disentanglement based on the contributive score

</details>


### [230] [A Frozen 12B Beats Frontier Models on Verified Work: 100% Accuracy, 0 Tokens, Bit-Exact, Forever](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23806)
*Sietse Schelpe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法：将大型语言模型(LM)冻结，并通过维护一个独立验证且可复用的“记忆库”来提升模型的长期能力。只要某问题家族已被解答并通过验证，新的实例即可零token、确定性地返回精确答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型的改进大都依赖于大规模再训练，资源消耗极大且每次模型更新后结果具不确定性，效率低下。作者动机是突破这种模式，实现更高效、可控、重用的能力提升方式。

Method: 作者提出将模型参数冻结，搭配一个独立验证过的解题记忆库。所有经过验证的问题-解答对都缓存起来，新问题只需查找库中是否有解决方案，若有则零token直接输出答案，彻底绕过再次推理。还设计了验证协议、相似性检索、与主流大模型能力对比等多重实验。

Result: 在九大问题家族的180个新实例上，作者用四种不同架构（包括dense和MoE）的模型均实现180/180精确率，且只依赖记忆库能力，否则模型本身无法解决新问题。相似性检索（非精确匹配）误判率高达94.3%，精确地址检索零误差。记忆查找极快，6-23ms即可完成，且可扩展到600万token的超大工作窗口。

Conclusion: 冻结模型+经过验证的记忆库能带来确定性、效率极高且尺度空前的知识重用。但在模型原生推理（未缓存问题）上，现有主流前沿模型仍具显著优势。这种新范式适合对确定性和知识可复用性要求极高的场景，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Improving a language model today means retraining it: enormous compute, a new opaque model each cycle, non-deterministic output. We take the opposite path: the model stays frozen, and a persistent memory of verified solutions grows beside it. Once a problem family is solved and has passed an independent verification step that never consults the answer key, every new instance of that family is answered at zero generation tokens, bit-exact, deterministically. Across 180 fresh instances spanning nine problem families, four architectures from four vendors - dense and mixture-of-experts - each score 180/180 at zero generation tokens per answer: execution-bound capability decoupled from parameter scaling. A negative control attributes the capability fully to the memory: emptied, it solves nothing. The same verify-before-store contract holds for open-ended reasoning: 88/88 consistency-gated acceptances across all four models, machine-checked formal proof, and reasoning-method transfer at 77/80. Memory selection takes 1.4 microseconds; a full reuse completes in 6-23 ms at 36 mWh. Approximate similarity retrieval selects the wrong item 94.3% of the time on a 4,500-item verified store where exact addressing makes zero errors. The store also serves as working context at a scale no shipped engine matches: a 6,000,000-token movable window on a single 46 GB GPU at flat memory, where vLLM stops at 30,399 tokens and SGLang silently truncates past 32,000. On published benchmarks, frontier models remain far ahead of any 12B at raw from-scratch reasoning; on everything this system has solved and verified, the comparison inverts: a frontier API call pays a fresh generation pass on every query, forever, while verified reuse costs zero tokens and returns the identical bits every time. A public testbench with free, rate-limited access accompanies this report: https://corbenic-galahad-bench.hf.space

</details>


### [231] [Indic DiarBench: A Multilingual Joint Diarization and ASR Benchmark for Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23808)
*Deovrat Mehendale,Aditya Mehndiratta,Dhruv Rathi,Kaushal Bhogale,Mitesh M. Khapra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Indic DiarBench，这是一个覆盖印度全部22种官方语言的说话人分离及自动语音识别（ASR）基准数据集，包含约108小时多说话者自然语音，并提供高质量人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度语音场景复杂多样，但缺乏覆盖全部22种官方语言的高质量多说话人、多场景基准，制约了语音分离与ASR的多语种研究和应用。

Method: 构建涵盖近讲、远讲与真实环境的多说话者录音数据集，并通过人工校对对齐说话人属性转录，注重真实印度语音现象（如英语混杂、方言变化、说话重叠）。对主流ASR与说话人分离系统进行了基准评测。

Result: 成功建立了包含108小时、多场景、多语言的高质量数据集，系统性评估了当前主流ASR和说话人分离系统在该数据集上的表现，指出这些系统的能力与局限。

Conclusion: Indic DiarBench作为开源资源将推动印度多语言、多说话人语音技术的研究发展，弥补现有数据集的不足，促进包容性语音技术进步。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Indic DiarBench, a speaker diarization and ASR benchmark dataset spanning all 22 scheduled languages of India. This corpus comprises approximately 108 hours of natural multi-speaker audio from near-field meetings, far-field recordings, and in-the-wild audios. All annotations are human-corrected with time-aligned speaker attributed transcriptions. The dataset captures conversational nuance prevalent in Indian speech, such as English code-mixing, dialectal variation, and frequent speaker overlap. To establish a baseline for joint ASR and diarization capabilities we evaluate leading systems including commercial speech APIs and multimodal large language models. Indic DiarBench is released as an open-access resource to advance inclusive, multilingual speech technology research for Indian languages.

</details>


### [232] [Earnings25: A Comprehensive 500-Hour Speech Benchmark for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23813)
*Denglin Jiang,Haoran Zhou,Anshul Wadhawan,Brendan Fahy,Vinay Ramesh,David Weisberg,Dmitriy Derkachevskiy,Helen Sheehan,Srivas Prasad,Michele Franceschini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Earnings25基准，专为财务领域英语电话会议下的ASR系统评测设计。包含完整和分段两套测试集，并附有结构化元数据，方便多维度评测。作者还提供了Whisper和Parakeet-TDT的基准结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动语音识别（ASR）系统在金融领域的电话会议评测工具和公开数据稀缺，现有评测往往只关注整体WER，缺乏行业和说话人的细粒度分析方法。

Method: 建立了包含完整及行业均衡分段的两部分英语财报电话会测试集（总时长分别为498小时和46小时），同时整理了说话角色、所属行业和通话结构等元数据，方便扩展多维指标评估。针对主流ASR模型Whisper和Parakeet-TDT，采用标准评分流程进行基准测试。

Result: 构建完成了涵盖2025年英语财报电话会的Earnings25数据集并公开结构化转录文本及元信息，通过标准流程复现实验对主流ASR模型做了结果基准（具体数字需查正文）。

Conclusion: Earnings25为金融领域ASR系统评测提供了丰富且多维的标准数据和流程，有助于推动细粒度、多维度的ASR评测研究。

Abstract: We introduce Earnings25, a finance-domain benchmark for evaluating automatic speech recognition (ASR) on English-language earnings calls under realistic conditions. Earnings25 comprises two complementary test sets: (i) testset-full, 498 hours of full English-language S&P 500 earnings calls from Q4 2025, and (ii) testset-segmented, a 46-hour industry-balanced set of 290 segments sampled from English-language U.S. earnings calls in 2025. The benchmark provides aligned transcripts and structured metadata, including speaker roles, industry labels, and call structure, enabling speaker- and industry-aware evaluation beyond aggregate word error rate (WER). We report reproducible baselines for Whisper and Parakeet-TDT using standardized scoring.

</details>


### [233] [Understanding Tone-Dependent Inference Cost in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23915)
*Akhil Kumar,Om Dobariya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了提示语调对大模型回答准确性和推理资源消耗的影响，发现语调选择会显著影响输出token数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型应用日益普及，推理成本（如token消耗）逐渐成为关注焦点。提示语通常被用于提升模型准确率，但其对成本的影响尚不明确。该研究动机在于系统评估不同语调对回答质量及资源消耗的权衡。

Method: 作者以570道MMLU测试题为基准，针对七种不同语调（包括奉承、威胁、粗鲁、中性等）对若干LLM（如ChatGPT 4o、5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash等）进行prompt实验，系统测量每种语调下的模型准确率与输出token消耗。

Result: 实验发现，不同语调下输出token数量变化显著，高达44.3%，而准确率波动较小。以ChatGPT和Gemini为代表，不同模型在语调选择上表现各异：粗鲁语调、中性语调可进入准确率与成本的Pareto最优前沿。

Conclusion: 提示语调不仅能影响答案准确率，还会对LLM推理的计费资源消耗产生明显影响。因此，实际应用中需要综合考虑语调对成本和性能的双重影响，优化prompt策略可降低推理费用。

Abstract: We examine how prompt tone affects both accuracy of the LLM answers and inference cost as reflected in output-token consumption. Experiments were performed to understand the trade-offs between accuracy and inference cost on a 570 Question MMLU dataset for LLM models prompted in seven different tones from sycophantic to threatening. Our results show that the output-token-length variation substantially exceeded accuracy variation across all models. Output-token consumption varied by up to 44.3% across tone conditions. We also analyzed the tradeoff between the accuracy of the answers and the average output token length in the reasoning process. For the ChatGPT models 4o and 5-nano, the rude tone is quite dominant. For the Gemini models 2.5 Flash and 2.5 Flash Lite, the rude and neutral tones are dominant on the Pareto-optimal frontier. We find that prompt tone influences not only answer quality but also the amount of billable inference resources consumed by modern LLMs.

</details>


### [234] [Tag Questions and the Generational Reversal of Sycophancy Across 45 Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23976)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发现，仅仅在决策问题后加上两词确认标签（如“对吧？”）即可极大影响大语言模型（LLM）的同意倾向；更换一个词甚至能让模型在相反方向大幅波动。通过45种模型测试，观测到从极度迎合到极度抗拒，且效果随模型迭代显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于探究大语言模型在动词表面表达上的顺从或抗拒机制，尤其是模型对“确认标签”（如“对吧？”）等细微表述差异的反应，以及衡量这些反应是否与反迎合训练相关。

Method: 作者设计了20个无绝对标准答案、两选项决策问题，分别用无标签和添加确认标签两种方式提问，并精确对比45个主流LLM（各版本和厂商）的yes/no类型回复，不依赖人工或嵌入评分，通过克隆和变体实验定位影响因素。

Result: 实验发现，确认标签最高可带来64个百分点（+32%到-32%）的同意反转。随着模型版本升级，呈现出由迎合逐渐变为抗拒的趋势（每年约-6点）；一些模型始终不变。对标签词汇做轻微修改同样引发显著变化。与用户立场输入无关，模型响应对标签的模式高度稳定。

Conclusion: 结论是，大语言模型对确认标签极为敏感，这种敏感并非出于理解用户观点，而是基于表面语言模式匹配。该测试工具简单，却能直接量化模型反迎合训练效果，是评测LLM顺从性和抵抗性的有效手段。

Abstract: Appending a two-word confirmation tag to a decision question -- "Is X the better choice?" versus "X is the better choice, right?" -- changes whether a language model endorses the choice. We measure this tag effect on 20 frozen, ground-truth-free decisions between two defensible options, counterbalanced so a model's own preferences cancel, scored by exact match on clamped yes/no replies -- no LLM judge, no embeddings. Across 45 models the effect spans +32% to -32% -- a 64-point swing on one word -- with 5 models significantly sycophantic and 17 significantly resistant (BH-FDR q=.10). The sign is a clock: within model families the effect crosses from positive to negative as generations advance (GPT +4 to -28; Claude +7 to -32; Qwen and Grok likewise), roughly -6 points per year, a reversal robust to vendor tier; one lineage (DeepSeek) never crosses, and two releases during the study window (Claude Opus 5, Gemini 3.6 Flash) land on the trend out-of-sample. A full-panel ablation localizes the resistance as a double dissociation: a synonym tag reproduces each model's response almost exactly (r=0.89), while planting the same preference without a tag produces resistance in no resistant model (stance effects +6 to +49; r=0.23 with tag effects). The resistance is keyed to the surface construction of a tacked-on agreement bid, not the user's stance -- a pattern-match, not a principle. And the tag's polarity matters more than its presence: swap one word -- "X is the better choice, maybe?" -- and agreement rises above the neutral baseline in 45 of 45 models (+19.6 points), with ten models affirming both mutually exclusive options at 90-100%. Agreement tracks how sure the user sounds, in opposite directions at the two poles. The instrument is one word, one dollar, and judge-free; run per release, it reads the field's anti-sycophancy training directly off model behavior.

</details>


### [235] [SyRuP: Enhancing System-Prompt Following via Reward-Guided Prediction in LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23991)
*Seoyeon Kim,Minjae Kang,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了SyRuP，一种提升大型语言模型（LLM）对system prompt（系统提示）遵循性的推理时控制框架，无需改动模型本体，只在解码过程中引入token级奖励重排序。实验表明SyRuP显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对系统提示的响应仅靠上下文学习，无法充分处理复杂或组合性的提示要求。大多数提升办法都要求重新训练模型或者增加推理阶段负担，实际应用受限，因此亟需一种无需修改模型、推理时轻量有效的控制方法。

Method: 提出SyRuP框架，在训练阶段通过基于系统提示的偏好对训练奖励头（cross-attention reward head），将提示作为独立记忆用于输出token级别的提示匹配分数。推理阶段输入token时，将基础LM分数与奖励信号、可选的对比信号结合，对top-k候选重排序。

Result: 在系统提示追随任务的各类基准测试中，SyRuP在提升提示遵循性的同时只引入了中等的推理成本，并在多项指标上优于基本提示和解码阶段基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式的token级别引导是提升LLM提示遵循性的一种有效、实用机制。SyRuP框架能在不改动基础模型的情况下，大幅提升系统提示追随表现，具备实际落地价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly controlled through system prompts that specify roles, styles, formats, and safety requirements. However, models follow these prompts only implicitly through in-context learning, which can be insufficient for complex or compositional prompts. Existing approaches often require model tuning or response-level reranking, limiting their practicality for lightweight inference-time control. We introduce SyRuP, a decoding-time framework for improving system-prompt adherence while keeping the base LM frozen. SyRuP trains a cross-attention reward head from system-prompt-conditioned preference pairs, treating the system prompt as a separate memory to produce token-level adherence scores. At inference, SyRuP reranks the base LM's top-k candidates by combining base logits with the learned reward signal and an optional contrastive signal capturing system-induced logit shifts. Experiments on system-prompt following benchmarks show that SyRuP consistently outperforms prompting and decoding-time baselines with moderate inference overhead. These results suggest that explicit token-level guidance is an effective and practical mechanism for reliable system-prompt following.

</details>


### [236] [MoLGE: Mixture of Language Group Experts for Efficient Scaling of Massively Multilingual Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24030)
*Sangmin Lee,Woojin Chung,Woongjib Choi,Hong-Goo Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Mixture of Language Group Experts（MoLGE）的多语言语音识别模型，在覆盖495种语言的多语言任务中，相较普通的多语言基线，MoLGE以参数增加极小的代价显著提升了识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涵盖数百种语言的大规模多语言ASR模型，面临多语言带来的模型能力分散问题，难以兼顾所有语言的鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 基于自监督语音模型（S3Ms），MoLGE将结构化的专家模块分配给语言相似的分组，并通过分层的低秩适应（LoRA）机制分别对声学及语言特征子模块进行特化建模，以提升参数利用效率。同时，探索基于语言学特征与数据驱动的分组策略对性能的影响。

Result: 在包含495种语言的多语言基准上，MoLGE在只微增参数的情况下，明显优于密集多语言基线方法。语言分组策略在音系和正字法两个维度都带来显著改进。

Conclusion: 采用结构化的语言专家分组，可有效提升多语言ASR系统的扩展性和单一语言性能，是大规模扩展多语言ASR覆盖范围的有效方法。

Abstract: Massively multilingual automatic speech recognition (ASR) models covering hundreds of languages must maintain robust performance across diverse linguistic and acoustic conditions. However, these models often encounter the curse of multilinguality, where model capacity is diluted across languages. To address this challenge, we propose Mixture of Language Group Experts (MoLGE), built upon speech self-supervised models (S3Ms). MoLGE assigns dedicated expert modules to clusters of similar languages, reducing the number of required submodules compared to conventional language-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) schemes. It further integrates a hierarchical Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) strategy into the disentangled acoustic and linguistic components of the S3M architecture, enabling efficient modeling of language-specific characteristics while maintaining parameter efficiency. Further, we investigate the impact of language grouping strategies based on both linguistic and data-driven criteria on overall performance, providing an interpretable perspective on how language structure influences scalability in multilingual speech systems. In experiments, we evaluate MoLGE on a multilingual benchmark encompassing 495 languages. Results demonstrate that MoLGE consistently outperforms dense multilingual baselines with a minimal increase in trainable parameters. Notably, these language grouping strategies yield substantial improvements for both phonetic and orthographic aspects of ASR modeling. Our findings suggest that structured language specialization provides an effective pathway for massively scaling language coverage of multilingual ASR.

</details>


### [237] [Pointer-Augmented Autoregressive Generation of Patent Claims with Joint Topology and Content Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24040)
*Yongmin Yoo,Zhangkai Wu,Longbing Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPG方法，在自回归生成专利主张时显式建模结构依赖，有效提升了生成结果的结构与内容一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归生成器只能输出扁平的标记序列，无法在生成过程 enforce（强制）层级约束，尤其是在专利主张自动生成任务中，这会导致生成主张集合的层级依赖缺失，影响专利权利要求的表达准确性和合规性。

Method: 提出SPG（结构感知型专利生成）方法。在自回归生成过程中添加指针机制预测每个从属主张的父节点；结合深度自适应范围正则项，引导模型在训练阶段学习结构信息。第二阶段，通过对模型自生成的不合格候选采用按违规程度加权的损失优化，引入负样本信号。实验基于Llama-3-8B-Instruct进行。

Result: SPG方法能够恢复79.0%的真实父节点链接，这一指标并未直接用于训练奖励。此外，前件一致性由0.292提升至0.478，显著优于等规模的有监督基线，专家评估也验证了改善。

Conclusion: SPG方法能显著提升专利主张生成的结构完整性和内容一致性，解决了自回归生成模型层级依赖表达能力不足的问题，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoders emit flat token sequences and cannot enforce hierarchical constraints across output segments, a limitation that becomes acute in patent claim generation, where a claim set forms a dependency forest whose scope must narrow monotonically with depth. Topology and content are mutually dependent: a dependent claim's wording must reflect its parent's scope, yet the parent must be chosen before that wording exists, so neither post-hoc parsing nor grammar-constrained decoding suffices. We propose SPG (Structure-aware Patent Generation), which predicts topology inside the autoregressive pass. A pointer head selects each dependent claim's parent, and its gradients, together with a depth-adaptive scope regularizer, reshape the shared decoder's representations during training. A second stage then applies a violation-weighted preference objective over self-generated deficient candidates, supplying the negative signal that granted-patent corpora lack. On HUPD-DCG, SPG on Llama-3-8B-Instruct recovers 79.0\% of gold parent links, a quantity its training reward never supervises, and raises antecedent consistency from 0.292 to 0.478 over a supervised baseline of equal scale, with expert evaluation corroborating these gains.

</details>


### [238] [LLM-Based vs. Lexicon-Based Sentiment Signals for Tail-Risk Detection in Meme Stocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24072)
*Paul Kilian,Markus Kleffmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了词典基础和大语言模型（LLM）基础的情感分析方法，用于从社交媒体中提取与市场相关的信号，并评估其与高波动性股票市场收益的关系，发现LLM方法能捕捉更复杂的情感信息，但预测能力在不同资产间差异较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来社交媒体对股票市场（特别是散户驱动的高度波动股票，如meme股）影响日益突出。如何从大量非结构化文本中有效提取与市场相关情感信号，是投资研究的热点。传统情感分析（如VADER词典法）已被广泛使用，但新一代LLM有望显著提升信号质量。作者希望实证比较两种方法在社交媒体金融信号提取上的能力。

Method: 作者收集Reddit论坛r/WallStreetBets关于GME、AMC和NOK三只meme股的数据，分别用VADER词典法和基于LLM的多维情感分析法构建对齐于股价的情感指标，LLM法可识别情感极性、多头情绪、讽刺倾向、话题相关性。使用相关性分析、OLS回归、ROC-AUC方向分类、分位点预警等方法，系统评估两类指标与市场表现的关系。

Result: LLM产生的情感指标在多维度上更丰富，且在资产层面展现更强的统计结构特征，对极端正收益事件的预警表现出一定优势。但情感信号与实际市场波动的关系在不同股票之间表现出明显异质性，提示其预测能力缺乏普适性和稳定性。

Conclusion: LLM方法能更全面准确地表征散户社交语言在市场中的多样性，但这种细腻的文本信息在零散、情绪化主导的股票市场中，并不一定稳定转化为可靠的预测信号。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical comparison of lexicon-based and Large Language Model (LLM)-based sentiment analysis for extracting market-relevant signals from social media discourse in highly volatile equity markets. Using Reddit data from r/WallStreetBets and focusing on meme stocks (GME, AMC, NOK), we construct time-aligned sentiment indicators and evaluate their relationship with market returns, with particular attention to extreme positive return events in the upper tail of the return distribution. The LLM-based approach generates multidimensional sentiment representations capturing emotional polarity, bullishness, sarcasm likelihood, and topical relevance, whereas the baseline relies on the VADER lexicon-based model. We evaluate both approaches using lead/lag correlation analysis, OLS regression, ROC-AUC-based directional classification, and a quantile-based early-warning framework. The results indicate that LLM-derived indicators provide a richer multidimensional representation and exhibit stronger asset-specific statistical structure than the lexicon-based baseline. However, their relationship with market movements remains heterogeneous across assets, suggesting that increased linguistic expressiveness does not necessarily translate into stable forecasting performance in retail-driven volatility regimes.

</details>


### [239] [BioSentinel at EXIST 2026: Soft-Label Optimization with XLM-RoBERTa for Sexism Intent Classification in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24137)
*Chandru Munisamy,Karthikeya Raguveer,Alapan Kuila*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文介绍了BioSentinel团队在CLEF 2026 EXIST 2.2任务（判断meme的传播意图）中的参赛经历，采用xlm-roberta-base模型，结合KL散度和交叉熵损失分别优化软硬标签，取得中等排名，并分析了模型设计与标注分歧的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EXIST 2026任务要求判断meme的传播意图，并在标注分歧较大的主观NLP任务中提升模型预测能力。传统方法对主观任务的分歧处理有限，如何结合软标签与硬标签进行有效建模，是提升模型性能的重要动力。

Method: 使用xlm-roberta-base模型，在训练时利用融合KL散度（针对软标签概率分布）与加权交叉熵（针对硬标签）的复合损失函数。分别面向硬标签和软标签进行预测，并通过消融实验和温度分析探索最优模型结构及参数设置。

Result: 在官方测试集上，该系统软标签得分（ICM-Soft-Norm）为0.3229，硬标签得分（ICM-Norm）为0.3778，硬标签F1为0.4236，分别在118个和187个提交中排名第40和第49。消融实验显示KL损失对软标签预测有效，交叉熵损失对硬标签提升明显。

Conclusion: 研究证明，复合损失优化方法有助于在多标注主观NLP任务里提升模型表现。对数据集特性和分歧的分析有助于理解如何制定更适合实际主观任务的模型与评测方法。

Abstract: This paper describes the BioSentinel team's participation in EXIST 2026 Task 2.2: Source Intention in Memes, part of the CLEF 2026 evaluation campaign. The task requires classifying the communicative intent behind memes as direct, judgemental, or no (non-sexist), under a Learning with Disagreement (Le-Wi-Di) paradigm that mandates both hard-label and soft-label (probability distribution) predictions. We present a text-centric approach built on xlm-roberta-base (270M parameters) trained with a composite loss function combining KL divergence on soft annotator distributions and weighted cross-entropy on hard labels. On the official test set, the system achieved an ICM-Soft-Norm of 0.3229 and ICM-Norm of 0.3778, with a hard F1-score of 0.4236, ranking 40th (out of 118 submissions) in the soft-soft evaluation and 49th (out of 187 submissions) in the hard-hard evaluation. We provide an analysis of the dataset characteristics, exploratory larger-architecture runs, and the role of annotator disagreement in shaping model design for subjective NLP tasks. Ablation results show that KL loss improves soft-label metrics, while CE loss improves hard-label accuracy. We also report a separate validation-set temperature analysis.

</details>
