### [240] [Looking for Affect in Spontaneous Finnish Speech through Linguistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24155)
*Kalle Lahtinen,Liisa Mustanoja,Okko Räsänen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了语音中的文本和音频特征在芬兰语情感感知（愉悦度和唤醒度）预测中的作用，结果表明两者结合对愉悦度预测更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然现有研究表明声学和内容特征都与语音情感感知有关，但两者在感知过程中的相对贡献尚不清楚，特别是在芬兰语的研究中大多只关注单一特征。

Method: 作者采用了一个新发布的芬兰语自发性情感语音语料库，系统性地将文本和音频特征结合，用于建模和回归分析人的愉悦度和唤醒度感知，并对比了单独和组合特征的效果。

Result: 实验表明，文本和音频特征结合可以提高愉悦度（valence）回归的准确性，但对唤醒度（arousal）回归没有显著互补提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合特征对于愉悦度的建模优于单一特征，这与其他语言的结论一致，为芬兰语的自发性情感语音感知提供了新的数据和见解。

Abstract: Existing research on affect in speech has shown how acoustic surface characteristics and content-related linguistic aspects of speech both relate to perceived emotional arousal and valence. However, it is not clear what the relative contributions of these two factors are in the perceptual process. This is especially true for Finnish, for which most existing studies focus on either acoustic-phonetic or text analysis. This paper presents a study where we systematically explore the combinatory role of text- and audio-based features in modeling the human perception of valence and arousal using a newly released affective speech corpus for spontaneous Finnish. We show that the combination of text- and audio-based features improves valence regression results over the individual modalities, whereas for arousal regression the complementary effect is not substantial. The results support prior findings from other languages, providing new data and knowledge on spontaneous Finnish speech.

</details>


### [241] [Where Quality Breaks in Compressed Short-Text Generation: Staged Bottleneck Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24176)
*Alexey Gavrilov,Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了压缩简短文本生成系统可能出现的两种主要失效模式，并提出了一种分阶段诊断方法以明确质量瓶颈在哪一环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前压缩文本生成模型存在整体性能瓶颈，但不易追踪其根源。一些研究未能区分是码器（codec）丢失信息还是潜在生成器（latent generator）弱导致了质量下降，从而可能在错误组件上消耗算力和研究精力。因此有必要明确区分各组件失效贡献。

Method: 作者设定了一个受控实验环境——64-to-16 TinyStories案例，包含分层VQ-VAE-2码器和掩码离散扩散生成器（MDLM）。采用分阶段验证协议，独立评估码器重构保真度、潜在生成质量，并借助外部GPT-2评分器和几何语义指标综合评判各环节表现。

Result: 仅码器重构步骤就大幅增长困惑度，表明性能损失主要在生成前已发生。使用MDLM在码空间上的生成质量优于token空间，显著降低多项困惑度指标。几何感知正则化虽改善了潜在空间度量，但未提升解码文本质量。

Conclusion: 本文主要贡献是提出了可复用的分阶段诊断流程，并证明在该设定下，码器保真度决定了最终文本质量上限，对研究者确定优化重点具有指导意义。

Abstract: Compressed short-text generators can fail in two different places: the codec may discard information before generation starts, or the latent generator may produce weak codes. Without separating these failure modes, researchers can spend compute improving the wrong component. We study this problem in a controlled 64-to-16 TinyStories case study built from a hierarchical VQ-VAE-2 codec and a masked discrete diffusion generator (MDLM). We use a staged validation protocol that separates codec reconstruction fidelity, latent generation quality, and auxiliary latent diagnostics under one shared external GPT-2 scorer, while reporting complementary semantic metrics for the geometry study. In the tested configuration, codec reconstruction alone raises median external perplexity from 15.17 to 27.36 (+80.4%) and p95 from 25.10 to 98.91 (+294.1%), showing that the dominant quality loss appears before latent generation begins. Under the same scorer, code-space MDLM remains materially stronger than token-space diffusion, reducing mean, median, and p95 by 32.9%, 30.9%, and 36.6%, respectively. Geometry-aware regularization improves local latent proxies but does not improve decoded-text metrics in the available runs. The contribution is methodological rather than algorithmic: the paper presents a reusable staged diagnosis for one concrete pipeline and shows that, in this setting, codec fidelity rather than latent denoising sets the practical quality ceiling.

</details>


### [242] [StanceFlip: A Comprehensive Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Multimodal Conversational Stance Flipping Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24191)
*Heyan Chai,Xin Li,Wenjie Wang,Jianyang Qin,Chaoyang Li,Lu Wang,Hao Chen,Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了StanceFlip，多模态对话立场反转的基准数据集，及其专用的ConStaFF方法框架，实现了更细致的立场检测与归因任务，取得了明显优于现有多模态大模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态对话立场检测基准存在三大不足：无法捕捉立场的动态演变（如立场反转）、情感与逻辑推理难以区分、忽视多模态线索在分析讽刺等语用歧义中的重要作用。

Method: 提出StanceFlip基准，覆盖五种模态与多场景，并设立两个新子任务：1）多模态立场六元组抽取，包括持有者、目标、情感、情绪、立场、推理等要素；2）动态立场反转归因，追踪对话中的立场反转及其诱因。提出了基于大语言模型的ConStaFF框架，通过“立场思考”推理和自反式验证机制，实现结构化建模与准确归因。ToS推理将流程分解为多个认知角色，分别负责命题制定、跨模态冲突解决与历史立场轨迹推断。

Result: 在六元组抽取与立场反转归因两项任务上，ConStaFF在实验中显著优于现有主流多模态大模型基线，达到了当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 结合新颖的数据集与方法后，论文在多模态对话场景下实现了对立场动态变化的细致建模和归因，促进了更深入的对话理解。

Abstract: Conversational stance detection has shifted from static text analysis to dynamic multimodal modeling. However, existing benchmarks exhibit three key limitations: failure to capture the dynamic evolution of beliefs, particularly during stance reversals; difficulty in disentangling affective states from logical reasoning; and neglect of the critical role of multimodal cues in resolving pragmatic ambiguities such as sarcasm. To address these limitations, we propose StanceFlip, a benchmark designed for multimodal conversational stance flipping forecasting over multi-turn dialogues across five modalities and multi-scenarios, which includes two novel subtasks: 1) Multimodal Stance Sextuple Extraction, extracting holder, target, emotion, sentiment, stance, and rationale as static state snapshots of dialogue to capture fine-grained cognitive structures. 2) Dynamic Stance Flip Attribution, tracking stance reversals across the conversation and identifying their underlying triggers. Alongside the dataset, we propose a dedicated framework, named ConStaFF, for Multimodal Conversational Stance Flipping Forecasting (MCSFF). Built upon a large language model, ConStaFF performs end-to-end stance reasoning, with a Thought-of-Stance (ToS) reasoning framework and a self-reflective verification mechanism integrated for structured stance modeling and faithful flip attribution. Specifically, ToS decomposes the reasoning process into specialized cognitive personas to formulate target propositions, resolve cross-modal conflicts, and infer historical stance trajectories. Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on both sextuple extraction and flip-trigger attribution, outperforming strong multimodal large language model baselines by substantial margins.

</details>


### [243] [A New Role for Relevance: Guiding Corpus Interaction in Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24223)
*Jiangnan Li,Yuqing Li,Mo Yu,Jinchao Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为RARG（Relevance-Aware RipGrep Search Agent）的新型检索代理，通过结合相关性信息实现了更高效和准确的语料交互搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索代理主要根据文档相关性选取内容，但面对复杂问题时，仅凭文档相关性无法定位、组合和验证证据。直接语料交互（DCI）能支持细粒度检索，但缺乏相关性指导导致检索效率不高。研究动机是赋予细粒度搜索以相关性优先级，提高复杂问答或推理任务的准确性与效率。

Method: 作者提出了RARG方法：在细粒度grep式语料探索时，利用相关性信息对文档和片段进行排序，先遍历全局更相关的文档，并在文档内部优先呈现与查询更相关的段落，再对grep检索得到的片段重排名，从而减少冗余、提高重要信息曝光速度。

Result: 在涉及复杂浏览问答和推理密集型检索的任务上，RARG在准确率和效率之间实现了更优权衡，超过了传统基于召回和直接交互的检索代理。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将相关性信号引入细粒度交互搜索可显著加速且提升机器检索问答系统的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Relevance is a query-dependent estimate of whether a document or excerpt contains useful evidence. Existing retrieval agents use relevance to select top-$k$ content, but document relevance alone cannot localize, compose, or verify the evidence required by complex questions. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) enables such fine-grained operations through grep-style exploration, but its relevance-agnostic search can expose useful clues late and delay convergence. Recent advances use relevance to narrow the corpus into a working space for interaction. Once interaction begins, however, relevance still does not directly guide which documents grep searches first or distinguish informative excerpts from a broad set of matches to let LLMs see them first. We introduce the Relevance-Aware RipGrep Search Agent (RARG), which turns relevance into an execution prior for corpus interaction. RARG provides coarse-to-fine relevance guidance: it orders documents for sequential 'ripgrep' traversal to expose globally relevant clues earlier, initializes promising entry points with query-relevant paragraphs, and reranks grep matches to surface informative excerpts that document-level ranking may otherwise obscure. Across challenging browse question answering and reasoning-intensive retrieval, RARG improves the accuracy--efficiency frontier over retrieval-based and direct-interaction agents. These results demonstrate that relevance-aware interaction enables faster and more reliable search convergence.

</details>


### [244] [CAGE: Cognitive Attribution Graphs for Faithful Inline Citation Generation in Long-Form Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24236)
*Zhichao Yan,Shizhao Li,Jiapu Wang,Haoran Luo,Qingang Zhang,Jiaoyan Chen,Ru Li,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的长文本问答系统CAGE，通过显式地建立认知归因图，提升问答系统引用证明的准确性和可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长文本问答系统虽然能插入引用，但引用往往只与话题相关，却不足以支持具体论据。解决这一难题，有助于提升模型输出的可验证性和学术严谨性。

Method: 提出CAGE（Cognitive Attribution Graphs for Citation Generation），包括两个阶段：先利用认知归因图，实现答案单元与支持文档之间的明确对应关系；再通过结构化推理模型，将这些对应关系体现在带引文的句子中。两步提升支持证据与生成内容的精准配对。

Result: 在ASQA、ELI5和ExpertQA等数据集上，CAGE在准确性和引用质量方面均取得了新的最佳表现，说明该方法能更有效地收敛归因空间和引导内容生成。

Conclusion: CAGE通过显式地图和结构化推理，为长文本问答生成提供了更清晰、可靠的证据链，显著提升了输出可验证性和引用的可溯源性。

Abstract: Long-form question answering increasingly relies on retrieved evidence to make LLM outputs verifiable, with inline citations tracing claims to source documents. However, existing systems often attach citations that are topically related but insufficient to support their claims. We identify attribution ambiguity as a structural challenge: end-to-end generation must implicitly resolve combinatorial claim--document assignments, obscuring evidential boundaries and increasing the risk of evidence-boundary overrun, where claims exceed cited support. To address this challenge, we propose CAGE (Cognitive Attribution Graphs for Citation Generation), a two-stage framework that introduces an explicit cognitive attribution map before answer generation. CAGE first trains a plug-and-play Cognitive Map Induction Model to construct answer-centered support subgraphs, aligning each semantic answer unit with supporting documents through explicit relations. A Structured Citation Reasoning Model then realizes these units as sentence-level claims with map-aligned citations. Experiments on ASQA, ELI5, and ExpertQA show that CAGE achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of attribution-space contraction and map-guided citation generation.

</details>


### [245] [Accuracy Hides How Language Models Fail: Measuring Failure States Under Matched Output Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24268)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两层评估框架，将语言模型输出的“可执行状态”（例如终止状态、答案是否暴露、可解析性、完成长度等）与“答案正确性”解耦，实现更细致和公正的评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型的基准测试将“能否产生有效输出”和“答案是否正确”混为一谈，用单独的准确率评分，这可能掩盖模型实际性能，不足以反映执行机制和评测策略之间的区别。

Method: 提出“两层评估框架”，第一层评估输出是否进入可评判状态（如终止、答案暴露、可解析等），第二层才由评分者判断答案是否正确。采用Qwen和DeepSeek两种模型，在MATH和ARC-Challenge数据集上设定不同token上限，检验两种评估分离对准确率和输出混合类型的影响，并进行有覆盖性的目标性验证。

Result: 同样的token上限下，Qwen和DeepSeek在输出中终止但未给出最终答案的情况分布大不相同；在高token上限时终止问题减少。此外，评估策略的改变能显著影响准确率结论，说明原有准确率评价掺杂了执行状态与评测政策的混杂效应。

Conclusion: 单一准确率评分混淆了输出状态分布与答案认证策略。推荐未来测试报告输出状态、验证覆盖率和评价者来源，使评测更科学透明。

Abstract: Language-model benchmarks collapse two distinct measurement questions into a single accuracy score: whether a response reached an evaluable state, and whether its answer was judged correct. We introduce a two-layer evaluation framework that separates scorer-independent execution evidence, including termination, answer exposure, parseability, and completion length, from scorer-dependent correctness. Across 2,550 outputs from five fixed Qwen and DeepSeek configurations on MATH and ARC-Challenge, matched 2,048-token limits produce sharply different execution mixtures: 49 of 450 Qwen MATH outputs terminate without a final answer, compared with 5 of 300 DeepSeek MATH outputs and none of the 750 ARC outputs. Among the same 300 DeepSeek MATH question-model pairs, no missing-final length termination is observed at 8,192 tokens. A coverage-audited targeted verification study further shows that candidate-selection and aggregation policies can substantially alter comparative accuracy estimates. These results demonstrate that accuracy conflates execution case mix with verification policy. Evaluations of test-time methods should therefore report pre-intervention execution states, verification coverage, and scorer provenance alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [246] [INS-ActBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Assessing Professional Actuarial Capability of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24273)
*Changyu Chen,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了INS-ActBench基准，用于全面评估大语言模型（LLMs）在精算领域的专业能力，涵盖知识、案例推理和工具实践三个方面，并揭示了当前LLMs在复杂、实际精算任务上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多为分散评估LLMs在金融领域的知识、数值推理或工具使用等单一能力，难以覆盖实际专业工作对情境、可审计性及工具集成的高要求。作者希望通过构建更贴近专业场景的综合性精算评测，推动LLMs在该领域的实际应用。

Method: INS-ActBench包含来自16个精算协会的12050道公开考试及样题，分为知识（INS-Act-Know）、案例推理（INS-Act-Case）、和可验证工具实践（INS-Act-Practice）三类。作者在九个代表性LLMs及人类专家上进行系统对比实验。

Result: 实验显示，先进LLMs在标准化精算知识题表现优异，但在复杂案例推理、工具应用（如表格与R代码）及需兼顾不同司法区实践时远落后于人类专家。

Conclusion: INS-ActBench为未来精算领域LLMs的研究与开发奠定了可复现、系统的评测基础，有助于推动LLMs向更可靠、更实用的专业支持工具发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential in financial reasoning, but existing benchmarks often evaluate domain knowledge, numerical reasoning, long-context understanding, and tool use in separate settings. This limits their ability to assess realistic professional workflows that require auditable, context-grounded, and tool-executable decisions. We introduce \textbf{INS-ActBench}, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating professional actuarial capability in LLMs. INS-ActBench contains 12,050 Q\&A pairs from public exams and sample questions released by 16 actuarial associations. It covers three subsets: \textbf{INS-Act-Know} for standardized actuarial knowledge, \textbf{INS-Act-Case} for long-context insurance case reasoning, and \textbf{INS-Act-Practice} for spreadsheet and R-code tasks with verifiable numerical outputs. Experiments on nine representative LLMs and human actuarial experts reveal a clear capability boundary: frontier LLMs perform strongly on standardized knowledge, but remain much weaker in case reasoning, tool-based workflows, and jurisdiction-sensitive practice. INS-ActBench provides a reproducible foundation for developing actuarial LLMs toward reliable professional assistance. The code is available at https://github.com/FDU-INS/INS-ActBench.

</details>


### [247] [The Tokenizer Tax: Quantifying and Explaining the Cross-Lingual Cost of Subword Tokenization for Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24276)
*Priyansh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了主流大语言模型常用的分词器对印度语言处理中的‘分词税’，发现印地语等的token数远高于英文，使得实际可用上下文窗口大幅缩减，部分模型可以显著缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大语言模型的分词器主要基于英语语料训练，对非英语语言，尤其是印度语言，可能带来不公平的‘分词税’，从而影响模型可用性与公平性。

Method: 基于FLORES-200多语种平行语料，对六种主流分词器在十四种印度语言上的分词率（tokenization fertility）进行对比，分析byte-pair编码失败与分词税的关系，并进一步测试多语种分词器缓解该问题的能力。还衡量实际文档表达能力和阅读理解任务表现的相关性。

Result: 以GPT分词器（cl100k_base）为例，印度语言平均分词税为8倍，最高达13倍，极大缩减实际语义承载能力。主要因字节对合并失败，多语种分词器如XLM-R可将分词税降低73%。文档实际可保留内容远少于英文，同步发现阅读理解表现更受资源丰富度影响。

Conclusion: 当前主流分词器设计对印度等非英语语言天然不公，但并非技术不可解决。采用多语种优化的分词器能显著降低分词税，有助于提升多语种公平性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) process text through subword tokenizers rather than directly reading characters or words. Because these tokenizers are trained predominantly on English-centric corpora, they introduce a systematic and often overlooked disadvantage for many non-English languages. In this work, we quantify this tokenizer tax for Indian languages using the FLORES-200 parallel corpus, measuring tokenization fertility across six widely used tokenizers and fourteen languages. Under cl100k_base (used by GPT-3.5 and GPT-4), Indian languages experience an average 8.0x tokenization tax relative to English, reaching 13.0x for Malayalam, reducing the effective context window to as little as 12% of that available to English users for equivalent semantic content. We identify the primary mechanism behind this disparity: failed byte-pair merges that leave text fragmented into single-byte tokens, with merge failure strongly correlating with tokenizer tax (Pearson r = 0.89). We further show that this phenomenon is not an inherent property of Indic scripts but a consequence of tokenizer design. Multilingual tokenizers such as XLM-R and OpenAI's o200k_base reduce the average Indic tokenizer tax by 73%, demonstrating that the disparity is largely remediable. Beyond token statistics, we quantify a practical consequence by showing that, under fixed context budgets, Indian-language documents preserve substantially less original content than equivalent English documents. Finally, we examine the relationship between tokenizer fertility and reading comprehension performance on the Belebele benchmark, finding that the apparent correlation is largely explained by language resource availability rather than tokenizer behavior alone.

</details>


### [248] [Self-Authored Verification Is Unreliable in Heuristic Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24300)
*Diandian Guo,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Yingqi Wang,Yueshan Wang,Dakui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了自主自我改进智能体在依赖自我验证信号时，部署性能与验证表现之间出现偏差的问题，并提出了SEAL机制以缓解该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体常通过自我修改策略和规则以增强能力，但当前做法多自我验证，可能导致测试表现良好而实际应用效果下降。因此，作者希望探索自我验证机制失败的情形，并提出解决方案。

Method: 文章提出了Verifier–Deployment Gap（验证-部署差距）概念，并设计了SEAL（Sealed Exogenous Acceptance Loop）机制：智能体仍可自测，但其策略修改必须通过一个不可控的外部审计，该机制通过对比候选与现任方案，仅反馈通过/未通过信号，且若回退至更低性能则保留现任状态。实验覆盖了六个模型与三个随机种子。

Result: 实验表明，依赖自写测试的基线方法常常在启发式学习任务中出现验证-部署偏差，尤其是较弱的智能体容易退化性能而未被自测发现。SEAL机制能有效避免这种问题，性能超过未保护的基线方法。

Conclusion: 可靠的自主智能体自我改进，不必完全抛弃自我验证，但需引入至少一个智能体无法干预的外部部署验收信号以防止真正的性能退化。

Abstract: Self-improving agents accumulate capability by repeatedly rewriting procedural policies, controllers, or heuristic rules. They typically rely on self-authored tests or metrics to decide whether to accept subsequent edits. The agent controls both the optimized object and its verifier. As a result, self-assigned scores can remain near perfect while real deployment performance degrades or stays low. We study this problem through the verifier--deployment gap. This gap refers to the discrepancy between an agent's self-authored verification signal and a sealed deployment evaluation that the agent cannot observe or access. We ask how self-authored verification fails under iterative policy-and-test rewriting, how the failure changes with capability, and how little exogenous trust is sufficient to prevent real regressions from being deployed. To address this problem, we introduce a Sealed Exogenous Acceptance Loop (SEAL). SEAL retains self-authored tests but compares each candidate with the incumbent through a fixed harness-side audit. The agent cannot author or inspect the audit, receives only accept/reject, and the whole incumbent state is retained after a clear regression. Our experiments show that this problem often appears in heuristic learning settings. These settings require trial-and-error discovery of the target objective. We further find that failures of self-written verification are stratified by capability. Weaker agents tend to damage previously acquired strategies behind easy self-tests. Stronger agents are more stable, but they still mismeasure the deployment distribution. Standard self-written constraints do not reliably close this gap. In contrast, SEAL outperforms unprotected baselines across six models and three random seeds. Reliable self-improvement need not abandon self-verification, but it requires at least one deployment-acceptance signal outside the agent's control.

</details>


### [249] [Rethinking the Generation Order of Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24306)
*Kai Syun Hou,James Kwok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种新型采样方法PARD，用于提升Block Diffusion Language Models（BDLMs）的生成表现，在生成质量和速度之间实现了优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散语言模型具备灵活的任意顺序生成能力，但已有采样方法主要针对较早的掩码扩散模型（MDM）设计，难以发挥新型BDLM的优势，因此有必要研究更适合BDLM采样特性的高效方法。

Method: 作者分析了BDLM天然更适应从左到右顺序解码，并据此提出无需训练的采样方法——Parallel Autoregressive Decoding（PARD），该方法保持了从左到右的unmask顺序，但可并行提交token，从而在效率和效果间取得平衡。

Result: 大量实验表明，PARD在生成质量上始终优于当前主要的并行采样器，并且在生成速度上显著快于纯自回归采样，仅有极小的质量损失。

Conclusion: PARD为BDLM提供了更高效、高质量的采样选择，为扩散语言模型的实际应用带来新的可能性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models enable flexible arbitrary-order generation, but existing sampling methods are mostly designed for early masked diffusion models (MDMs). In this work, we study sampling for recent block diffusion language models (BDLMs). We show empirically and analytically that these models are naturally more aligned with left-to-right decoding than MDMs. Based on this observation, we propose Parallel Autoregressive Decoding (PARD), a simple training-free sampling method that preserves left-to-right unmasking structure while allowing parallel token commitment. Extensive experiments show that PARD consistently outperforms existing parallel samplers in generation quality, while achieving substantial speedups over pure AR decoding with only a small quality gap.

</details>


### [250] [CONSISTRE: A Unified Consistency-Aware Framework for Document-Level Relation Extraction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24312)
*Mingxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于文档级关系抽取（DocRE）的CONSISTRE一致性框架，通过推理时和训练时两条路径增强预测一致性，有效缓解大语言模型在关系抽取任务中的基础约束违背及矛盾问题。实验表明方法在DocRED上优于基线，并提升了开源模型的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在文档级关系抽取任务中虽能进行推理，但预测往往独立处理每个三元组，容易违背诸如传递性、对称性、功能唯一性等基本关系约束，从而导致结果矛盾且缺乏可靠性。

Method: 提出CONSISTRE一致性感知框架，包括两条路径：1）推理时路径，通过约束提示、校验和自反思，改进黑盒LLM的预测，无需精调；2）训练时路径，将一致性知识通过知识蒸馏及强化学习灌输进开源小模型，利用教师模型的推理轨迹与联合奖励机制对学生模型进行微调和对齐。

Result: 在DocRED数据集实验中，两条路径均优于各自基线。其中推理时路径在不开源LLM上获得有竞争力F1，训练时路径则大幅缩小了7-8B开源模型与SOTA专有LLM的性能差距且推理成本更低。消融实验证实一致性建模可减少矛盾，提升模型可靠性。

Conclusion: CONSISTRE通过推理与训练层面对一致性进行融合与优化，不仅提升了LLM在DocRE任务的准确性和可靠性，也为API与本地部署两类场景提供了一致解决方案，验证了一致性约束在信息抽取中的关键作用。

Abstract: Document-level relation extraction (DocRE) aims to extract relations among multiple entities across extended contexts while maintaining consistency across predicted triples. Although large language models (LLMs) show remarkable reasoning capabilities in information extraction, their predictions are typically generated independently for each candidate triple and may violate fundamental relational constraints such as transitivity, symmetry, and functional uniqueness, leading to contradictory and unreliable outputs. We propose CONSISTRE, a unified consistency-aware framework for DocRE that addresses this limitation through two complementary tracks. The first operates at inference time for black-box LLMs, combining constraint-aware prompting, constraint-based verification, and iterative self-reflection to refine predictions without task-specific fine-tuning. The second injects consistency knowledge into smaller open-source models via a knowledge distillation and reinforcement learning pipeline: reasoning traces from a powerful teacher are distilled into a student via supervised fine-tuning, followed by GRPO alignment using a composite reward that jointly optimizes extraction performance and relational consistency. Together, the two tracks cover both API-accessible and locally deployable scenarios under a unified consistency formulation. Experiments on DocRED show that both tracks outperform their baselines, with the inference-time track achieving competitive F1 using off-the-shelf black-box LLMs and the training-time track substantially narrowing the gap between 7--8B open-source models and state-of-the-art proprietary LLMs at a fraction of their inference cost. Ablation studies confirm that explicit consistency modeling mitigates relational contradictions and enhances the reliability of LLM-based DocRE across both deployment paradigms.

</details>


### [251] [Cross-Attention Calibrated Deduplication for Retrieval-Augmented Generation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24332)
*Phuong Le Huy,Nam H. Nguyen,Quan V. Dang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新去重方法CACD，结合cross-encoder和信息得分，实现RAG中的高效语义去重，比主流方法更快且去重效果好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的数据分块（chunking）方法易导致冗余，增加召回库体积，影响检索速度。主流的余弦相似度阈值法只用单一向量，容易丢失细粒度信息，无法精准区分重复与主题相同但内容不同的块，因此需要更细致的去重方案。

Method: CACD方法用cross-encoder对比新chunk与已保留块，保存细粒度token信息；引入注意力熵计算的新信息分数（NIS）；采用多候选多数投票机制而非单一最佳匹配。对比五种过滤方法、九种chunk策略和18种配置，并在SQuAD验证集上测试。

Result: CACD平均去除9.75%的块，效果和其他语义级方法接近，远超精确匹配法。平均处理时间51秒，比最强基线（NERExact, 69.6秒）快27%，比余弦相似度快7倍。

Conclusion: CACD在SQuAD 1.1数据集上的效果和速度均优于主流方法，但结论主要基于单数据集测试，后续需更广泛评估。

Abstract: Common chunking strategies in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems often create redundant chunks. These redundant chunks make the vector database bigger and slow down retrieval. A common fix is cosine-similarity thresholding. This method reduces each chunk to a single vector, then compares vectors using a similarity score. But a single vector can lose the fine-grained, token-level detail needed to tell a true duplicate apart from a chunk that just shares the same topic. We propose Cross-Attention Calibrated Deduplication (CACD). CACD checks each new chunk against an in-memory pool of chunks already kept, using a cross-encoder instead of a single pooled vector. This keeps token-level detail all the way to the final comparison. CACD combines three parts: the cross-encoder comparison itself, a New Information Score (NIS) that measures how much of a chunk is not explained by a candidate already kept, and a majority vote across several candidates rather than a single best match. NIS is calculated from the attention entropy of the cross-encoder. We tested CACD against five existing filtering methods, nine chunking strategies, and 18 configurations, all on the full SQuAD 1.1 validation set. In our experiments, CACD removes 9.75% of chunks on average. This drop rate is close to other semantic-level methods, and much higher than exact-match filters, which barely remove anything. In these experiments, CACD also processes each configuration in 51.0 seconds on average, about 27% faster than the strongest baseline, NERExact (69.6s), and about 7x faster than cosine-similarity filtering (356.7s). These results come from a single dataset, so we present them as an early comparison, not a general claim. Code for the baseline evaluation and for CACD is available at https://github.com/lehuyphuong/rag_bench and https://github.com/lehuyphuong/cacd_dedup.

</details>


### [252] [Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models as Components of Cognitive Computing architecture for Regulatory Knowledge Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24352)
*Dariusz Nowak-Nova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文验证了将本地大语言模型（LLM）与RAG架构结合，可显著增强生成内容的可靠性、可追溯性和规范性，适用于法规管理等需高可靠性的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM虽强大，但仅作为生成模型在专业合规领域存在事实准确性和内容可追溯性不足的问题。作者希望通过结合RAG架构，提升其在复杂法规解读和管理中的应用价值。

Method: 提出基于本地部署LLM（如Bielik、PLLuM）的混合架构方案，结合外部知识库，采用RAG实现受控检索和信息溯源。通过Ollama和LM Studio等执行环境，并在消费级硬件上进行实验验证。

Result: 结合RAG的本地LLM在生成文本的事实一致性、领域特异性和规范精准性三方面显著提升，降低了无依据生成内容的风险，并实现了内容可审核与动态知识更新。

Conclusion: 带RAG的本地部署LLM不应仅看作文字生成工具，而应视为可嵌入认知计算基础设施中的语义处理模块，尤其适合高法规和信息变动环境下的合规和决策支持。

Abstract: The aim of this article is to verify whether integrating large language models (LLMs) with the Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architecture enables their transformation from standalone generative models into components of cognitive computing infrastructure with enhanced epistemic reliability. The study proposes an architectural approach based on locally deployed LLMs operating in on-premises environments without high-end GPU accelerators and examines their applicability in supporting regulatory management processes requiring continuous analysis and interpretation of legal acts. The proposed solution combines local LLMs with external knowledge repositories, creating a hybrid cognitive architecture in which the language model performs semantic interpretation while the RAG layer provides controlled knowledge retrieval, contextualization, and traceability of information sources. The implementation was validated using the Ollama and LM Studio execution environments together with the Polish language models Bielik and PLLuM running on consumer-class hardware. The results demonstrate that augmenting LLMs with RAG significantly improves the factual consistency, domain specificity and normative precision of generated texts while reducing the risk of unsupported content generation. Furthermore, the study shows that integrating RAG introduces auditability, controlled knowledge management and dynamic updating of regulatory information without retraining the language model. The findings indicate that locally deployed LLMs enhanced with RAG should be regarded not merely as text generation tools but as semantic processing modules within cognitive computing infrastructures supporting regulatory compliance and organizational decision-making in environments characterized by high legal and informational volatility.

</details>


### [253] [Keep It InMind: Benchmarking the Implicit-Association Blind Spot in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24368)
*Ruizhe Li,Mingxuan Du,Benfeng Xu,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 文章指出现有长期记忆系统存在“隐含关联盲点”问题，即存储的事实与查询之间缺乏直接文本联系时，检索系统难以发现间接关联。作者提出新的InMind基准体系来量化并分析该问题。实验显示，即使记忆可直接回忆，但通过检索关联信息回答问题的能力极弱，暴露出模型接口本身的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于解决当前长期记忆系统对隐性关系检索能力极度不足的问题，尤其在需要常识或世界知识场景下，检索系统无法发现非表面相似的答案资讯，限制了其应用效果。

Method: 1. 定义并形式化了“隐含关联盲点”（implicit-association blind spot）问题。2. 构建了包含125项任务的专家验证基准InMind，覆盖10个生活领域，大部分任务可溯源并有对照组，以区分知识存储缺失、桥接知识缺失、检索接口三类原因。3. 对比创新性记忆检索系统（如向量、图结构、智能体方法）与直接上下文提供事实时的性能差异。

Result: 如果将关键事实直接置于上下文，模型可正确回答84%的间接查询。但要求模型通过记忆系统检索同样事实时，无论采用何种先进记忆系统，最佳仅达14.4%的间接查询正确率，尽管对直接事实能100%回忆。即便提升嵌入维度，也仅少量提升性能，核心差距依然存在。而通过让记忆在查询前可见，则能显著缓解该差距。

Conclusion: 长期记忆系统在“如何使相关事实在理解查询前持续可用”这一核心环节仍面临重大挑战。要实现更自然的世界知识利用，关键突破点应集中在优化检索接口与事实可见性决策。InMind基准为该难题的定量评估和未来算法创新提供了工具。

Abstract: Long-term memory systems store what a user says in an external store and retrieve it when a related query arrives. This interface rests on an assumption so natural that it is rarely stated: a memory that is needed will resemble the query that needs it. World knowledge breaks the assumption. A tree-nut allergy should change the answer to a macaron request through their almond-flour ingredient, yet the two texts share no cue a retriever can see. We call this failure mode the implicit-association blind spot and introduce InMind, a 125-task, expert-verified benchmark spanning ten life domains, with 113 tasks grounded in citable public sources. Its paired controls separate three explanations that existing evaluations conflate: the fact was never stored, the model lacks the bridging knowledge, or the fact was stored and never surfaced. The verdict is clean. With the decisive memory placed in context, the backbone answers 84.0 percent of indirect queries; when the same memory must be retrieved, six vector, graph, and agentic memory systems reach at most 14.4 percent, even though they recall the same facts on demand at up to 100 percent. An embedding with eight times the dimensionality raises answer-blind target recall for every system yet leaves the gap essentially intact. A minimal diagnostic probe that keeps memory visible before the query arrives recovers most of the gap, locating the failure in the query-conditioned interface itself and pointing to routing, deciding which facts must stay visible, as the open problem InMind is built to score.

</details>


### [254] [Closed-Loop Validation-Repair for Healthcare Interoperability: A Multi-Model Study of Schema Compliance in Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24371)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估三种开源大语言模型在医疗场景下输出标准化结构结果（如ICD-10、CPT、HL7 FHIR）的合规性，并提出验证-修复框架显著提升合规率，实现医疗信息系统间高效互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型具备临床推理能力，但其输出往往不符合医疗信息系统标准（schema），阻碍与电子病历等系统集成。因此，提升模型输出的标准化合规性是实现医疗互操作的关键。

Method: 本研究在本地部署Qwen2.5 7B、Llama 3.1 8B和Gemma2 9B三种开源模型，设置320个临床场景，涵盖10个医学专科，得出960组模型-场景配对。分别在基础和验证-修复两种条件下，评估模型输出标准结构的合规性，分析模型一致性和常见错误类型。

Result: 三种模型基础合规率在85.9%~91.6%，主要错误为医学缩写和代码前缀等表示层格式违规，模型对IT标准不敏感。引入验证-修复框架后，合规率提升至98.4%~99.4%，绝对提升7.8~12.5个百分点，统计学显著（McNemar p<0.001）。大多数错误经一两次迭代可修正。

Conclusion: 验证-修复框架可作为医疗互操作的有效模型级安全机制，极大提升大模型输出结构化数据的标准合规性，为后续临床系统集成和应用打下坚实基础。

Abstract: Healthcare interoperability requires AI systems to produce structured outputs conforming to standardized schemas including ICD-10 for diagnostic coding, CPT for procedure billing, and HL7 FHIR for data exchange. While large language models demonstrate clinical reasoning capabilities, their integration into electronic health record systems faces a critical barrier: schema noncompliance. We evaluate three open-source models, Qwen2.5 7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Gemma2 9B, via local deployment across 320 clinical scenarios spanning ten medical specialties, yielding 960 model-scenario pairs assessed under paired baseline and validation-repair conditions. First, schema noncompliance is consistent across the three model families, with baseline compliance rates ranging from 85.9 to 91.6 percent despite varying architectures and training data, suggesting shared gaps in medical training corpora rather than model-specific limitations. Second, 96 percent of validator-detected failures are representation-level format violations such as alternative medical abbreviations and code prefixes, indicating models follow clinical writing conventions but lack awareness of healthcare IT standards. Third, the validation-repair framework achieves 99.0 percent overall compliance, ranging from 98.4 to 99.4 percent across models, with most errors resolving within one or two iterations. Exact McNemar p-values below 0.001 and absolute improvements of 7.8 to 12.5 percentage points across model sizes confirm statistical significance. These results support closed-loop validation-repair as an effective system-level safeguard for healthcare interoperability, improving schema-level readiness for downstream clinical system integration.

</details>


### [255] [LEX-EC: A Lexical Evidence-Channel Audit Framework for Zero-Shot LLM Personality Classification in Black-Box Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24435)
*Brittany Harbison,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LEX-EC的框架，用于解释大语言模型在性格标签分配中的解释性问题，并通过不同文本类型和模型提示方式分析其表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大语言模型可以轻松根据文本判断性格特质，但模型的可解释性仍存在空白。为解决这一问题，本文提出了LEX-EC框架，使得对模型判断的来源和局限性有更细致的了解。

Method: 设计了LEX-EC黑盒审计框架，通过结合出现率、相关性和受控的词汇遮蔽分析手段，区分文本本身的分布影响和能反映特质信号的语言特征，并分析不同文本类型、词汇遮蔽、提示手段等对模型输出的影响。

Result: 发现不同文本体裁对性格特质的表现不同，随意写作文本含有最广泛但信号仍有限，部分遮蔽后特质信号减弱，单条社交媒体状态几乎无法支撑稳定判断；遮蔽主题或人口属性词后，有些特质信号被削弱但仍能通过功能词、情感词等检测，提示内容长度和特定词类的重要性。

Conclusion: LEX-EC框架能系统评估模型的分类、词级关联、词汇变化下的鲁棒性和提示敏感性，有助于推进大模型在性格标签可解释性的研究，为黑盒解释提供一种全新视角。

Abstract: Large language models may easily assign personality labels from text, but model interpretability remains an open problem. To address this gap, we introduce LEX-EC, a reusable black-box audit framework combining prevalence and agreement diagnostics with controlled lexical ablation to distinguish marginal-distribution effects from trait-associated signal recoverable under restricted evidence. Using this framework, we illustrate how various text genres may exhibit sharply different profiles: free-form essay text contains the broadest, but still weak, signal; in graduate student introductions, an observable Extraversion association weakened after masking; and single Facebook statuses yield little stable evidence even in a trait-balanced sample, indicating a possible lower bound of content or length. Masking topical and demographic content weakened some associations while leaving others detectable from function words, affective terms, and cognitive-style vocabulary. Linguistic prompting shifted model self-explanations but did not eliminate topical content. LEX-EC jointly evaluates classification prevalence, item-level association, chance-corrected agreement, persistence under lexical restriction, and prompt sensitivity in model-generated explanations. Across datasets, models, and prompts, LEX-EC characterizes how trait associations may vary with available lexical evidence, introducing a novel application of lexical methods to black-box interpretability in personality labeling.

</details>


### [256] [Grounding latent algorithm routing in transformer reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24471)
*Xiangbo Zhang,Xiaoxu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过设计ROUTEBENCH基准对transformer在给定不同隐含数据生成机制（算法族）时的“路由”能力进行系统性测试，发现训练得到的dense transformer确实可以基于情境调整内在推理流程，但尚未证实大语言模型在通用场景下的普遍路由能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前关于transformer模型的in-context learning机制研究较多，但尚不清楚其能否在不同的归纳偏置（算法族）下实现自适应调整。论文希望通过受控实验证明transformer是否可以基于上下文情境调整其内部推理/解题流程。

Method: 作者提出了ROUTEBENCH基准，刻意设计了利于全局收缩、稀疏性、鲁棒性和局部性等不同算法偏好的数据生成机制，并用岭回归、Lasso、Huber和kNN作为代表，将这些机制混合在一起进行测试。对44M-612M参数规模的decoder-only transformer从头进行训练，并测量其能否根据隐含的机制实现不同“路由”行为。同时加入控制实验（扰动、激活patching等）验证结果稳健性。

Result: 在306M参数模型上，“路由”能力达到oracle gap的80.9%，并实现route F1 84.1；且该能力对不同自然语言表述和支持样本扰动较稳定。即使采用mixture、Gumbel router等适应性更强的替代方案，route F1和泛化性能也未超过最优的transformer模型。神经内部的“路由”方向可解码且对输出有实际贡献。

Conclusion: 在受控环境下，dense transformer确实可以学会基于不同数据机制的“算法路由”，但此能力尚不能证明现有大语言模型在开放自然语言或未知推理任务中的普适可用性。

Abstract: A central question in the in-context learning literature is whether transformers can organize episode-level adaptation around different inductive-bias families. We study this question in a controlled setting through latent algorithm routing: route-like behavior in which the solver-family preference changes with the latent data-generating regime while prompt form is held fixed, remains stable under nuisance perturbations, and is selectively influenced by targeted activation interventions without large losses in answer quality. We introduce ROUTEBENCH, a diagnostic benchmark whose regimes differentially favor global shrinkage, sparsity, robustness, and locality, operationalized by ridge-like, lasso-like, Huber-like, and kNN-like family representatives. Across dense decoder-only transformers trained from scratch at 44M-612M parameters, a 306M model closes 80.9 percent of the oracle-routing gap and achieves route F1 of 84.1. The effect remains substantial under natural-language renderings, shuffled supports, lexical paraphrases, and a unified four-way routing setting. Stronger adaptive alternatives, including an input-conditioned soft mixture and an unsupervised Gumbel router, narrow the gap but remain below the 306M and 612M models on route F1 and OOD performance. Probe controls and matched activation-patching controls further show that route-relevant internal directions are decodable and functionally involved in solver-family-consistent output behavior. These results provide controlled evidence that dense transformers trained on ROUTEBENCH can develop route-like internal variables, but they do not establish universal routing in pretrained language models or unrestricted natural-language reasoning.

</details>


### [257] [SINT-Flow: Schema Integration using Large Language Model Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24492)
*Keti Korini,Christian Bizer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大模型的全自动模式集成框架SINT-Flow，能够高效地将多个关系表集成为统一的全局模式，尤其能处理包含多个实体类型属性的非规范化表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模式集成方法难以处理含有多种实体类型属性的非规范化数据表，并且通常缺乏自动化和处理复杂情况的能力。因此，亟需一种能自动且高效处理复杂模式集成任务的方法。

Method: SINT-Flow框架由五个基于大语言模型（LLM）的处理单元组成，可以组合成端到端自动化工作流，支持模式分解、实体识别、属性提取和表合并等功能。其创新点之一在于能自动将混杂多种实体信息的表分解为实体特定的子表。并且，框架集成了自一致性策略和审查循环机制以提升匹配准确性。

Result: 通过自建的SINT-Bench基准集（包括93个含多实体类型的数据表），分别用GPT-5.2和Qwen-3.6-27B等大模型评估了SINT-Flow。结果显示，实体类型识别F1分数≥96%，属性检测≥85%，模式映射≥83%。消融实验也证明了自一致性和审查机制的有效性。

Conclusion: SINT-Flow实现了高准确度的全自动化复杂模式集成，显著优于传统方法，且能灵活处理多实体类型的非规范化表。其自动化、灵活性和高性能为数据整合任务提供了强有力的技术支撑。

Abstract: The goal of schema integration is, given a set of input schemata or tables, to derive a global, unified schema that is able to represent the concepts, attributes, and relationships of all input tables in a coherent fashion. This paper presents SINT-Flow, a schema integration framework composed of five LLM-based operators that can be combined into workflows to perform fully automated, end-to-end schema integration. In contrast to existing approaches, SINT-Flow can process denormalized source tables that contain attributes describing multiple entity types. During the schema integration process, these tables are decomposed into separate entity-specific relations. To evaluate SINT-Flow, we introduce SINT-Bench, a schema integration benchmark comprising 10 schema integration tasks consisting of altogether 93 relational tables, including tables that describe multiple types of entities. We evaluate SINT-Flow using GPT-5.2 as well as the open-weight model Qwen-3.6-27B as alternative backbone models. Using these models, SINT-Flow achieves F1 scores of at least 96% for entity-type detection, 85% for attribute detection, and 83% for schema mapping. Furthermore, we perform an ablation study to prove the utility of the applied self-consistency strategy as well as the inclusion of a review loop into the schema matching operator.

</details>


### [258] [Systematic Analysis of Large Language Models and Transformer-Based Machine Translation for English-Tamil and Tamil-English Across Diverse Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24515)
*Sriharshaa S,Sangeetha Sivanesan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了多语种翻译模型在英-泰米尔及泰米尔-英方向的表现，并探讨数据质量、领域匹配及注意力机制对低资源语言翻译的影响，显示大模型少样本学习也具有较好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对泰米尔等低资源语言平行语料稀缺、领域多样和形态复杂带来的翻译难题，亟需评估不同翻译模型及方法的有效性。

Method: 在多个数据集（NTREX、EnTamV2、WikiMatrix、PMIndia）上，使用监督式NMT系统、NLLB、mBART等模型，结合BLEU和chrF进行评估，并通过注意力机制分析模型内部对齐。此外，尝试以泰米尔大语言模型(TamilLaMA)进行上下文提示的少样本翻译，并与传统监督方式做定性比较。

Result: 1. 数据集质量与领域匹配显著影响模型翻译性能。2. 注意力机制有助于模型结果的可解释性。3. 少样本大语言模型也能产生结构合理的泰米尔翻译。

Conclusion: 多语种大型模型和注意力机制能提升低资源语言翻译的可解释性和质量，数据选择极为关键，利用大模型少样本能力为低资源翻译提供新思路。

Abstract: The challenge of Machine Translation for low resource languages such as Tamil is primarily caused by the restricted amount of parallel data for these languages, as well as their substantial amount of domain variation and morphological complexity. This research presents the comprehensive evaluation of the performance of several multilingual translation models on English-Tamil and Tamil-English translations across multiple datasets: NTREX, EnTamV2, WikiMatrix and PMIndia. This study evaluates supervised NMT systems, NLLB and mBART, using both the BLEU and chrF metric, and examines how these systems perform on data of different quality levels and domains. This performs an attention-based analysis to increase model interpretability by visualising the alignments of tokens in an English source text and their Tamil translations and vice-versa to provide insight into how they make translations. This study also demonstrates that using in-context prompting can provide an excellent way to perform a few-shot translation of English to Tamil and Tamil-English using a Tamil capable TamilLaMA model, and compare this to supervised approaches qualitatively. These findings show that the quality of the datasets and their alignment with the domain will greatly affect the performance of the model, that attention-based mechanisms can aid in explain ability, and that few-shot large language models can still produce structurally coherent translations of Tamil.

</details>


### [259] [From transcription to semantic corpus analysis: unsupervised learning of sentence representations for ancient languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24542)
*Th{é}otime de la Selle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文针对自动文本识别（ATR）在处理古代语言时带来的噪声和异构文本问题，提出了两种完全无监督的句子嵌入自适应方法（TSDAE和CSE），显著提升了文本复用检测等下游任务的表现，并发布了在线工具Paraphrasis。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语种编码器和专用语言模型在古代语言上的句子嵌入迁移效果较差，且缺少标注的相似性数据，严重影响了数字人文学科如文本重用检测、语义搜索等任务。研究意在探索如何无需标注数据、直接利用原始句子对古代语言模型进行自适应，以满足下游任务需求。

Method: 研究提出并评估了两种无监督适应策略：TSDAE和对比式句子嵌入（CSE），以已存在的专用token级语言模型为基础，仅依赖原始句子数据将其优化为特定语料的句子编码器。以拉丁语和古希腊语的教父文学为案例，设置二元检测和对应检索两项评估任务，并设计噪声模拟实验，与多语种、专用、蒸馏和有监督微调等多种基线方法进行对比。

Result: 自适应后的TSDAE和CSE句子编码器在两个任务上均超越所有基线；TSDAE在大规模域内语料中检测任务最佳，CSE在检索任务上表现突出，且仅用4-8k原始句子即可实现最优，并可迁移跨作者、跨作品及抗噪声能力强，训练门槛低。通过UMAP可视化分析了不同策略对嵌入空间的影响。

Conclusion: 所提无监督适应方法可高效将古代语言token级模型转化为强大的句子编码器，极大提升了文本重用检测等任务的性能。相关完整流程及工具已公开，方便非专业用户利用。

Abstract: Automatic Text Recognition (ATR) now supplies digital humanities with large volumes of unstructured, heterogeneous, and often noisy text in ancient languages. Downstream semantic analysestext reuse identification, alignment, and semantic search-rely on sentence embeddings, yet existing methods transfer poorly to ancient languages: generic multilingual encoders underperform, specialized language models yield anisotropic representation spaces, and labeled similarity data is unavailable. We study two fully unsupervised strategies - TSDAE and contrastive sentence embedding (CSE) - that adapt a specialized token-level language model into a corpus-specific sentence encoder using only raw sentences. On the philologically central case of biblical reuse in patristic literature (2,935 expert-verified parallels in Latin and Ancient Greek, from Augustine, Jerome, and Athanasius), we decompose reuse identification into two separately evaluated tasks-binary detection and correspondence retrieval-and benchmark the adapted encoders against multilingual, specialized, distilled, and supervised fine-tuned baselines, as well as on artificially noised data simulating HTR artifacts and scribal abbreviations. The adapted encoders outperform all baselines on both tasks, with complementary profiles: TSDAE leads detection given a large in-domain corpus, while CSE leads retrieval, reaches its optimum with as few as 4-8k raw in-domain sentences-a few tens of seconds of training on a laptop GPU-and transfers across works and authors, including to noisy post-ATR text when retrained directly on it. UMAP atlases relate the geometric effect of each strategy to the measured gains, and the full pipeline-segmentation, fine-tuning, cross-corpus semantic search-is made available to non-specialists through the online tool Paraphrasis.

</details>


### [260] [From Data to Device: ELMOD An Efficient German-First 2.7B Language Model for Mobile Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24585)
*Darina Gold,Alexander Schwirjow,Viktor Haag,Viktor Hangya,Joel Schlotthauer,Fabian Küch,Luzian Hahn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ELMOD是一种体积小、专为德语及硬件资源有限设备设计的高效语言模型，仅2.7B参数，但性能可与7B模型媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前主流大语言模型多以英语为主，且模型大、推理资源需求高，难以在设备端高效部署。针对德语环境的高效小模型亟需开发。

Method: 使用55,000小时H100 GPU、仅用公开数据训练，专门开发德语专属数据预处理（针对形态变化、复合词及正字法），引入质量筛选及重述流程以提升指令数据质量并降低算力消耗。

Result: ELMOD在德语任务中表现优异，在参数小于3B级别具领先性，效果接近七十亿参数模型。

Conclusion: 通过德语专向设计和高质量数据流程，ELMOD实现了强大的紧凑德语模型，适合在资源受限硬件部署，推动德语模型高效化发展。

Abstract: We present ELMOD - Efficient Language Model for On-Device Deployment - a compact (2.7B) German language model designed for efficient inference on resource-constrained hardware. ELMOD was trained on a limited computational budget (55k H100 GPU hours) using exclusively publicly available data. We developed a suite of German-specific data pre-processing, which differ from English-oriented counterparts in their handling of morphological variation, compounding, and orthographic conventions. Furthermore, we introduced a quality filtering and rephrasing step, which increased the instructional quality of the data, improved performance during the annealing phase, and reduced overall compute requirements. Thanks to our architectural model and data choices, including prefiltering, our educational-quality filtering and rephrasal to raise the educational-quality, ELMOD is the strongest performer in its size class (<3B), matching the performance of 7B-parameter models in German.

</details>


### [261] [D-Score: A Spectral Hidden-State Signal for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24586)
*Bianca Raimondi,Davide Evangelista,Maurizio Gabbrielli,Elena Loli Piccolomini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了D-Score指标，通过模型隐层激活的奇异值分布检测大语言模型生成文本中的虚假与幻觉内容，仅需一次前向传播即可实现高效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常生成流畅但虚假、不被证据支持或与自身内部知识不一致的文本，现有检测方法依赖外部验证或多次生成，效率低下。本文旨在寻找一种只借助模型自身隐层表示、无需外部资源、能高效检测幻觉内容的方法。

Method: 作者提出D-Score指标，分析单次前向传播过程中模型某隐层激活矩阵的奇异值分布。具体地，通过统计激活矩阵中多少个奇异值接近第一大的奇异值，将该数量作为幻觉评分。当D-Score超出阈值时，判定该输入文本为幻觉内容。理论上，这反映了模型同时编码主张内容和不确定性或反证信息，使得激活分布在更多方向上扩展。

Result: 在FAVA-Annotation和RAGTruth数据集上实验表明，D-Score能够有效区分幻觉与非幻觉文本，是一种强有力的隐藏态信号，同时无需外部验证者、检索或多轮生成，方法简单高效。

Conclusion: D-Score为检测大模型文本幻觉问题提供了一种高效新颖的内部判别方式，有望在无需外部资源的场景下辅助大模型输出可信内容。

Abstract: Large Language Models can produce fluent text that is false, unsupported by the available evidence, or inconsistent with information that appears to be internally represented by the model. We study hallucination detection from the geometry of hidden activations and introduce the D-Score, a simple spectral statistic computed from a single forward pass. For a fixed model, layer, and tolerance parameter, the D-Score counts how many singular directions of the hidden activation matrix have singular values that remain close to the leading one. We use this quantity as a hallucination score, classifying an input text as hallucinated when its D-Score is larger than a pre-defined quantity. The motivation is that, when a model processes a text that conflicts with information available in its own internal state, the hidden representation may encode both the asserted content and some form of counter-evidence, uncertainty, correction, or lack of support; this can make the hidden trajectory spread across additional singular directions. We formalize this intuition through a lightweight spectral argument and evaluate the resulting detector on FAVA-Annotation and RAGTruth. The experiments indicate that the D-Score is a strong hidden-state signal for hallucination detection, while requiring no external verifier, no retrieval step, and no multiple generations.

</details>


### [262] [PIVOT: Efficient Query-Group Indexing for Token-Level Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24593)
*Hong Liu,Yuan Cheng,Lin Niu,Yi Su,Yufei Xue,Anmin Liu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PIVOT，一种用于Token级稀疏注意力机制的新型索引方法，通过共享前缀扫描提升大模型推理效率，同时基本保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的Token级稀疏注意力（如DeepSeek Sparse Attention, DSA）虽优化了注意力计算，但索引器需每层对全部前序Token打分，计算复杂度仍为O(L^2)，成为新瓶颈。且实际中相邻Query选择的top-k token高度重叠，打分分布呈长尾，可加以利用。

Method: PIVOT通过将相近的queries分组，合并成一个代理query，进行一次全前缀扫描获得候选集合，然后为每个query从中选择各自的top-k，有两种变体：PIVOT-Reuse（全组共享top-k，极致加速）和PIVOT-Refine（对候选集逐query重排，保准确性，开销略升），推理阶段分组方式不同。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.2与GLM-5.1长序列评测任务上，PIVOT能在准确率基本保持的情况下，实现最多4倍的索引加速，端到端时延降低1.6倍。

Conclusion: PIVOT无需额外训练且可直接替换现有索引器，在高效性和准确性之间通过多种策略灵活权衡，有望提升大模型的长序列推理能力。

Abstract: Token-level sparse attention, as implemented by DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) in production systems, makes the downstream attention efficient but shifts the bottleneck to the indexer that feeds it. To select the top-k tokens for each query, the indexer must still score every preceding token, incurring a cost of O(L^2) per layer for a sequence of length L. We observe that this per-query scan is largely redundant: nearby queries select highly overlapping top-k tokens, and the indexer scores are long-tailed along the key axis. We exploit these properties in PIVOT, Proxy Indexing Via One full-prefix Traversal, a training-free, drop-in replacement for the DSA indexer that shares one prefix scan across a group of nearby queries. PIVOT aggregates a group into a single proxy query, performs one shared full-prefix scan to obtain a candidate set, and then selects a top-k for each query from that set. Two variants trade speed for fidelity: PIVOT-Reuse shares the proxy top-k across the group for maximum speed, whereas PIVOT-Refine re-scores the candidate set with the indexer of each query and then selects an individual top-k, matching the dense indexer at a small additional cost. A single algorithm covers both inference phases, differing only in how groups are formed: fixed-size groups of consecutive queries in prefill, and the queries decoded together in one multi-token prediction (MTP) step in decode. On DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5.1 across LongBench and RULER, PIVOT matches the accuracy of the dense DSA indexer while accelerating it by up to 4x and reducing end-to-end latency by up to 1.6x at long context.

</details>


### [263] [Looping Is Not Reliability: State-Bound Evidence and Typed Revision Contracts for Agentic Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24604)
*Xueping Gao,Jianwei Yang,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了编码代理中常见的生成-测试-修改(GTR)循环，特别关注在反复修复过程中正确性验证与保留的难题，并提出了一种基于证据绑定的可执行机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然GTR循环广泛应用于自动修复，但单纯的反复尝试无法保证修复的可靠性。当前正确补丁可能在后续迭代中丢失或无法正确验证，缺少机制确保修复的持续正确性和可追溯性。

Method: 作者通过一组严密的实验证明传统GTR流程在多轮修订中的正确性衰减，并在多种实验（含对照分支与大型模型复制）下分析了当前与陈旧执行痕迹对修复成效的影响。此外，论文还提出并实现了基于证据绑定的修复合约，自动保存和认证已验证的修复状态，并生成可审计的接收收据。

Result: 实验显示，使用陈旧的执行痕迹显著损害修复正确性，但采取前瞻性政策消除了正确分支的损害同时降低了错误起点的修复率。多模型、不同仓库和技术栈的实验未展示出统计学显著提升，但展示了GTR流程复杂性和关键因素。实现了一套类型化的证据绑定合约并发布了参考实现。

Conclusion: 单纯依靠GTR循环并不能保证修复的持续可靠性。需要理论上有保障的合约机制确保修复结果的可追溯性和可验证性。论文的贡献在于理论分析和可验证参考实现，为后续可信自动修复奠定基础。

Abstract: Generate--test--revise loops are common in coding agents, but repetition alone provides no reliability guarantee. We study the gap between finding a correct patch and retaining, verifying, and submitting it. A sealed five-seed study over 30 HumanEval repairs produces 900 three-revision trajectories. Under forced revision, current correctness with current traces falls from 0.820 after one revision to 0.673 after two, although ever-correct rises to 0.847. Two common-state studies use 2,430 branches from identical frozen programs to remove post-treatment risk-set bias. In a prespecified 14B replication, stale traces harm 34/135 correct starts versus 4/135 with current traces, a 22.2-point increase (task-cluster 95\% CI $[8.9,37.0]$, exact Holm $p=0.0337$). A prospective 540-rollout policy eliminates observed correct-start harm but reduces wrong-start repair and fails its joint criterion. Repository experiments over 24 bugs and four coder stacks expose floor effects and component heterogeneity without Holm-significant effects. We therefore separate admission, preservation, grounded certification, competence, and liveness. We derive an evidence-bound typed loop contract and instantiate its mechanically enforceable subset in a reference implementation that binds verifier evidence to exact code states, preserves verified checkpoints, and emits auditable admission receipts. The implementation is an executable specification and conformance artifact, not evidence of improved repair competence or calibrated verifier dependence.

</details>


### [264] [Kimi K3: Open Frontier Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24653)
*Kimi Team,Tongtong Bai,Yifan Bai,Yiping Bao,M. C.,Jianfeng Cai,Xinyuan Cai,Peizhou Cao,Yuxuan Cao,Ziwei Chai,Y. Charles,H. S. Che,Guanduo Chen,Guangyu Chen,Guanzheng Chen,Huarong Chen,Jia Chen,Jianlong Chen,Jun Chen,Kexin Chen,Peng Chen,Ruijue Chen,Wentao Chen,Xin Chen,Yang Chen,Yanru Chen,Yifei Chen,Yingjiang Chen,Yuankun Chen,Yujie Chen,Yutian Chen,Zhirong Chen,Dazhi Cheng,Yean Cheng,Jialei Cui,Jingbing Cui,Anqi Dai,Jiaqi Deng,Hao Ding,Rui Ding,Shaofeng Ding,Mengfan Dong,Mengnan Dong,Yuhao Dong,Yuxin Dong,Angang Du,Chenzhuang Du,Dikang Du,Jusen Du,Yulun Du,Yu Fan,Jing Feng,Qiulin Feng,Yichen Feng,Kelin Fu,Qiang Fu,Fuxuan Gao,Hongcheng Gao,Jingyue Gao,Tong Gao,Weijia Gao,Shangyi Geng,Jie Gong,Linhu Gong,Shengao Gong,Xiaochen Gong,Qizheng Gu,Yicheng Gu,Shuhao Guan,Haiqing Guo,Shiqi Guo,Xiang Guo,Zhengyan Guo,Beixi Hao,Wenxin Hao,Xiaoru Hao,Dailan He,Haotian He,Lehan He,Qi He,Weiran He,Xinran He,Xinyi He,Yibo He,Yunjia He,Chao Hong,Tiange Hong,Hao Hu,Jiaxi Hu,Ruikun Hu,Weiming Hu,Yangyang Hu,Zhenxing Hu,Liang Hua,Jinbin Huang,Ke Huang,Ruiyuan Huang,Siying Huang,Weixiao Huang,Yan Huang,Zhengjie Huang,Zhiqi Huang,Yulong Hui,Chaobo Jia,Yutong Jiang,Zhejun Jiang,Zuoyou Jiang,Wenyi Jin,Xinyi Jin,Yu Jing,Huanjun Kong,Guokun Lai,Aidi Li,Cheng Li,Chengyuan Li,Cong Li,Fang Li,Guanyu Li,Haoyang Li,Jia Li,Junxiong Li,Lei Li,Letian Li,Lincan Li,Weihong Li,Wentao Li,Xintong Li,Yang Li,Yishen Li,Yiwei Li,Yuxiao Li,Zhaowei Li,Zhaoxi Li,Zheming Li,Zhengxiao Li,Zhiyuan Li,Jiawei Lin,Xiaohan Lin,Yibo Lin,Zichao Lin,Ziyan Lin,Bill Liu,Boxiao Liu,Chuan Liu,Liang Liu,Shaowei Liu,Shudong Liu,Shuran Liu,Tianwei Liu,Weizhou Liu,Yangyang Liu,Yanming Liu,Yibo Liu,Yipeng Liu,Zhengying Liu,Zhiheng Liu,Enzhe Lu,Haoyu Lu,Linqiang Lu,Tingzhan Lu,Zhiyuan Lu,Aotian Luo,G. Luo,Junyu Luo,Yifan Luo,B. Xu,Qingtao Xu,Shuyao Xu,Suting Xu,Tiantian Xu,Tianxiang Xu,Weixin Xu,Xinran Xu,Yangchuan Xu,Ye Xu,Yueni Xu,Ziyao Xu,Haonan Xue,Junjie Yan,Yaoyao Yan,Fan Yang,Guangyao Yang,Hao Yang,Junwei Yang,Ruoyu Yang,Wenjie Yang,Xiaofei Yang,Xinyu Yang,Yi Yang,Yiling Yang,Ying Yang,Yuchen Yang,Zhen Yang,Zhilin Yang,Zian Yang,Zuhao Yang,Haotian Yao,Dan Ye,Haoran Ye,Wenjie Ye,Zhanbo Ye,Bohong Yin,Haoxiang Yin,Xietong Yin,Chengzhen Yu,Haozhen Yu,Longhui Yu,Shengnan Yu,Shuying Yu,Tianxiang Yu,Enming Yuan,Mengjie Yuan,Tongtian Yue,Wei Yue,Yang Yue,Dunyuan Zha,Haobing Zhan,B. Zhang,Dehao Zhang,Fei Zhang,Hao Zhang,Haoyuan Zhang,Huanyu Zhang,Jiapei Zhang,Jiaxuan Zhang,Jin Zhang,Kaiyi Zhang,Miaozhen Zhang,Puqi Zhang,Qinglei Zhang,Rong Zhang,Rui Zhang,Shaoshuai Zhang,Shiyi Zhang,Xiaobin Zhang,Xiaoyun Zhang,Y. Zhang,Yangkun Zhang,Ye Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Yikun Zhang,Yizhi Zhang,Yongting Zhang,Yu Zhang,Yutao Zhang,Yutong Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Zijing Zhang,Bin Zhao,Chenguang Zhao,Feifan Zhao,Jinglun Zhao,Jinxiang Zhao,Shuai Zhao,Wenshuo Zhao,Xiangyu Zhao,Xuanle Zhao,Yikai Zhao,Zijia Zhao,Haozhi Zheng,Huabin Zheng,Ruihan Zheng,Shaojie Zheng,Tengyang Zheng,Haofeng Zhong,Lei Zhong,Longguang Zhong,M. Zhou,Qiankang Zhou,Runjie Zhou,Ruozhang Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yiqiao Zhou,Zaida Zhou,Jinguo Zhu,Liya Zhu,Xinhao Zhu,Yangjunfeng Zhu,Yuxuan Zhu,Zhen Zhu,Chen Zhuang,Weiyu Zhuang,Xinxing Zu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Kimi K3 是一个拥有 2.8 万亿参数、支持百万 Token 长上下文，并具原生视觉处理能力的 Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）大模型，在多项评测中达到前沿水准，其全部权重也已开放释放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动大模型在超长上下文、异构专家激活、高效推理等方面的研究，并提供开源权重以带动领域发展。

Method: 采用 Kimi Delta Attention 和 Attention Residuals 提升信息流动，配合 Stable LatentMoE（896个专家，激活16个/Token），以及优化的训练和数据策略，并引入多领域强化学习训练。

Result: 在推理、编程、视觉等多任务中达到领先开源/专有模型的水平，整体扩展效率较前代提升约2.5倍，支撑百万 Token 上下文。

Conclusion: Kimi K3 综合表现处于当前开源大模型的前沿，仅次于顶级闭源模型（如Claude Fable 5和GPT-5.6 Sol），已开放模型权重，为社区研究和应用提供重要资源。

Abstract: We introduce Kimi K3, a 2.8T parameter Mixture-of-Experts model with 104 billion activated parameters, native vision capabilities, and a 1-million-token context window. Kimi K3 is built on Kimi Delta Attention and Attention Residuals, which improve information flow across sequence length and model depth. Together with Stable LatentMoE, which effectively activates 16 of 896 routed experts per token, and refined training and data recipes, these advances yield an approximately 2.5x improvement in overall scaling efficiency over Kimi K2. Post-training highlights reinforcement learning across general, agentic, and coding domains and multiple reasoning-effort levels, enabling compositional generalization and robust long-horizon execution. At 2.8T scale, Kimi K3 is supported by infrastructure advances in multiple areas: algorithm-system co-design for KDA, perfectly balanced expert-parallel training with efficient memory management, million-token agentic RL with persistent rollout and sandbox states, and deployment innovations. Extensive evaluations show that Kimi K3 achieves frontier-level performance across long-horizon coding, agentic, knowledge, reasoning, and vision tasks. While its overall performance still trails the most powerful proprietary models, namely Claude Fable 5 and GPT-5.6 Sol, Kimi K3 consistently outperforms other open and proprietary models evaluated in our suite. We release the full Kimi K3 model weights to facilitate future research and accelerate the broader deployment and adoption of frontier intelligence.

</details>


### [265] [DataOrchestra: Learning to Orchestrate Per-Example Curation of Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24717)
*Zhen Huang,Yikun Wang,Shijie Xia,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为DataOrchestra的数据预处理框架，通过为每个训练样本定制数据处理流程，从而提升大语言模型的下游表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型预训练数据处理方法通常采用统一的、固定的策略，缺乏针对单个样本的自适应处理，影响模型效果。

Method: DataOrchestra统一不同处理操作，并为每个样本动态生成处理流程。具体包括判断数据块是否丢弃、保持原样或清洗，并为清洗的数据块选择多种后续处理操作，如程序化编辑或基于LLM重写。每步重写还会生成具体指令，由相应模型执行。

Result: 在Web数据上使用DataOrchestra预处理并从零训练0.5B到7B模型，在11项基准测试中，相较于单一数据处理方法都有稳定提升。同时对数学预训练也更有效，并能减少不必要操作带来的计算消耗。

Conclusion: DataOrchestra通过自适应并具体化每个数据样本的处理流程，优于传统统一策略，提升了LLM性能且降低了部分计算成本。

Abstract: Pretraining data processing is critical to the downstream performance of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, many existing approaches define a fixed processing strategy at the corpus or domain level and apply it uniformly to many examples, without adapting to the needs of each example. We propose DataOrchestra, a framework that unifies different processing operations and orchestrates an example-specific pipeline for each example. Given a chunk of pretraining data, an orchestrator decides whether to drop, untouch, or clean it. For a chunk to be cleaned, it selects one or more downstream operations, ranging from programmatic editing to different forms of LLM-based rewriting. For each rewriting step, it further generates a concrete instruction, which is executed by the corresponding downstream tool model. We pretrain models from 0.5B to 7B from scratch on web data processed by DataOrchestra and observe stable average gains over individual data-processing methods across 11 benchmarks. DataOrchestra is also effective for math continued pretraining and outperforms stronger processing baselines, while reducing processing compute by skipping unnecessary downstream operations.

</details>


### [266] [The Physics of Multi-Turn Long-Horizon Planning: From Pre-training to Post-training via Single- and Multi-Teacher On-Policy Agentic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24720)
*Tianyi Men,Zhuoran Jin,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可控的多轮长程规划研究环境，系统性地分析了基础模型Agent在三阶段中的规划能力习得、塑造和整合过程，发现显式世界模型、多教师蒸馏等提升了长期泛化与多环境适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮长程规划模型主要依赖互联网上不可控的数据，无法系统分析模型的规划能力是如何习得、塑造与融合的，存在数据不透明与可迁移性不足问题。

Method: 提出一个统一且可控的多轮环境，按照三个阶段：1）预训练时探究数据格式与显式建模对规划能力的影响；2）利用GRPO和OPD两种后训练方法，通过互信息区分通用与任务内规划能力，并分析各自有效区间和缺陷；3）采用多教师on-policy蒸馏（MOPD）以融合不同来源的能力，测试跨环境泛化、持续学习与冲突影响。

Result: 实验发现：基于CoT状态转移的显式世界模型可显著提升长程规划泛化，仅具原子技能难以组合泛化；少量高质量长程数据远优于大量原子数据；劣质样本对长程表现伤害明显；OPD较GRPO在低质长程场景表现更稳健；MOPD可融合兼容的能力，但模式冲突时干扰显著。

Conclusion: 多轮长程规划能力可通过数据构造与明确定义的后训练手段系统提升与融合。精细设计数据与后训练方式（如显式世界模型、多教师蒸馏）是提升基础模型Agent跨任务、跨环境泛化与持续学习的关键。

Abstract: Multi-turn long-horizon planning is critical for foundation model agents, yet how to fundamentally improve it remains unclear. Existing models are trained on uncontrollable and opaque Internet data, making it difficult to identify how planning ability is acquired, shaped, and integrated. To address this challenge, we introduce a unified and controlled multi-turn environment that enables precise control. It allows systematically study long-horizon planning across three stages. (1) Planning ability acquisition during pre-training. We study data format, distribution, and quality. Explicit world model construction through CoT state transition modeling yields stronger long-horizon generalization. Atomic skills alone are insufficient for compositional generalization, whereas a litte long-horizon data works. Moreover, suboptimal trajectories severely impair performance because errors amplify over long horizons. (2) Planning ability shaping via GRPO and OPD post-training. Through mutual information, we distinguish general planning patterns from task-specific planning knowledge. For planning patterns, we identify three application regions of post-training: unnecessary, effective, and unsupported. OPD has a broader effective region than GRPO under low-quality and long-horizon settings, as it provides more consistent update directions. For planning knowledge, distilling unseen procedures from a teacher with different knowledge may impair student's prior world modeling without fully establishing new knowledge. (3) Planning ability integration through MOPD post-training. We show that multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates capabilities by converging to shared planning-pattern across environments. Compatible patterns enable cross-environment generalization, partially shared patterns support continual learning, while completely conflicting patterns cause severe interference.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [A Replay-Constrained Simulation Framework for Personalization of Powered Knee--Ankle Prosthesis Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22858)
*Duong Le,Ryan Posh,Shihao Cheng,Maani Ghaffari,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于模拟的个性化假肢腿阻抗控制器优化框架，在模拟器中重现步态动态，大大提升了高维参数控制的可扩展性和效率，实现了从模拟到硬件的可靠迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假肢腿阻抗控制器个性化方法效率低，主要依赖真人反复实验，无法高效优化多参数。受益于机器人领行领域的sim-to-real技术，作者希望突破人体难建模的问题，实现假肢控制高维参数的个性化优化。

Method: 搭建了一个新颖的MuJoCo仿真平台，通过重放真实步态时的髋关节运动和地面反作用力，绕过对人体神经肌肉系统的建模难题。利用深度强化学习同时优化膝踝两个关节的阻尼、刚度、平衡角等多个参数，全部由假肢器件自身传感器信号驱动奖励函数。

Result: 在三名大腿截肢者0.8m/s平地步行实验中，仿真最优策略与硬件最优策略高度一致（Pearson r=0.96-0.997）；硬件实验中，最优控制策略能被仿真预测到前五名。对比未个性化基线，仿真学习控制器的生物拟态奖励提升了42-59%。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了假肢腿阻抗控制的个性化优化效率和维度扩展能力，为假肢及其他辅助设备高维、拟态控制器参数化（如神经网络控制器）的未来个性化优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Personalization of impedance controllers for powered prosthetic legs is critical to accommodating individual gait biomechanics but remains challenging. Existing methods rely on time-intensive human-in-the-loop exploration and/or constrain optimization to low-dimensional, single-joint parameter subspaces. Sim-to-real transfer has enabled high-dimensional locomotion control for legged robots, but in assistive device control the human partner remains un-modelable. We present a replay-constrained simulation framework: a MuJoCo-based simulator reproduces prosthetic knee-ankle dynamics while replaying recorded hip kinematics and feedback-based ground reaction forces from individual walking data, bypassing the need to model complex human neuromuscular control mechanisms. We demonstrate the framework with a deep reinforcement learning policy that personalizes phase-dependent stiffness, damping, and equilibrium angle at both joints simultaneously, maximizing a biomimicry-based reward computed solely from onboard prosthesis measurements. Experiments with three participants with transfemoral amputation during level-ground walking at 0.8~m/s demonstrate strong simulation-to-hardware predictive validity (Pearson $r=0.96$--$0.997$). The best-performing policy on hardware was consistently predicted within the top five simulation policies for all participants. The learned controllers improved overall biomimicry rewards by 42--59\% relative to the unpersonalized baseline. The framework supports scalable high-dimensional personalization of powered prosthetic legs and is amenable to extension to higher-dimensional controller parameterizations such as neural-network controllers.

</details>


### [268] [Pose-Aware Modeling to Mitigate Pose-Related Artifacts in Tactile Gloves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22964)
*Tianhong Catherine Yu,Ziyi Kou,Mia Huang,Taylor Niehues,Yiyue Luo,Li Guan,Dingtian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需手套硬件改动、基于算法对手势相关伪影（PRAs）进行抑制的方法，提高了触觉手套在弱力检测中的准确性和实际应用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性触觉手套因其灵活和舒适，被广泛用于机器人操作和远程操控，但现有柔性传感器容易受到手势变化的影响，在检测小接触力时常出现伪影（PRAs），导致误检或迟检，限制了低力范围的感知能力。因此，需要新的方法来降低这些伪影，提高检测灵敏度。

Method: 作者系统分析了PRAs与手势、力度之间的关系，在此基础上提出了一种与具体手套硬件无关的算法框架。该方法结合了手势信息（通常已可获取），在触觉转力估计算法中增加一条残差预测支路，专门对手势导致的传感器形变引起的误差进行补偿。

Result: 该方法在3种手套设计和15名用户上进行验证，最低可检测力分别降低了10.4%、12.2%和18.3%，同时在所有评估指标上都获得了一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 该pose-aware算法框架不需更改手套硬件，切实改善了触觉手套在数据采集和各类机器人应用中的可用性，是实际提升柔性手套性能的一种有效途径。

Abstract: Tactile gloves digitize contact and force during hand-object interactions, enabling robotics applications in dexterous manipulation, teleoperation, and learning from demonstration. To preserve hand dexterity and capture the nuances of natural interactions, these gloves and the integrated tactile sensors are designed to be soft, flexible, and comfortable. However, such flexible sensors are sensitive not only to contact forces but also unavoidably to hand pose changes, resulting in pose-related artifacts (PRAs). PRAs are especially problematic in the low-force range, resulting in misdetections or late-onset detections of contact, which raises the minimum detectable force (MDF) of the glove. In this work, we characterize the PRAs in relation to pose and force. Building on these insights, we introduce a glove-agnostic algorithmic framework that leverages hand pose information, which is increasingly available, to mitigate PRAs without glove modifications. Our pose-aware force estimation model augments tactile-to-force pipelines with a residual prediction branch that explicitly accounts for pose-induced sensor deformations. We validate our approach across 3 glove designs and 15 users, reducing MDF by 10.4%, 12.2%, and 18.3%, with consistent improvements across all evaluated metrics. This method provides a practical path to improving the usability of tactile gloves in data collection and diverse robotic applications.

</details>


### [269] [Real2Sim2Real for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation: An AMD ROCm-Based Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22997)
*Qing Yang,Xun Wang,Ziguan Wang,Zhenjiang Li,Hongqiang Wang,Dongdong Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向实体智能体物理AI的端到端AMD技术栈，涵盖数据中心训练、仿真、边缘推理，以及完整的软件支撑，并通过多项实验证明了其效果和兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前主流物理AI（集视觉-语言-动作模型与现实世界智能体）研究多依赖于特定硬件生态（如CUDA）。作者希望打破硬件平台的限制，打造跨平台、效果可复现、开放的物理AI解决方案，加速物理AI的普及和应用。

Method: 作者基于AMD全线硬件（训练用加速卡、仿真GPU、边缘AI芯片等）及ROCm开源软件栈，构建了全流程物理AI系统。通过SmolVLA模型，结合训练、虚实结合、三维重建与物理引擎、以及大规模强化学习，实现了从虚拟到现实的多任务、多平台实验流程。

Result: 文章通过四个实验展示了系统的性能：1）Sim-to-Real机械臂操作；2）基于语言的语义选物任务；3）“现实到模拟”的数据生成流程；4）多平台下的强化学习行走任务。所有实验均在AMD硬件和ROCm平台上原生运行，并可在Radeon云平台复现。结果表明无需依赖CUDA生态也能取得良好性能。

Conclusion: AMD基于ROCm的软件硬件生态为物理AI的发展提供了开放、可复现、可扩展的新路径，有望推动物理AI在实际场景中的普及和落地。

Abstract: Physical AI -- the integration of large vision-language-action (VLA) models with embodied agents that act in the real world -- has emerged as the next major frontier for AI, echoed by industry leaders such as Jensen Huang (``the next big thing is Physical AI, AI with a body,'' GTC Paris, June 2025) and Dr. Lisa Su (`we're entering the world of Physical AI ... this is where AI enters the real world,' CES 2026). This paper presents an end-to-end, fully AMD-accelerated technology stack for embodied manipulation, spanning data-center training silicon, Radeon PRO simulation/rendering GPUs, and Ryzen AI edge compute, unified by the open ROCm software stack. We demonstrate that training and deploying VLA-based manipulation policies does not require a CUDA-locked ecosystem. Four progressive demonstrations are presented: (1) a Sim-to-Real manipulation pipeline trained with SmolVLA and deployed on a physical Franka arm; (2) a semantic, language-grounded object-selection task (`one-of-three'); (3) a Real2Sim synthetic-data generation pipeline that fuses 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions of real scenes with the Genesis physics engine; and (4) large-scale reinforcement learning for quadruped and humanoid locomotion benchmarked across multiple hardware platforms. All pipelines run natively on ROCm + PyTorch on RDNA4 (Radeon AI PRO R9700) and RDNA3.5 (Radeon PRO W7900) hardware and are reproducible on the free Radeon Cloud Platform.

</details>


### [270] [WCM: World-Cognition Model for Generalizable Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22999)
*Yuzhen Chen,KC Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的人机协作模型WCM，使机器人在与人类互动中变得更易于理解和纠正，并通过人类教学持续改进自身能力，在九项现实任务中取得了73.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的机器人主要关注于指令执行，用户难以理解机器人的决策原因，也缺乏有效的纠正或教学机制。为提升人与机器人的协作和交互，亟需更具透明性和可教性的机器人系统。

Method: 本研究提出基于SLAK（感知、逻辑、行动、知识）结构和异步运行架构的世界-认知模型（WCM）。该模型将感知、推理、控制和记忆分开，并实现对话、推理和执行的并行处理。同时引入“人在环教学”模式，使用户能够通过交互机制教学机器人，并将教学过程与自主任务回滚转化为chain-of-thought（CoT）监督，不断优化模型能力。

Result: WCM在九项真实世界的人机交互任务中实现了平均73.8%的成功率，包括未用作CoT微调的保留任务以及通过人类教学学习的长周期任务。

Conclusion: WCM模型显著提升了机器人与人类交互的透明度、可教性和任务完成能力，证明该体系结构对提高现实场景下人机协作类机器人性能具有实际价值。

Abstract: Language agents can now interact fluently with users in software, but robots still struggle to bring comparable interaction to physical tasks. Current robot-control paradigms, including vision-language-action policies and world-model-based planners, are mainly optimized for instruction execution, leaving users with little visibility into why an action is chosen and few mechanisms to redirect, correct, or teach the robot through interaction. To solve this problem, we present the World-Cognition Model (WCM), a human-centered embodied agent built on the SLAK architecture (Sensing, Logic, Action, and Knowledge) and an asynchronous runtime. SLAK separates perception, reasoning, control, and memory, while the runtime allows reasoning, dialogue, and execution to proceed concurrently. WCM further introduces a human-in-the-loop teaching mode that enables users to interactively teach the robot difficult or long-horizon tasks. Teaching episodes and autonomous task rollouts are refined into chain-of-thought supervision to continually improve the model. WCM achieves a 73.8% average success rate across nine real-world human-robot interaction tasks, including tasks held out from CoT fine-tuning and a long-horizon task learned through teaching.

</details>


### [271] [The Curse of Precision: A Data Scaling Law for High-Precision Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23108)
*Cuijie Xu,Yuanfan Xu,Min Xue,Jianjie Lin,Jian Wang,Xudong Zhang,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的针对机器人装配等闭环任务中的精度-数据规模规律（scaling law），发现精度目标越高，所需示范数量增长越快，并给出了精确的数学模型。论文通过实验证明系统改进可提升极限精度，为高精度机器人系统的开发和评估提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规模规律研究主要聚焦开放世界下的泛化能力，缺乏针对如机器人装配这种高精度、闭环任务中精度与数据量关系的系统性分析。实际中实现高精度至关重要，因此迫切需要理论指导如何高效提升系统精度。

Method: 系统性实验，分析在不同精度要求下，所需演示数量的变化规律，提出并验证了数学模型log(N)∝1/(P−c)；通过改变传感器和专家策略等系统组件，进一步测试极限精度c的可调性。

Result: 发现为达成固定成功率，所需示范数量随目标精度P临近极限值c时超指数增长，且log(N)与1/(P−c)成正比。极限精度c取决于整个系统而非物理常数。系统改进（如增加腕部摄像头、优化专家）可以显著降低c，即提升精度上限。

Conclusion: 论文为机器人操作精度的理解和提升提供了新理论框架，量化了系统性能上限，并为高精度操作系统的开发和调试提出了实践指导。

Abstract: While scaling laws for imitation learning have primarily focused on generalization in open-world settings, the relationship between data and precision in closed-world tasks like robotic assembly remains largely unexplored. This paper systematically investigates this relationship and introduces a novel scaling law. We find that to achieve a fixed success rate, the required number of demonstrations $N$ grows super-exponentially as the target precision $P$ approaches a limit $c$. This relationship is accurately captured by the model $\log(N) \propto 1/(P-c)$. Crucially, we reveal that the limit precision $c$ is not a static physical constant of the task but an emergent property of the entire agent system, including its sensors and expert policy. Through experiments on canonical manipulation tasks, we validate this law and demonstrate that improving system components, such as adding a wrist camera or using a more effective expert, measurably lowers $c$, thus expanding the system's achievable precision. Our work provides a new theoretical framework for precision in robotics and a quantitative metric to evaluate system capabilities. Furthermore, these findings provide a practical methodology for guiding the development and debugging of high-precision manipulation systems.

</details>


### [272] [Low-Latency Turn-Taking via Context-Aware Preface Generation in a Real-World Dialogue Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23204)
*Yuki Okafuji,Koji Inoue,Yoshiki Ohira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段增量式对话生成框架，通过分离引言式回复与语音启动来减少大语言模型对话系统中的响应延迟。田野实验结果显示，与无填充或固定填充相比，语境依赖型引言在时间分布上存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的对话系统因需等待全部语音识别后才开始回复生成，导致显著的响应时延。虽然可以用固定填充语（如“请稍等”）缓解，但长时间使用会变得不自然，影响用户体验，因此亟需更高效、自然的解决方案。

Method: 作者提出了两阶段增量框架：一旦用户意图变得可预测，即由意图就绪检测器触发LLM生成短引言式回复。同时，利用语音活动预测模型决定何时播放该引言。在购物中心引导机器人场景下，对无填充、固定填充和上下文引言三种模式进行了对比实验。

Result: 实验证明，固定填充和上下文引言都能显著缩短起始响应延迟（较无填充）；不过，上下文引言的起始延迟比固定填充长，但主回复与引言之间的间隔更短。主观评分差异不大——结果表明引言和主回复的时间分配存在权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效缓解对话系统响应延迟，尤其在特定情境下实现了更自然的交互表现。未来设计需在即时性和整体响应流畅性间权衡，优化用户体验。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based dialogue systems suffer response delays because generation begins only after final speech recognition. While fixed fillers are a workaround, they become unnatural over time. We propose a two-stage incremental framework that decouples prefatory-response preparation from speech onset. Once user intent becomes predictable, an intent readiness detector triggers LLM-based generation of a short prefatory response. Concurrently, a voice activity projection (VAP) model determines when to deliver it. Through a field experiment with a route-guidance robot in a shopping mall, we evaluated three conditions: no-filler, fixed-filler, and contextual-preface. Both fixed-filler and contextual-preface significantly reduced initial response latency relative to no-filler. Relative to fixed-filler, contextual-preface had significantly longer initial response latency but a significantly shorter initial-to-main gap. Exploratory ratings showed no significant differences. These results indicate a timing trade-off.

</details>


### [273] [Sling2Sim2Real: One-Shot Elastic System Identification for Non-Destructive Slingshot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23268)
*Wonjae Kang,Geonwoo Kim,Minseok Song,Daehyung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对弹性物体操纵的高效结构化学习框架Sling2Sim2Real，能够通过一次非破坏性操作确定弹性参数，并实现高效的策略学习与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性物体由于其复杂的高维、非线性和弹性变形特性，导致状态空间巨大，真实环境中学习操纵策略成本高且存在损坏风险。现有方法难以仅凭视觉准确还原真实弹性性质，阻碍了高效的仿真—现实迁移。

Method: 提出Sling2Sim2Real框架，包括两阶段：1）多点初始化的Real2Sim参数辨识方法，利用参数协方差估计弹性属性；2）用校准后的模拟器进行策略学习，并实现零样本Sim2Real迁移。参数辨识仅需一次安全交互，显著减少真实试验需求。

Result: 在弹弓操纵任务与不同弹性物理属性下，采用Franka Emika Panda机械臂实验。结果表明该方法能准确学习策略，在各类目标距离和弹性属性间表现出强泛化能力，大幅降低了真实交互的次数。

Conclusion: Sling2Sim2Real框架有效缩减了真实世界探索需求，实现弹性物体策略学习的高效Sim2Real迁移，为弹性或高维变形物体的操纵提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Elastic object manipulation (EOM) involves highdimensional, nonlinear, and elastic deformations. The diverse deformation properties of elastic objects substantially expand the relevant state space, requiring extensive exploration to learn accurate manipulation policies for tasks such as slingshot manipulation.
